### [1] [Intuition with Evidence: Adaptive Dual Memory for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33019)
*Qiuhe Hong,Jingya Wang,Cuzhen Geng,Shenyao Peng,Munan Ning,Yuyang Liu,Yonghong Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向多模态智能体的双记忆框架，将模型内生的潜隐记忆与多索引情景记忆自适应结合，以同时提升细粒度证据召回和全局理解能力，并在多模态基准上取得整体最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆在多模态智能体中承担两类互补功能：一是对历史交互中细粒度证据的准确召回，二是通过内化经验形成全局理解。已有情景记忆依赖显式历史记录，但存在证据覆盖与整合不足的问题；潜隐记忆能内化经验，却可能缺乏充分的事实依据。单一记忆范式难以适应不同任务对记忆偏好的多样需求。

Method: 作者提出一种双记忆框架，将模型原生潜隐记忆作为直觉先验，与多索引情景记忆联合使用，通过检索条件联合对齐实现精确证据重建。在推理阶段，框架引入无需训练的路由机制，基于Jensen-Shannon散度敏感性和置信度代理指标，根据查询动态判断更适配的记忆来源，并自适应组合两类记忆。

Result: 在多个多模态基准上的广泛实验表明，该方法取得最先进的整体性能，相比原始情景记忆基线提升4.67%。进一步分析显示，情景记忆在证据定位型任务中表现更强，而潜隐记忆有助于整合分散信息，并在检索结果不理想时进行补偿。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，面向多模态智能体长期记忆任务，潜隐记忆与情景记忆并非互斥，而可通过查询感知的自适应路由机制协同增强。双记忆框架能够兼顾精确证据召回与全局语义理解，提升不同任务下的鲁棒性和性能上限。

Abstract: As multimodal agents engage in sustained interactions, long-term memory serves two distinct but complementary functions: fine-grained evidence recall and internalized interpretation for global understanding. Although episodic and latent memory address these demands through explicit historical records and internalized experience, neither paradigm alone can accommodate the diverse memory affinities across tasks, owing to limitations in episodic evidence coverage and integration, and in latent factual grounding. To bridge this divide, we propose a dual-memory framework, unifying a model-native latent memory as an intuitive prior with a multi-indexed episodic memory for exact evidence reconstruction via retrieval-conditioned joint alignment. At inference, the framework adaptively composes these memories through a training-free routing mechanism driven by Jensen-Shannon Divergence (JSD) sensitivity and confidence proxies for query-dependent memory affinity. Extensive evaluations across multimodal benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, outperforming single-paradigm baselines overall (+4.67\% over vanilla Episodic), with further analysis highlighting episodic strengths in evidence-localized tasks and latent contributions to integrating dispersed information and compensating for imperfect retrieval.

</details>


### [2] [Nemotron 3.5 Bolt 74B-A9B 24GB: A Hybrid Vision-Language Model for Local AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33039)
*Ganesh Ajjanagadde,Chris Alexiuk,Luke Alonso,Kumar Anshuman,Maor Ashkenazi,Nave Assaf,Gorkem Batmaz,Omer Belhasin,Shai Ben Nun,Akhiad Bercovich,Pritam Biswas,Caroline Blanco,Tijmen Blankevoort,Michael Boone,Christopher Borden,Kari Briski,Bryan Catanzaro,Zijia Chen,Aishwarya Chhabra,Hung-Yueh Chiang,Annamalai Chockalingam,Jonathan Cohen,Joey Conway,Olivier Delalleau,Gerardo Delgado Cabrera,Leon Derczynski,Li Ding,Will Efstratis,Michael Elad,Ellie Evans,Wes Feely,Michael Fukuyama,Gaurav Garg,Rishabh Garg,Arvind Gopalakrishnan,Shishir Goyal,Kartik Gulia,Hao Guo,Negar Habibi,Scott Hara,Ehsan Hosseini Asl,Rohit Jena,Udi Karpas,Bilal Kartal,Mayank Kaushik,Manoj Kilaru,Mackenzie Kosseim,Oleksii Kuchaiev,Matthieu Le,Raymond Lee,Jian Leong,Jonathan Li,Sheldon Liang,Mingjie Liu,Eileen Long,Katherine Luna,Chenjie Luo,Mingyuan Ma,Arsh Maknojia,Richard Mazzarese,Jessica Medina,Puhui Meng,Yoav Miron,Pavlo Molchanov,David Mosallanezhad,Chetan Mungekar,Marina Neseem,Oluwatobi Olabiyi,Jason Paul,Bhavesh Pawar,Seth Poulos,Anav Prasad,Meredith Price,Sweta Priyadarshi,Haifeng Qian,Vinay Raman,Kaustubh Rao,Hayley Ross,Matan Rusanovsky,Sudeep Sabnis,Karan Sapra,Micah Schaffer,Schmitty,Alex Settle,Ashton Sharabiani,Haran Kumar Shiv Kumar,Rameshwar Shivbhakta,Guillermo Siman,Barnaby Simkin,Oliver Simons,Andrii Skliar,Padmavathy Subramanian,Yoshi Suhara,Ali Taghibakhshi,Nima Tajbakhsh,Andrew Tao,Roy Uziel,Prasoon Varshney,Jose Vergara,Huy Vu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Nemotron 3.5 Bolt 74B-A9B 24GB 是一个面向本地智能体工作负载的混合 Mamba-Transformer 视觉-语言模型，通过 Star Elastic 压缩、2-bit Quantization-Aware Distillation 和 LiLiCorr 推测解码器，在约 24GB 内存约束下兼顾较高精度与 84 TPS 的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在显存受限的本地部署场景中，智能体任务通常需要较强的长时程推理、工具调用、网页浏览和终端交互能力，同时要求可接受的生成速度；现有大型视觉-语言模型往往难以同时满足内存、速度与能力三方面约束。

Method: 该模型源自 Nemotron 3.5 Super VL 120B-A12B 模型族，采用混合 Mamba-Transformer 架构，并结合 Star Elastic 压缩与 2-bit Quantization-Aware Distillation 来降低部署内存占用。为达到目标推理速度，作者还训练了配套的 LiLiCorr speculative drafter。模型整体针对 reasoning、agentic tool use、web & terminal interactions 等智能体场景进行优化。

Result: 在多个智能体相关基准上，该模型在相近内存占用和速度水平下取得了优于领先模型的表现，例如 GDPVal v2 代表通用智能体能力，BrowseComp 代表网页浏览任务，Terminal-Bench 2.1 代表终端使用任务。其在 RTX Spark 上的推理速度约为 84 TPS，虽然这一速度通常与更小激活参数模型相关，但被认为足以支撑长时程智能体应用。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过 Mamba-Transformer 混合架构、压缩量化蒸馏和推测解码的协同设计，可以在 24GB 级内存约束下部署具有较强视觉-语言与智能体交互能力的模型，并在速度-精度权衡上取得较有竞争力的结果。

Abstract: We introduce Nemotron 3.5 Bolt 74B-A9B 24GB, a hybrid Mamba-Transformer vision-language model that optimizes speed and intelligence per GB of memory used for local agentic workloads. Derived from the Nemotron 3.5 Super VL 120B-A12B model family, Nemotron 3.5 Bolt 74B-A9B 24GB combines Star Elastic compression with 2-bit Quantization-Aware Distillation for memory-constrained deployment. The model is optimized for reasoning, agentic tool use, and web & terminal interactions. We also trained a LiLiCorr speculative drafter that is released alongside the model to reach our target inference speed. The resulting model achieves a higher accuracy than leading models with similar memory footprints and speed across many benchmarks for general agentic capabilities (e.g., GDPVal v2), web browsing (e.g., BrowseComp), and terminal use (e.g., Terminal-Bench 2.1), with a relatively high TPS of 84 on RTX Spark that is comparable to models with much lower active parameter counts and more than sufficient for long-horizon agentic applications.

</details>


### [3] [EKT-Router: Expert-Evidence Alignment for Embodied Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33044)
*Weilin Ruan,Yingjie Xu,Yangkai Wei,Bohan Zhou,Shuaibo Li,Harold Haodong Chen,Kaiwen Zhou,Yinchuan Li,Ying-Cong Chen,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 EKT-Router，一种受限联合路由框架，用于具身视觉语言问答。它同时选择推理专家、可选知识和感知工具，并通过预算约束与证据验证保证外部信息只在接受且对选定专家有正向增益时参与作答，从而提升部分观测下的回答效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身视觉语言模型通常只能获得局部且动态变化的观测，因此回答质量不仅取决于模型内部推理能力的选择，也取决于能够获取的外部证据。已有方法中，专家路由主要调整内部计算，工具增强系统主要获取外部证据，但若二者独立选择，容易出现“选中的专家缺少所需证据”或“获取的证据无法帮助专家”的问题。因此需要将专家、知识与感知工具作为一个整体路径进行联合决策。

Method: EKT-Router 构建了一个受调用预算约束的联合路由框架。首先，框架包含三类能力专家：General、Spatial Reasoning 和 Spatiotemporal，并通过从结果中导出的签名刻画不同专家之间重叠的优势能力。其次，系统联合选择专家、可选知识和感知工具，使用统一效用函数对完整的“专家—证据”路径打分。在获取证据后，一个 packet verifier 会验证证据包是否有效，并只在预测该证据对选定专家具有正向条件增益时接受。最终，被选中的专家基于验证后的证据只回答一次；如果所有证据包都被拒绝，则回退到原始观测继续作答。

Result: 在 6 个具身视觉问答基准上，EKT-Router 的平均得分为 61.4，高于所比较的系统；在 10 个通用视觉语言基准上平均得分为 74.7。进一步实验表明，当禁用所有外部证据时，仅基于问题条件进行专家选择，也比最佳固定专家高 1.75 分。消融实验显示，学习型路由是最主要的性能来源，而知识获取和感知工具补充之间具有互补作用。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在具身视觉语言问答中，内部推理能力选择和外部证据获取不应独立优化，而应作为联合路由问题进行建模。EKT-Router 通过预算约束、专家—证据路径效用和证据包验证机制，实现了更可控、更一致的工具增强专家路由。实验结果支持其核心观点：专家路由贡献最大，知识与感知证据能够互补提升性能；但摘要未充分讨论路由成本、实时性、跨域泛化以及工具失败鲁棒性等潜在限制。

Abstract: Embodied vision-language models must answer questions from partial, evolving observations. Their success depends on both the reasoning capability selected and the evidence available to it. Expert routers adapt internal computation, while tool-augmented systems acquire external evidence; choosing these independently can leave a suitable expert without the evidence it needs. We introduce EKT-Router, a bounded framework that jointly selects a capability expert, optional knowledge, and perceptual tools. Outcome-derived signatures represent the overlapping strengths of General, Spatial Reasoning, and Spatiotemporal experts. A joint utility scores complete expert-evidence routes under a call budget. After acquisition, a packet verifier admits valid evidence with positive predicted expert-conditioned gain. The selected expert then answers once, reverting to the unchanged observation if all packets are rejected. Across 6 embodied QA benchmarks, EKT-Router achieves a mean score of 61.4, the highest among the compared systems, and averages 74.7 on 10 general vision-language benchmarks. With all external evidence disabled, question-conditioned expert selection alone improves over the best fixed expert by 1.75 points. Ablations show that learned routing is the dominant component and that knowledge and perception are complementary.

</details>


### [4] [Detection of false positives by human reviewers depends on the face recognition algorithm and its security configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33045)
*Esha Edakoth,John J. Howard,Isabelle M. Shuggi,Yevgeniy B. Sirotin,Robert Wood*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估了人工审核者在商业人脸识别算法产生假阳性时，在不同安全阈值/操作配置下发现并纠正这些错误的能力，发现算法越严格，人工监督有效性下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别算法已达到超越人类的速度和精度，但其高运行能力可能削弱人类对算法错误的有效监督，尤其是在身份审核等安全关键流程中，需要评估人机协同团队的实际可靠性与风险。

Method: 作者基于四个同类商业人脸识别算法产生的假阳性，构建新数据集“困难人脸图像比较测试”（DFICT），并招募801名人工审核者，在三种代表实际部署的生物识别阈值的安全配置下，测量其识别算法假阳性的能力，同时比较不同算法下审核者表现差异。

Result: 人工审核者能够在一定程度上降低人脸识别算法的假阳性错误，但当安全阈值更严格时，审核者表现下降。不同算法对应的审核者表现并不一致，表明某些算法与人类监督机制的兼容性更好。

Conclusion: 在高安全要求的人脸识别应用中，系统设计应考虑人工审核者监督效果的差异，算法选择和人类监督策略需要纳入AI风险管理，以减少算法错误在实际部署中带来的风险。

Abstract: Integration of artificial intelligence (AI) technology into identity workflows, such as automated face recognition (FR), has enhanced operational capabilities, with FR algorithms achieving super-human scale and accuracy. However, these gains can make human oversight of FR more difficult, raising questions about the effectiveness of these human-AI teams. We measured human reviewers' ability to detect false positive errors made by commercial FR algorithms across varying security configurations. Using a novel dataset, the Difficult Facial Image Comparison Test (DFICT), derived from false positives made by four comparable commercial face recognition algorithms, we measured the ability of 801human reviewers to detect algorithmic false positives across three security configurations, representing operationally relevant biometric thresholds. Our findings show that human reviewers can reduce algorithmic false positives, but performance declines at stricter security thresholds. Further, reviewer performance varied by algorithm indicating that some algorithms are more compatible with human oversight. We suggest that biometric systems should be designed to account for human reviewers' variable effectiveness, particularly in high-security applications. Our findings should be considered during algorithm selection and inform human oversight strategies to minimize AI-related risks in operational FR deployments.

</details>


### [5] [MARC: Mechanism-Aware Reflective Correction for Articulated Object Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33060)
*Xiong Yang,Junkun Hao,Zijian Xiao,Zipeng Ye,Yuchen Xie,Ran Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MARC（Mechanism-Aware Reflective Correction），通过共享可执行运动学图，将图像/文本生成关节物体的部件、连接、闭环与耦合关系显式化，并用运动求解反馈指导修正，使生成机构真正能执行预期运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭图像和文本生成关节物体时，即使部件和关节看起来合理，装配后的机构也可能无法运动；尤其闭环和耦合机构中，一处连接或几何错误会导致机构卡死或部件脱离。现有方法缺乏对物理连接和运动约束的可执行建模与诊断修正机制。

Method: MARC 构建并共享一个可执行运动学图，将物理连接绑定到局部附着坐标系，并显式表示闭环约束和关节坐标耦合。给定驱动输入后，通过数值求解器计算从动部件运动，并将误差定位到具体连接和配置。随后由智能体利用这些诊断信息探查可能原因，修正连接或几何，并沿原运动路径重新测试。

Result: 在 20 个物体的测试集上，MARC 结合 Kimi-K3 达到 67.8% 的有效运动覆盖率和 60.0% 的整体成功率，优于六个基线方法。从相同初始候选开始，修正模块分别将有效运动覆盖率和整体成功率提升 13.7 和 20.0 个百分点。消融实验进一步验证了基于图的机构支持和图引导修复的作用。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将生成式模型与可执行运动学图及求解反馈结合，能够显著提升关节物体生成的功能正确性，尤其适用于闭环和耦合等容易因局部连接错误而失效的机构。

Abstract: Generating an articulated object from images and text requires more than plausible parts and movable joints: the assembled mechanism must execute the intended motion. This is particularly challenging for closed-chain and coupled mechanisms, where an error in one connection can block motion or separate connected parts. We introduce MARC, Mechanism-Aware Reflective Correction, which shares an executable kinematic graph across program construction, motion solving, and correction. The graph binds physical connections to local attachment frames and represents loop closures and joint-coordinate couplings. Given prescribed driver inputs, a numerical solver computes follower motion and associates errors with specific connections and configurations. The agent uses these diagnostics to probe possible causes, revise connections or geometry, and retest the mechanism along the original motion paths. On a 20-object test set, MARC with Kimi-K3 achieves 67.8% valid motion coverage and 60.0% overall success, outperforming six baselines. Starting from the same initial candidates, correction improves these scores by 13.7 and 20.0 percentage points, respectively. Ablations further show the benefits of graph-based mechanism support and graph-guided repair. Project homepage: this https URL.

</details>


### [6] [Oddin: Latent Visual Reasoning in Unified Multimodal Models via Self-Distillation from Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33062)
*Jaeyong Ko,Hankyeol Kim,Pilsung Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Oddin 框架，使统一大型多模态模型能够在不生成显式中间图像的情况下进行潜在视觉推理，从而降低交错图文生成的计算开销并提升多步视觉推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型常通过交错文本-图像生成实现视觉推理，但中间图像生成开销高；而已有的潜在视觉推理方法多依赖不具备图像生成能力的视觉-语言模型，难以同时兼顾理解、推理与最终生成。

Method: Oddin 将中间图像替换为紧凑的潜在视觉 token，这些 token 仅由理解分支处理，生成分支只负责最终输出图像。模型通过离线自蒸馏训练，使用三类目标：最终图像生成损失、文本蒸馏损失以及特殊 token 的交叉熵损失。

Result: 在七个 VBVR-Pro 视觉推理任务上，Oddin 平均表现优于交错图文生成基线和闭源视觉-语言模型。定性分析表明，潜在 token 能够关注输入图像中的相关区域，说明统一多模态模型可以在不生成中间图像的情况下完成复杂视觉推理。

Conclusion: Oddin 证明了统一大型多模态模型能够利用潜在视觉 token 实现高效视觉推理，同时保留最终图像生成能力，为降低多模态推理中的显式图像生成开销提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Unified large multimodal models (ULMMs) are able to perform visual reasoning through interleaved text-image generation, but generating explicit intermediate images incurs significant computational overhead. While visual latent reasoning addresses this, most existing methods rely on vision-language models (VLMs) incapable of visual generation. We propose Oddin, a novel framework enabling latent visual reasoning in ULMMs. Oddin replaces intermediate images with compact latent tokens processed entirely by the understanding branch, reserving the generation branch for the final output image. We train the model via offline self-distillation with three objectives, final image generation losses, text distillation losses, and cross-entropy losses for special tokens. Oddin surpasses interleaved generation baselines and closed-source VLMs on average across seven VBVR-Pro visual reasoning tasks. Qualitative analysis reveals that latent tokens attend to relevant regions in an input image, demonstrating that ULMMs can perform complex visual reasoning without generating intermediate images.

</details>


### [7] [OvisOCR3 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33066)
*Yu Xia,Yinglun Li,Yuhui Chen,Jinlong Huang,Yuyang Wanyan,Shengze Shi,Yifei Song,Yue Duan,Shubo Lin,Jianshan Zhao,En Lin,Shiyin Lu,Qing-Guo Chen,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OvisOCR3 是一个 0.8B 的轻量级视觉语言模型，统一支持文档解析、通用 OCR、关键信息抽取、文档图表描述和跨页表格重建等多种 OCR 相关任务，并在多个公开与内部基准上取得领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCR 相关任务通常数据规模、输出长度和目标指标差异较大，传统方法往往依赖多个专用模型或大参数量模型。该工作旨在探索是否可以用一个紧凑的 0.8B 模型，在保持较小模型规模的同时，统一覆盖多种 OCR 任务并达到强于通用视觉语言模型和专用 OCR 模型的性能。

Method: 模型基于真实与合成数据训练，首先进行继续预训练，并通过领域平衡的中期训练来缓解不同任务在数据量和响应长度上的不平衡。随后采用多教师在线策略蒸馏，从五个 4B 领域教师模型向共享 0.8B 模型传递专业知识，并结合混合任务强化学习与领域特定奖励进一步优化多任务性能。

Result: OvisOCR3 在 OmniDocBench v1.6 上取得 96.74 的总体得分，并在 PureDocBench、Real5-OmniDocBench 和 MDPBench 上获得最高总体分数。在 OCRBench v2 上，其英文和中文子集分别取得 65.9 和 61.3 的分数并排名第一。它还在 OCRBench 和四个 KIE 基准上优于基线模型。在 DocFigCapBench 上，模型取得最高定位 F1，描述分数接近 Qwen3.8-Max；在跨页表格任务中，模型在 CrossTabBench 的 Relation F1 和 MPDocBench-Parse 的截断表格 TEDS 上领先。

Conclusion: 实验表明，通过领域平衡训练、多教师在线蒸馏和任务强化学习，一个 0.8B 的紧凑模型能够在多种 OCR 相关任务上实现强统一性能，为轻量化、多任务 OCR 模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce OvisOCR3, a 0.8B model for a broad range of OCR-related tasks. OvisOCR3 unifies document parsing, general OCR, key information extraction (KIE), document figure captioning, and cross-page table reconstruction in a single model. We train the model on real and synthetic data through continued pretraining and use domain-balanced mid-training to balance supervision across tasks with different data volumes and response lengths. Multi-teacher on-policy distillation transfers expertise from five domain-specific 4B teachers into the shared model, followed by mixed-task reinforcement learning with domain-specific rewards. We compare OvisOCR3 with general-purpose vision-language models and specialized OCR models on public and in-house benchmarks covering all five task families. In these comparisons, it achieves an overall score of 96.74 on OmniDocBench v1.6 and the highest overall scores on PureDocBench, Real5-OmniDocBench, and MDPBench. On OCRBench v2, it ranks first, scoring 65.9 and 61.3 on the English and Chinese splits, respectively. It also outperforms the baselines on OCRBench and all four KIE benchmarks. On DocFigCapBench, it achieves the highest localization F1 and a caption score close to that of Qwen3.8-Max. For cross-page tables, it leads in Relation F1 on CrossTabBench and truncated-table TEDS on MPDocBench-Parse. These results demonstrate that a compact 0.8B model can achieve strong performance across diverse OCR-related tasks. OvisOCR3 is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [8] [Decentralized Multi-View, Multi-Zone Tracking for Scalable Camera Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33069)
*David Voihanski,Mor Sinai,Ben Zion Bobrovsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向可扩展相机网络的分布式多视角目标跟踪系统，通过将单视角检测映射到俯视视角并在空间分区上并行跟踪，再借助轨迹统一与缓冲机制维持全局身份一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模相机网络中的多视角跟踪需要高可扩展性，但跨分区、跨设备的异步协调容易导致轨迹分裂和全局ID不一致，因此需要一种分布式、低延迟且稳定的统一方案。

Method: 系统首先将单视角检测结果投影到鸟瞰图坐标，再由多个独立的多视角跟踪器分别负责动态空间区域。为支持跨区协同，引入去中心化轨迹统一层，采用锁定协议和周期性的“global collate”轨道合并操作，并通过可配置延迟窗口的延迟输出缓冲机制回溯修正历史轨迹。

Result: 所设计的方法能够处理空间分区的相机网络，并缓解异步协调延迟带来的影响，在评估配置中以较小精度损失维持全局身份一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，结合BEV投影、区域化多视角跟踪、去中心化协作和延迟校正机制，可在保证可扩展性的同时实现稳定、准确的多相机全局目标跟踪。

Abstract: We present a decentralized multi-view tracking system for scalable camera networks. Single-view detections are projected to bird's-eye-view (BEV) coordinates and fused by independent multi-view trackers, each managing a dynamic spatial zone. To support scalability across spatially partitioned camera networks, we introduce a decentralized layer for trajectory unification, utilizing a locking protocol and periodic track-merging operations termed global collate. This process is supported by a delayed-output buffering mechanism that retroactively applies history corrections. The configurable delay window absorbs asynchronous coordination latency and helps stabilize cross-zone identity consistency. We detail the design of dynamic zone polygons, consensus-based coordination, and the validation framework, which together maintain global ID consistency with minimal accuracy loss compared to a single-instance baseline in the evaluated configurations.

</details>


### [9] [PhaseBreak: Proactive Temporal Protection against Unauthorized Image-to-Video Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33070)
*Jiashu Zhang,Xuanyou Li,Qixin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出 PhaseBreak，一种面向图像到视频扩散模型的主动视觉保护方法，通过在发布图像上施加有限扰动，扰乱其被未经授权动画化的时间生成过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Image-to-Video 扩散模型可将单张肖像或照片自动动画化，导致共享图像在未获授权的情况下被用于生成运动视频。现有基于频谱扰动的防护方法通常全局削弱时间表征，难以精细控制局部或细微运动，因此需要一种既能保留图像正常分享、又能有效破坏下游运动生成的主动保护机制。

Method: 核心思想是：生成视频中的动态不仅取决于时间特征的强度，还取决于这些时间特征事件的发生顺序。作者提出 PhaseBreak，一种白盒频谱重计时保护方法。该方法将扩散中间去噪器的特征与其干净对应特征进行匹配，但对干净特征施加受约束的时间重排；同时在真实时间轴上操作，并借助主体感知掩码将扰动限制在图像中的主体区域，从而实现对局部运动生成的破坏。

Result: 在 Stable Video Diffusion 和 DynamiCrafter 上的实验表明，经过 PhaseBreak 保护的图像会导致生成结果出现更大的短期运动变化和更低的时间规律性，使模型难以从被发布的图像中稳定定制或延续原主体运动。

Conclusion: PhaseBreak 通过扰动时间特征顺序而非仅削弱时间频谱，提供了更可控的图像发布前主动保护方式，可在不破坏正常图像分享流程的前提下降低图像被滥用于未经授权视频生成的风险。

Abstract: Image-to-Video (I2V) diffusion models can animate a single portrait or photograph, enabling shared images to be repurposed for motion synthesis without the subject's authorization. We study proactive visual protection against such use: before an image is released, its owner applies a bounded perturbation that preserves the sharing workflow but disrupts downstream motion generation. Existing spectral disruption objectives globally attenuate temporal representations and therefore provide limited control over subtle or spatially localized motion. Our key idea is that generated dynamics depend not only on temporal feature strength, but also on the order in which feature events occur. We propose PhaseBreak, a white-box spectral retiming protection method that matches intermediate denoiser features to a constrained temporal rearrangement of their clean counterparts. PhaseBreak operates along the true temporal axis and uses a subject-aware mask to confine the protective perturbation to the depicted subject. Evaluations on Stable Video Diffusion and DynamiCrafter show that protected images induce greater short-term motion variation and lower temporal regularity, impeding stable motion customization from the released image.

</details>


### [10] [VLMProbe: An Open-source Library for VLM Probing and Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33072)
*Gexin Huang,Haisheng Fu,Yanting Yang,Chen Zhao,Ruinan Jin,Jiaqi Liu,Xiaoxiao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 vlmprobe 开源库，将视觉语言模型中的透镜读出、注意力归因、因果干预和稀疏自编码器分析统一到同一模型接口下，用于比较不同可解释性方法的结论与适用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解视觉语言模型如何利用视觉信息，需要把模型内部表示与模型行为联系起来；但现有解释方法分散、难以系统比较，导致单一种局部归因或表示读出可能与实际行为影响不一致。

Method: 作者构建 vlmprobe，整合 lens readouts、attention attribution、causal interventions、sparse autoencoder（SAE）分析，并提供共享适配器、校准与检查点工具、评估流程和可视化模块。框架在视觉语言、语言和视觉模型上开展匹配案例研究与受控实验，重点评估预训练稀疏字典的权衡。

Result: 实验表明，表示读出和空间解释高度依赖分析目标与观察位置；局部归因估计可能与因果干预测得的行为影响不一致。SAE 评估揭示了重建保真度、概念读出和空间定位能力之间存在的不同权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作支持结合多种证据评估模型解释，vlmprobe 为选择可解释性方法、比较其结论以及检验模型行为假设提供了可复用基础。

Abstract: Understanding how vision-language models use visual information requires connecting internal representations to model behavior. We present vlmprobe, an open-source library that integrates lens readouts, attention attribution, causal interventions, and sparse autoencoder (SAE) analysis within a common model interface. Shared adapters, calibration and checkpoint utilities, evaluation pipelines, and visualizations support systematic analysis across architectures. Using this framework, we compare methods across vision-language, language, and vision models through matched case studies and controlled evaluations of pretrained sparse dictionaries. The comparisons show that representation readouts and spatial explanations depend on the analysis objective and observation site, and that local attribution estimates can disagree with measured intervention effects. SAE evaluations further expose distinct trade-offs in reconstruction fidelity, concept readout, and spatial localization. Together, these results support combining multiple forms of evidence to evaluate an interpretation. vlmprobe provides a reusable basis for selecting methods, comparing their conclusions, and testing hypotheses about model behavior. The code is available on this http URL

</details>


### [11] [AGQ: Action-Guided Ultra-Low-Bit Quantization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33074)
*Xuhong Qiu,Zhuoyu Wu,Qiawei Zheng,Wenhui Ou,Wenqi Fang,Raphaël C.-W. Phan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出面向视觉-语言-动作模型的Action-Guided Quantization（AGQ），通过动作敏感度与权重恢复成本联合分配矩阵位宽，并结合校准、无损编码和分块恢复，在极低比特量化下兼顾任务性能与端侧部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型参数规模大，难以部署在内存受限的机器人端侧平台。超低比特量化可显著减小模型体积，但会引入较大精度损失，同时权重解码或恢复还会带来额外计算与缓冲开销，因此需要在压缩率、任务成功率和部署成本之间进行优化。

Method: 作者提出Action-Guided Quantization，将动作敏感性作为任务影响指标，并结合可测量的权重恢复成本代理，在给定存储预算下为不同矩阵分配位宽。该方法采用逐通道校准补偿量化误差，在每个矩阵内部保持统一位宽以维持规则存储布局，同时使用依赖位宽的分组无损编码降低常驻权重大小，并通过分块权重恢复限制临时缓冲区开销。实验在LIBERO任务基准上评估。

Result: 在LIBERO上，AGQ在W3配置下达到92.7%任务成功率，高于AWQ和GPTQ分别约1.2和3.7个百分点；在W2.5配置下仍保持70.5%成功率。其非对称配置W1.5*采用W4视觉权重和语言权重参数加权平均1.5 bit，在无损编码下相对BF16实现6.10倍完整模型压缩，同时保留25.5%任务成功率。

Conclusion: AGQ表明，面向动作影响的位宽分配和恢复成本建模能够有效提升VLA模型在超低比特量化下的端侧部署可行性。该方法在较高位宽下保持强任务性能，在极低比特设置下则通过非对称压缩和无损编码实现显著模型压缩，为资源受限机器人平台提供了实用权衡。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable robots to perform tasks specified through natural-language instructions, but their large weight footprint challenges deployment on memory-constrained onboard platforms. Ultra-low-bit quantization reduces this footprint, yet preserving task performance and controlling weight recovery costs remain challenging. We propose Action-Guided Quantization, which combines action sensitivity with a measured proxy for weight recovery cost to allocate matrix bitwidths under storage budgets. Per-channel calibration compensates for quantization error, while a uniform bitwidth within each matrix preserves regular layouts. Bit-dependent grouped lossless coding reduces resident weight storage, and block-wise recovery limits temporary buffers. On LIBERO, AGQ achieves 92.7% task success at W3, exceeding AWQ and GPTQ by 1.2 and 3.7 percentage points, respectively, and retains 70.5% success at W2.5. An asymmetric configuration, denoted W1.5$^{*}$, uses W4 vision weights and a parameter-weighted average of 1.5 bits for language weights. With lossless coding, it achieves $6.10\times$ complete-model compression relative to BF16 while retaining 25.5% task success. Code and experimental results are available at this https URL

</details>


### [12] [Diagnosing and Mitigating Evidence Misgrounding in Multimodal Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33075)
*Zhiwu Wang,Yichen Wu,Jinqian Yang,Yizhou Yang,Renzhen Wang,Qian Zhao,Deyu Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究指出多模态大模型知识编辑中的“答案级指标”可能掩盖编辑未真正依赖目标视觉证据的问题，并提出VACE？实际为VICE：通过外部记忆和视觉上下文示例进行知识编辑，同时构建WaterBird-Edit诊断集评估背景扰动下的编辑稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态知识编辑需要更新与图像绑定的事实知识，同时保持其他文本和视觉行为不变。然而，仅用目标答案是否正确来评估编辑效果，可能隐藏“证据错位”（evidence misgrounding）：模型可能在原图上输出目标答案，但实际仍依赖无关背景等偶然上下文，而不是编辑所要求的目标视觉证据。

Method: 作者首先构建WaterBird-Edit分析集，将鸟类栖息地标签与背景信息解耦，用于检查当目标证据保留、背景变化时，编辑后的预测是否保持稳定。基于这一诊断，提出VICE（Visual In-Context Editing）：一种检索式知识编辑框架，将编辑信息存储在外部记忆中，并通过专门设计的视觉上下文示例应用编辑，使模型更关注任务相关证据。

Result: 在三个标准知识编辑基准上，VICE取得了较强的终身编辑（lifelong editing）性能；在WaterBird-Edit上，VICE显著降低了模型对受控背景变化的敏感性，说明其编辑结果更稳定且更依赖目标视觉证据。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了多模态知识编辑评估中仅依赖答案正确率的不足，强调需要检查编辑是否真正基于目标视觉证据。VICE通过外部记忆与视觉上下文示例提升了编辑稳定性和证据对齐程度，同时提出WaterBird-Edit为评估多模态编辑的证据接地问题提供了受控诊断工具。

Abstract: Multimodal knowledge editing aims to update visually grounded facts in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) while preserving unrelated text and visual behaviors. However, standard answer-level metrics can hide evidence misgrounding: an edit may produce the target answer on the original image yet remain sensitive to incidental context rather than the intended visual evidence. To examine this issue in a controlled setting, we construct WaterBird-Edit, an analysis set that decouples bird habitat labels from background context and measures whether edited predictions remain stable when the target evidence is preserved but the background changes. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose VICE (Visual In-Context Editing), a retrieval-based framework that stores edits in external memory and applies them through visual in-context demonstrations designed to expose task-relevant evidence. Experiments on three standard benchmarks show that VICE achieves strong lifelong editing performance, while results on WaterBird-Edit show that it substantially reduces sensitivity to the controlled background shifts. Code and dataset are available in this https URL.

</details>


### [13] [Evaluating Visual Language Models on Visual Grounding for Visually Impaired People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33078)
*Fairooz Azim,Lydia Achour,Claudia Borg,Lina Rojas-Barahona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估面向视障辅助场景的视觉语言模型视觉定位能力，提出VG-SHAP用于解释黑盒VLM的视觉依据，并发现模型虽能答对问题却难以准确定位图像相关区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉定位是视觉问答中多模态理解的重要能力，对视障人群辅助应用尤其关键。此时它不仅是技术指标，也关系到可信性与安全性。需要评估开源视觉语言模型在真实视障辅助场景中的视觉定位表现。

Method: 研究基于VizWiz-VQA-Grounding数据集和挑战，使用由视障人士拍摄的真实图像评估开源视觉语言模型。作者提出VG-SHAP，将已有可解释性方法扩展到视觉定位解释，以便评估黑盒VLM的决策依据和空间关注区域。

Result: 实验表明，许多视觉语言模型能够生成正确回答，但在将答案与图像中正确区域建立空间对应方面仍存在明显不足。然而，这些结论受到VG-SHAP中所用分割模型质量的限制。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型在视障辅助视觉定位任务中的可靠性仍不足，正确回答并不等于正确定位。VG-SHAP为评估黑盒VLM提供了可解释性工具，但后续需要改进底层分割与定位评估机制。

Abstract: Visual grounding (VG) in visual question answering (VQA) is a fundamental capability of Vision-Language Models (VLMs), enabling the detection of the most relevant region in an image to answer the question. This reflects the model's capability of multimodal comprehension, which is crucial when interacting with visually impaired people (VIP). Indeed, visual grounding, when applied to assistive scenarios, is not merely a technical feature, but rather a trust and safety requirement. In this work, we study visual grounding in VLMs for visually impaired people. To this end, we assess open-source VLMs on visual grounding using the VizWiz-VQA-Grounding dataset and challenge, which contains real-life images taken by VIP. We propose VG-SHAP, which extends an existing explainability framework to provide visual grounding explanations, allowing the evaluation of black-box VLMs. Our results on the VizWiz-VQA-Grounding challenge show that despite generating correct answers, VLMs struggle to ground in the proper region in the image. However, these results are constrained by the quality of the segmentation model of our explainability framework and further research is necessary.

</details>


### [14] [Rethinking Video Storage for AI Training: Breaking Cross-Frame Dependencies for Efficient Random Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33079)
*Zetian Song,Qize Yu,Yue Chen,Bohan Zhou,Bowen Ping,Yuqi Ye,Haocheng Tang,Haotian Zhang,Qizhe Wang,Shiyu Huang,Jiaqi Zhang,Siwei Ma,Wen Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文针对大规模AI视频训练中的随机时间采样解码瓶颈，提出依赖感知的稀疏解码和可选的依赖解耦随机访问编码，在保持帧间压缩收益的同时显著减少无关重建与参考链解码开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模AI训练通常需要对海量视频进行随机时间片段采样，但传统视频存储范式面向顺序播放优化，依赖复杂的帧间预测结构以提高压缩率。面对随机采样工作负载时，这种结构会导致解码放大：为得到目标帧，系统往往需要重建无关帧或长参考链；若改用独立JPEG帧或短GOP，虽可降低解码代价，却会增加存储和传输开销。

Method: 作者识别出两类解码放大来源：一是顺序重建无关帧，二是为采样目标帧重建其所需参考链。针对二者，论文联合设计解码调度与视频参考结构：依赖感知稀疏解码仅重建被请求的目标帧及其必要参考帧，跳过无关重建且无需转码源视频；可选的离线依赖解耦随机访问编码进一步限制每个内部帧仅依赖两个边界帧内编码锚点，从而将每个目标帧的重建代价限制为最多三帧，且不依赖 intra period。

Result: 在大规模机器人视频语料上的实验表明，在相近重建质量条件下，该方法相比 LeRobot v3 实现了 1.45 倍的加载吞吐量，并减少 58.7% 的媒体负载。

Conclusion: 通过同时优化解码调度和视频帧间依赖结构，该方法能够在保留压缩效率的同时缓解随机视频采样训练中的解码瓶颈，为大规模AI视频数据加载提供了更高效的存储与访问方案。

Abstract: Large-scale AI training increasingly relies on massive video datasets with random temporal sampling patterns. However, existing video storage paradigms are optimized for sequential playback and employ sophisticated inter-frame reference structures to maximize compression efficiency. When applied to the random-sampling workloads of AI training, these paradigms face substantial decoding-efficiency bottlenecks. Workarounds such as independent JPEG frames and short groups of pictures (GOPs) reduce decoding work at the cost of larger storage and transfer volumes. In this paper, we identify two sources of decode amplification: sequential reconstruction of unrelated frames and reconstruction of the reference chains required by sampled targets. We address both sources by co-designing the decoding schedule and video reference structure. Dependency-aware sparse decoding reconstructs only requested frames and their required references, skipping unrelated reconstruction without transcoding the source videos. Optional offline Dependency-Decoupled Random Access (DDRA) encoding further reduces reference reconstruction by restricting each interior frame to two bounding intra-coded anchors. Together, sparse decoding and DDRA retain inter-frame prediction while bounding reconstruction to at most three frames per requested target, independent of the intra period. Experiments on a large-scale robotics corpus show that, at comparable reconstruction quality, our method achieves $1.45\times$ the loading throughput of LeRobot v3 with 58.7% less media payload.

</details>


### [15] [The Paired Thermal Channel Is Two Orthogonal Axes: A Controlled Attribution Study of Calibration Cost and Detection Gain in Infrared Person Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33118)
*Tian Zhang,Yang Chen,Bing Bai,Weikai He,Xiang Gao,Zhirong Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文围绕红外人体检测中的“校准热通道”展开控制变量归因，发现其包含校准轴与检测轴两个正交机制：校准优势主要来自配对的物理校准信息，而检测增益则来自破坏空间结构后的统计/频率特征变化，并以 TEL 方法为实验载体验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外人检中灰度伪热图虽可与校准热图尺度匹配，但二者并非等价。作者希望厘清：校准热图带来的性能提升究竟来自“绝对温度校准/配对关系”还是来自空间结构本身，从而避免对热通道增益来源的混淆归因。

Method: 作者以温度证据学习（TEL）为测试床，设计五臂受控归因框架，比较真实校准 ΔT 与尺度匹配灰度伪 ΔT、恒定值/打乱配对等条件，并用成对 Wilcoxon 检验、Cohen’s d、ECE、2×2 交互检验以及事后温度缩放分析校准成本与配对成本。另通过相位随机化破坏空间结构但保留逐样本方差，评估检测轴上的结构解构效应。

Result: 真实校准 ΔT 相对灰度伪 ΔT 提升 +0.0221 mAP（p=0.0098，d=+1.08），说明校准通道包含灰度通道缺失的信息。同时保留配对对应关系与绝对校准尺度会使 ECE 从约 0.05 升高到 0.142，且 2×2 交互 p=0.0059；单独破坏配对或尺度会使 ECE 回落到单因素破坏水平。事后温度缩放后，配对成本仍显著（p=0.002），尺度成本基本被吸收（p=0.16）。检测轴上，相位随机化在 FLIR ADAS 上相对真实 ΔT 提升 +0.0185 mAP（p=0.014，d=+0.96），FLAME 上为 +0.0064 mAP（p=0.078，n=8）；校准轴上该干预在 FLIR ADAS 不显著（p=0.47），但在 FLAME 显著（p=0.0078）。

Conclusion: 校准热通道的优势并非单一来源：校准轴增益依赖配对性与绝对校准尺度的联合条件，而检测轴增益可部分来自空间结构的破坏性特征。TEL 通过单一证据路由标量和诊断熵统一刻画这一机制，并提出有限证据退化定理与闭式界。研究强调在红外检测中应严格区分物理校准信息、配对关系和空间结构特征的不同贡献。

Abstract: The calibrated thermal channel in infrared person detection comprises two structurally orthogonal axes. True calibrated $\Delta T$ outperforms scale-matched grayscale pseudo-$\Delta T$ by $+0.0221$ mAP (paired Wilcoxon $p=0.0098$, Cohen's $d=+1.08$), confirming that the calibrated channel carries information the grayscale channel lacks. The calibration axis is driven by the joint pairing-scale condition. Preserving both paired correspondence and absolute calibrated scale raises expected calibration error (ECE) from $\approx 0.05$ to $0.142$. The $2\times 2$ interaction ($p=0.0059$) localizes the rise to this condition. Breaking either factor alone returns ECE to the broken-factor level (true-vs-const $p=0.002$; true-vs-shuffled $p=0.002$). Under post-hoc temperature scaling, the pairing cost survives recalibration ($p=0.002$), while the scale cost is largely absorbed ($p=0.16$). As regards the detection axis, the gain stems from structural de-structuring. Phase randomization, which destroys spatial structure while preserving per-sample variance, yields $+0.0185$ mAP over true $\Delta T$ on FLIR ADAS ($p=0.014$, $d=+0.96$). The FLAME~3 gain is $+0.0064$ mAP ($p=0.078$, n=8). On the calibration axis, the same intervention is insignificant on FLIR ADAS ($p=0.47$) but significant on FLAME~3 ($p=0.0078$). These findings rest on a five-arm controlled attribution with temperature-evidential learning (TEL) as the testbed. TEL obtains a single evidence-derived routing scalar together with a complementary diagnostic entropy, and establishes a finite-evidence degeneracy theorem proving that the acquisition product degenerates to this scalar with a closed-form bound. A physical $0.5^\circ$C threshold admits cross-dataset physical readings, whereas a gray threshold of $20$ inverts without physical interpretation.

</details>


### [16] [Ampe-DB: A Dataset for Human Activity and Interaction Analysis Using the Ghanaian Game Ampe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33130)
*Ruth Senior Biney,Isaac Kojo Yedonu Aboah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出 Ampe-DB，一个基于加纳传统游戏“Ampe”的多模态人类活动识别数据集，并通过固定基准与留出参与者/配对评估揭示：RGB 模型在跨人、跨配对泛化时性能显著下降，而姿态模型更稳健，融合模型仅在部分协议中提升有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人类活动识别基准往往偏重通用动作类别，较少覆盖具有文化情境性的人际互动和精细运动模式。作者希望提供一个能反映文化特定动作、双人协调和身份泛化挑战的数据集，以评估模型在真实跨人、跨配对场景中的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建包含 15 名参与者、637 个规范片段的多模态数据集，提供 RGB 视频、双人姿态表示、参与者与配对元数据、四类动作标签及二值动作关系标签。评估四类动作识别和二值关系分类，采用三种协议：去重安全的固定基准、四折配对留出评估、以及针对完整类别覆盖参与者的参与者留出评估。基线包括冻结的 Kinetics-400 预训练 R(2+1)D-18 RGB 编码器、两层双向 GRU 姿态序列模型，以及验证集选择的 RGB-姿态延迟融合。

Result: 四类动作识别中，RGB 在固定基准上 macro-F1 为 0.955，但在配对留出和参与者留出协议下降至 0.418 和 0.384；姿态模型分别为 0.957、0.811 和 0.839，泛化更稳健。融合模型在固定基准达到 0.976，在配对留出达到 0.824，但在参与者移位时未能稳定优于姿态基线。二值动作关系识别在留出评估下明显变难，尤其对不同腿配置的错误更突出。

Conclusion: Ampe-DB 表明固定基准上的高分数可能掩盖模型跨个体和跨配对泛化失败。该数据集适合用于细粒度动作识别、人-人交互建模、多模态学习和身份感知泛化研究，并提示需要重视跨人、跨关系设置下的鲁棒评估。

Abstract: Human activity recognition benchmarks underrepresent culturally situated forms of movement and interaction. We introduce Ampe-DB, a multimodal dataset based on Ampe, a traditional Ghanaian game involving coordinated jumping, leg movements, and two-person interaction. It contains 637 canonical clips from 15 participants, with RGB video, two-person pose representations, participant and pair metadata, four-class movement labels, and derived binary movement-relation labels. We evaluate four-class movement recognition and binary movement-relation classification using three protocols: a duplicate-safe fixed benchmark, four-fold pair-held-out evaluation, and participant-held-out diagnostics for participants with complete class coverage. Baselines comprise a frozen Kinetics-400-pretrained R(2+1)D-18 RGB encoder, a two-layer bidirectional GRU for pose sequences, and validation-selected RGB-pose late fusion. For four-class recognition, RGB macro-F1 falls from 0.955 on the fixed benchmark to 0.418 under pair-held-out evaluation and 0.384 under participant-held-out evaluation. Pose is more robust, achieving 0.957, 0.811, and 0.839 across the same protocols. Fusion reaches 0.976 on the fixed benchmark and 0.824 under pair-held-out evaluation, but does not consistently improve on pose under participant shift. Binary movement-relation recognition also becomes substantially harder under held-out evaluation, especially for different-leg configurations. These findings show that strong fixed-benchmark scores can mask failures to generalize across people and pairings. Ampe-DB supports research on fine-grained movement recognition, human-human interaction, multimodal learning, and identity-aware generalization.

</details>


### [17] [WorldCast: World-Consistent Character Video Generation with Large-Angle Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33135)
*Lei Cao,Yu Liu,Wenhua Liao,Peng Zhang,Fei Wang,Hao Zhang,Jiajun Le,Daiguo Zhou,Jian Luan,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向电影级角色视频生成的大视角相机轨迹控制数据集 CLACT 和世界一致性生成框架 WorldCast，通过深度反投影点云提供显式几何引导，并引入几何对齐记忆注意力 GAMA 以利用多视角角色锚点补全不可见区域，从而在大角度旋转、平移和半径变化下保持角色几何、外观与身份一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有角色视频生成方法难以在大视角相机轨迹下精确控制相机，尤其是同时包含旋转、平移和半径变化且初始方位角不定的情况。视角大幅变化会暴露初始帧中未出现的角色区域，导致几何失真、外观退化和身份漂移。因此，需要系统数据集、基准以及能够利用多视角角色信息并进行世界一致性建模的生成方法。

Method: 1. 构建大规模以角色为中心的数据集与基准 CLACT，覆盖多样大角度相机轨迹，并提供准确相机几何与多视角角色观测。2. 提出 WorldCast 框架：先将初始帧基于估计深度反投影为 3D 点云，再将点云尺度与目标相机轨迹对齐，并沿轨迹渲染，生成显式几何引导和覆盖掩码。3. 针对初始帧中角色信息不完整的问题，提出 Geometry-Aligned Memory Attention（GAMA）。该方法利用由初始帧和多视角角色锚点联合估计的相对相机位姿，将锚点视角与目标视角几何统一到公共坐标系中。4. 在生成过程中，GAMA 基于该几何对齐关系检索并注入与目标视角相关的角色特征，从而补全不可见区域并保持跨视角一致性。

Result: 在多种大角度相机轨迹上的评估表明，所提方法能够较准确遵循目标相机运动轨迹，同时保持角色几何结构、外观细节和身份信息稳定，减少因视角暴露未知区域带来的几何失真、外观退化和身份漂移。

Conclusion: 论文通过数据集 CLACT 与框架 WorldCast 系统性地研究大角度相机控制下的角色视频生成问题，验证了显式点云几何引导和几何对齐多视角记忆机制对世界一致性视频生成的有效性，为电影级可控角色视频生成提供了新的数据基准与方法方案。

Abstract: Precise camera control is essential for cinematic character video generation, yet existing methods struggle to follow large-angle trajectories with combined rotation, translation, and radius variation from arbitrary initial azimuths. Such viewpoint changes also reveal character regions unseen in the initial frame, causing geometric distortions, appearance degradation, and identity drift. To systematically study these coupled challenges, we introduce CLACT, a large-scale Character-centric dataset and benchmark covering diverse Large-Angle Camera Trajectories, with accurate camera geometry and multi-view character observations for training and evaluation. We further propose WorldCast, a framework for world-consistent character video generation under diverse large-angle camera motions. Specifically, we back-project the initial frame into a 3D point cloud using estimated depth, align the point-cloud scale with the target camera trajectory, and render it along the trajectory to obtain explicit geometric guidance and coverage masks. To complement the incomplete character information, we introduce Geometry-Aligned Memory Attention (GAMA). Using relative camera poses estimated jointly from the initial frame and multi-view character anchors, we express anchor and target-view geometry in a common coordinate frame. GAMA uses this geometric alignment to guide the retrieval and injection of view-relevant character features during generation. Evaluations across diverse large-angle camera trajectories demonstrate accurate trajectory following while preserving character geometry, appearance, and identity.

</details>


### [18] [Gaussian Episodic Memory for Long-Term Spatiotemporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33158)
*Ling-Chia Chen,Abdelrhman Werby,Kaan Aytekin,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GEMS：一种面向现实世界半静态场景变化的持久化时空3D高斯泼溅记忆框架，并结合智能体推理流程完成新视角渲染、时空问答和3D物体定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境会持续发生半静态变化，机器人需要同时记录空间结构和时间演化的场景记忆。现有结构化记忆受限于建模时已捕获的实体和属性，基于图像的记忆则难以超越已观测视角，因此难以支持灵活的时空推理和自然语言查询。

Method: GEMS首先一次性重建3D高斯泼溅场景图，并仅在发生变化区域进行增量更新，只存储修改部分以保留视觉细节和存储效率。随后通过智能体推理流程按需渲染新视角，迭代积累证据，回答时空问题并从自然语言查询中定位物体。作者还提出GEMS-Bench，用于评测半静态环境中的时空问答能力。

Result: 在场景更新下的新视角合成质量、3D物体定位、时空问答和存储效率等任务上进行评估。GEMS在3D物体定位和时空问答上优于近期基线，同时渲染质量与专门的场景更新方法相当。

Conclusion: GEMS通过持久化时空3DGS地图和智能体推理相结合，为半静态环境中的机器人记忆、场景问答和物体定位提供了高效方案；GEMS-Bench也为该方向的后续研究提供了评测基准。

Abstract: Real-world environments undergo continual semi-static changes, requiring a memory representation that captures both the spatial and temporal evolution of a scene. Existing spatio-temporal memories for robots typically rely either on structured representations, which are limited to the entities and attributes captured during construction, or on image-based memories, which are constrained to previously observed viewpoints. We introduce GEMS, a framework that combines a persistent spatio-temporal 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) map with an agentic reasoning pipeline. The 3DGS map is reconstructed once and subsequently updated only in regions where the scene changes, storing only these modifications while preserving visual detail across time. The agentic pipeline leverages this map to render novel views on demand, iteratively accumulating evidence to answer spatio-temporal questions and ground objects from natural-language queries. To advance future research, we introduce GEMS-Bench, a spatiotemporal question-answering benchmark focused on semi-static environments. It comprises 3 real-world and 12 synthetic environments, spanning 473 question-answer pairs. We evaluate GEMS on novel-view synthesis quality across scene updates, 3D object grounding, spatiotemporal question answering, and storage efficiency. GEMS outperforms recent baselines in 3D object grounding and spatiotemporal question answering, while achieving rendering quality on par with dedicated scene-update methods. Code and data: this https URL

</details>


### [19] [World-in-Context: Interactive Video World Modeling with In-Context Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33169)
*Harold Haodong Chen,Rui Peng,Rongjin Guo,Lingteng Qiu,Kejie Qiu,Chao Xu,Zilong Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 World-in-Context，一种基于 In-Context Conditioning（ICC）的交互式视频世界模型框架，将视角、动作和相机控制统一为显式 token，并直接注入共享自注意力空间；同时提出 Interaction-Transport RoPE（IT-RoPE）以利用累积控制转移保持时间连续性。该方法在 WBench 上以 5B 模型达到接近 22B EchoWM 的导航性能，并显著提升视角切换成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式视频世界模型通常存在三类问题：一是将观看视角隐含在文本 caption 中，难以显式控制；二是容易混淆角色动作与相机运动；三是通过碎片化的专用条件通路注入控制信号，只能间接建模视角、动作和相机之间的相互依赖。因此，需要一种统一、显式、可扩展的控制建模方式。

Method: 作者提出 World-in-Context 框架，核心是 In-Context Conditioning：不依赖专门的条件分支，而是将 view、action、camera 控制信号转换为显式 token，并与视频生成上下文共同放入统一 self-attention 空间。该设计使显式视角 token 能够解耦冲突动态，并从单视角数据中涌现视角切换能力；同时表明动作输入主要负责局部响应性，相机输入主要负责全局稳定性。为保持跨执行区间的时序连续性，进一步引入 IT-RoPE，根据累积控制转移来参数化时间注意力。

Result: 在 WBench 上，World-in-Context-5B 的导航性能为 81.5，与 22B EchoWM 的 81.6 基本持平；视角切换成功率达到 93.8%，大幅高于现有 5B 基线的 18.8%。受控分析还显示，在更丰富的条件组合、数据效率和因果 rollout 场景下，该方法具有持续优势。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将视角、动作和相机控制作为显式上下文 token 统一建模，比分散式条件通路更有效，能够更好地解耦不同控制因素、保持时序连续性，并提升交互式视频世界模型的可控性、视角切换能力和数据效率。

Abstract: Interactive video world models require coordinated control over viewing perspectives, character actions, and camera motion. Existing methods typically leave view modes implicit in text captions, conflate action with camera motion, and inject controls through fragmented architectural pathways that capture interdependencies only indirectly. In this work, we present World-in-Context, an interactive world modeling framework powered by In-Context Conditioning (ICC). Rather than relying on specialized conditioning pathways, World-in-Context unifies view, action, and camera controls into explicit tokens contextualized directly within a shared self-attention space. Our formulation reveals that (i) explicit view tokens disentangle conflicting dynamics and unlock emergent view switching from single-view data, while (ii) action and camera inputs play complementary roles in governing local responsiveness and global stability. To preserve temporal continuity across execution intervals, we further introduce Interaction-Transport RoPE (IT-RoPE), parameterizing temporal attention with accumulated control transitions. On WBench, World-in-Context-5B achieves navigation performance on par with 22B EchoWM ($81.5$ vs. $81.6$) and attains a $93.8\%$ perspective-switching success rate, substantially outperforming existing 5B baselines ($18.8\%$). Controlled analyses further demonstrate compounding advantages of World-in-Context under richer conditioning mixtures, favorable data efficiency, and natural applicability to causal rollouts.

</details>


### [20] [Support-guided OOD Removal with Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33194)
*Alireza Kheirandish,Sara Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SORD利用预训练干净信号扩散模型和已知腐蚀区域掩码，无需配对数据或腐蚀统计模型即可去除局部结构化OOD腐蚀并恢复干净信号，在图像与音频任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实信号常受局部、结构化且分布未知的离群分布腐蚀影响，例如音乐中的间歇背景噪声、照片水印和阴影。传统方法通常需要配对数据、明确腐蚀模型或多个专门模型，实际部署成本高且泛化受限。

Method: 提出Support-guided OOD Removal with Diffusion（SORD），一种迭代信号恢复算法。其仅需两类输入：腐蚀区域的二值支持图/掩码，以及一个在干净信号上预训练的扩散模型。算法在支持区域内外分别约束观测一致性与先验生成，不依赖配对训练数据，也不对腐蚀分布建模。

Result: 在自然图像、人脸、手写数字、图像叠加、水印、阴影等空间局部腐蚀，以及乐器录音中其他乐器声和人声等时间局部腐蚀上进行评估。SORD在相同扩散先验和掩码条件下，与后验采样式修复具有竞争力，常优于其；也优于需要为每个信源单独训练扩散模型的源分离基线；在若干基准上甚至超过使用配对数据训练的监督网络。

Conclusion: SORD表明，仅靠腐蚀支持区域掩码和一个干净信号扩散先验即可有效处理未知分布的局部结构化OOD腐蚀。该方法具有无需配对数据、无需腐蚀建模和跨图像/音频场景的通用性优势，代码已开源。

Abstract: Many real-world signals are corrupted by localized, structured perturbations whose distribution is unknown: an intermittent background noise in a music recording, a watermark stamped on a photograph, a cast shadow. We study the task of recovering a clean signal $x$ from an observation $y$ containing such a localized out-of-distribution (OOD) corruption. We propose Support-guided OOD Removal with Diffusion, SORD, an iterative algorithm to recover $x$ using only two ingredients: a binary map of the corruption support and a single diffusion model pretrained on clean signals. SORD does not require any paired data or any statistical model of the corruption. We evaluate SORD on natural images and human faces with spatially localized corruptions including handwritten digits, other images, watermarks, and shadows, as well as audio recordings of different musical instruments with temporally localized corruptions including other instruments and human speech. SORD is competitive with, and often outperforms, posterior-sampling inpainting given the same diffusion prior and the same mask, source-separation baselines that require a separate diffusion model for each source, and, on several benchmarks, supervised networks trained on paired clean and corrupted signals. The code is released here: \href{this https URL}{this https URL}.

</details>


### [21] [Of Chains and Gains: What Reinforcement Learning changes on Reasoning-based Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33203)
*Yixuan Li,Yuxuan Jiang,Hanwei Zhu,Hancheng Zhu,Fan Zhang,Shiqi Wang,David Bull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统检验 RLVR 在视觉质量评估中究竟带来了多少真实推理增益，发现评分提升部分来自采样效应和可利用的文本捷径，而非必然依赖显式推理链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点常将 RLVR 对大视觉语言模型图像质量评估的性能提升归因于更强的先推理后打分能力，但尚不清楚该提升是否超出采样效应，以及评分结果是否真正依赖于显式 Chain-of-Thought 推理。

Method: 作者通过采样预算敏感评估、受控推理干预和捷径实验进行分析：比较基础模型与 RL 训练模型在较大 max_g@k 下的表现；将 RL 模型生成的推理链移植给基础模型；固定推理链观察评分变化；测量推理时评分对推理链的依赖；对 thinking token 中的质量方向进行 steering；并检验模型是否利用与 MOS 相关的文本线索。

Result: RL 训练模型在大预算采样下优于基础模型，但超过平均化收益的改进主要集中在同分布情形。RLVR 生成的推理链能够提升基础模型表现，并在固定推理链时改善评分；然而推理阶段评分对推理链的依赖下降，且 7B 模型比 32B 模型更明显。对 thinking token 中质量方向进行干预几乎不影响评分。RL 还可能利用与 MOS 相关的文本线索提高打分，从而夸大基准表现。

Conclusion: RLVR 确实提升了质量评分并生成更有用的推理链，但更好的评分不等于更强的显式推理依赖；基于分数的奖励可能诱导与真实质量无关的捷径，需要更具体、更可靠的推理设计与评估机制。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has improved the performance of large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) on image quality assessment (IQA). Existing approaches often attribute these gains to improved reasoning before scoring, supported by increasingly plausible Chain-of-Thought (CoT). However, whether these gains exceed sampling effects and how they depend on explicit reasoning remain unclear. We rigorously investigate these questions through sampling-aware evaluation, controlled reasoning interventions, and shortcut experiments. We find that RL-trained models outperform base models under large-budget $\operatorname{max}_g@k$ evaluation, while improvements beyond averaging are concentrated in-distribution. RLVR produces chains that raise base-model performance when transplanted and improves scoring with the chain held fixed. Yet scoring becomes less dependent on the chain during inference, more clearly at 7B than at 32B. Steering the probed quality direction in thinking tokens also has little effect on scoring. Finally, RL can improve scoring accuracy by exploiting textual cues correlated with mean opinion scores (MOSs), which could inflate benchmark performance. Together, these findings show that RLVR raises quality scoring and produces more useful chains, but that better scoring does not imply a stronger reliance on explicit reasoning, while score-based rewards can also encourage reliance on quality-irrelevant shortcuts. We hope this preliminary study can enlighten more and more concrete reasoning schemes in IQA.

</details>


### [22] [The Locality Trade-off in Masked Patch Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33223)
*Xi Weng,Zhaoyu Liu,Lianyu Hu,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉基础模型中的 masked patch self-distillation 在提升语义表征的同时，会带来与规模相关的局部信息代价：小模型中空间对应性被削弱但外观可恢复性较好保留，大模型中对应性改善但像素/纹理证据在深层特征中的线性可及性下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DINOv2 和 DINOv3 等现代视觉基础模型广泛使用 masked patch self-distillation，但其对局部视觉信息，尤其是外观恢复与空间对应关系的影响尚不清楚。本文旨在评估这种训练机制是否以及如何造成局部性损失，并为表征设计与评价提供依据。

Method: 研究使用两种无需人工标注的诊断方法，分别区分“可恢复的局部外观”和“空间对应性”。作者在不同规模的视觉模型中分析添加 iBOT 类 masked patch self-distillation 后的特征表现，并进一步考察 Gram anchoring 对 patch 关系保持及其与局部属性改善之间关系的影响。

Result: 在较小模型中，加入 iBOT 会显著削弱 patch 间的空间对应性，但大体保留局部外观的可解码性。在较大模型中，空间对应性有所改善，但像素和纹理证据在深层特征中变得更不易通过线性方式访问。Gram anchoring 分析表明，保持 patch 关系并不必然同时改善外观保留和空间对应性。因此，规模扩大改变的是局部性损失的表现形式，而不是均匀消除该损失。

Conclusion: 视觉表征的评价与设计应同时关注语义抽象、外观保留和空间对应性这三类互补需求。masked patch self-distillation 有助于语义表征，但可能以不同方式影响局部视觉信息，因此需要更细致的表征诊断与机制设计。

Abstract: Masked patch self-distillation is a key component of modern vision foundation models, including DINOv2 and DINOv3, yet its implications for local visual information remain unclear. We show that its semantic benefits can coexist with a scale-dependent locality cost. Using two annotation-free diagnostics, we distinguish recoverable local appearance from spatial correspondence. In smaller models, adding iBOT substantially weakens patch correspondence while largely preserving appearance decodability. In larger models, correspondence improves, but pixel and texture evidence becomes less linearly accessible in deep features. Thus, scaling changes the form of the locality cost rather than uniformly eliminating it. Our analysis of Gram anchoring further shows that preserving patch relations does not guarantee simultaneous improvements in both properties. These findings motivate evaluating and designing visual representations around the complementary demands of semantic abstraction, appearance preservation, and spatial correspondence. Our code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [23] [KINESYS: Test-Time Adaptation for Reference-Based Effect Editing in Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33292)
*Noah Frahm,Luchao Qi,Haoze Li,Dan B Goldman,Sambit Bhattacharya,Roni Sengupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 KINESYS，一种面向“效果参考编辑”的测试时自适应框架，利用参考图像中的视觉效果和语言指令指导编辑，同时通过参考感知与源内容感知反馈，在保持原始构图与内容的情况下迁移目标效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多视觉效果难以用文字精确描述，更适合通过参考图像展示。现有单遍图像编辑方法在细粒度编辑上容易失败，常导致源图像内容或构图发生意外改变，也无法准确复现参考效果，因此需要一种能够平衡效果迁移与源内容保持的编辑方法。

Method: KINESYS 采用测试时自适应策略，针对每张待编辑图像在推理阶段进行个性化优化。框架结合参考感知反馈与源感知反馈：前者用于捕捉参考图像中的目标视觉效果，后者用于约束编辑结果保留原始镜头的内容、构图和主体属性。为保证生成的编辑多样且稳定地保持源结构，作者进一步设计鲁棒优化策略，在多个噪声样本和多次优化运行中对生成模型进行微调，从而降低优化偏差和过拟合风险。

Result: 实验使用视觉语言模型评判、源保持指标（LPIPS、TPIPS）以及用户研究进行评估。结果显示，KINESYS 在源内容保持方面优于当前最先进的图像编辑器；在效果迁移质量方面，它与 Nano Banana 2 表现相当，并优于开源权重编辑器。

Conclusion: KINESYS 证明了测试时自适应结合参考/源反馈能够有效提升细粒度图像编辑中的可控性，在迁移难以语言描述的视觉效果时，可同时保持源图像构图和内容完整性，是一种兼顾效果迁移与编辑安全性的实用框架。

Abstract: Visual effects are often easier to demonstrate via reference than to describe in words. We study effect-reference editing, where a reference image shows a desired visual effect and language identifies which properties should guide the edit. Existing single-pass editing techniques often fail to make fine-grained edits, resulting in unintended changes to source content or deviations from the reference effect. We introduce KINESYS, a test-time adaptation framework for effect-reference editing that combines reference-aware and source-aware feedback to transfer the desired effect while preserving the composition and content of the original shot. To ensure KINESYS can produce a diverse set of edits that preserve the source, we develop a robust optimization strategy in which the generative model is fine-tuned across multiple noise samples and optimization runs. Evaluation with VLM judges, source preservation metrics (LPIPS, TPIPS), and a user study shows that KINESYS outperforms state-of-the-art image editors in source preservation; on effect transfer, it remains competitive with Nano Banana 2 and outperforms open-weight editors.

</details>


### [24] [Real Time Motion Detection in Video Inspired by Photonic Time Stretch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33351)
*Callen MacPhee,Yiming Zhou,Bahram Jalali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受光子时间拉伸物理启发的运动感知算法 MoViD，通过将视频像素视为光学时间序列波形，利用“运动引起频谱展宽并经过群延迟色散后产生仅由运动诱导的时间相位”这一机制，实现全分辨率、实时的高精度运动估计、运动放大与轨迹分类，在 4K/8K 视频中展现出显著超越传统光流和学习方法的计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动感知是计算机视觉和物理 AI 的核心能力，对机器人、自动驾驶、人机交互和视频压缩等应用至关重要。传统方法如背景减除、帧差法和光流算法在复杂动态场景中鲁棒性不足，且通常为了计算效率而牺牲空间分辨率；现有基于相位的运动分析流水线往往依赖窄带空间通道化和金字塔重建，计算代价较高。因此，需要在不降低分辨率的情况下实现更快速、更物理一致的运动感知算法。

Method: MoViD 的核心思路是将视频中的每个像素序列建模为光学时间序列波形。当物体穿过某个像素时，会改变该像素时间序列的频谱宽度；随后通过群延迟色散（group delay dispersion, GDD）操作，将这种频谱展宽转换为可检测的时间相位变化。该相位仅在存在运动时出现，因而可作为运动存在的物理标志。算法进一步构造时空 GDD 模板，这些模板可类比为介电函数，用于对特定运动轨迹进行分类。与需要窄带空间滤波和多尺度金字塔重建的传统相位分析不同，MoViD 直接处理宽带视频信号，从而大幅减少计算开销。

Result: 论文声称 MoViD 在运动表征和运动放大两方面均取得前所未有的性能。该算法可实现全分辨率实时运动映射：4K 视频接近 200 fps，8K 视频约 70 fps。与经典光流方法或基于学习的方法相比，其速度提升数个量级，使超高分辨率场景中的实时运动检测与补偿成为可能，适用于机器人、自动驾驶及其他自主系统。

Conclusion: MoViD 将光子时间拉伸中的物理原理迁移到视频运动感知，通过宽带、相位驱动且无需金字塔重建的机制，实现了高分辨率、低延迟、强实时的运动分析。该结果说明物理启发式信号处理可以在部分计算机视觉任务中替代或超越经验式相位分析框架，为面向机器人和物理 AI 的超高速、超高分辨率运动感知提供了新路径。

Abstract: Motion perception is fundamental to computer vision and physical AI, and plays a central role in robotics, autonomous vehicles, human-computer interaction, and video compression. Traditional approaches such as background subtraction, frame-differencing and optical flow algorithms often struggle in complex or dynamic scenes, typically sacrificing spatial resolution for computational tractability. Here we introduce MoViD (Motion estimation via Virtual dispersion and phase Detection), a physics-inspired algorithm grounded in the principles that govern photonic time stretch - a highly successful ultrafast realtime data acquisition platform. Drawing inspiration from the optical physics therein, MoViD treats video as a collection of optical time series waveforms associated with pixels. Motion of an object through a pixel broadens the time series spectrum and, after group delay dispersion, induces a temporal phase that exists only if there is motion. Spatiotemporal group delay dispersion templates, corresponding to dielectric functions, classify specific motion trajectories. Unlike conventional empirical phase-based motion-analysis pipelines that require narrow-band spatial channelization and pyramid reconstruction, the physics-based MoViD operates directly on the broadband video signal, vastly increasing its computational speed. The resulting algorithm achieves unprecedented performance in both motion characterization and motion magnification, providing full-resolution motion mapping in real time at nearly 200 frames per second at 4K resolution and 70 frames per second for 8K video - orders of magnitude faster than classical optical flow approaches or learning-based methods. Notably, such extreme performance enables real time motion analysis at ultrahigh resolution for motion detection and compensation in robotics and other autonomous devices.

</details>


### [25] [Detecting Temporal Concepts in Video Transformers via a No-False-Positives Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33353)
*Jianghuai Li,Andrew Ren,Greg Shakhnarovich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出No-False-Positives (NFP)统计测试，用于以较高统计特异性识别视频模型中真正编码时间概念的特征；实验表明VideoMAE、V-JEPA2和TimeSformer均存在时间特征，但仅对Sparse Autoencoder标记的时间特征进行干预并不一定优于非时间特征基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频模型理论上可以编码跨多帧的时间概念，例如物体运动。然而，运动信息和外观/位置变化往往相互纠缠，一个看似编码“物体运动”的特征，可能实际编码的是不同空间位置上的出现情况，只是其激活恰好与运动相关。因此需要一种能够控制假阳性、可靠区分真正时间概念与非时间相关概念的测试方法。

Method: 作者提出NFP测试，一种具有统计特异性保证的假设检验，用以识别表示空间中真正编码时间概念的表示方向或特征。该方法使用专门设计的时间刺激视频，并将其应用于VideoMAE、V-JEPA2和TimeSformer等视频模型的表示。为验证测试有效性，作者先在DINOv2图像编码器上检验，确认其不应检出不合理的时间特征；随后在合成表示空间中分析检测率与方向在时间子空间上投影之间的关系，并考察被Sparse Autoencoder标记为时间特征与非时间特征的方向差异。最后对时间SAE特征进行干预实验。

Result: NFP测试在DINOv2中没有检出不合理的时间特征，说明其具有较强特异性；在VideoMAE、V-JEPA2和TimeSformer中均检出了时间特征。合成实验显示，检测率随表示方向在时间子空间上的投影增大而上升；被NFP标记为时间特征的SAE特征，其归一化后在时间子空间上的投影明显高于未被标记的特征。然而，对不同模型中的时间SAE特征进行干预得到的结果并不一致，表明干预时间特征未必优于干预非时间特征。

Conclusion: NFP测试为识别视频模型中的真正时间概念特征提供了一种统计上可靠、假阳性受控的方法，证明现有视频模型确实编码了某些时间表示。但该工作同时表明，识别到时间特征并不等同于能够可靠地通过干预这些特征来改变模型行为；视频时间概念的可控性仍需更谨慎的因果评估。

Abstract: Video models have the architecture to support encoding temporal aspects of the input, i.e. concepts that require more than one frame to define, like object movement. However, because motion and appearance change are entangled, a feature that appears to encode temporal concepts like object movement may instead encode non-temporal concepts like spatial presence at different positions whose activations merely correlate with motion. To resolve this, we introduce the No-False-Positives (NFP) test, a statistical test with a formal guarantee of statistical specificity for identifying features encoding temporal concepts. Using specially designed stimuli, we apply this test to representations produced by VideoMAE, V-JEPA2, and TimeSformer, and observe that temporal features exist in each model. We validate the test first by showing it yields no temporal features for the DINOv2 image-encoder. Then, using a synthetic representation space, we show that detection rate increases with a direction's projection onto the temporal subspace, and that Sparse Autoencoder features test-flagged as temporal project much more of their norm onto the temporal subspace than unflagged features. Interventions on temporal SAE features yield mixed results across models, suggesting that intervening on temporal features may not outperform a non-temporal feature baseline.

</details>


### [26] [Tight Learned Event-Inertial Odometry (TLEIO): a Transformer-Based Architecture for End-to-End Motion from Event Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33356)
*Matteo Cascone,David Jensen,Alessandro Pirini,Angelo Stefanini,Pietro Venè,Marta Tintoré Gazulla,Javier Hidalgo Carriò*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TLEIO，一种基于滤波的紧凑事件-惯性里程计管线，直接从不规则体素化事件流学习短窗口相对运动，并与IMU传播紧耦合，实现高效、无显式特征跟踪和位姿优化的事件驱动状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有微秒级时间分辨率、高动态范围和稀疏异步测量特性，适合快速运动和复杂光照下的里程计任务。但现有事件-惯性系统常将事件作为帧的补充，或依赖特征跟踪、匹配搜索和重优化后端，限制了事件传感器在效率上的优势，且高精度状态估计仍然困难。

Method: 作者提出Tight Learned Event-Inertial Odometry（TLEIO）。其前端EventsFormer直接从不规则体素化事件流中，以单次前向传播预测局部位移和各轴不确定性；后端采用随机克隆扩展卡尔曼滤波，将重叠窗口学习到的位移约束与IMU传播紧耦合，从而生成米制轨迹。该方法推理阶段无需显式特征跟踪或位姿优化，网络也仅从位姿位移监督训练，不依赖事件光流标签。

Result: 在TartanAir/TartanEvent基准和真实DAVIS240c序列上的实验表明，相比仅使用IMU的TLIO基线，TLEIO将平均绝对轨迹误差（ATE）从2.26米降低到1.10米，同时相比基于优化的事件里程计方法运行时间最多降低48.9%。

Conclusion: TLEIO通过从事件体素直接学习短程相对运动，并用EKF将其与IMU紧耦合，在保持较高定位精度的同时显著降低计算负担，验证了滤波式学习前端能够替代传统重匹配和重优化后端，为高效事件-惯性里程计提供了一种紧凑方案。

Abstract: Event cameras provide microsecond temporal resolution, high dynamic range, and sparse asynchronous measurements, making them compelling for odometry under fast motion and challenging illumination. Yet, accurate event-based state estimation remains difficult: existing systems often use events as a complement to frames or rely on feature tracking, correspondence search, and optimization-heavy backends, limiting the efficiency promised by event sensing. We introduce Tight Learned Event-Inertial Odometry (TLEIO), a compact filter-based pipeline that learns short-window relative motion directly from voxelized event streams and tightly fuses the resulting constraints with IMU propagation. Its learned front-end, EventsFormer, predicts local displacements and per-axis uncertainty from event voxels in a single forward pass, while a stochastic-cloning EKF fuses these overlapping predictions with IMU propagation, producing metric trajectories without explicit feature tracks and pose optimization at inference time. The network is trained directly from pose displacements, without requiring event-flow labels. We evaluate TLEIO on the TartanAir/TartanEvent benchmark and on real DAVIS240c sequences. On TartanAir, TLEIO reduces average ATE from 2.26 m to 1.10 m over the IMU-only TLIO baseline, while achieving up to 48.9% lower runtime than optimization-based event odometry methods.

</details>


### [27] [Dynamic Query as Budgeting for Tiny Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33430)
*Yi Zhang,Di Xiong,Ge Gao,Xiangyue Zhang,Yihang Qiu,Yuxuan Zhou,Shuo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出BUTR，将DETR类小目标检测器中的动态查询机制解耦并重新解释为“查询预算分配”，即根据图像中目标密度动态决定解码器查询数量，而不是依赖复杂的学习型查询筛选模块；BUTR仅用轻量、非学习的预算器从编码器置信度估计输入相关查询预算K(x)，并保持标准Top-K(x)查询初始化，从而在AI-TOD-v2和VisDrone上取得较高AP和FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DETR的小目标检测器常用动态查询机制来缓解小目标数量密集、密度不均的问题，但已有设计往往把“解码多少查询”和“选择哪些查询”两个因素耦合在一起，导致难以判断性能提升真正来源，也增加额外学习模块、启发式过滤带来的开销与错误选择风险。

Method: 作者将动态查询的作用解耦，提出“预算分配”视角：动态查询的核心是根据目标密度分配解码器查询容量。基于该原则，设计单调且保守的预算机制，提出BUTR框架。BUTR使用轻量、非学习的预算器，从编码器输出的置信度估计与输入相关的查询预算K(x)，随后仅采用标准Top-K(x)查询初始化，不引入额外学习型模块或启发式过滤，以减少开销和潜在选择误差。

Result: 在800×800分辨率上，BUTR-L在AI-TOD-v2达到35.7 AP、47.8 FPS，在VisDrone达到29.8 AP、42.1 FPS（NVIDIA T4 GPU）。此外，在COCO上的推理时集成实验表明该方法也可用于一般场景检测。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 动态查询在小目标DETR检测器中的主要增益可来自自适应查询预算分配，而非复杂查询选择机制。BUTR通过简洁的预算器与标准Top-K查询初始化，兼顾效率与精度，说明“解耦、简化、可解释的预算分配”是动态查询设计的有效方向。

Abstract: Recent DETR-based tiny object detectors adopt dynamic query mechanisms to handle severe density imbalance. However, existing designs entangle two effects: how many and which queries are decoded. This entanglement obscures what drives the gains and complicates dynamic-query design; additional learned predictors and heuristic filtering also introduce overhead and potential selection errors. We disentangle these effects and show that dynamic query primarily acts as \emph{budgeting}---allocating decoder query capacity according to object density---rather than intricate query this http URL by this view, we formulate a monotonic and conservative budgeting principle and propose a budgeting-based DETR (BUTR), a streamlined framework that uses a lightweight, non-learned budgeter to estimate an input-dependent query budget $K(x)$ from encoder confidences. BUTR retains standard Top-$K(x)$ query initialization without additional learned modules or heuristic filtering in the this http URL $800\times800$ resolution, BUTR-L achieves 35.7 AP at 47.8 FPS on AI-TOD-v2 and 29.8 AP at 42.1 FPS on VisDrone on an NVIDIA T4 GPU. Inference-time integration on COCO further demonstrates its applicability to general scenario detection. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [28] [Semantic-First MixGRPO: Reliable Emotional Image Generation via Temporal Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33460)
*Zhenxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出在流式图像生成中将“随机采样窗口的位置”作为属性控制变量，用早期确定性ODE建立内容、后期短暂SDE调节情感外观，以在保持语义结构的同时实现可控情感化图像合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控图像合成需要在改变视觉情感风格时保留提示词指定的对象、布局和关系。现有方法多依赖条件架构或辅助损失，而混合ODE/SDE采样主要被用于加速GRPO类优化，尚未被系统研究为属性控制机制。

Method: 作者使用FLUX.1-dev并结合EmotiCrafter式valence-arousal条件，研究概率流采样中随机窗口位置对情感控制与语义保持的影响。对比固定早期、中期、晚期四段式SDE窗口、渐进式MixGRPO和全路径Flow-GRPO。提出语义优先调度：步骤0-19使用确定性ODE采样建立内容，步骤20-23使用随机SDE采样和策略更新进行属性调节。

Result: 在三个随机种子下，该方法获得CLIPScore 29.662±0.276、跨情感DINO一致性80.1±0.9、语义漂移2.9%，并将训练成本相对全路径Flow-GRPO降低33.5%。全路径随机训练在valence-arousal目标上更准确，说明存在真实性权衡。相比渐进式调度，语义优先方案同时提升情感指标和语义指标，在五个极端或中性目标下保留主要物体的比例为95.98%，并在30人用户研究中获得最高语义保持和总体偏好评价。

Conclusion: 概率流采样中的时间位置可作为实际可行的图形控制维度，用于可靠的图像情感外观操控；该研究揭示了情感精度、语义保持和训练效率之间的权衡，并证明早确定性、晚随机的语义优先窗口是更实用的可控合成策略。

Abstract: Controllable image synthesis should modify visual affect without replacing the objects, layout, or relations specified by a prompt. Existing methods address this tension through conditioning architectures or auxiliary losses, while mixed ordinary differential equation (ODE) and stochastic differential equation (SDE) sampling has mainly been used to accelerate group relative policy optimization (GRPO). We instead study stochastic-window position as an attribute-control variable for flow-based image generation. Using FLUX.1-dev with EmotiCrafter-style valence-arousal conditioning, we compare fixed early, middle, and late four-transition SDE windows, progressive MixGRPO, and full-path Flow-GRPO under matched settings. Because step 0 has the highest noise, our semantic-first schedule uses deterministic ODE sampling at steps 0-19 to establish content, then confines stochastic exploration and policy updates to steps 20-23. Across three seeds, it achieves a CLIPScore of $29.662\pm0.276$, cross-affect DINO consistency of $80.1\pm0.9$, and semantic drift of 2.9%, while reducing training cost by 33.5% relative to full-path Flow-GRPO. Full-path stochastic training remains more accurate on valence-arousal targets, revealing a genuine trade-off. Semantic-first nevertheless improves both affect and semantic metrics over progressive scheduling, preserves the principal subject in 95.98% of images at five extreme or neutral targets, and receives the highest semantic-preservation and overall-preference ratings in a 30-participant study. These results identify probability-flow time as a practical graphics control dimension for reliable affective appearance manipulation.

</details>


### [29] [Qwen-Image-PE: Learning Visual Design for Text-to-Image Prompt Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33472)
*Zekai Zhang,Yanran Zhang,Shengming Yin,Yan Shu,Zhening Liu,Bingxuan Zhao,Chenyang Zhu,Donghao Zhou,Jiahao Li,Jiahao Wang,Jie Zhang,Kaiyuan Gao,Kun Yan,Lihan Jiang,Ningyuan Tang,Qihang Ai,Rui Song,Shuo Cao,Xiao Xu,Xiaoyue Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Zhendong Wang,Zhouyang Li,Zihao Liu,Zikai Zhou,Bo Zheng,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao,Chenfei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出Qwen-Image-PE，通过监督微调和基于设计奖励的强化学习两阶段训练，使提示增强模型显式学习细粒度视觉设计，并提升文本到图像生成质量、美学和跨模型泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示增强方法通常保留用户意图并改善整体视觉效果，但对光照、构图、布局和细节等细粒度设计维度关注不足，难以稳定地把欠定用户请求转化为高质量、连贯的视觉规格。

Method: 采用两阶段训练：第一阶段使用基于密集图像描述的Caption-Based Reverse Distillation进行监督微调，将精心设计图像的描述作为视觉设计监督；第二阶段使用Groupwise Design Reward进行强化学习，对同一用户请求的多个候选图像进行组内联合比较，重点优化光照、构图、布局和细节等设计维度。

Result: 在Qwen-Image-Bench和Qwen-Image-2.1上，Qwen-Image-PE-397B相比原始请求提升7.81分，Qwen-Image-PE-9B优于所有被评测的提示增强基线；同时在视觉设计、美学、图像质量和人类偏好上均有提升。研究还发现提示增强可沿训练数据、模型规模和信息密度三个维度扩展，且足够高的信息密度是有效增强的前提。此外，该方法可泛化到四个额外文本到图像骨干模型，并在HunyuanImage-2.1和FLUX 3 Image Klein上超过其原生提示增强器。

Conclusion: 显式建模细粒度视觉设计能够显著提升文本到图像提示增强效果；结合密集设计监督、组内设计奖励和信息密度扩展规律，可使增强器在不同规模和多类生成骨干上保持有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: Learning visual design is essential for prompt enhancement (PE) to turn underspecified user requests into coherent visual specifications for text-to-image (T2I) generation. Existing methods preserve user intent and improve overall visual appeal, with less explicit emphasis on fine-grained design dimensions. To address this gap, we introduce Qwen-Image-PE, which learns visual design through two-stage training. Supervised fine-tuning with Caption-Based Reverse Distillation (CRD) uses dense captions of well-designed images as design supervision. Reinforcement learning with Groupwise Design Reward (GDR) jointly compares all candidate images for the same request to refine lighting, composition, layout, and detail. On Qwen-Image-Bench with Qwen-Image-2.1, Qwen-Image-PE-397B improves over original requests by 7.81 points, while Qwen-Image-PE-9B outperforms all evaluated PE baselines. Further evaluations show gains in visual design, aesthetics, image quality, and human preference. We find that PE scales along three axes: training data, model size, and information density, with sufficient information density as a prerequisite for effective enhancement. Moreover, we demonstrate that Qwen-Image-PE generalizes to four additional T2I backbones, even surpassing native prompt enhancers on HunyuanImage-2.1 and FLUX 3 Image Klein. We release Qwen-Image-PE-9B at this https URL.

</details>


### [30] [Can One Hear Geometry? Gauge-Invariant Spectral Descriptors for Text-to-B-rep Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33484)
*Hongzhu Yi,Hao Liu,Tao Yu,Tiankun Yang,Yujia Yang,Zhenghao Zhang,Tianyu Zong,Zhenyu Guan,Bingkang Shi,Haopeng Jin,Yuanxiang Wang,Zhongtian Luo,Jungang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于群作用与谱几何不变量的文本到B-rep实体检索框架，显式构建对平移、旋转、镜像、缩放及轮廓记录惯例不变的几何描述子，从而提升Text-to-3D实体检索的精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Text-to-3D solid retrieval需要将自然语言与精确几何对齐，但实体常受位姿、尺度和建模记录惯例变化影响。现有方法将B-rep离散化为点云或网格，或从面、边和拓扑中提取特征，容易造成信息损失，并且缺乏显式的不变性保证。

Method: 作者用群作用刻画由平移、旋转、镜像、均匀缩放和轮廓记录惯例引起的几何等价变换，并将不变性作为描述子构造的显式约束。针对草图拉伸类实体，构造固定280维谱几何描述子，融合截面区域、轮廓边界和拉伸结构三个互补层面的几何信息；同时采用描述子一致的多正样本集合与几何属性监督，促进文本与3D实体之间的结构对齐。

Result: 在Text-to-3D实体检索任务上，该方法达到88.6%的Recall@5，较此前最优结果提升13.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 将显式几何不变量引入B-rep实体描述，有助于缓解位姿、尺度和建模惯例变化带来的检索干扰，并提升自然语言与精确3D几何之间的匹配能力。

Abstract: Text-to-3D solid retrieval requires aligning natural language with precise geometry despite variations in pose, scale, and recording conventions. Existing methods discretize Boundary Representation (B-rep) models into point clouds or meshes, or learn features from faces, edges, and topology, risking information loss or lacking explicit invariance guarantees. We propose a text-to-B-rep retrieval framework based on group actions and spectral geometric invariants, making invariance an explicit constraint on descriptor construction. Specifically, we use group actions to characterize equivalence transformations induced by translation, rotation, reflection, uniform scaling, and contour recording conventions. For sketch-extrusion solids, we construct a fixed 280-dimensional spectral geometric descriptor by integrating geometric information from three complementary levels, including cross-sectional regions, contour boundaries, and extrusion structures. Furthermore, we use descriptor-consistent multi-positive sets and geometric attribute supervision to promote structural alignment between text and solids. On Text-to-3D solid retrieval, our method achieves 88.6% Recall@5, exceeding the previous state of the art by 13.6 percentage points and demonstrating the effectiveness of explicit geometric invariants for precise 3D solid retrieval.

</details>


### [31] [Explicit Frictional Contact Modeling in Neural Garment Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33492)
*Thanh-Nguyen Truong,Vu-Hoang Tran,Ching-Chun Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需重训练和迭代求解的模块化接触算子，在GNN服装模拟预测后显式施加库仑摩擦修正，使 stick/slip 行为随摩擦系数可调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的物理自监督服装模拟难以显式设置推理时的摩擦系数μ；系数特定摩擦项重训练对μ响应弱。优化求解器能逐接触判断stick-slip，但每步需重新优化服装状态，计算代价高。

Method: 在冻结的GNN预测器之后附加模块化接触算子：估计法向载荷，沿身体三角形表面路径施加闭式库仑摩擦修正，并通过线性化膜波动传播由摩擦诱发的布料运动，从而显式控制接触摩擦而不重训练或迭代求解。

Result: 在HOOD和ContourCraft上，该方法随μ单调增加stick、降低slip，接近参考求解器的响应；相比系数特定重训练（对μ几乎不响应）表现更强，同时保持低穿透和参考一致的拉伸，运行时间仅为参考求解器的1/8。

Conclusion: 将摩擦从训练目标中的隐式惩罚或系数特定重训练中解耦，改为预测后的显式库仑修正，是一种高效、可控的服装-身体摩擦模拟方案，适合需要推理时设置μ的GNN模拟场景。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) simulate garments efficiently through physics-based self-supervision, but leave body-garment friction implicit or inside training energies, with no friction coefficient $\mu$ to set at inference; retraining the tested GNNs with a coefficient-specific friction term barely responds to $\mu$. Optimization-based solvers decide each contact's stick-slip state from the reaction that governs it, but only by re-optimizing the garment state at every step; a friction term in a one-pass training objective acts as a fixed-weight penalty on sliding, and the trained weights apply no per-contact Coulomb condition at inference. We therefore resolve friction explicitly after prediction: a Modular Contact Operator, attached to a frozen predictor without retraining or an iterative solve, estimates the normal load, applies a closed-form Coulomb correction along a path across body triangles, and propagates the induced motion through the cloth as a linearized membrane wave. On HOOD and ContourCraft, it raises sticking and lowers slip monotonically in $\mu$, tracking a reference solver's response while coefficient-specific retraining stays flat, with low penetration and reference-consistent stretching at an eighth of the solver's runtime.

</details>


### [32] [FreqPKC: Frequency-Aware PeakConv for Radar Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33504)
*Yangyong Deng,Pengyu Zhang,Shaoqiu Song,Xiaotao Huang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从频域角度重新审视毫米波雷达图像中的PeakConv算子，提出频率感知的PeakConv（FreqPKC）及其语义分割框架FreqPKC-Net，通过自适应调节有效带宽来平衡高低频信息，从而提升多视角雷达语义分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车毫米波雷达在恶劣天气和光照条件下具有鲁棒性，已用于自动驾驶语义分割。现有雷达专用神经算子PeakConv（PKC）虽能建模雷达图像的峰值特性，但通常将guard bandwidth作为可学习参数，缺乏对PKC频域响应特性的显式刻画，限制了网络对不同频率成分的选择性利用。

Method: 提出FreqPKC，显式建模并利用PKC的频谱特性，使其能够捕获更广频率成分并自适应调整有效带宽。引入频率感知（FA）模块，推断合适的guard bandwidth以平衡高低频信息：在目标区域增强高频成分以提升分割精度，在背景区域通过低频滤波抑制噪声。基于FreqPKC构建FreqPKC-Net，用于雷达语义分割，结合频率引导自适应调制与多视角输入下的跨视角融合，并可适配单视角数据。

Result: 在公开数据集CARRADA、CARRADA-RAC以及自建RS-SAR数据集上进行实验，结果表明FreqPKC-Net在range-Doppler和range-angle视角上均持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 从频域显式刻画PKC算子的有效带宽和频率响应有助于更好地利用雷达图像中的高低频信息，提升语义分割精度与鲁棒性；FreqPKC及其网络框架为雷达语义分割提供了频率感知的改进方向。

Abstract: Automotive millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar has attracted increasing attention for semantic segmentation in autonomous driving, owing to its robustness under adverse weather and lighting conditions. Recent works have introduced radar-specific neural operators such as PeakConv (PKC), inspired by constant false alarm rate (CFAR) detection, to better model the peak-dominated characteristics of radar images. However, existing methods typically treat the guard bandwidth as learnable parameters, without explicitly characterizing the frequency response of the PKC operator. In this work, we revisit PKC from a frequency-domain perspective and propose Frequency-Aware PeakConv (FreqPKC), which explicitly models and exploits its underlying spectral characteristics. By analyzing the frequency response of PKC, FreqPKC enables the network to capture a broader range of frequency components and adaptively adjust its effective bandwidth. With the proposed Frequency-Aware (FA) module, the network can infer appropriate guard bandwidths to balance high- and low-frequency information: it enhances high-frequency components in target regions for more precise segmentation while suppressing noise through low-frequency filtering in background areas. Building on FreqPKC, we further develop FreqPKC-Net, a radar semantic segmentation (RSS) framework that integrates frequency-guided adaptive modulation with cross-view fusion for multi-view inputs, and can also be adapted to single-view data. Experiments on the public datasets CARRADA, CARRADA-RAC, and a self-built dataset named RS-SAR demonstrate that FreqPKC-Net consistently outperforms existing SoTA methods on both range-Doppler and range-angle views.

</details>


### [33] [Patch-Derived or Register Tokens? Comparing Variable-Length Image Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33540)
*Philipp Schenkel,Tobias Schürmann,Omer Sahin Tas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究比较图像 tokenizer 中变长图像 token 的两种策略：保留空间组织的 patch-derived tokens 和没有预设空间布局的 register tokens，并评估其在不同压缩预算下的重建质量、下游生成性能与空间表征特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像 tokenizer 多将潜变量排列在降采样二维网格上，使序列长度与图像分辨率绑定。为了支持高效生成建模，需要探索可变长度 latent 表示方式，并理解不同 token 设计在压缩、重建、生成和空间组织上的权衡。

Method: 作者比较两类变长图像 token：一是计算高效、具有显式空间结构的 patch-derived tokens；二是计算代价更高、没有预定义空间布局的 register tokens。研究评估不同 token 预算下的重建质量、下游生成任务表现，并分析两种 token 是否学习空间局部化表示。进一步引入与外部预训练视觉编码器的 representation alignment，检验其对生成性能的影响。

Result: 在极端压缩下，patch-derived tokens 的重建效果明显优于 register tokens；在中等 token 预算下，register tokens 重建质量略优；高 token 预算下两者接近。下游生成任务中，register tokens 通常优于 patch-derived tokens，但加入表示对齐后，patch-derived tokens 的生成性能显著提升，可在不同预算下追平甚至超过 register tokens。两类方法都倾向学习空间局部化表示：patch-derived tokens 保留显式空间组织，register tokens 虽无预设布局，却发展出隐式二维空间结构。表示对齐会减弱这种空间组织约束，使单个 token 具有更强的非局部影响。

Conclusion: 图像 tokenizer 的设计需在空间结构、压缩效率和生成性能之间权衡。Patch-derived tokens 更适合高压缩重建，register tokens 更有利于生成，但通过外部视觉表示对齐可以增强空间化 token 的生成能力。研究还表明，显式或隐式空间组织会影响 token 的作用范围，表示对齐可促进更全局、非局部的特征建模。

Abstract: Image tokenizers convert pixels into compact latent sequences for efficient generative modeling. Most approaches arrange latents on a downsampled two-dimensional grid, tying sequence length to image resolution. We compare two variable-length strategies: computationally efficient patch-derived tokens, which inherit explicit spatial organization, and more demanding register tokens, which have no predefined spatial layout. Our reconstruction results reveal that patch-derived tokens perform substantially better under extreme compression, whereas register tokens achieve slightly better reconstruction quality at intermediate budgets. At high token budgets, both approaches achieve similar reconstruction quality. For downstream generative tasks, register tokens outperform patch-derived tokens. Adding representation alignment with external pretrained visual encoders substantially improves the generative performance of patch-derived tokens, allowing them to match or outperform register tokens depending on the token budget. We further find that both approaches learn spatially localized representations. Patch-derived tokens largely retain their explicitly imposed spatial organization, while register tokens develop an implicit two-dimensional spatial structure despite having no predefined layout.
