</details>


### [68] [LegoOcc++: Towards Consistent Open-Vocabulary 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34093)
*Changqing Zhou,Yueru Luo,Han Zhang,Zeyu Jiang,Changhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于语言嵌入高斯的开放词汇3D占用预测框架，仅使用二值占用标签即可实现室内外环境中的开放词汇3D场景理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇3D占用预测对复杂环境中的具身智能体很重要，但室内场景面临密集遮挡、复杂几何和细粒度长尾语义等挑战；同时，昂贵的体素级语义标注限制了模型训练与扩展。

Method: 方法以Language-Embedded Gaussians作为几何与语言对齐语义的统一表示。几何方面，引入不透明度感知的泊松型Gaussian-to-Occupancy公式，使模型在仅二值占用监督下实现稳定的体积聚合。语义方面，将高斯特征渲染到图像并与冻结开放词汇模型对齐，同时使用渐进温度衰减减少高斯泼溅过程中的特征混合。进一步引入实例级语义一致性以缓解碎片化预测，并采用共视感知支持视图选择以获得更有信息量且更少冗余的多视角语义监督。该框架还被扩展到户外驾驶场景。

Result: 在室内和户外基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在开放词汇3D占用预测任务上取得较强性能，验证了框架的有效性和跨环境泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作以较低的几何标注成本实现了开放词汇3D占用预测，并通过语义对齐与多视角监督设计提升了预测一致性和鲁棒性，为室内外具身场景理解提供了通用表示框架。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D occupancy prediction is important for embodied agents operating in complex environments whose semantic categories extend beyond fixed taxonomies. While recent studies have explored open-vocabulary occupancy in outdoor driving scenarios, indoor environments remain challenging due to dense occlusions, complex geometry, and fine-grained long-tailed semantics. To reduce the need for expensive voxel-level semantic annotations, we study a geometry-only supervision setting requiring only binary occupancy labels. We build upon Language-Embedded Gaussians as a unified representation coupling 3D geometry with language-aligned semantics. For geometry learning, we introduce an opacity-aware Poisson-based Gaussian-to-Occupancy formulation that enables stable volumetric aggregation under binary occupancy supervision. For semantic learning, we render Gaussian features into images and align them with a frozen open-vocabulary model, together with Progressive Temperature Decay to reduce feature mixing during Gaussian splatting. Building on this framework, we further introduce Instance-Level Semantic Consistency to alleviate fragmented object predictions, and Covisibility-Aware Support-View Selection to provide informative and less redundant multi-view semantic supervision. We also extend our framework to outdoor driving scenarios to evaluate its generality across substantially different environments. Extensive experiments on indoor and outdoor benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves strong open-vocabulary occupancy performance, validating the effectiveness and generality of our framework. Code will be available at this https URL.

</details>


### [69] [StickFigure: Enabling Pose-Only IoT Cameras for Privacy-Preserving Edge Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34130)
*Aveline Xiaoyuan Han,Chenshu Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StickFigure，一种面向室内IoT相机的隐私保护姿态感知系统，仅导出人体骨架流用于动作识别，而不上传原始RGB帧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内IoT相机可支持辅助生活和活动感知空间，但导出原始RGB视频会泄露人脸、衣着和家庭环境等隐私信息，而人体动作识别通常并不需要这些细粒度视觉内容。同时，在资源受限的MCU级IoT相机上实时、准确地提取姿态流仍具有挑战。

Method: StickFigure设计了一种激活感知的姿态主干网络，在空间下采样之后分配通道容量；同时提出轻量级分层MLP精修器，结合中间视觉特征和运动学上下文，有选择地修正肘部和腕部坐标。系统仅在本地保留短暂原始帧，导出紧凑骨架流供下游HAR使用，并在公开数据集和真实部署中评估。

Result: 在NTU RGB+D和Penn Action上，StickFigure相比SOTA MCU姿态模型取得了最高姿态精度和下游HAR效用。与RTMPose-S基线相比，参数量减少96.5%，每帧MACs减少99.3%，并在STM32H753 MCU上实现0.107秒p95延迟。

Conclusion: StickFigure通过隐私友好的骨架流导出和高效端侧姿态建模，为大规模IoT相机上的隐私感知边缘视觉建立了新的运行点。

Abstract: Indoor IoT cameras can support assisted living and activity-aware spaces, but exporting raw RGB frames raises privacy concerns by exposing facial, clothing, and household details that body-motion tasks often do not require. We present StickFigure, a pose-only IoT camera system that, during real-time monitoring, keeps raw frames local and ephemeral while exporting only compact skeleton streams sufficient for downstream human activity recognition (HAR). While pose estimation has matured on GPUs, extracting accurate pose streams on resource-constrained IoT cameras remains difficult. To overcome these challenges and enable on-device pose streams on (almost) any IoT camera, we present two core designs in StickFigure: an activation-aware pose backbone that allocates channel capacity after spatial downsampling and a lightweight hierarchical MLP refiner that selectively corrects elbow and wrist coordinates using intermediate visual features and kinematic context. We fully implement StickFigure and evaluate it on public benchmarks and real-world deployment. Compared with state-of-the-art MCU pose models, StickFigure achieves the highest pose accuracy and downstream HAR utility on both NTU RGB+D and Penn Action, while using 96.5% fewer parameters and 99.3% fewer MACs per frame than RTMPose-S-based baselines, achieving 0.107 s p95 latency on a representative STM32H753 MCU. We believe StickFigure establishes a new operating point for privacy-aware edge vision on massive IoT cameras.

</details>


### [70] [Reversion, Retention, and Semantic Intervention: Measuring Functional Content in CLIP Fine-Tuning Deltas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34144)
*Chih-Ting Liao,Kezhen Bao,Xin Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文针对微调权重变化的因果作用难以解释的问题，提出将“还原选定参数”和“只保留选定更新”配对使用，以评估CLIP微调中注意力值输出更新等权重差值的功能贡献，并发现这些更新虽与性能提升语义对齐，但与其他更新存在可恢复相同行为的操作性冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重空间解释方法可以为微调带来的权重变化赋予语义描述，但无法判断任务性能在多大程度上依赖这些被识别出的更新，尤其当其他权重更新也可能支持相同预测时，语义描述与因果功能之间的关系仍不清楚。

Method: 作者在CLIP模型上开展微调实验，包括ViT-L/14用于合成金融图表、Flowers102、EuroSAT和FGVCAircraft，以及ViT-B/16用于Flowers102；采用共享配方微调最后六个视觉块。方法上，将“还原”（reversion）即把选定参数恢复为预训练值，与“保留”（retention）即仅保留选定微调更新配对使用，并比较注意力value-output（VO）、MLP、随机子集以及语义方向移除，在准确率、交叉熵损失和正确类别分数margin等指标上分析微调权重变化的功能作用。

Result: 仅保留注意力VO微调更新可恢复43%至87%的准确率提升，但在图表任务中还原VO并未降低准确率，说明存在冗余。VO更新恢复损失的幅度优于每个设置中的十组等规模、层匹配随机子集。VO与MLP保留联合可修正25%至81%的预训练错误，体现出重叠恢复行为。此外，在所有五个设置中，移除语义选定方向对VO保留恢复的削弱程度大于匹配的随机移除。

Conclusion: 论文表明，微调权重更新的语义对齐、反事实移除代价和保留后的性能恢复是三种互补证据，不能仅凭语义描述判断权重变化的功能角色。在该CLIP微调配方中，注意力VO更新对性能提升具有显著且优于随机更新的作用，但其贡献与MLP等更新存在操作性冗余，因此需要结合还原、保留和移除等干预实验才能更准确刻画微调权重的因果功能。

Abstract: Fine-tuning adapts pretrained models to new tasks, but identifying which weight changes support the resulting behavior remains difficult. Weight-space interpretability assigns semantic descriptions to these changes, called fine-tuning deltas. Such descriptions leave open how much task performance depends on the identified updates, particularly when other updates can support the same predictions. We address this gap by pairing reversion, which restores selected parameters to their pretrained values, with retention, which keeps only selected fine-tuning updates. We evaluate Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) models on synthetic financial charts, Flowers102, EuroSAT, and FGVCAircraft with ViT-L/14, and on Flowers102 with ViT-B/16, using a shared recipe that tunes the final six visual blocks. Our contributions are threefold. First, attention value-output (VO) updates alone recover 43-87% of the accuracy improvement, although reverting them leaves chart accuracy unchanged. Cross-entropy loss preserves this contrast on Charts and EuroSAT, while correct-class score margins reveal additional contributions. Second, VO weights recover more loss improvement than all ten equally sized, layer-matched random subsets in each setting. VO and multilayer perceptron (MLP) retentions jointly correct 25-81% of pretrained errors, demonstrating overlapping recovered behavior, which we call operational redundancy. Third, removing semantically selected directions reduces VO-retained recovery more than matched-random removal in all five settings. Together, these results distinguish semantic alignment, removal cost, and retained recovery as complementary evidence about the functional role of fine-tuning updates within the evaluated CLIP recipe.

</details>


### [71] [MuSTEL: A Large-Scale Multilingual Synthetic Text-Line Image Dataset for OCR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34148)
*Majid Adibian,Hossein Saberi,Hossein Zeinali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MuSTEL，一个大规模合成多语种 OCR 文本行数据集，包含超过 1450 万张带标注文本行图像，用于训练和评测涉及波斯语、阿拉伯语和英语的行级多语种 OCR 系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学字符识别系统高度依赖训练数据的规模与多样性。面向波斯语、阿拉伯语和英语的多语种文本行识别尤其困难，因为同一文本行内可能同时出现不同书写方向、字体和样式。现有资源在覆盖这些混合语言、混合方向和复杂排版条件方面不足，因此需要大规模、多样化的行级 OCR 数据来支持训练、评测和鲁棒性研究。

Method: 作者构建 MuSTEL 合成数据集，从自然语言语料中生成样本，包括源文本中已有的多语种内容。生成框架考虑能力感知的字体选择、同一文本行内的字体和样式变化、混合方向文本渲染、高亮和下划线等排版效果。数据集中包含训练集、预设的小型测试集和大型测试集。作者还使用经校验的 2009 个字体文件（来自 711 个字体家族）提升排版多样性，并通过多样背景、模糊、几何失真、倾斜、笔画宽度和图像保存质量等增广条件生成接近真实文档的图像。最后，对语言构成、转录长度、词汇与字符序列多样性、图像尺寸、字体排印和渲染条件进行统计分析。

Result: MuSTEL 包含超过 1450 万张带标注的文本行图像，覆盖波斯语、阿拉伯语和英语，并具备大规模合成生成、丰富字体家族、多方向混排、多样视觉退化和明确测试集划分等特点。统计分析进一步刻画了数据集的语言、文本长度、字符分布、图像尺寸和渲染条件等属性。

Conclusion: MuSTEL 为训练和评估多语种文本行级 OCR 系统提供了大规模资源，特别适合研究跨语言、跨书写方向和跨视觉退化条件下的识别鲁棒性。

Abstract: Optical character recognition (OCR) systems depend strongly on the scale and diversity of their training data. One approach to document OCR first analyzes the page layout and extracts text-line images, which are then transcribed by a recognition model. Multilingual line-level recognition involving Persian, Arabic, and English presents additional challenges because different writing directions, fonts, and styles may coexist within a single line. This paper introduces MuSTEL, a large-scale synthetic multilingual OCR dataset containing more than 14.5 million annotated text-line images, organized into a training partition and predefined small and large test sets. Samples are generated from natural-language corpora, including multilingual content already present in the source texts. The generation framework incorporates capability-aware font selection, within-line font and style changes, mixed-direction text rendering, highlighting, and underlining. A validated repository of 2,009 font files from 711 families supports broad typographic coverage, while varied backgrounds, blur, geometric distortion, skew, stroke width, and image save quality provide diverse document-like appearances. Statistical analysis characterizes the dataset's language composition, transcription lengths, lexical and character-sequence diversity, image dimensions, typography, and rendering conditions. MuSTEL provides a resource for training and evaluating multilingual line-level OCR systems and investigating recognition robustness across languages, writing directions, and visual conditions.

</details>


### [72] [Humans in Space: Can MLLMs Construct Human-Centric Spatial Understanding via Active Exocentric Exploration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34149)
*Tianchen Guo,Tengyue Zhang,Chen Liu,Ling Chen,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出SATE-Bench，通过真实世界多视角交互问答评估多模态大语言模型在遮挡场景中的视觉自我意识和主动外视角探索能力，发现现有模型倾向于过早停止、过度自信且难以融合多视角证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型的空间能力评测主要依赖静态、被动的观察，容易忽略遮挡场景中的关键几何证据；被动基准迫使模型猜测而非识别自身视觉不确定性，因此需要评估其“知道该往哪里看、如何整合所见证据以及何时停止”的主动空间推理能力。

Method: 作者构建包含316个真实场景和2525组多轮问答的交互式基准SATE-Bench，同时设置被动多视角控制任务与主动探索任务；在主动设置中，模型从严重遮挡视角出发，逐步查询相邻视角，直到获得足以回答问题的证据，并以带步数惩罚的效率奖励评分。进一步对前沿多模态模型进行评估，并通过匹配视角控制与GRPO微调分析探索行为与性能关系。

Result: 实验表明，当前先进模型往往在证据不足时提前终止探索却给出高置信度答案，且在细粒度三维几何任务上表现较弱；匹配视角控制显示主动与被动之间的性能差距主要源于证据获取不足。GRPO微调可促使模型增加视角探索，但整体准确率下降，原因是模型过度探索且难以跨轮次融合不断扩展的多视角上下文。

Conclusion: 扩大被动多视角感知并不能自然转化为主动空间推理能力；未来模型需要具备主动选择观察视角、整合多轮视觉证据并判断何时停止探索的能力，SATE-Bench为评估这类能力提供了新的基准。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at zero-shot reasoning, their spatial capabilities are primarily evaluated through static, passive observation. In complex, human-centric scenes, occlusion often hides crucial geometric evidence, and passive benchmarks force models to guess rather than recognize their own visual uncertainty. To bridge this gap, we introduce SATE-Bench, a novel interactive benchmark comprising 316 real-world scenes and 2,525 multi-turn QA pairs to evaluate visual self-awareness and active exocentric view-exploration. It combines a Passive multi-view control with an Active exploration setting, where models start from a severely occluded view and must iteratively query adjacent viewpoints until they gather enough evidence to fully ``sate'' the question, scored by a step-penalized efficiency reward. Evaluating state-of-the-art MLLMs reveals that they terminate exploration prematurely while reporting high confidence, and struggle with fine-grained 3D geometric tasks; matched-view controls further attribute the Active--Passive gap primarily to insufficient evidence acquisition. Moreover, fine-tuning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) shows that rewarding view-seeking increases active exploration but lowers overall accuracy, as models over-explore and fail to fuse expanding multi-view contexts across turns. These findings indicate that scaling passive multi-view perception does not intrinsically yield active spatial reasoning, highlighting the need for models that know where to look, how to integrate what they see, and when to stop. Code and data are available at this https URL

</details>


### [73] [Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Crowd Flow Control Using Dynamic Barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34163)
*Alhanof Alolyan,He Wang,Amani Alonayzan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于仿真环境的强化学习框架，用于自动学习大规模人流场景中人群隔离栏的优化布局；该方法无需标注最优栏位数据，通过 Soft Actor-Critic 智能体在仿真中持续调整隔离栏位置，以减少行人停留并维持有效人流调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型公共活动如朝觐场景中，固定隔离栏布局虽然能够控制整体人流，但常导致不必要的行人停留和局部拥堵。现有方法面临关键数据瓶颈：不存在针对给定人群场景的最优隔离栏布局标注数据集，因此难以通过监督学习直接训练高性能布局优化模型。

Method: 作者首先从分割后的真实人群场景出发，构建可复现观测行人分布的仿真环境；随后在该环境中训练 Soft Actor-Critic 强化学习智能体，使其能够根据行人运动状态连续调整隔离栏位置。与依赖标注数据的监督方法不同，该框架允许智能体探索真实数据中未出现过的隔离栏配置，从而发现更优布局策略。

Result: 在未见过行人数量条件下评估所学习到的策略，结果表明该策略相比现有人工设计的隔离栏配置能够持续取得更好的控制效果：一方面减少行人停留，另一方面保持有效的人流调节能力。

Conclusion: 该研究证明将计算机视觉驱动的人群仿真与强化学习相结合，能够为人群控制基础设施的优化提供有效框架。即使缺乏最优布局标注数据，也可以通过交互式仿真学习发现优于人工设计的隔离栏配置。

Abstract: Crowd barriers are widely used to regulate pedestrian movement during large public events such as pilgrimages. However, fixed barrier layouts often create unnecessary stopping and local congestion even when they successfully control the overall crowd flow. Finding better barrier configurations is difficult because there is no dataset that provides the best barrier placement for a given crowd scene. We address this challenge by introducing a simulation-driven reinforcement learning framework that learns barrier configurations without requiring labelled training data. Starting from a segmented crowd scene, we construct a realistic simulation environment that reproduces the observed pedestrian distribution. This environment is then used to train a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) agent that continuously adjusts barrier positions to improve pedestrian movement. Unlike supervised approaches the proposed framework allows the agent to explore barrier configurations that have never been observed in real data. We evaluate the learned policy under unseen numbers of pedestrians. The learned policy consistently outperforms the existing manually designed barrier configuration by reducing pedestrian stops while maintaining effective flow regulation. These results demonstrate that combining computer vision crowd simulation and reinforcement learning provides an effective framework for optimizing crowd control infrastructure.

</details>


### [74] [Freeze What Is Settled, Cache What Is Smooth: Spatial Freezing Keeps Step Caching in Its Low-Error Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34177)
*Baiyan Li,Ganxuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向指令图像编辑的免训练加速方法 FreezePlan，通过重新分配“区域冻结”和“步骤缓存”的加速预算，在保持较高编辑质量的同时实现约 5 倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令式扩散 Transformer 图像编辑器通常需要数十步去噪，计算开销大。现有免训练加速主要包括两类：跨步骤复用特征的 step caching，以及跳过未被编辑区域的 region freezing。虽然二者可组合使用，但论文进一步追问：在追求高加速倍率时，如何合理划分两种机制各自承担的加速预算，才能在速度提升和误差控制之间取得更好平衡。

Method: 论文以 Qwen-Image-2.1 为对象，发现区域冻结不仅减少 token 计算，还会降低每一步的整体开销，因此允许 step cache 使用更保守的阈值并减小误差。作者构建了逐步开销模型，用于预测延迟并复现现有区域方法的速度上限。对于冻结区域，使用模型一步 x0 估计比简单返回参考图像更有效。基于这些观察，提出 FreezePlan：保留少量完整去噪步骤，并将剩余加速预算交给 TaylorSeer 这类 step cache 方法。实验还通过替换已有 region selector 来比较调度与选择器的贡献。

Result: FreezePlan 在预注册测试集上实现 5.05 倍端到端加速，相对原始输出的 PSNR 为 29.8 dB，且 VLM 评分没有显著下降；作为对比，TaylorSeer 在 5.02 倍加速时仅达到 23.5 dB。该方法的性能曲线可进一步延伸到 5.99 倍加速、28.0 dB PSNR。开销模型预测延迟的中位误差为 4.0%，并能预测现有区域方法的加速上限。若仅替换区域选择器而保持 FreezePlan 的调度，PSNR 仅下降约 0.5--0.6 dB，说明主要增益来自调度与预算分配，而非特定选择器。

Conclusion: 指令图像编辑中的高加速目标不应只关注“冻结区域”和“步骤缓存”能否同时使用，而应重视二者如何分配预算。区域冻结降低单步成本，使得更保守的 step cache 也能达到高加速；因此将少量步骤保留为完整计算、其余交给 step cache 的调度策略更有效。FreezePlan 的核心贡献在于揭示并利用了 region freezing 与 step caching 之间的成本—误差耦合关系，从而在质量接近原输出的情况下实现显著加速。

Abstract: Instruction-based image editors built on diffusion transformers run tens of denoising steps. Training-free acceleration either reuses features across steps (step caching) or stops computing the regions an edit leaves untouched (region freezing), and the two have been combined before. We ask how a high speed-up target should be split between them. On Qwen-Image-2.1, freezing does more than remove tokens: it lowers the cost of every step, so the step cache can reach the same overall speed-up with a conservative threshold that keeps its error small. A per-step cost model fitted on development runs predicts held-out latency with a median error of 4.0% and reproduces the speed ceilings of the region methods we re-implemented (2.73$\times$ predicted vs. 2.70$\times$ measured for RegionE). Driving frozen tokens to the model's one-step $x_0$ estimate beats returning them to the reference image on every development sample, and in 90% of measured cases the composed error stays below the sum of the two individual errors. The resulting method, FreezePlan, keeps five full steps and gives the rest of the budget to TaylorSeer. On a pre-registered test split it runs 5.05$\times$ faster end to end at 29.8 dB PSNR to the original output, with no significant drop in a VLM judge score; TaylorSeer at 5.02$\times$ reaches 23.5 dB. The curve extends to 5.99$\times$ at 28.0 dB. Swapping in the region selectors of prior methods under our schedule lowers median PSNR by only 0.5--0.6 dB, which places the gain in the schedule and budget split rather than in the selector.

</details>


### [75] [GeoPrompt: Geometry-Conditioned Prompt Learning for Underwater Open-Vocabulary Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34190)
*Shanshan Han,Yuxi Wang,Can Qin,Yujie Chen,Cong Pan,Haitao Li,Guoqiang Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GeoPrompt，一种面向水下开放词汇实例分割的几何条件化提示学习框架，利用水下场景中更稳定的几何结构信息来增强视觉-语言对齐，提升已知与未知类别的识别和分割能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下开放词汇实例分割依赖视觉区域特征与文本类别嵌入的对齐，但水下环境中的颜色衰减、散射、浑浊和低对比度会严重破坏视觉外观，使区域特征偏离真实语义概念，导致视觉与文本分类器之间的相似度不可靠，尤其削弱无法通过类别监督修正的未知水下类别识别性能。

Method: 提出 GeoPrompt 框架。首先设计几何引导视觉适配模块（GVAM），从深度信息中提取结构化先验，并注入多尺度视觉特征中以补偿外观退化。随后提出几何条件化提示学习模块（GCPL），由结构表示生成具有几何感知能力的文本分类器。最后将这些几何感知分类器与固定文本分类器以及图像条件化文本分类器融合，以增强开放词汇语义对齐。

Result: 在 MARIS-OpenIS 基准上的实验表明，GeoPrompt 在水下开放词汇实例分割任务上取得了 state-of-the-art 性能，并带来稳定一致的改进。

Conclusion: 在水下退化场景中，几何结构比视觉外观更具稳定性。通过深度引导的结构先验和几何条件化提示学习，GeoPrompt 有效缓解了水下开放词汇实例分割中视觉-语言对齐脆弱的问题，为海洋感知中的未知类别识别与分割提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary instance segmentation (OVIS) provides a promising solution for marine perception by recognizing and segmenting categories beyond predefined label spaces. Existing OVIS methods usually rely on vision-language alignment, where visual region features are matched with textual category embeddings to recognize both seen and unseen classes. However, this alignment assumption becomes fragile in underwater environments. Color attenuation, scattering, turbidity, and low contrast distort visual appearance, causing region features to deviate from their corresponding semantic concepts. As a result, the similarity between degraded visual features and textual classifiers becomes unreliable, which particularly weakens the recognition of unseen underwater categories that cannot be corrected by category-specific supervision. To address these issues, we propose GeoPrompt, a geometry-conditioned prompt learning framework for underwater open-vocabulary instance segmentation. Our key insight is that geometric structure is more stable than visual appearance in degraded underwater scenes. Specifically, GeoPrompt introduces a geometry-guided visual adaptation module (GVAM) to extract depth-derived structural priors and inject them into multi-scale visual features. It further develops a geometry-conditioned prompt learning (GCPL) that generates geometry-aware textual classifiers from structural representations. These geometry-aware classifiers are integrated with fixed and image-conditioned textual classifiers to improve open-vocabulary semantic alignment. Experiments on the MARIS-OpenIS benchmark show that GeoPrompt achieves state-of-the-art performance and brings consistent improvements on underwater open-vocabulary instance segmentation.

</details>


### [76] [Timing Matters Twice: When to Query and When to Skip in Flow-Matching On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34191)
*Renye Yan,Junyi Zhan,Jikang Cheng,Wei Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出PhaseOPD，一种面向flow-matching模型在线策略蒸馏的相位感知查询与跳过框架，能够在线判断生成阶段，为不同任务教师选择合适的监督状态，并跳过不必要的后续rollout，从而降低训练开销并提升多个图像生成指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy distillation方法通常从预设时间步分布中选择监督状态，忽略了不同评价维度和任务对去噪过程不同阶段的侧重，导致监督效率不足和额外计算开销。需要解决两个时机决策问题：何时向教师请求有效监督，以及何时跳过剩余rollout以避免冗余计算。

Method: PhaseOPD利用学生模型已有的速度预测，在线通过预测clean latent的修正识别生成阶段，不需要额外模型评估。对于任务对应教师，框架根据该教师的相位偏好选择监督状态，并在完成查询决策后跳过未使用的rollout后缀，同时保持单次查询速度蒸馏目标。

Result: 在SD3.5-M上，PhaseOPD每个local batch仅执行16个rollout位置中的5.56个，相比DanceOPD训练时间减少41.3%，同时提升Aesthetic、GenEval和OCR分数。

Conclusion: PhaseOPD证明在flow-matching模型蒸馏中，根据生成阶段自适应选择教师查询和跳过冗余rollout比固定时间步监督更高效，可同时降低训练成本并改善生成质量、构图和文字相关能力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) enables flow-matching models to acquire complementary capabilities from specialized teachers through supervision on student-generated trajectories. However, different evaluation dimensions, such as aesthetics and OCR accuracy, and different tasks, such as image generation and editing, emphasize different aspects of the denoising process, suggesting that their effective supervision may occur at different stages. Existing methods typically select supervision states from predefined timestep distributions, which may overlook these phase-dependent differences and incur unnecessary computational overhead. This motivates two critical timing decisions: when to adaptively query teachers for effective supervision based on the evolving generative process, and when to skip the remaining rollout to avoid redundant computation. In this paper, we introduce PhaseOPD, a phase-aware query-and-skip framework that identifies generative phases online from revisions of predicted-clean latents, using existing student velocity predictions without additional model evaluations. For each task-assigned teacher, PhaseOPD selects a supervision state based on its phase preference and skips the unused rollout suffix once all query decisions are finalized, preserving the single-query velocity-distillation objective. On SD3.5-M, PhaseOPD executes only 5.56 of 16 rollout positions per local batch, reducing training time by 41.3% relative to DanceOPD while improving Aesthetic, GenEval, and OCR scores.

</details>


### [77] [CropReward: A Human-Preference Benchmark and Reward Model for Aesthetic Image Cropping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34221)
*Xiangfei Sheng,Xiaofeng Pan,Leida Li,Qian Zhang,Biao Wang,Johan Wagemans,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作提出大规模美学图像裁剪人类偏好基准CropBench、基于多模态大语言模型的奖励模型CropReward，并利用其监督训练偏好对齐裁剪模型CropAlign，以解决现有裁剪评价指标与人类偏好不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美学图像裁剪（AIC）需要可靠评价来反映人类偏好，但现有指标主要依赖IoU等几何指标或图像美学质量评价模型，长期存在与人类偏好不一致的风险，其鲁棒性和可靠性尚未被充分研究。

Method: 作者构建包含1,019张图像、106,995组人类偏好对的CropBench，候选包括原图及14种AIC方法生成的裁剪结果，并系统评估现有指标；进一步提出MLLM-based奖励模型CropReward，通过比较候选裁剪图与原图输出显式美学增益或损失分数；随后用CropReward构建CropReward-300k数据集，并在仅使用CropReward监督、不依赖人工裁剪框的条件下训练CropAlign偏好对齐AIC模型。

Result: CropBench实验表明，从IoU到现成IAA模型在内的现有评价标准与人类偏好对齐有限；CropReward在反映人类偏好方面显著优于现有指标；CropAlign超越当前先进AIC方法，显示CropReward能有效引导AIC模型优化。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了AIC评价中几何指标和传统美学模型的不足，并通过人类偏好基准、MLLM奖励模型和自动监督训练形成闭环，为AIC评测、数据构建和偏好对齐模型训练提供了更可靠的新范式。

Abstract: Aesthetic image cropping (AIC) aims to improve image composition and aesthetic quality by removing extraneous or distracting content. Over the past decade, AIC methods have evolved from specialized vision models to multimodal large language models(MLLMs). Reliable evaluation that reflects human preferences is essential for benchmarking AIC methods and guiding model optimization. However, the current AIC evaluation relies largely on geometry-based metrics such as intersection over union (IoU), as well as image aesthetic assessment(IAA) models. Although inconsistencies with human preferences have long been observed, the robustness and reliability of these evaluation criteria remain underexplored. To bridge this gap, we first construct CropBench, a large-scale benchmark comprising 106,995 human preference pairs for 1,019 images, with each candidate set containing the original image and candidate crops produced by 14 AIC methods. A systematic study on CropBench reveals that the existing criteria, ranging from IoU to off-the-shelf IAA assessment models, only exhibit limited alignment with human preferences. Building upon CropBench, we further propose CropReward, an MLLM-based reward model that evaluates a candidate crop by comparing with the original uncropped image and outputs a score indicating explicit aesthetic improvement or degradation relative to the original. In addition, we employ CropReward to construct CropReward-300k, the largest AIC dataset to date, and further develop CropAlign, a preference-aligned AIC model trained solely with CropReward supervision, without human-annotated crop boxes. Experimental results demonstrate that CropReward aligns with human preferences significantly better than existing criteria. CropAlign also surpasses the state-of-the-art AIC methods, demonstrating the advantage of CropReward in guiding AIC model optimization.

</details>


### [78] [BoundedGS: Memory-Efficient High-Resolution Training for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34233)
*Xiaobin Deng,Changyu Diao,Min Li,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 BoundedGS，针对高分辨率 3D Gaussian Splatting 训练中显存峰值过高的问题，将光栅化与投影反向传播解耦，并限制活跃局部光栅化工作集，从而在保持重建质量的同时大幅降低渲染相关显存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率 3D Gaussian Splatting 训练会显著增加像素与 Gaussian tile 的交叉数量，使光栅化缓存、投影缓存和梯度状态在峰值内存中共存，导致大场景或高分辨率训练难以在有限显存中完成。

Method: BoundedGS 采用完整视图级梯度聚合策略，使每个区域的前向和反向缓存可在贡献被收集后释放；同时分离光栅化反向与投影反向传播，并根据实测负载限制活跃局部光栅化工作集。此外，通过候选索引投影和紧凑梯度存储缓解视图级峰值，利用 CPU 辅助结构重建和受限图像供给管理渲染之外的资源。

Result: 在固定约 6M Gaussians 的分辨率扫描中，1K 到 16K 分辨率下渲染工作集稳定在 1.073–1.077 GiB；在 11K 分辨率下，热运行耗时 19.79 秒，低于全帧执行的 21.76 秒。在五个原生 5K 户外场景中，完整训练将平均渲染工作集从 9.97 GiB 降至 1.76 GiB，降低 82.3%，同时保持相近重建质量，并将训练时间从 36.7 分钟缩短至 33.9 分钟。

Conclusion: BoundedGS 能有效约束高分辨率 3DGS 训练中的显存增长，使渲染工作集在很宽分辨率范围内近似恒定，并在显著降低峰值显存的同时保持或略微提升训练效率与重建质量。

Abstract: High-resolution 3D Gaussian Splatting training increases pixel and Gaussian-tile intersection counts, expanding rasterization caches that coexist with projection and gradient state at peak memory. A complete-view update aggregates gradients across the view; each region's forward and backward caches can be released after its contribution is collected. BoundedGS separates rasterization and projection backward passes and bounds the active local rasterization working set according to its measured load. Candidate-indexed projection and compact gradient storage address the view-level peaks exposed by this change, while CPU-assisted structure rebuilding and bounded image supply manage resources outside rendering. In a resolution sweep at a fixed count of about 6M Gaussians, the measured rendering working set stays within 1.073-1.077 GiB from 1K to 16K; at 11K, a warm pass takes 19.79 seconds versus 21.76 for full-frame execution. Across five native 5K outdoor scenes, full training reduces the mean rendering working set from 9.97 to 1.76 GiB (82.3%), retains similar reconstruction quality, and shortens training time from 36.7 to 33.9 minutes. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [79] [ParetoGS: High-Quality Continuous-Budget 3D Gaussian Splatting with Sparse Adapted Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34235)
*Xiaobin Deng,Changyu Diao,Min Li,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ParetoGS 针对可缩放 3D Gaussian Splatting 中“同一场景表示服务不同高斯数量预算时质量下降”的问题，提出通过稀疏预算锚点、残差图集和连续预算解码，实现无需为每个预算单独优化的近似最优质量—规模权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可缩放 3D Gaussian Splatting 允许一个场景表示在不同高斯数量预算下使用，但质量—预算曲线可能低于针对每个预算单独训练的方法。原因是降低高斯数量不仅改变被保留的成员，还改变剩余成员需要承担的渲染任务；从完整模型继承的参数可能并不适配低预算表示。因此，需要在保持可缩放性的同时，让成员选择与参数演化随预算一致地适应。

Method: 提出 ParetoGS。其核心是用少量“适配后的预算锚点”描述成员变化和参数演化，并解码中间预算，避免为每个请求的高斯数量单独优化。方法交替进行剪枝和固定成员适配，构建具有持久身份关系的嵌套锚点；将这些锚点相对于完整模型的参数变化编码为紧凑残差图集。解码时，对共享成员的状态进行插值，并逐步引入新增成员的渲染贡献，从而把稀疏锚点连接成连续预算表示。

Result: 在 13 个场景中，ParetoGS 在 10% 自身完整模型预算下，以更少高斯获得更好的平均 PSNR、SSIM 和 LPIPS，优于所比较的前缀方法。基于同一个共享完整模型，它在 1%、5% 和 10% 训练预算上优于 FlexGS-imp，并在 1% 和 0.1% 预算上优于 NanoGS-imp。在 Garden 场景中，其非锚点质量在较高保留率下接近独立优化 GNS 模型的经验参考值，并在部分较低预算点超过该参考。编码将平均额外状态从 390.0 MB 降至 85.7 MB，同时平均 PSNR 变化仅为 0.01 dB。

Conclusion: ParetoGS 表明，可缩放 3D Gaussian Splatting 的质量瓶颈可通过显式建模“成员替换”和“参数随预算演化”来缓解。其嵌套锚点、残差图集和连续预算解码机制，使模型无需为每个预算单独优化即可逼近逐预算优化效果，同时显著降低附加状态开销，为低开销、连续可控的 Gaussian 预算缩放提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Scalable 3D Gaussian Splatting allows one scene representation to serve different Gaussian-count budgets, yet its quality-budget curve may lag behind models optimized separately for each budget. Reducing the number of Gaussians changes both the selected members and the reconstruction tasks carried by the survivors; parameters inherited from a high-budget model may fit a low-budget representation poorly. We propose ParetoGS, which describes member changes and parameter evolution with a small set of adapted budget anchors and decodes intermediate budgets without separate optimization at every requested count. ParetoGS alternates pruning and fixed-member adaptation to build nested anchors with persistent identities, and encodes their parameter changes relative to the full model in a compact residual atlas. At decoding, it interpolates the states of shared members and gradually introduces the rendering contributions of new members, connecting sparse anchors into a continuous-budget representation. Across 13 scenes, ParetoGS attains better average PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS with fewer Gaussians than the compared prefix methods at 10% of each method's own full model. From a shared full model, it also outperforms FlexGS-imp at its trained 1%, 5%, and 10% budgets and NanoGS-imp at the 1% and 0.1% budgets. On Garden, its non-anchor quality approaches an empirical reference from independently optimized GNS models at higher retention ratios and exceeds it at some lower-budget points. Across 13 scenes, encoding reduces mean additional state from 390.0 to 85.7 MB with an average PSNR change of 0.01 dB. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [80] [RacerGS: Residual-Autoregressive Context for 3D Gaussian Splatting Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34263)
*Wenqing Wang,Huimin Zeng,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RacerGS，一种面向3D高斯泼溅的高效压缩框架，通过哈希投影、FiLM调制自回归GRU熵模型和残差自适应熵编码，在大幅降低存储占用的同时保持较高渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽能实现快速高质量新视角渲染，但其参数量巨大，导致存储和传输成本高，因此需要面向实用部署的紧凑表示与高效压缩机制。

Method: RacerGS采用锚点哈希编码到紧凑上下文特征的哈希投影模型，获得空间连贯表示；引入FiLM调制的自回归GRU上下文模型，利用锚点特征组之间的因果依赖并通过循环隐状态优化熵模型参数；同时对量化后的锚点属性残差进行自适应熵编码，利用上下文预测均值和空间一致性降低符号熵，从而提升码率效率。

Result: 在五个数据集上，相比原始3DGS实现超过150倍压缩，相比Scaffold-GS实现约28倍压缩，渲染质量可与原始方法相当，并在存储减少和率失真性能上优于已有3DGS压缩方法。

Conclusion: RacerGS通过紧凑特征表示、自回归熵建模和残差熵编码显著降低了3DGS模型体积，同时保持较高质量，是面向存储与传输受限场景的有效3DGS压缩方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a promising approach for fast, high-quality novel-view rendering. However, its large parameter size remains a bottleneck for storage and transmission. We propose RacerGS, a compact 3DGS compression framework that achieves scene reconstruction at significantly reduced storage sizes with preserved fidelity. To obtain compact and spatially coherent features, we design a hash projection model that projects the anchor hash encodings into compact context features. Additionally, we introduce a FiLM-modulated autoregressive GRU context model that utilizes causal dependencies among anchor feature groups and uses recurrent hidden states to refine entropy-model parameters, improving bitrate efficiency. Furthermore, we adaptively entropy-code quantized anchor-attribute residuals relative to their context-predicted means, leveraging spatial consistency to reduce symbol entropy. Overall, RacerGS achieves more than 150x compression over vanilla 3DGS and 28x over Scaffold-GS, while providing comparable rendering quality. Extensive experiments across five datasets demonstrate that RacerGS outperforms prior 3DGS compression methods in both storage reduction and rate-distortion performance.

</details>


### [81] [Robust Single-Parameter Point Cloud Registration using Truncated Lp Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34271)
*Seong Hun Lee,Patrick Vandewalle,Javier Civera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅含一个可调参数的鲁棒点云配准方法 TLP，通过截断 $L_p$ 函数为对应关系分配优先级、筛选假设并优化最终模型，在 GPU 并行加速下实现快速高效的点云配准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际三维特征匹配中常存在高比例外点，点云配准鲁棒性要求高；许多多参数方法需要针对不同数据集精细调参，实用性和可迁移性受限。

Method: 该方法使用不同 $p$ 值的截断 $L_p$ 函数为点对应关系分配优先级分数，用于假设筛选和最终模型优化；同时利用 GPU 并行化高效评估大量假设，使千级对应关系下的配准可在平均一秒内完成。整个方法仅保留一个可调参数。

Result: 在公开基准数据集上，TLP 的配准准确率高于现有先进单参数方法；与两个广泛使用的多参数方法相比，TLP 在四个数据集中的三个上表现更优，同时保持有竞争力的运行时间。

Conclusion: TLP 在保持单参数易用性的同时，通过截断 $L_p$ 鲁棒建模和 GPU 并行加速，有效兼顾了高外点场景下的鲁棒性、精度和实时性，具有较强实用性。

Abstract: We propose a novel method for robust point cloud registration with only one tunable parameter. Our method, dubbed TLP, uses truncated $L_p$ functions with different values of $p$ to assign priority scores to correspondences, select the hypothesis, and refine the final model. By leveraging the robustness of truncated functions, TLP effectively handles the high outlier rates commonly encountered in real-world 3D feature matching. We also exploit GPU parallelization to evaluate hypotheses efficiently, enabling registration from thousands of correspondences in under a second on average. With only one tunable parameter, our method offers greater practicality than multi-parameter methods that require dataset-specific tuning of several parameters. Evaluations on public benchmark datasets demonstrate that TLP achieves higher accuracy than state-of-the-art single-parameter methods. Furthermore, it outperforms two widely used multi-parameter methods on three of the four evaluated datasets while maintaining competitive runtime.

</details>


### [82] [ChronoReplay: One Answer Interface Is Not Enough for Streaming Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34283)
*Yihao Hu,Yitong Lin,Pan Wang,Zhihao Wen,Hang Wang,Yiwen Zhao,Deyuan Liu,Yutong Li,Mingda Wan,Yuchen Mao,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出“ChronoReplay”来缓解空间视觉语言模型（spatial VLM）中的“接口锁定”（interface lock-in）问题：当后训练只强调某一类监督信号（如左右关系或距离估计）时，模型可能在某一任务接口上提升，但难以迁移到其他空间判断接口。ChronoReplay 通过按时间顺序重放异质空间监督、保留原始目标并平衡监督家族，在同等的观测访问条件下，同时提升 OVO-S-Bench、OST-Bench 和 UCS-Bench 的整体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练空间 VLM 回答“物体在某一侧”这类问题时，模型不必真正保留决定答案的度量位移信息；而针对距离估计的监督也可能不会充分约束类别化空间关系。因此，如果后训练重点强化某一种读出方式，收益往往集中在该接口上，向其他空间任务接口的迁移可能弱甚至退化。作者将这种现象称为“interface lock-in”，并希望通过统一的监督组织方式提升跨接口空间推理迁移能力。

Method: 方法包括：1）将异质空间监督以时间顺序的 episodes 形式重放；2）保留每个监督来源的原生目标形式，而不是统一强行改写成单一答案格式；3）在不同监督家族之间进行平衡，避免某类监督主导训练。实验还设置同数据对照，分别分析答案序列化、家族平衡学习和时间顺序重放的作用。此外，在推理阶段采用自适应帧选择，在固定训练模型的前提下改变证据访问方式，以获得额外性能收益并降低视觉输入成本。

Result: 在相同观测访问条件下，单一 ChronoReplay checkpoint 同时提升 OVO-S-Bench、OST-Bench 和 UCS-Bench，并在 OST-Bench 上取得 13.41 分增益，在所比较的监督设置中获得三个基准的最高平均分。消融结果显示：答案序列化会在 OST 与 UCS 之间重新分配收益；家族平衡学习优于普通联合 SFT 和自然 turn weighting；时间顺序重放相比错位顺序对照在两个配对种子上约提升 2.41 个 OST 分。自适应帧选择进一步带来推理阶段的增益，同时降低视觉成本。

Conclusion: 论文表明，流式空间性能不仅取决于模型能力，还取决于三件事：监督训练保留哪些空间区分，异质历史在训练时如何组织，以及测试时推理能获得哪些因果证据。ChronoReplay 通过保留多源监督、平衡监督家族并按时间顺序重放，能缓解单一读出训练导致的接口锁定，实现跨空间基准的协同提升。

Abstract: Training a spatial VLM to answer which side an object lies on does not require it to preserve the metric displacement that determines that answer, while supervision for a distance estimate may leave categorical relations largely unconstrained. When post-training emphasizes one readout, improvements can therefore concentrate on that interface while transfer to others remains weak or even degrades; we call this observed specialization interface lock-in. ChronoReplay addresses it by replaying heterogeneous spatial supervision as chronologically ordered episodes, retaining source-native targets, and balancing supervision across families. Under the same observation access, a single ChronoReplay checkpoint improves OVO-S-Bench, OST-Bench, and UCS-Bench together, including a 13.41-point gain on OST-Bench, and achieves the highest three-benchmark mean among the supervision settings compared here. Same-data controls further separate the ingredients of this transfer: answer serialization redistributes gains across OST and UCS, family-balanced learning improves over ordinary joint SFT and natural turn weighting, and chronological replay exceeds a shifted-order control by about 2.41 OST points across two paired seeds. Adaptive frame selection then changes evidence access while keeping the trained model fixed, providing an additional inference-time gain at lower visual cost. Streaming spatial performance depends on what distinctions supervision preserves, how heterogeneous histories are organized during learning, and what causal evidence inference makes available at test time.

</details>


### [83] [EditDistill: Is It Possible to Guide Video Editing with Image Editing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34303)
*Guojun Lei,Hong Li,Hongbing Yang,Lixue Gong,Chi Wang,Zheng Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EditDistill，通过图像编辑模型的首帧编辑结果蒸馏出紧凑“编辑描述子”，并注入视频扩散模型，实现高可控的视频编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像编辑已实现细粒度和高可控修改，但视频编辑仍面临挑战，尤其是在精确局部编辑、动态时间效果以及视频内光照一致性方面。

Method: 给定源视频和编辑提示，先用现成图像编辑模型生成初始帧的编辑结果；再将“原始帧—编辑帧”之间的视觉变换蒸馏为紧凑的编辑描述子。该描述子通过轻量级融合模块注入视频扩散模型，以引导去噪过程。训练时使用编辑描述子对齐损失和DINO结构一致性损失，并在流匹配框架下推导等效的推理时引导形式。

Result: 实验表明该方法在标准基准上取得较强性能，并能在多种场景下生成高质量视频编辑结果。

Conclusion: EditDistill通过把图像级编辑变换蒸馏为视频扩散模型可使用的编辑描述子，有效提升了视频编辑的可控性，为精确局部编辑、动态时间效果维持和一致性视频编辑提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in image editing have enabled fine-grained and highly controllable visual modifications. However, achieving similar levels of controllability in video editing remains an open challenge, particularly for precise local edits, dynamic temporal effects, and consistent lighting across this http URL this paper, we propose EditDistill, a framework that distills image-level visual transformations from edits to guide video this http URL, given a source video and an edit prompt, we first generate an edited version of the initial video frame using an off-the-shelf image editing model. We then distill the visual transformation of this original-edited image pair into a compact representation termed an edit this http URL descriptor is injected into a video diffusion model through our lightweight fusion module, which steers the denoising process toward the desired edit. To align the video trajectory with the demonstrated image edit while preserving motion and layout, we train with edit-descriptor alignment and DINO structural consistency losses, and derive an equivalent inference-time guidance formulation under flow this http URL experiments demonstrate that our method achieves strong performance on standard benchmarks and produces high-quality video editing results across diverse scenarios. For more details and videos, please refer to the anonymous webpage: this https URL.

</details>


### [84] [Real-Time Conveyor Belt Defect Detection via Edge TPU-Accelerated YOLOv11 on Raspberry Pi 5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34304)
*Muhammad Nur Bijak Bestari,Mulki Indana Zulfa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种低功耗边缘AI视觉系统，用于在工业现场实时检测传送带缺陷。该系统部署在Raspberry Pi 5配合Google Coral Edge TPU上，采用INT8全整数量化后的YOLOv11n模型，可在320x320分辨率下达到30-40 FPS，并保持低延迟和稳定运行，体现了边缘AI在预测性维护中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传送带是水泥等重工业连续物料输送系统中的关键设备，纵向撕裂、孔洞、修补退化、冲击穿孔等结构缺陷若未被及时发现，可能导致皮带断裂、高昂停机损失和严重安全风险。虽然深度学习检测精度高，但现有模型通常依赖高功耗工作站GPU，难以部署在远离数据中心、条件恶劣的工业边缘侧，因此需要低成本、低功耗、可实时运行的边缘视觉检测方案。

Method: 作者构建了一个端到端的边缘AI视觉检测系统，硬件平台为Raspberry Pi 5与Google Coral Edge TPU协处理器，并通过RTSP PoE工业相机和IP防护机箱进行现场部署。算法采用优化后的YOLOv11 nano模型，在包含水泥厂运行现场数据、开源数据集和自动采集样本的大于3000张增强图像的多源工业语料上训练。随后对模型进行训练后INT8全整数量化，并编译到Edge TPU的脉冲张量执行路径，以提升推理效率并降低功耗。系统在水泥厂严苛环境下评估检测性能、帧率、延迟和热稳定性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该系统在四类传送带缺陷上具有较强检测能力，达到精确率0.742、召回率0.730以及mAP@0.5 0.764。现场部署后，在320x320分辨率下可稳定实现30-40 FPS吞吐率，端到端延迟为70-90 ms。在粉尘严重的熟料环境中运行后，主机CPU达到约55 ± 5 °C的热平衡状态，Edge TPU约为65 °C，且未出现帧丢失，证明系统具备工业现场连续稳定运行能力。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，经过INT8量化和边缘硬件加速的轻量YOLO模型可以在低成本、低功耗的工业边缘设备上实现实时传送带缺陷检测，从而弥合高精度计算机视觉与经济可行预测性维护之间的差距。该方法尤其适合连续制造和重工业场景中无法部署高功耗GPU的边缘侧监控应用，为提升设备安全、减少非计划停机和降低维护成本提供了可落地的技术路径。

Abstract: Conveyor belts are vital arteries in continuous bulk material handling, particularly in heavy process industries such as cement manufacturing. Undetected structural anomalies (longitudinal tears, holes, degraded patch repairs, and impact punctures) cause catastrophic belt snapping, costly downtime, and severe safety hazards. While deep learning offers high accuracy, existing models rely on power-hungry workstation GPUs, hindering practical deployment at remote industrial edge points. In this paper, we propose, implement, and validate an end-to-end, low-power edge AI vision system for real-time conveyor belt defect detection deployed on a Raspberry Pi 5 accelerated by a Google Coral Edge TPU coprocessor. The system leverages an optimized YOLOv11 nano (YOLOv11n) architecture trained on a multi-source industrial corpus exceeding 3,000 augmented samples from an operational cement plant in West Java, open-source datasets, and automated captures. The model undergoes post-training full integer quantization (INT8) compiled for systolic tensor execution. Experimental results demonstrate robust detection capability, achieving precision of 0.742, recall of 0.730, and mAP@0.5 of 0.764 across four defect classes. Deployed on-site in an IP-rated enclosure interfaced with an RTSP PoE industrial camera, the system achieves stable throughput of 30-40 FPS at 320x320 resolution with an end-to-end latency of 70-90 ms. Operational profiling under harsh clinker dust confirms thermal equilibrium at 55 +/- 5 deg C on the host CPU and ~65 deg C on the Edge TPU with zero frame loss. This demonstrates that edge AI bridges the gap between high-accuracy computer vision and cost-effective predictive maintenance in continuous manufacturing.

</details>


### [85] [Learning Discriminative Prototypes via Boundary Repulsion MixUp for Object Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34305)
*Athena Psalta,Vasileios Tsironis,Konstantinos Karantzalos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 PBR-ReID，一种基于原型的行人/车辆重识别框架，通过可学习身份原型显式建模易混淆类别间的边界几何，并引入 Boundary Repulsion MixUp（BRM）将标准 MixUp 在混淆类别间插值导致的边界模糊问题转化为增强判别性的正则化信号，从而提升对视角、光照和遮挡变化的鲁棒性与相似身份间的可分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标重识别（ReID）需要嵌入特征既能抵抗同一身份内部的视角、光照、遮挡等差异，又能保持视觉上高度相似身份之间的清晰边界。最难错误往往发生在易混淆类别之间，而传统成对损失（pairwise losses）只能隐式塑造这些类间几何边界，缺乏对“混淆边界”的直接、显式控制。标准 MixUp 虽然能增强泛化，但在混淆身份之间线性插值会进一步模糊最需要保持清晰的类别边界，反而削弱判别能力。因此需要一种机制能实时识别最易混淆身份，并显式强化其边界分离。

Method: PBR-ReID 的核心方法包括：
1. 原型表示：为每个身份设定一个可学习原型（learnable prototype），并与 DiNOv3 骨干网络联合优化。原型与样本特征共同决定表示空间。
2. 原型相似性矩阵：在训练过程中计算原型间相似性，实时揭示哪些身份最容易混淆，为边界增强提供显式信号。
3. 边界排斥 MixUp（BRM）：针对最易混淆的类别对，构造一个显式“边界代理”（boundary proxy），不是让样本在混淆类别间插值，而是将两侧特征从该边界推开，从而把边界模糊转化为判别性增强信号。
4. 两阶段训练协议：先进行原型预热（prototype warmup），再进行由指数移动平均（EMA）稳定的联合微调（EMA-stabilized joint fine-tuning），以保证原型学习稳定并有效引导判别特征形成。

Result: 文章称在行人重识别和车辆重识别基准上进行了大量实验，结果表明 PBR-ReID 取得了强性能；并通过充分消融实验验证了各组件的贡献，包括原型学习、原型相似性驱动的易混淆身份发现、BRM 边界排斥正则以及两阶段训练协议。代码已公开。

Conclusion: PBR-ReID 将目标重识别中“易混淆类别之间的边界几何”从隐式优化提升为显式学习目标，通过可学习原型实时识别混淆身份，并用 BRM 对边界进行主动排斥，克服标准 MixUp 在混淆类别间插值造成边界模糊的问题。该框架在保持类内鲁棒性的同时增强类间可分性，为 ReID 特征学习提供了一种更具判别性的原型与边界优化范式。

Abstract: Object re-identification requires embeddings that are robust to intra-class variation in viewpoint, illumination, and occlusion, yet preserve sharp separation between visually similar identities. The hardest errors arise precisely where confusable identities meet, a region that pairwise losses shape only implicitly. We present PBR-ReID, a prototype-based framework that makes this inter-class geometry an explicit learning target. Each identity is represented by a learnable prototype jointly optimized with a DiNOv3 backbone and the resulting prototype similarity matrix reveals, at every training step, which identities are most confusable. On this signal we build Boundary Repulsion MixUp (BRM), a geometry-aware regularizer that converts standard MixUp's weakness into a discriminative signal. Rather than interpolating samples across confusable identities, which blurs the boundaries that matter most, BRM constructs an explicit boundary proxy between each confusable pair and repels their features away from it, converting boundary ambiguity into a discriminative signal. We adopt a two-stage training protocol consisting of a prototype warmup phase followed by EMA-stabilized joint fine-tuning. Extensive experiments on person and vehicle ReID benchmarks demonstrate strong performance, with thorough ablations confirming each component's contribution. Code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [86] [TopX: Text-to-Motion for Unseen Skeletons from the Skeleton File Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34321)
*Tengjiao Sun,Pengcheng Fang,Xiaoyu Zhan,Dongjie Fu,Xiaohao Cai,Hansung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TopX，一种仅依赖骨架树、静置姿态和关节名称的文本到动作生成模型，可在无重定向、无微调的情况下对训练中未见过的任意运动学树生成合理人体/角色动作，并构建 SkelShift 基准用于系统评估跨骨架泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到动作模型通常只能生成训练时所用骨架对应的动作，而实际新角色往往只提供骨架文件，缺乏该角色的动作样本、模板、重定向或微调数据，因此模型需要真正理解不同关节数、不同拓扑、不同体型和物种之间的运动学差异。

Method: TopX 是 73M 参数的流匹配 Transformer，生成关节旋转和根轨迹；动作以骨架静置姿态和尺度归一化，静置姿态作为世界坐标描述子，不依赖单个关节的局部轴向约定，并在训练时随机化静置约定。模型将关节维度与帧维度上的注意力分解，并采用面向任意骨架图的空间—时间编码：跨关节使用骨架图的 heat-kernel 坐标，跨帧使用帧序旋转编码。作者构建 SkelShift：四个动作语料统一编码，143,334 个带文本描述片段，覆盖 4 到 142 个关节的骨架，并留出 4,232 个片段、2,043 种骨架，按拓扑、关节数、体型、物种和骨架来源等维度组织。

Result: 与使用相同训练片段的 UniMate 相比，在未出现训练动作片段的新骨架上，TopX 在将 UniMate 指导权重调优用于动作 FID 时仍把 descriptor FID 从 5.73 降至 4.58；在将 UniMate 调优用于 descriptor FID 时，把 kinetic FID 从 1.48 降至 0.55。此外，TopX 能生成训练骨架从未执行过的动作，并且更多帧保持在地下之上。

Conclusion: TopX 将文本到动作生成从“记忆特定骨架”推进到“按运动学结构泛化”的新设定，能够在无重定向、无微调条件下利用骨架拓扑、静置姿态和关节名称为任意骨架生成高质量动作；SkelShift 则为跨骨架泛化和运动质量评估提供了系统化基准。

Abstract: Text-to-motion models generate for the skeletons they were trained on, while a new character arrives as a skeleton file and nothing else. We study text-to-motion for skeletons that no training clip has used, with no clips of the character, no template, no retargeting and no fine-tuning. We present TopX, a 73M-parameter flow-matching transformer that generates joint rotations and a root trajectory for any kinematic tree from its tree, rest pose and joint names. Motion is normalised by the rest pose and the size of the skeleton, and the rest pose enters as a world-frame descriptor that does not depend on how the rig's author oriented each joint, with rest conventions randomised in training. Attention is factorised over the joints and the frames of a motion and carries a spatial--temporal encoding designed for skeleton graphs of any topology: queries and keys are rotated across joints by a heat-kernel coordinate of the skeleton graph, which is defined for every tree and does not change with the order of its joints or the eigenbasis of its Laplacian, and across frames by a rotary encoding of frame order. To measure what this buys, we build SkelShift: four motion corpora under one codec, 143,334 captioned clips on skeletons of 4 to 142 joints, from which 4,232 clips on 2,043 skeletons are held out by clip, rig, tree class or species and organised along topology, joint count, body plan, species and rig source, and scored by motion quality: Fréchet distances on public kinetic features and on kinematic descriptors, foot skating and ground contact. Against UniMate trained on the same clips, on test rigs without a training clip, TopX lowers the descriptor FID from 5.73 to 4.58 when UniMate's guidance is tuned for the kinetic FID, and the kinetic FID from 1.48 to 0.55 when it is tuned for the descriptor FID, also on motions no training skeleton performs; fewer of its frames sink below the ground.

</details>


### [87] [CrossPair: Unlocking Generalist Visual Transformation Learning in Image Editors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34333)
*Yibo Zhao,Lifan Jiang,Senlin cheng,Tianrun Wu,Bolei Xu,Xiaobo Li,Can Ye,Xiaofei He,Dehai Zhao,Boxi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CrossPair，使预训练多模态图像编辑器能够仅通过一个 before–after 范例对迁移多种视觉变换；其核心是让 VLM 抽取操作语义、VAE 保留外观与结构，并通过训练阶段的 DINOv3 关系对齐目标提升跨场景编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉任务通常依赖独立的修复、编辑、风格化系统，而语言模型能从示例中切换任务。若要让“前—后”示例成为通用指令，需要将可迁移的变换关系与示例中的具体场景内容分离，因此作者希望构建统一的范例式视觉编辑框架。

Method: CrossPair 将范例前图、范例后图和查询图分配到预训练多模态编辑器的 VLM 与 VAE 固定路径中：VLM 路径负责理解变换语义，VAE 路径负责保持细粒度外观与结构。训练阶段引入 change-aware DINOv3 目标，对齐不同内容场景之间的特征变化方向，使输出遵循示例所展示的关系，而非复制示例场景；推理时不需要额外编码器。作者还构建 Transform-360K，从修复、条件生成、编辑、虚拟试穿和风格化等数据中整理共享变换类别。

Result: 在由 Relation252K 得到的已见与留出的变换设置下，CrossPair 在目标一致性和编辑方向相似度上优于已发布系统。消融实验表明语义范例路径与视觉范例路径互补，DINO 关系对齐能进一步增强迁移效果；基于 Transform-360K 的训练也提升了整体留出操作的表现。

Conclusion: CrossPair 证明，通过将可迁移的操作语义与具体场景内容分离，并利用关系式特征变化对齐，预训练多模态编辑器可以从单个 before–after 范例中执行多样化视觉变换，推动统一的范例式图像编辑。

Abstract: Language models switch tasks from demonstrations, but vision still often relies on separate systems for restoration, editing, and stylization. A before--after example offers a shared instruction only if the transferable change can be separated from the scene that demonstrates it. We introduce CrossPair, which adapts a pretrained multimodal image editor to transfer diverse transformations from one exemplar pair. Exemplar-before, exemplar-after, and query images take fixed roles in its native VLM and VAE pathways: the VLM extracts operation semantics, while the VAE retains fine appearance and structure. A training-only, change-aware DINOv3 objective aligns feature-change directions across content-distinct scenes, guiding the output to follow the demonstrated relation without an inference-time encoder. We construct Transform-360K by grouping filtered pairs from conditional representations, restoration, editing, virtual try-on, and stylization under shared transformations. On Relation252K-derived seen and held-out transformations, \method improves target agreement and edit-direction similarity over released systems. Ablations show that the semantic and visual exemplar routes are complementary and that DINO relation alignment further strengthens transfer; Transform-360K training also improves aggregate held-out-operation scores.

</details>


### [88] [Birth-Death Structural Learning for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34344)
*Quan Tran Hong,Long Nguyen-Chi,Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于重构损失一阶变分的3D高斯泼溅密度控制方法，用可微分“出生/死亡”得分替代启发式克隆、分裂与剪枝规则，并在3DGS-MCMC中实现更优的重建质量，尤其在小规模高斯预算下提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS的自适应密度控制依赖位置梯度、透明度和图像误差等代理信号，无法直接回答“应在何处新增高斯以降低多视角重建损失”以及“哪些现有高斯可被最小代价删除”这一结构性问题。

Method: 在每次结构更新时冻结当前高斯集合，为每个高斯基元附加辅助质量坐标，并对多视角重建损失关于这些坐标求导。作者证明该得分是Splat Regression Model一阶变分的坐标，可作为出生/死亡移动的高质量局部下降替代信号：得分高的高斯被选为新增位置，得分低且透明度低的高斯被选为删除对象。

Result: 在标准3DGS基准上，将该变分得分集成到3DGS-MCMC中后，取得最优重建质量并优于现有方法；在严格限制高斯数量的设置下，收益最为明显。

Conclusion: 基于梯度的变分指标能够为3DGS密度控制提供比代理启发式更严谨的结构化决策依据，证明一阶变分方法可作为3DGS中自适应密度控制的原则性替代方案。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become a standard representation for real-time novel-view synthesis. Yet, its quality-efficiency trade-off still relies heavily on adaptive density control. Existing cloning, splitting, and pruning rules use proxy signals, such as position gradients, opacity, and image-space error. Although effective in practice, these signals do not directly answer the structural question behind density control: where should new Gaussians be allocated to reduce the multi-view reconstruction loss, and which existing primitives can be pruned with minimal penalty. To address this, we propose first-variation birth-death control for 3DGS, a principled approach that replaces heuristic structural decisions with a variational score derived from the reconstruction loss. At each structural update, we freeze the current Gaussian population, attach auxiliary mass coordinates to each primitive, and differentiate the multiview loss with respect to these coordinates. We demonstrate that this score is a coordinate of the first variation of the Splat Regression Model, giving a rigorous local descent surrogate for birth-death moves: high-score Gaussians are selected as birth sites, while low-score, low-opacity Gaussians are selected for removal. Across standard 3DGS benchmarks, integrating this score into 3DGS-MCMC yields state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and outperforms existing methods, delivering the most significant gains under tight Gaussian budgets. Ultimately, our work provides compelling evidence that gradient-based variational metrics offer a principled alternative to proxy heuristics in 3DGS.

</details>


### [89] [Spatially-Aware Self-Organizing Graph Autoencoders via Localized Hebbian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34346)
*Robin Koç,Hazal Moğultay Özcan,Fatoş T. Yarman Vural*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出空间感知自组织图自编码器 S-LooM，通过空间互相关、可学习空间核和软融合训练课程，使卷积特征图之间形成更稳定、可解释的动态拓扑连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有受 Hebbian 可塑性启发的动态自编码器常使用全局平均池化和标量连接来建模特征图关系，难以充分利用图像空间信息；同时，损失函数与训练动态的突变会造成表示冲击，导致特征学习不稳定。

Method: S-LooM 利用空间互相关建立特征图间的动态连接，并引入空间核替代静态标量消息传递；在解码器侧采用最近邻上采样以抑制高频反卷积伪影；通过软融合训练课程使潜在拓扑平滑对齐。

Result: 实验表明，S-LooM 相比先进动态架构能产生更稳定的图拓扑，对特征纠缠和噪声具有更强抗性，并能形成对应照度、形状和纹理等视觉属性的功能社区。

Conclusion: S-LooM 通过空间化连接与平滑训练缓解了现有动态图自编码器的结构瓶颈，为图像潜在表示提供了更稳定、更具可解释性的自组织拓扑学习机制。

Abstract: Traditional autoencoders rely on static network architectures to learn latent representations. Recently we suggested a dynamic architecture inspired by Hebbian plasticity, enabling topological self-organization among convolutional feature maps (R. Koç et al., Proceedings of the SIU, 2026). Early implementations of this paradigm infer relationships among feature maps using global average pooling and model their interactions through scalar-valued connections. This approach falls short to fully exploit the underlying spatial information, embedded in input images. Furthermore, abrupt transitions between different loss functions and training dynamics can introduce representation shocks, leading to unstable feature learning. In this paper, we introduce a Spatially-Aware Self-Organizing Graph Autoencoder (S-LooM) to address these structural bottlenecks. By using spatial cross-correlation, and spatial kernels, S-LooM establishes dynamic connections among feature maps. We replace static scalar message passing of autoencoders with learnable spatial kernels and eliminate high-frequency deconvolutional artifacts observed in decoders using nearest-neighbor upsampling. Via a soft-blending training curriculum, the latent topology aligns smoothly during training. Our experiments show that S-LooM yields a more stable topology, compared to the state of the art dynamic architectures. Meaning, the graph structure that emerge has more resistance to feature entanglement and noise. It forms functional communities responsible for visual attributes, such as illumination, shape and texture even under high connection densities.

</details>


### [90] [No Cones, No Duals: A Fast and Scalable Solver for NRSfM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34403)
*Suryansh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种快速、可扩展的非刚性运动恢复结构（NRSfM）求解器，替代传统中昂贵的半定规划（SDP）欧氏校正和ADMM低秩形状恢复步骤，在稠密、长序列等基准上实现大幅加速并保持竞争力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能 NRSfM 方法依赖 SDP 求解器进行欧氏校正、ADMM 求解器恢复低秩形状，计算开销大，难以扩展到长序列和稠密点跟踪场景。作者希望在不牺牲重建精度的前提下，设计更适合现代加速器的快速可微求解流程。

Method: 旋转估计采用可微欧氏校正，直接优化每个序列的小型因子，并通过行极分解投影将相机块映射到最接近的行正交矩阵；形状估计采用展开式谱近端求解器，在重排形状域中结合重投影梯度与加权奇异值收缩，并引入 FISTA 式重启和延拓策略。

Result: 在稀疏、稠密、噪声、缺失数据和长序列基准上，该方法显著降低运行时间并保持具有竞争力的重建精度，在含 28,887 个跟踪点的稠密序列上实现 7 倍至 1000 倍加速。

Conclusion: 该 NRSfM 求解器类别无关、无需外部训练数据，并将关键几何约束嵌入固定可微求解流程中，为大规模、稠密、长序列非刚性三维重建提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Non-rigid structure from motion (NRSfM) aims to recover camera motion and time-varying 3D shape from tracked 2D image measurements. Recent high-performing methods show that shape-space factorization remains highly effective for this task. These methods often use costly solvers for two key steps. First, a semidefinite programming (SDP) cone solver to perform Euclidean correction. Next, an alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) solver to recover the low-rank shape. These steps often scale poorly to long sequences and dense point tracks. We propose a fast, scalable NRSfM solver that avoids these costly steps. For rotation estimation, we introduce a differentiable Euclidean correction that directly optimizes small factors for each sequence. A row-polar projection then maps each estimated camera block to the closest row-orthonormal matrix. For shape estimation, we introduce an unrolled spectral-proximal solver. It combines reprojection-gradient steps with weighted singular-value shrinkage in the reshuffled shape domain. This solver further uses FISTA-style restart and continuation. The spectral shrinkage operator itself is classical, but we embed it in a fixed differentiable solver, while preserving NRSfM's geometric constraints. The proposed approach is category-agnostic, requires no external training data, and maps naturally to modern accelerator-friendly linear algebra primitives. Across sparse, dense, noisy, missing-data, and long-sequence benchmarks, our solver achieves substantial speedups with competitive reconstruction accuracy, including 7x-1000x runtime improvements on dense sequences with 28,887 tracked points.

</details>


### [91] [On Detection Reliability for Small Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34432)
*Olivier Rukundo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究考察：是否应将小目标分割限制在检测器预测框内，以避免误检；实验表明，这种限制可能把真实的分割预测排除在框外，从而引入假阴性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小目标分割流程中，常见做法是用目标检测器先生成预测框，再仅在框内进行分割或候选区域生成。但研究需要验证：匹配成功的分割预测是否会出现在预测框之外；如果强制限制在预测框内，是否会错误地丢弃本应被保留的分割结果。

Method: 以腺病毒颗粒作为小目标样本，分别比较 YOLO26x 检测器结合 YO-LO26x-Sem 分割模型，以及 Mask R-CNN 同时进行检测与分割。通过将预测掩码质心与 ground-truth 掩码或参考对象质心进行匹配，判断匹配后的预测掩码质心是否落在至少一个检测器预测框内、落在所有预测框外，或 ground-truth 掩码没有匹配到任何分割预测。优先使用 ground-truth 掩码质心作为匹配参考。

Result: YOLO26x 相关实验中，71 个参考对象里 34 个（47.89%）有匹配分割质心位于至少一个预测框内，8 个（11.27%）质心位于所有预测框外，29 个（40.85%）没有匹配分割。Mask R-CNN 实验中，69 个（97.18%）有匹配分割质心位于至少一个预测框内，0 个位于所有预测框外，2 个（2.82%）没有匹配分割。

Conclusion: 初步实验说明，将分割严格限制在检测器预测框内并不总是安全的，尤其是在 YOLO26x 场景下，会存在匹配分割预测位于预测框外的情况，从而可能造成假阴性；Mask R-CNN 在该小样本实验中未出现框外匹配分割，但仍需更大规模验证。

Abstract: This study investigates whether predicted bounding boxes are reliable for determining where small-object segmentation should be performed without risking false negatives outside those boxes. The investigation leverages adenovirus particles as small objects, YOLO26x for detection, YO-LO26x-Sem for segmentation, and Mask R-CNN for both detection and segmentation. Segmenta-tion-model predictions were matched to ground-truth adenovirus masks or reference objects using their centroids, with ground-truth mask centroids preferred over ground-truth bounding-box centroids as references for matching. Matched predicted-mask centroids were considered as inside at least one detector-predicted bounding box or outside all such boxes, while ground-truth masks without matched predictions were recorded as having no matched segmentation. For the YOLO26 models, 34 reference objects (47.89%) had matched segmentation centroids inside at least one pre-dicted box, 8 (11.27%) had centroids outside all predicted boxes, and 29 (40.85%) had no matched predicted segmentation. For the Mask R-CNN model, the corresponding counts were 69 (97.18%), 0 (0.00%), and 2 (2.82%). These preliminary experiments demonstrated that restricting segmentation to detector-predicted bounding boxes risks excluding matched segmentation predictions outside those boxes, potentially introducing false negatives.

</details>


### [92] [Task Relevance-Driven Prompt Expansion for Federated Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34461)
*Jiahua Dong,Wenqi Liang,Yang Cong,Yichen Li,Hongyuan Yu,Henghui Ding,Yulun Zhang,Salman Khan,Fahad Shahbaz Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TRPE：一种面向联邦持续学习的任务相关性驱动提示扩展方法，用于在不知晓新任务到达时间与类别数量的情况下检测新任务、推断任务结构，并利用全局正相关的任务语义关系进行提示学习与抗遗忘训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦持续学习方法大多假设全局服务器预先知道新任务的到达时间和类别数量，这不符合真实场景；同时它们忽视任务之间正向语义关系，且容易受到旧任务中无关关系的干扰，从而加剧灾难性遗忘。

Method: 方法包含三个核心设计：首先，在本地客户端使用分布外检测器识别新任务到达，并在代理服务器端采用半径自适应 DBSCAN 推断新任务中的类别数量，从而摆脱对新任务先验知识的依赖。其次，通过计算学习新任务的原始梯度与其相对于旧任务特征空间的正交梯度之间的亲和力，从全局视角度量新旧任务的语义相关性；随后利用该任务相关性为新任务选择 key-prompt 对，并识别高度相关的旧任务，拼接其提示进行联合训练，以正向利用跨任务语义关系。最后，对本地 key 和 prompt 梯度施加软正交约束，以平衡新任务学习与旧任务知识保持，缓解灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在多种数据集和主干网络上的实验表明，TRPE 能够在缺乏新任务先验信息的联邦持续学习场景下取得有效性能，验证了其对任务相关性建模和提示扩展机制的促进作用。

Conclusion: TRPE 通过新任务检测、任务类别推断、全局任务相关性建模、提示扩展以及软正交梯度约束，解决了联邦持续学习中任务到达未知、类别数量未知和灾难性遗忘严重的问题，为更现实、更可扩展的联邦持续学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated continual learning (FCL) has attracted considerable interest by enabling local clients to continually acquire knowledge from new tasks. However, most FCL methods assume that the global server has prior knowledge of the arrival times and number of categories for new tasks, which is unrealistic in the real world. Furthermore, they neglect the importance of positive inter-task semantic relationships when learning new tasks and are significantly affected by irrelevant relationships from old tasks, thereby exacerbating catastrophic forgetting. To resolve the above challenges, we develop a novel Task Relevance-driven Prompt Expansion (TRPE) model. Specifically, to eliminate the reliance on prior knowledge of new tasks, we propose an out-of-distribution detector to identify the arrival of new tasks within local clients, along with a radius-adaptive DBSCAN to infer the quantity of categories in new tasks on the proxy server. Moreover, to explore positive inter-task relationships, we calculate the affinity between the original gradient for learning new task and its gradient orthogonal to the feature space of old tasks to evaluate semantic relevance between old and new tasks from a global perspective. Then, we use this global task relevance to determine the key-prompt pair for new tasks and identify highly related old tasks. After concatenating their prompts for training, we can leverage such positive inter-task relations to improve the continual learning of newly arriving tasks. To balance the learning of new tasks and the forgetting of old ones, a soft orthogonality constraint is applied to the local key and prompt gradients. Experiments spanning diverse datasets and backbones confirm the efficacy of our TRPE model.

</details>


### [93] [PhysCreator: Creating Physically-Attributed 3D Assets at Will](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34473)
*Shiyan Zheng,Ming Li,Lei Han,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysCreator 是一个统一框架，能够从单张或多张图像直接生成具有空间可变物理属性的可仿真 3D 表示，实现几何、外观和物理属性的前馈式联合建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 资产生成方法多偏向静态表示，难以在动态交互中表现物理上合理的行为；而物理属性估计常依赖事后预测或高成本的逐实例优化，缺乏可扩展性和泛化能力。

Method: 作者提出 voxel-aligned latent space，将图像观测提升为空间结构化的 3D 表示，并在同一 3D 表示学习框架中联合建模几何、外观与物理属性，实现 feed-forward 推断。为解决密集物理真值标注难以获取的问题，引入 vision-language distillation，将视觉语言模型中的材质相关语义先验迁移为体素级监督信号，从而支持可扩展学习。

Result: 实验表明，PhysCreator 在视觉质量、物理合理性和泛化能力方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 将物理属性估计纳入统一 3D 表示学习框架，并结合视觉语言先验蒸馏，可实现从图像直接生成可仿真、具空间变化物理属性的高质量 3D 资产。

Abstract: Significant advancements in 3D asset creation have enabled high-fidelity 3D generation from images. However, most existing representations remain inherently static, lacking the ability to model physically plausible behaviors under dynamic interactions. We present \textbf{PhysCreator}, a unified framework that directly generates simulation-ready 3D representations with spatially varying physical properties from images. Unlike prior approaches that treat physical attributes as simple post-hoc predictions or rely on expensive per-instance optimization, we reformulate physical property estimation within a unified 3D representation learning framework. We introduce a voxel-aligned latent space by lifting image observations into a spatially structured 3D representation, in which geometry, appearance, and physical properties are jointly modeled and inferred in a feed-forward manner. To enable scalable learning without densely annotated physical ground truth, we leverage vision-language distillation to transfer material-aware semantic priors from powerful vision-language models into spatially grounded voxel-level supervision. Experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior visual quality, physical plausibility, and generalization compared to prior approaches.

</details>


### [94] [VTC-AUDIT: An Interventional Reliability Audit for Visual-Token Compression under Paired Image Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34545)
*Isaac*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VTC-AUDIT，用于评估视觉-语言模型中视觉令牌压缩在图像分布偏移下的“增量失败风险”；实验发现，若在偏移图像上重新选择保留的令牌位置，DivPrune 与 ApET 的失败率均显著上升，且“支持集不稳定性”对风险的判别力弱于压缩模型在干净图像上的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉令牌压缩研究通常只关注干净数据上的平均准确率和效率，但这些指标无法揭示压缩在图像偏移场景下新增的失败：即使未压缩模型在干净和偏移图像上均正确，压缩模型仍可能在偏移输入上出错。作者希望量化并审计这种由压缩引入、相对于基础模型的增量可靠性风险。

Method: 作者提出 VTC-AUDIT，仅统计“完整模型在干净图像和偏移图像上都正确，且压缩模型在干净图像上正确”的样本对，以评估压缩在偏移下的增量失败风险。该方法通过四个实验臂交叉两个二选一：一是由哪张图像决定保留令牌位置，二是由哪张图像提供视觉特征。作者在三个 8–9B 开源视觉-语言模型、四个任务和八种预设图像偏移上进行审计，并使用两个免训练压缩器：硬剪枝 DivPrune 与“剪枝后合并”ApET；两者均在 25% 保留位置预算下比较，共生成 708,000 条推理记录。

Result: 实验表明，与复用干净图像选择的令牌位置相比，在偏移图像上重新选择保留位置会在全部六个“模型-压缩器”组合中提高失败率：DivPrune 增加 3.04–9.60 个百分点，ApET 增加 2.05–4.20 个百分点，且所有 95% 置信区间均高于零。该结果在第二个独立重采样的审计清单上全部复现。跨四个基准的混合监控审计中，“支持集 Jaccard 不稳定性”在六个设置中均未达到实用 AUROC 阈值 0.65，而“压缩模型在干净图像上的不确定性”则达到该阈值。

Conclusion: 视觉令牌压缩的可靠性风险不能仅由干净准确率和效率反映；令牌位置选择对输入分布变化敏感，但由支持集变化构成的稳定性信号在实际跨基准风险排序中较弱。相比之下，压缩模型在干净图像上的不确定性更可用于预测偏移下的失败。未来研究应在压缩评估中同时报告条件可靠性指标，而不仅是平均性能与效率。

Abstract: Visual-token compression is usually evaluated by clean accuracy and efficiency, but these averages do not reveal failures that compression introduces under an image shift where the uncompressed model stays correct. We introduce VTC-AUDIT, which estimates this incremental risk only on pairs where the full model is correct on both images and the compressed model is correct on the clean image. The four arms then cross two binary choices: which image selects the retained token positions, and which image supplies the visual features. We audit three open 8-9B vision-language models on four tasks and eight pre-specified shifts. We evaluate two training-free compressors---hard-pruning DivPrune and prune-and-merge ApET---at a matched 25% retained-position budget, producing 708,000 inference records. Reselecting positions on the shifted image, rather than reusing the clean-image positions, raises the failure rate in all six model-compressor settings. The increase is 3.04-9.60 percentage points for DivPrune and 2.05-4.20 percentage points for ApET, with every 95% interval above zero. Repeating the audit on a separately resampled second manifest reproduces all six increases. In a pooled monitor audit across the four benchmarks, support Jaccard instability fails the practical AUROC criterion of 0.65 in all six settings, whereas clean compressed-model uncertainty clears it. Thus, support turnover is a weak ranking signal under the audited benchmark mixture, while clean compressed-model uncertainty is substantially more informative. These results suggest that future visual-token compression evaluations should report conditional reliability alongside clean accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [95] [Recursive CLIP: Vision-Language Distillation with Looped Transformer Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34563)
*Szymon Mazurek,Alberto Presta,Michał Byra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究将递归/共享的Transformer块用于CLIP类视觉-语言模型，通过让少量N个块重复M次来保持标准计算深度同时显著增加参数共享，并评估其在零样本分类、跨模态检索、少样本学习、视觉定位和概念遗忘等任务中的可行性与影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等对比式视觉-语言模型通常沿深度方向使用独立参数的Transformer块，导致参数量和存储/适配成本较高。本文想回答：在保持CLIP可迁移能力的前提下，这些块能否被递归复用，即通过参数共享构造更紧凑的模型，并理解这种共享对训练、跨模态对齐、下游适配和参数效率的影响。

Method: 研究递归Transformer主干：仅维护N个不同的Transformer块，每次前向传播重复使用M次；固定N·M等于标准ViT-B深度，以在相同计算深度下逐步增加参数共享。构建两类递归CLIP：rCLIP只递归使用视觉编码器；r²CLIP同时在文本编码器中引入递归。训练方式包括直接对比训练和从在DFN-2B上预训练的强ViT-B/16教师进行知识蒸馏，数据集为较小的CC3M和CC12M。评估涵盖零样本分类、跨模态检索、少样本学习、视觉定位和概念遗忘。

Result: 结果表明CLIP能够容忍相当程度的递归块复用，但其收益或损失依赖于具体编码器（视觉/文本）和下游任务类型。递归结构天然带来参数效率，因为需要存储和适配的不同Transformer块更少。整体显示递归复用不必然破坏CLIP的可迁移能力，但也并非对所有模态和任务都同等有利。

Conclusion: 递归块复用可作为视觉-语言模型的一种实用设计空间，在保持计算深度的同时显著减少模型参数。该研究揭示了参数共享对知识迁移、下游适配和效率的影响，为构建更紧凑、更易于适配的CLIP类模型提供了依据。

Abstract: Contrastive vision-language models such as CLIP typically rely on independently parameterized transformer blocks across depth. We investigate the extent to which these blocks can instead be reused recurrently while preserving the transferable capabilities of CLIP. We study recursive transformer backbones in which a small set of $N$ transformer blocks is applied $M$ times per forward pass. By holding $N \cdot M$ fixed to the depth of a standard ViT-B, our models preserve computational depth while progressively increasing parameter sharing. We consider two recursive CLIP model families: rCLIP, where only the vision encoder is recursive, and r$^2$CLIP, where recurrence is also introduced in the text encoder. We study these models under both direct contrastive training and through knowledge distillation from a strong ViT-B/16 teacher pretrained on DFN-2B, using the substantially smaller CC3M and CC12M datasets. We evaluate the resulting models on zero-shot classification and cross-modal retrieval, and further study their adaptability through few-shot learning, visual grounding, and concept unlearning. Our results show that CLIP can tolerate substantial recurrent block reuse, although its effect depends on the encoder and downstream task. Recurrence also provides parameter efficiency by construction, since fewer distinct transformer blocks need to be stored and adapted. Our results establish recurrent block reuse as a practical design space for vision-language models and highlight its implications for knowledge transfer, downstream adaptation, and parameter efficiency.

</details>


### [96] [FlockWorld: Toward Population-Scale Multi-Agent Video World Models through Artificial Life Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34564)
*Meryem Koksal,Yunzhe Wang,Volkan Ustun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于Boids算法的群体规模多智能体视频世界模型测试环境FlockWorld，以及面向多智能体同步第一人称视频生成的动作条件潜在扩散模型FlockDiT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件视频世界模型多限于单智能体，或仅支持小规模固定数量的智能体，难以研究人口规模、多视角同步生成与智能体身份保持等问题。

Method: 构建FlockWorld人工生命环境，利用Reynolds Boids规则生成群体行为，渲染多个智能体同步第一人称裁剪画面，用不同颜色保持相机智能体身份，并将规则推导的加速度作为动作，同时允许相机智能体数量与整体群体规模独立配置；进一步训练FlockDiT，即基于flow matching的动作条件潜在扩散Transformer，用于联合生成多个智能体的同步第一人称视频。

Result: 实验表明，联合生成在密度匹配设置下有利于智能体检测，扩散强制（diffusion forcing）可提升长程rollout保真度，FlockWorld与FlockDiT共同构成可控的多智能体视频生成测试平台。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了面向群体规模多智能体视频世界模型的可控基准和生成模型方案，有助于研究多视角同步生成、动作条件预测和智能体身份保持等关键问题。

Abstract: Most action-conditioned video world models remain limited to a single player, while existing multiplayer models support only small, fixed groups of agents. We introduce FlockWorld, an artificial-life environment built on Reynolds's Boids algorithm for studying population-scale multi-agent video world models. Specifically, FlockWorld renders synchronized egocentric crops, assigns distinct colors to preserve camera-agent identity across views, exposes rule-derived accelerations as actions, and lets the number of camera agents be configured independently of the size of the surrounding population. We further introduce FlockDiT, an action-conditioned latent diffusion transformer trained with flow matching on FlockWorld data to jointly generate synchronized egocentric videos for multiple agents. Our results suggest that joint generation benefits agent detection at density-matched settings and that diffusion forcing improves rollout fidelity. Together, FlockWorld and FlockDiT provide a controlled testbed for multi-agent video generation.

</details>


### [97] [Finetuning Prompt-Based Image Editor for Geometry-Consistent Room Defurnishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34597)
*Prakhar Kulshreshtha,Brian Pugh,Zachary Nussdorfer,Salma Jiddi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于LoRA微调FLUX.2的单图“家具移除/房间空置化”方法，通过2500对 furnished-empty 图像训练，在保持纹理真实感的同时提升房间3D几何一致性，面向XR中的弱化现实与虚拟布景应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单张室内照片生成不含家具的空房间对XR任务很有价值，例如弱化现实和虚拟布景。但难点是双重问题：既要为被移除家具区域生成真实、合理的内容进行补全，又不能破坏原房间的结构与3D几何。现有提示驱动图像编辑器如FLUX.2能生成高度真实纹理，但结构忠实度不如显式布局条件引导的补全方法。

Method: 作者采用训练配方微调LoRA适配器，使用2500组“有家具房间—空房间”配对图像数据，对FLUX.2进行适配，使其在完成家具区域补全时更尊重原房间的布局、轮廓和几何结构。方法本质上是将数据驱动的结构约束引入FLUX.2的图像编辑/补全过程。

Result: 加入该LoRA后，FLUX.2在单张房间照片空置化任务中显著改善几何一致性，同时保留较高的纹理真实度，说明该训练方式能够平衡生成真实感与结构忠实度。

Conclusion: 该研究将FLUX.2的强图像编辑能力与轻量LoRA微调相结合，形成可用于XR流程的单图补全基础组件，有助于构建更深度感知、3D一致的空房间生成管线。

Abstract: Erasing all furniture from a single room image to generate its empty counterpart is valuable for XR applications such as diminished reality and virtual staging. The challenge is twofold: inpainting the furniture regions with realistic content, and doing so without distorting the room's 3D geometry. Prompt-driven image editors, such as FLUX.2, handle realism impressively, but their structural faithfulness is inferior to explicit layout conditioned inpainting. To improve geometric consistency, we propose a training recipe for finetuning a LoRA on 2,500 furnished-empty room pairs. FLUX.2 with our LoRA adaptor significantly improves geometric consistency while retaining the high texture fidelity. This single-photo inpainter can be an effective building block for depth-aware and 3D-consistent XR pipelines.

</details>


### [98] [Do Words Matter? What Text Contributes to Few and Full-Shot Vision-Language Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34610)
*Arda Baris Basaran,Mesut Ceylan,Elgar Fleisch,Filipe Barata*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这篇论文挑战了“视觉-语言异常检测器的性能提升主要来自文本语义”这一常见解释。作者证明并实验发现：在带有可训练视觉适配器的少样本/全样本设置中，文本锚点真正起作用的主要是其差向量的有效尺度以及多类别场景下的锚点几何排列，而不是自然语言语义本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言异常检测方法通常用文本描述正常与异常外观，并将性能增益归因于文本的语义内容。这种归因在零样本设置中可能合理，但在少样本或全样本设置中会加入可训练的视觉适配器。由于改变文本描述会同时改变语义和嵌入几何，仅靠准确率比较无法区分二者。因此需要明确文本嵌入到底贡献了什么：语义、尺度，还是联合几何结构。

Method: 作者将文本提示抽象为固定的一对文本嵌入，即“锚点”，并分析单锚点对和多类别锚点集合的作用。对于单个锚点对，他们考察异常分数如何读取锚点信息，提出并证明：若替换锚点保持差向量长度不变，则允许产生完全相同的概率图和可达到的损失。实验覆盖多个医学数据集、不同视觉骨干、不同监督模式和不同适配器家族。进一步测试多类别共享适配器时锚点轴的联合排列，包括整体旋转、随机正交方向、分散近似共线的类别轴，以及冻结零样本迁移下的随机锚点帧与提示帧对比。

Result: 结果表明，对于单锚点对，文本语义方向不可识别，可训练适配器可以跟随任意替换方向；真正决定性能的是锚点差向量的有效尺度。尺度匹配下的随机锚点或正交锚点能够追踪正确文本提示的效果，而破坏尺度匹配会导致性能差距，恢复尺度则无需文本即可弥合。该现象在MVFA和MadCLIP中同样成立。在多类别设置中，整体旋转所有类别轴不改变结果；但随机或无意义旋转若使原本近似共线的类别轴分散开，则在工业基准上带来性能提升。即便在冻结零样本迁移中，随机锚点帧也能与真实提示帧相当，说明起作用的是坐标一致性而非语义。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：此类视觉-语言异常检测器从提示中读取的核心信息主要是尺度与锚点几何排列，而不是文本的语义内容。因此，许多看似由语言语义带来的增益，实际上可能主要来自嵌入空间的几何结构和训练适配器对方向的吸收与重排。

Abstract: Vision-language anomaly detectors describe normal and abnormal appearance with text, and their gains are commonly credited to the semantic content of these descriptions. This attribution is plausible in the zero-shot setting, but few- and full-shot detectors interpose a trained visual adapter, and in that setting it has not been isolated. Changing a description changes both its meaning and its geometry, so accuracy comparisons cannot separate the two. We ask what a fixed pair of text embeddings, or anchors, contributes to such a detector: semantic content, effective scale, or joint anchor geometry. For a single anchor pair, the answer is effective scale alone. The score reads the anchors only through their difference, and a trained adapter can rotate to follow any replacement direction, leaving that direction, and with it the linguistic content, unidentifiable. We prove that replacement anchors with the same difference-vector length permit exactly the same probability maps and attainable losses. Across seven medical datasets, three vision backbones, both supervision regimes, and three adapter families, scale-matched random and orthogonal anchors track correct prompts; breaking the scale match opens a gap that restoring it closes without text. The same pattern holds in MVFA and when MadCLIP's learned prompts are replaced by a text-free learned scale. When one adapter serves many categories, the answer expands to their joint arrangement. Rotating all category axes together changes nothing, as predicted. For the tested CLIP prompts, these axes are nearly collinear, and meaning-free rotations that spread them apart improve performance on both industrial benchmarks. Even under frozen zero-shot transfer, a random anchor frame transfers as well as the prompt frame: coordinate consistency, not meaning. What these detectors read from their prompts is scale and arrangement, not semantic content.

</details>


### [99] [P2P-Former: Bridging the Supervision Gap Between Point and Proposal in Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34618)
*Yunchuan Ma,Laiyun Qing,Sai Xu,Guorong Li,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出P2P-Former，将点级监督与提案级学习桥接起来，以缓解点监督时间动作定位中的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间动作定位的点监督标注成本低，但信息弱于完整提案标注，现有方法多依赖多示例学习，导致与全监督模型存在差距。

Method: 提出点感知提案生成策略，通过点感知重要性估计校正基础定位器生成提案的置信度，并用点标注与候选提案最优匹配选择可靠伪监督；同时设计点引导语义增强模块，将点标注特征原型注入片段特征；并引入点至提案约束损失，要求预测提案包含标注点。

Result: 在THUMOS14和ActivityNet-1.3数据集上实验表明，该方法优于多种现有先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过点级监督到提案级监督的显式建模，P2P-Former能在低标注成本下提升时间动作定位性能。

Abstract: Recently, point-supervised temporal action localization has attracted increasing attention due to its low annotation cost (only one frame per action instance) and competitive detection performance. However, since point annotations are less complete than proposal annotations, existing methods mainly rely on multiple instance learning for snippet-level classification, which leads to a performance gap compared to fully supervised models. To bridge this gap, we propose P2P-Former, a novel point-to-proposal Transformer-based framework that effectively connects point-level supervision with proposal-level learning for temporal action localization. In this work, we identify two key challenges in transitioning from point-level supervision to full supervision: (1) how to generate reliable proposals as pseudo ground-truth, and (2) how to effectively integrate point-level cues into the fully supervised paradigm. To address the first challenge, we propose a Point-aware Proposal Generation (PPG) strategy that refines the confidence of proposals produced by a base locator using a point-aware importance estimation function, and then performs optimal matching between point annotations and candidate proposals to select reliable proposals as pseudo ground-truth. To tackle the second challenge, we introduce a Point-guided Semantic Enhancement (PSE) module that aggregates prototypes from point-annotated features and injects them into snippet features through Transformer-based interactions. Moreover, we design a Point-to-Proposal Constraint Loss (PPCL) that enforces each predicted proposal to include the annotated point, thereby encouraging more accurate localization. Extensive experiments on the THUMOS14 and ActivityNet-1.3 datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms several existing state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>
