### [100] [RIDE-Splat: Reflection Removal for In-the-Wild Driving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34632)
*Kevin David Hayes,Alex Hanson,Allen Tu,Pranav Asthana,Matthias Zwicker,Tom Goldstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RIDE-Splat，一种无需干净图像、反射掩码或预训练网络的驾驶视频无反射3D重建方法，并将相机附着反射建模为共享有符号RGB图像以从3DGS中分离；同时构建首个面向驾驶视频反射去除的3D重建基准RIDE-Splat Dataset，实验表明其在多数据集上优于3DGS及现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶视频透过挡风玻璃拍摄时，道路场景随视角变化呈现世界空间一致性，而车内反射附着于相机，不符合标准3D Gaussian Splatting假设的世界空间一致性。这会导致反射内容被错误嵌入重建场景中，破坏干净3D重建质量。现有反射去除方法通常需要干净参考、反射掩码或预训练网络，难以直接用于真实驾驶视频。

Method: RIDE-Splat将透过挡风玻璃的世界一致透射内容分配给3DGS场景，而将主导的、附着于相机的反射建模为共享有符号RGB图像。方法分两遍构建干净重建：首先联合学习3D几何和该有符号反射图像，以同时表达叠加辐射亮度和透射衰减；然后利用去除反射后的训练图像拟合干净3DGS模型。推理时丢弃反射图像，保留标准3DGS渲染路径。作者还提出RIDE-Splat Dataset，包含真实驾驶场景、训练视角中的物理挡风玻璃反射，以及单独采集的无反射评测图像。

Result: 在RIDE-Splat Dataset、XLD和Wayve上，RIDE-Splat在全部九项聚合指标上优于3DGS、DC-Gaussian和RobustSplat，PSNR最高提升3.59 dB，LPIPS最多降低0.0876。在42项逐场景比较中取得38项最高指标，能够去除基线中仍可见的挡风玻璃反射，同时保持透射场景内容。

Conclusion: RIDE-Splat通过将相机附着反射从世界一致场景中以共享有符号RGB图像的方式分离，实现了无需干净图像、反射掩码或预训练网络的无反射驾驶视频3D重建。其方法兼容标准3DGS推理路径，并配合新基准数据集显示出优于现有3DGS反射处理方法的性能，适合面向真实驾驶视频的高质量3D重建与下游应用。

Abstract: Driving video captured through a windshield violates the world-space consistency assumed by 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS): the road scene exhibits parallax while cabin reflections remain attached to the camera, causing standard 3DGS to embed reflected content into the reconstructed scene. We introduce RIDE-Splat, a pipeline for reflection-free 3D reconstruction from posed driving video without clean images, reflection masks, or pretrained networks. RIDE-Splat assigns world-consistent transmission through the windshield to a 3DGS scene and the dominant camera-attached reflection to one shared, signed RGB image. It builds the clean reconstruction in two passes. First, it learns the 3D geometry and the signed reflection image, representing both added radiance and transmission attenuation. Then, it uses the reflection-removed training images to fit a clean 3DGS model. At inference, the reflection image is discarded, preserving the standard 3DGS rendering path. We also introduce the RIDE-Splat Dataset, the first 3D reconstruction benchmark for reflection removal from driving video -- with real driving scenes, physical windshield reflections in training views, and separately captured non-reflective evaluation images. Across the RIDE-Splat Dataset, XLD, and Wayve, RIDE-Splat outperforms 3DGS, DC-Gaussian, and RobustSplat on all nine aggregate metrics, improving PSNR by up to 3.59 dB and reducing LPIPS by up to 0.0876. We achieve the highest metrics in 38 out of 42 per-scene comparisons and removes windshield reflections that remain visible in the baselines while preserving the transmitted scene.

</details>


### [101] [Regional prediction of hematoma expansion from non-contrast computed tomography in spontaneous intracerebral hemorrhage patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34645)
*Natasha Ironside,Hongyu Kan,Kristofor Pas,Mohammad Shifat-E-Rabbi,Benjamin Newman,Shinjini Kundu,Kareem El Naamani,Tanvir Rizvi,Andrea Becerril-Gaitan,Harrison Snyder,Aarathi Manchikalapudi,Sarah Ratcliffe,Stacie L. Demel,Carl D. Langefeld,Daniel Woo,Stephan Mayer,E. Sander Connolly,Gustavo Rohde,ERICH Investigators,VISTA-ICH collaboration*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究构建并验证了一种基于脑出血部位的非增强CT（NCCT）影像特征模型，利用传输形态计量学（TBM）预测血肿扩大；验证集AUROC为0.787，并优于其他临床或机器学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑出血血肿的部位、是否扩大以及最终体积共同影响神经功能结局。临床需要一种可量化、可解释、基于部位的影像学方法，在基线NCCT上预测血肿扩大，以辅助风险分层并深化对血肿扩大病理生理机制的理解。

Method: 研究者使用ERICH研究作为模型推导队列，使用VISTA研究作为外部验证队列。对基线及24±6小时NCCT影像进行预处理、血肿分割，并按解剖区域划分为脑叶、基底节和丘脑血肿。血肿扩大的定义按部位并结合其对临床结局的影响设定。采用传输形态计量学（TBM）为每张NCCT血肿图像生成独特的传输表示，随后在线性分类器上构建预测模型。通过AUROC分析，将区域TBM模型与其他替代方法进行比较。

Result: 推导队列包括574例患者，其中脑叶血肿162例、基底节血肿257例、丘脑血肿155例；验证队列包括196例患者，其中脑叶血肿31例、基底节血肿119例、丘脑血肿46例。组合区域模型在验证队列中预测血肿扩大的AUROC为0.787。随血肿部位变化的主要影像特征包括血肿大小、异质性、偏心性以及密度向血肿外周分布。区域TBM模型优于其他基于临床判断或机器学习的方法。

Conclusion: TBM能够从基线NCCT中区分并可视化血肿扩大的区域特异性影像特征，为血肿扩大的区域性影像学表现提供初步证据，具有改善血肿扩大预测以及推动对其病理生理机制理解的潜力。

Abstract: Background The combination of hematoma location, expansion and final volume influence neurological outcome. The aim of this study was to derive and validate a quantitative and interpretable region-based NCCT image feature model for predicting hematoma expansion. Methods The Ethnic/Racial Variations of Intracerebral Hemorrhage(ERICH) study was used for derivation and the Virtual International Trials in Stroke Archive(VISTA) was used for validation. NCCT images at presentation and 24+/-6hours were pre-processed, segmented and categorized into lobar, basal ganglia or thalamic regions. Hematoma expansion was defined by region and according to its effect on clinical outcome. Transport-based morphometry (TBM) produced a unique transport-based representation from each NCCT hematoma image. Linear classifiers applied to transport representations were used to derive a predictive model. The regional TBM model's performance was compared to alternate methods using AUROC analyses. Results The derivation dataset included 574 patients, comprising 162 lobar, 257 basal ganglia and 155 thalamic hematomas. The validation dataset included 196 patients, comprising 31 lobar, 119 basal ganglia and 46 thalamic hematomas. A combined regional model predicted hematoma expansion in the validation dataset with an AUROC of 0.787. The principal image features which varied by location were size, heterogeneity, eccentricity and density distribution towards the hematoma periphery. The regional TBM model outperformed alternative clinician based and machine-learning methods. Conclusions TBM discriminated and visualized regional features of hematoma expansion from presentation NCCT scans. This provides preliminary evidence for regional radiographic features of hematoma expansion. It has the potential to improve hematoma expansion prediction and understanding of hematoma expansion pathophysiology.

</details>


### [102] [StreamVibe: Proactive Video Agents for Evolving User Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34662)
*Chengzhi Liu,Yuxiang Lai,Yuzhe Yang,Yanqing Liu,Jiheng Hou,Yue Cao,Haonan Wang,Ruixi Fan,Lan Wang,Sheng Liu,Songyou Peng,Yale Song,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Xin Eric Wang,Jui-Hsin Lai,Youbao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向主动式视觉-语言交互的 StreamVibe-Bench 基准、StreamVibe-VL 流式主动模型和 StreamVibe-Harness 层级记忆框架，用于建模用户显式、隐式和动态意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型多依赖明确用户请求，难以捕捉导航等场景中的隐含意图，也无法在请求持续变化时同步更新交互状态，限制其实际意图感知交互能力。

Method: 首先构建包含 4.1K 评测样本和 85K 训练样本的 StreamVibe-Bench，覆盖显式、隐式和动态用户意图；随后开发 StreamVibe-VL，通过连续观察推断隐含意图并动态调整交互；再提出 StreamVibe-Harness，利用层级记忆维护用户意图的持久状态与环境变化，实现最新意图追踪和响应适应。

Result: 实验表明，StreamVibe-VL 在保持较强通用视频理解能力的同时，将响应时机和回答质量较基线提升 10.7%；现有主动模型在理解用户意图和判断何时响应方面仍受限，尤其在隐式和动态意图场景；StreamVibe-Harness 进一步提升持续交互中的响应一致性与适应能力，主动交互提升 12.3%。

Conclusion: 该工作从基准、模型和状态管理三方面推进了在线主动式视觉-语言交互，证明建模隐含意图、动态意图更新和持久记忆对于实用场景中的意图感知交互至关重要。

Abstract: Vision-language models are shifting from offline passive understanding toward online proactive interaction. However, prevailing methods largely rely on explicit requests while miss the implicit user intent (e.g., "where should I click next?" while a user navigate a website), meanwhile fail to update the state simultaneously as user requests might update continuously. These limitations make them less suited to intent-aware interaction in practical. To address these challenges, we introduce StreamVibe-Bench, a new benchmark serving as testbed for user intent interaction, with 4.1K evaluation samples and 85K training samples spanning explicit, implicit, and dynamic intents. Building on this benchmark, we develop StreamVibe-VL, a streaming proactive model that infers implicit user intents from continuous observations and dynamically tailors its interactions as these intents evolve. We further introduce StreamVibe-Harness, which maintains user intents as persistent states with a fleixble lifecycles via a hierarchical memory to track the latest user intent and environmental state. Our experiments reveal three key findings: (i) StreamVibe-VL preserves strong general video understanding while improving response timing and answer quality by 10.7% over other baselines; (ii) existing proactive models remain limited in understanding user intent and deciding when to respond, especially under implicit and dynamic intents; and (iii) StreamVibe-Harness further improves response consistency and adaptation over sustained interaction, resulting in a 12.3% gain in proactive interaction.

</details>


### [103] [Good Rankings, Poor Choices? Rethinking Language-Only Vision-Language Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34685)
*Hyuk Kwon,Jaebyeong Jeon,Hyeonseo Jang,Kibok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对“仅基于文本信息的视觉语言模型选择”（LOVM）提出新的评测指标与训练无关的选择方法：用简单遗憾（SR）及其鲁棒归一化版本（ORNR）替代传统排序指标，并提出TC-ESR方法，在22个目标任务和43个VLM上取得最低平均SR和ORNR，同时修正了既有评测协议中的两类目标性能泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LOVM评测主要依赖top-5 recall、Kendall秩相关等排序指标，但这些指标不能直接反映被选中模型的实际性能好坏。同时，已有评测协议可能存在目标性能信息泄漏，导致模型选择效果被高估。因此需要更直接、鲁棒且无泄漏的评测方式。

Method: 论文首先重新审视LOVM评测协议，采用简单遗憾（Simple Regret, SR），即最佳模型与被选模型之间的性能差距，并进一步提出离群值鲁棒的归一化遗憾（ORNR），以适应不同任务间性能范围差异并降低离群点影响。在此基础上提出训练无关方法TC-ESR：根据目标条件与源任务相似性加权估计期望源遗憾，并选择具有最低加权平均遗憾的视觉语言模型。

Result: 在22个目标任务和43个视觉语言模型的实验设置中，TC-ESR在平均SR和ORNR上均优于所有比较方法，能够选出零样本性能更接近最优的VLM。此外，作者识别并修正了此前基于排序的评测协议中的两类目标性能泄漏来源。

Conclusion: LOVM任务更应以“被选模型实际性能与最优模型的差距”为评测目标，而非仅关注模型排序一致性；TC-ESR提供了一种无需训练、基于源任务遗憾相似性加权的有效选择机制，同时修正后的评测协议更能反映真实零样本模型选择能力。

Abstract: Language-Only Vision-Language Model Selection (LOVM) aims to identify the Vision-Language Model (VLM) with the highest zero-shot accuracy on a target task using only textual information. We revisit the existing LOVM evaluation protocol and argue that ranking metrics, such as top-5 recall and Kendall's rank correlation, do not directly assess the performance of the selected model. We therefore adopt simple regret (SR), defined as the performance gap between the best and selected models, and introduce Outlier-Robust Normalized Regret (ORNR) to account for the varying performance ranges across tasks while reducing sensitivity to outliers. Building on this revised evaluation protocol, we propose Target-Conditioned Expected Source Regret (TC-ESR), a training-free method that selects the model with the lowest similarity-weighted mean regret across source tasks. Across 22 target tasks and 43 VLMs, TC-ESR achieves the lowest mean SR and ORNR among all compared methods. Separately, we identify two sources of target performance leakage in the prior ranking-based evaluation protocol and provide corrections that remove both.

</details>


### [104] [RSKD: Grounding Semantic Consistency in Driving VLMs via Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34689)
*Chuchu Xie,Yang Zhong,Kangan Qian,Hao Ye,Kun Jiang,Diange Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RSKD：一种语义一致性引导的知识蒸馏框架，用于自动驾驶多模态模型压缩。它根据答案短语的任务重要性和与教师预测的语义一致性，动态分配监督信号，在保留关键规划语义的同时抑制教师错误传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶场景中将大型多模态模型蒸馏到紧凑模型时，传统的统一 token 级匹配会稀释与决策/规划相关的语义信息，并可能放大教师模型的错误，从而降低压缩模型的可靠性与任务相关性。

Method: RSKD 将答案拆分为语义短语，定位其中任务关键的短语，并度量其与教师预测的语义一致性。基于该短语级一致性分数，在 Jensen–Shannon 散度（JSD）蒸馏监督与 ground-truth 监督之间进行动态平衡：对语义一致的短语更多采纳教师信息，对语义不一致的短语增强真实标签监督。

Result: 在 DrivingVQA、LingoQA 和 MapLMv2 上评估。RSKD 在 DrivingVQA 和 LingoQA 上优于所比较的基于学生的基线方法，并在 MapLMv2 上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: RSKD 证明，面向自动驾驶的模型蒸馏不应简单进行统一的 token 级匹配，而应依据短语级语义重要性和一致性选择性迁移教师知识。该方法能够更有效地保留规划相关语义，并降低教师错误被蒸馏到学生模型中的风险。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation can transfer large multimodal models to compact models for autonomous driving, but uniform token-level matching can dilute planning-relevant semantics and amplify teacher errors. We propose RSKD, a semantic-consistency-guided distillation framework that routes supervision according to both the task importance of an answer phrase and its semantic consistency with the teacher prediction. Task-critical semantic phrases are localized in the ground-truth answer, and a phrase-level consistency score controls the balance between Jensen--Shannon divergence (JSD) distillation and ground-truth supervision. Teacher information is emphasized only where it is semantically consistent, while semantically inconsistent phrases receive stronger ground-truth supervision. We evaluate RSKD on DrivingVQA, LingoQA, and MapLMv2. RSKD improves over the compared student-based baselines on DrivingVQA and LingoQA and remains competitive on MapLMv2.

</details>


### [105] [FGD2HF: Frequency-guided Dual-domain Hypergraph Fusion for Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34734)
*Xiaofan Yang,Wei Pan,Yubin Liu,Junyu Wu,Junming Zhang,Jie Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出频率引导的双域融合语义分割网络 FGD2HF，通过邻域-超图注意力编码、小波频率增强和频域引导的空间融合，在保持较低计算成本的同时提升分割精度与边界质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于频域的语义分割方法存在频率信息利用不足、空频融合效率低以及计算复杂度较高等问题，难以在精度与效率之间取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出 FGD2HF 网络，包含三个核心设计：NAHA 编码器通过邻域注意力和超图注意力联合建模局部与全局关系；WTE 模块利用小波变换提取并增强前层特征中的低频与高频线索；FGDF 模块以频率线索引导当前阶段的空域分组与特征传播，实现频域与空域的高效融合。

Result: FGD2HF-B 在 ADE20K 上达到 48.7% mIoU，较 SegMAN-B 提升 0.3%，同时 GFLOPs 降低 18%；在 Cityscapes 上达到 83.2% mIoU，与 SegMAN-B 相当但 GFLOPs 降低 21%；在 SBD 上较 VWFormer-T 提升 1.1% mIoU 且 GFLOPs 降低 21%，并显著改善边界分割精度。

Conclusion: 频率引导的空频双域融合能够以较低计算成本有效提升语义分割性能，该设计在精度、效率和边界质量上均具有较强竞争力。

Abstract: Frequency-domain information is beneficial for semantic segmentation. However, existing frequency-based methods often suffer from insufficient frequency utilization, inefficient fusion, and high computational complexity. To address these issues, we propose FGD2HF, a dual-domain fusion network that leverages frequency features to guide spatial-domain fusion and enhancement. Specifically, we introduce a novel Hybrid-Attention Encoder (NAHA) that jointly models local and global relationships through neighborhood and hypergraph attention, enhancing the network's ability to extract effective features. We further introduce a Wavelet Transform and Enhancement (WTE) module to extract and refine low- and high-frequency cues from preceding-stage features, followed by Frequency-guided Dual-domain Fusion (FGDF) modules that use frequency cues to condition current-stage spatial grouping and feature propagation. This design incorporates both global and local frequency guidance into spatial feature modulation, enhancement and fusion with low computational cost. Extensive comparisons with numerous state-of-the-art semantic segmentation methods demonstrate that our method is highly competitive. Our full FGD2HF-B model achieves 48.7% mIoU on ADE20K (+0.3% over SegMAN-B with 18% fewer GFLOPs), 83.2% mIoU on Cityscapes (comparable to SegMAN-B with 21% fewer GFLOPs), and 1.1% higher mIoU than VWFormer-T on SBD with 21% fewer GFLOPs, while also achieving significant improvements in boundary segmentation accuracy.

</details>


### [106] [Efficient Multi-modal Video Fusion Network with Dynamic Region-Guided Temporal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34765)
*Jingxue Huang,Xiaosong Li,Yi Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多模态视频融合的高效时空交互网络，借助动态区域引导与测试时训练机制，在降低光流/特征对齐开销的同时提升时间一致性与融合质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态视频融合方法常依赖光流估计和特征扭曲实现帧间对齐与跨帧信息聚合，计算成本高，且易受光流估计误差影响，导致融合结果的时间一致性不稳定。

Method: 提出动态区域引导的多模态视频融合网络：构建基于测试时训练（Test-Time Training）范式的时空交互模块，以线性复杂度建模时间依赖；仅在视频中的关键动态区域进行时间交互，排除静态或低变化背景 token；利用预训练 MAE 特征的全局结构依赖能力，引入特征级时间一致性约束，通过约束融合视频与源视频帧间变化方向来抑制局部闪烁；并提出基于 MAE 的时间一致性指标，量化动态区域内时间变化方向之间的距离。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，该方法相比现有最先进方法获得了更优的融合质量与时间一致性，同时在边缘设备部署场景下表现出较高的计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过动态区域选择、线性复杂度时空建模和 MAE 特征级一致性约束，可在避免光流对齐瓶颈的前提下提升多模态视频融合效果与效率，适合资源受限的边缘部署场景。

Abstract: Multi-modal video fusion aims to generate temporally coherent videos with salient targets and rich structural details by integrating complementary information from heterogeneous sensors. However, existing video fusion methods commonly rely on optical flow estimation and feature warping for inter-frame alignment and cross-frame information aggregation to maintain temporal consistency, thereby introducing considerable computational overhead and making the fusion performance vulnerable to flow estimation errors. To address these limitations, this paper proposes an efficient multi-modal video fusion network with dynamic region-guided temporal interaction. Specifically, the proposed spatio-temporal interaction module leverages the Test-Time Training paradigm to model temporal dependencies with linear complexity. Building on this design, the module restricts temporal modeling to key dynamic regions in the video sequence, as static background regions provide limited temporal cues. By excluding low-variation tokens from unnecessary temporal interaction, this selective strategy contributes to improved fusion quality and temporal consistency. In addition, leveraging the strong ability of pretrained MAE features to capture global structural dependencies, we introduce a feature-level temporal consistency constraint to suppress local flickering by aligning the inter-frame variation directions of the fused and source videos. We further propose a new MAE-based temporal metric that quantifies directional distance between their temporal variations within dynamic regions. Experimental results demonstrate that, compared with existing state-of-the-art methods, our method achieves superior fusion quality and temporal consistency on multiple benchmark datasets, while exhibiting high computational efficiency under edge-device deployment settings.

</details>


### [107] [Factorized Gradients for Scalable Highly-expressive Parametric Diffeomorphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34766)
*Amit Aflalo,Eran Treister,Chaim Baskin,Oren Freifeld*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FG-CPAB，一种面向CPAB微分同胚变换的可扩展梯度计算方案。该方法将轨迹积分与对参数$\theta$的梯度投影解耦，把梯度计算复杂度从$\mathcal{O}(dTN)$降低到$\mathcal{O}(TN+dC)$，从而显著加速训练/优化过程、大幅降低显存占用，并提升CPAB在2D/3D精细网格下的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微分同胚变换在医学影像配准、时空形变建模等任务中非常重要，但如何同时实现高表达性和高效计算长期存在困难。连续分段仿射基CPAB变换通过相对于网格划分定义分段仿射速度场来参数化微分同胚族，其参数维度$d=\dim(\theta)$取决于网格精细程度而非数据分辨率，因此具有紧凑参数化优势。然而，传统CPAB优化中轨迹积分与关于$\theta$的梯度计算紧密耦合，导致计算和内存开销过大，限制了精细网格下大规模CPAB变换的应用。

Method: 作者提出FG-CPAB，即一种因子化梯度公式。核心思想是将CPAB变换优化中的两个过程解耦：一是沿连续速度场进行轨迹积分以计算变换后的点，二是将对网格划分相关参数$\theta$的梯度计算转化为参数空间投影。通过分离积分过程和参数空间投影，避免在每一步积分、每个变换点上同时处理所有$d$个参数的耦合梯度计算，从而实现复杂度降低和计算可扩展性提升。

Result: FG-CPAB在计算效率、内存占用和表达能力方面均取得显著提升。梯度计算复杂度从$\mathcal{O}(dTN)$降低为$\mathcal{O}(TN+dC)$，其中$T$为积分步数，$N$为变换点数量，$C$为网格单元数量。实验报告最高可获得四个数量级的加速，例如2D场景约$10^4\times$加速；同时显著降低显存需求，例如2D场景峰值GPU显存约$500\times$降低。该结果使精细网格CPAB在2D和3D中首次变得实际可用。

Conclusion: 本文解决了CPAB变换长期存在的优化瓶颈，通过因子化梯度方法使CPAB能够以更细网格和更高参数效率进行可扩展优化。FG-CPAB不仅大幅降低计算与内存成本，还释放了CPAB作为高表达力参数化微分同胚模型的能力，为复杂空间或时间形变建模提供了更具实用性和可扩展性的工具。

Abstract: Diffeomorphisms provide a flexible, topology-preserving mathematical tool for modeling complex spatial or temporal deformations and are used in various scientific fields. However, making these deformations simultaneously highly expressive and computationally efficient remains challenging. Continuous Piecewise-Affine Based (CPAB) transformations (Freifeld et al., ICCV 2015; IEEE TPAMI 2017) offer an attractive solution by parameterizing a family of diffeomorphisms via continuous velocity fields that are piecewise affine with respect to a chosen tessellation of the domain. Importantly, $d=\dim(\theta)$, the dimension of the parameter vector $\theta$, depends on the fineness of the tessellation rather than on the data resolution. Despite this compact parameterization, CPAB optimization has long been hindered by the tight coupling between trajectory integration and gradient computation with respect to $\theta$. We introduce FG-CPAB, a factorized-gradient formulation for scalable CPAB transformations. By separating integration from parameter-space projection, our method reduces gradient computation complexity from $\mathcal{O}(dTN)$ to $\mathcal{O}(TN+dC)$, where $T$ is the number of integration steps, $N$ is the number of transformed points, and $C$ is the number of tessellation cells. This reformulation yields speedups of up to four orders of magnitude (e.g., $\approx 10^4\times$ in 2D), substantial memory savings (e.g., $\approx 500\times$ reduction in peak GPU memory usage in 2D), and dramatically higher expressiveness. This makes fine-tessellation CPAB practical in 2D and 3D for the first time, enabling highly expressive parametric diffeomorphisms.

</details>


### [108] [ToDoPro: Faster and Better Reference-Based Generation: Early Token Reduction via Downsampled Self-Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34782)
*Caiyi Sun,Yujing Sun,Siu-Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ToDoPro，一种无需额外参数的早期 Token 压缩方法，通过在下采样参考图像上利用 DiT 自身进行少量去噪探测，评估参考 Token 重要性，并在主生成前进行层级式 Token 削减，从而加速多参考图像生成推理并降低峰值显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于参考图像的生成模型通常将多个参考图像 Token 与文本 Token 拼接成长序列，由单个 Diffusion Transformer 处理。该方式兼容性好，但会引发严重的 Token 数量爆炸和 O(N²) 注意力开销。多数加速方法在 DiT 内部操作，难以充分去除空间冗余或降低峰值显存；而部分模型外 Token 压缩方法又会引入额外模型和参数。因此，需要在不增加额外参数、且兼容模型内加速技术的前提下，实现推理加速和显存节省。

Method: ToDoPro 的核心思想是在主去噪阶段之前进行“早期 Token 压缩”。具体做法是：首先将参考图像在空间上 2× 下采样，随后仅执行少量去噪步骤，利用 DiT 模型自身产生低成本探测信号，以估计参考 Token 的重要性。基于探测得到的重要性，对参考 Token 进行层级式处理：低重要性 Token 被完全丢弃；中等重要性 Token 被替换为对应的下采样 Token，以较低代价保留全局上下文；高重要性 Token 则被完整保留。这样可以在进入主去噪前显著减少序列长度和注意力计算量，同时不依赖额外外部模型。

Result: 在三个基座模型和两个基准测试上的实验表明，ToDoPro 能够显著加速推理过程，并有效降低峰值 GPU 显存占用。此外，通过安全过滤噪声大、信息量低的参考 Token，该方法还能提升生成质量，使模型有限的注意力资源更集中于真正有用的参考信息。

Conclusion: ToDoPro 证明了利用模型自身进行低成本重要性探测，可以在不引入额外参数和外部模型的情况下，有效缓解多参考图像生成中的长序列与二次复杂度问题。该方法兼具加速、降显存和潜在质量提升效果，并且能够与模型内部优化技术兼容，适合集成到现有 DiT 参考生成系统中。

Abstract: Recent generic reference-based image generation models natively support multiple conditions by concatenating all reference image and text tokens into a joint sequence processed by a single Diffusion Transformer (DiT). However, this versatile paradigm comes at an efficiency cost: accommodating multiple references leads to a token explosion, incurring massive $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ computational complexity. Most acceleration methods operate inside the DiT, showing limited capability in reducing spatial redundancy and failing to alleviate peak VRAM. While some pre-model token reduction alternatives exist, they inevitably introduce external models. This raises a question: Is it possible to achieve acceleration and memory savings while preserving compatibility with in-model techniques and requiring no additional parameters? In this work, we realize this goal by proposing the early Token reduction via Downsampled self-Probing (ToDoPro). By executing a few denoising steps on spatially $2\times$ downsampled reference images, this cheap probing stage utilizes the DiT model itself to effectively and efficiently extract reference token importance. Using the probed importance, we apply a hierarchical reduction before the main denoising stage: we completely discard low-importance tokens, substitute mid-importance tokens with their downsampled counterparts to cheaply preserve global context, and keep high-importance tokens. Experiments across three base models and two benchmarks demonstrate that our early reduction accelerates inference significantly and mitigates peak GPU memory. Furthermore, we can improve generation quality by safely filtering out noisy, uninformative reference tokens, enabling the model to concentrate its limited attention capacity on genuinely useful tokens. The code will be released at this https URL

</details>


### [109] [Natural Jammers: When Non-Robust Features Become Antagonistic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34785)
*Jiaming Zhang,Fuyao Zhang,Pengjun Xie,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现：深度神经网络中的“非鲁棒特征”不只是提升干净样本表现的捷径，还可能成为“天然干扰源”（Natural Jammers），主动误导标准模型对未经修改图像的判断。作者通过标准模型与鲁棒模型的不一致识别出易被干扰的“Jammed Set”，并提出“谱—空间锁定”机制与“替代悖论”，将自然非鲁棒性重新理解为模型—数据几何中的可迁移失败场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究通常把非鲁棒特征视为模型在干净数据上可利用的捷径或统计线索，强调其对鲁棒性的潜在破坏；但该工作试图回答这些线索是否能够在正常、未修改的输入中主动造成错误，从而重新刻画自然条件下模型脆弱性的来源与机制。

Method: 作者比较标准训练模型与鲁棒训练模型的预测差异，以两者不一致为分析视角，筛出鲁棒模型正确而标准模型错误的样本集合，即“Jammed Set”。随后通过因果性实验检验干扰机制，分析高频成分与特定空间结构之间的精确对齐关系，并使用谱滤波或小幅度几何变换破坏这种对齐，以观察预测是否恢复正确。

Result: 研究发现，自然图像中确实存在能够主动误导标准模型的非鲁棒线索，即“Natural Jammers”。干扰并非任意出现，而是源于高频成分与空间结构之间的“Spectral-spatial Locking”：只有两者精确对齐时才会引发误判。移除高频成分或施加轻微几何偏移可恢复正确预测。进一步发现“Substitution Paradox”：单个干扰因素对几何扰动敏感，但整体干扰发生率近似稳定，只是在不同样本或空间位置之间重新分配。

Conclusion: 论文将自然非鲁棒性重新解释为一种存在于经验模型—数据几何上的“失败场”：小变换不会消除干扰，而是使其发生空间或样本层面的重新分布。这一观点强调模型脆弱性具有结构性和因果可干预性，为理解鲁棒模型优势、谱—空间耦合机制以及自然鲁棒性失效提供了新框架。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are known to rely on non-robust features that align poorly with human perception. Prior work has largely framed these features as opportunistic shortcuts that help standard models on clean data. We show instead that non-robust cues can be antagonistic: they can act as Natural Jammers that actively mislead standard models on clean, unmodified images. Using disagreement between a standard model and robust models as an analysis lens, we identify a Jammed Set on which robust models outperform standard models. We provide causal evidence for Spectral-spatial Locking: jamming arises only when high-frequency components are precisely aligned with specific spatial structures. Disrupting this lock through spectral filtering or small geometric shifts can restore correct predictions. Crucially, we uncover a Substitution Paradox: while individual jammers are fragile to geometric shifts, the aggregate prevalence of jamming remains approximately stable and is redistributed rather than eliminated under such transformations. Our results reframe natural non-robustness as a failure field over the empirical model-data geometry: one that redistributes rather than disappears under small transformations.

</details>


### [110] [Towards Spatio-Temporal Intelligence Systems in Urban Space: A Twin-Agent Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34795)
*Zhen Dong,Haiping Wang,Wei Tu,Yuan Liu,Bing Wang,Xuan Sun,Jinzhou Cao,Ronald Clark,Zhiheng Fu,Mohammed Bennamoun,Danfeng Hong,Yuanzheng Cui,Gemine Vivone,Pedram Ghamisi,Bisheng Yang,Qingquan Li,Deren Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从“城市数字孪生体—智能体”的双重视角，系统综述如何将城市时空智能（STI）中的孪生建模、智能体接口、推理、行动和演化等技术整合为统一的城市治理系统，并提出由“内环智能体闭环”和“外环系统闭环”组成的STI架构蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市治理需要动态协调基础设施、交通出行、环境管理、应急响应和公共服务等多个领域。随着城市状态不断变化，传统碎片化技术难以形成及时、有效、透明的闭环治理。数字孪生与智能体AI的发展为构建能够感知、分析、规划、执行和反馈适应的城市时空智能系统提供了基础，但需要系统级整合框架来明确其协同机制。

Method: 作者采用系统级综述视角，围绕“孪生体—智能体”关系分析城市STI系统。具体包括：1）考察数字孪生如何重构和表示持续更新、信息丰富的城市环境；2）分析智能体如何通过接口理解孪生体，并在此基础上进行推理、可靠行动和随城市演化而持续更新；3）综合上述技术，提出两环架构蓝图；4）基于该蓝图梳理研究议程，并通过共享原型说明其在多城市领域的适用性。

Result: 论文将原本分散的城市孪生、智能体推理、数据更新、行动反馈和系统演化等技术整合为一个连贯的STI系统框架。其核心成果是“双环蓝图”：内环为智能体的感知接地、推理与验证闭环；外环连接数据、孪生体、智能体行动、反馈与系统更新。该框架为城市STI研究提供组织路径，并展示共享原型如何支持交通、环境、应急、公共服务等不同城市应用领域。

Conclusion: 城市时空智能系统可通过数字孪生与智能体的协同，实现城市状态的持续感知、问题分析、干预规划、执行验证和反馈演化，从而提升城市治理的及时性、有效性和透明性。未来研究应围绕接口机制、可靠推理、安全行动、持续演化、跨域协同和原型验证展开，推动分散技术向统一、可落地的城市智能系统演进。

Abstract: Cities must coordinate domains like infrastructure, mobility, environmental management, emergency response, and public services as conditions evolve. An urban Spatio-Temporal Intelligence (STI) system helps the city monitor citywide states, analyze problems, plan and execute interventions, and adapt through feedback, making urban governance more timely, effective, and transparent. Recent urban twinning and agentic AI techniques provide foundations for such systems. From a twin-agent perspective, this survey takes a system-level view of how these disparate techniques can be coherently integrated into an urban STI system. Specifically, we review how the twin reconstructs and represents a persistent, information-enriched urban environment, and how the agent understands the twin via interfaces, reasons over it, acts reliably, and continuously evolves with the city. Built upon this synthesis, we summarize a two-loop blueprint, where an inner agent loop for grounding, reasoning, and verification, and an outer system loop linking data, twin, agentic action, feedback, and update. We then use this to structure an STI research agenda and show how a shared prototype supports diverse urban domains.

</details>


### [111] [Selective Monocular Precession Estimation under Geometric and Evaluation Ambiguity: An Auditable Synthetic Study with ORBIT-SCOUT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34819)
*Carlos Eduardo Sanoja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该摘要提出ORBIT-SCOUT，用于从单目相机观察的翻滚细长近似圆柱目标中，仅在多重检验通过后才给出相关的动量轴和平均进动率估计；在预注册的合成场景评估中，系统避免了错误估计，但未能达到预设覆盖率、无失败执行和全案例可重复性等目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目观测即使能拟合出精确轨迹，也不等于恢复出正确的物理轴，尤其对细长近似圆柱目标存在轴歧义和退化风险。因此需要一种把图像测量、轨迹族、几何约束、时间一致性和竞争假设检验联系起来的决策机制，以提高未知目标姿态/刚体参数重建的可信度。

Method: 研究针对标定静止相机观测的细长近似圆柱翻滚目标，提出ORBIT-SCOUT框架。该方法将图像导出的线状几何与边缘/轮廓几何信息耦合到恒锥角和无转矩三轴轨迹族中，仅当数值一致性、几何约束、时间连续性和竞争假设排除等检查全部通过时，才输出相关联的动量轴估计和平均进动率估计。作者进一步描述测量目标、选择策略与局限，并将其置于航天器姿态估计、未知目标重建、刚体辨识和选择性预测的框架中。实验采用内部预注册的96个新合成场景，进行两次冷启动处理，保留全部192次尝试。

Result: 在主通过中，38个场景产生联合正确估计，0个场景产生错误估计，57个场景被保留或拒绝，1个执行不完整。在64个获得支持的场景中，正确覆盖率为25/64。重复实验方面，95个重复对在冻结比较策略下科学等价，其余1对不完整。预注册的覆盖率、无失败执行和全案例可重复性目标均未达成。历史审计还暴露出此前使用的轴度量过于宽松，以及采集节奏依赖运动状态的问题。

Conclusion: 该工作的主要贡献是一套从图像测量到选择性决策的完整实现文档，以及一个保留失败情形的评估案例研究，而非新的刚体理论或最先进精度证据。观察到的零错误并不能建立校准风险保证，条件性时间检验也不能消除共享图像特征带来的偏差。真实图像迁移和外部复现仍是未解决问题。

Abstract: Monocular observations of a tumbling object can support a precise fitted trajectory without establishing that the recovered physical axis is correct. We study this distinction for elongated, approximately cylindrical targets observed by a calibrated stationary camera. ORBIT-SCOUT couples image-derived line and rim geometry to constant-cone and torque-free triaxial trajectory families, then returns a linked momentum-axis and mean-precession-rate estimate only when numerical, geometric, temporal and competing-hypothesis checks pass. We describe the measurement objective, selection policy and limitations, and situate them within spacecraft pose estimation, unknown-target reconstruction, rigid-body identification and selective prediction. An internally preregistered population of 96 new synthetic scenes was processed in two cold passes, retaining all 192 attempts. In the primary pass, 38 scenes produced jointly correct estimates, none produced an incorrect estimate, 57 were withheld and one execution was incomplete. Correct coverage on the 64 supported scenes was 25/64. Ninety-five repeated pairs were scientifically equivalent under a frozen comparison policy; the remaining pair was incomplete. The registered coverage, failure-free execution and all-case repeatability targets were not met. Historical audits also exposed an overly permissive axis metric and motion-dependent acquisition cadence. The contribution is a documented measurement-to-decision implementation and a failure-preserving evaluation case study, not a new rigid-body theory or evidence of state-of-the-art accuracy. Zero observed errors does not establish calibrated risk, and conditional temporal checks do not remove shared image-feature bias. Real-image transfer and external reproduction remain open.

</details>


### [112] [PosePlaner: Denoising Any Feed-Forward Pose Predictor from Pairwise Planar Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34845)
*Yihang Chen,Bin Tan,Yujun Shen,Yinghao Xu,Nan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 PosePlaner，通过流匹配将粗几何对应和初始姿态作为条件生成多个姿态假设并融合，作为多视角 Transformer 的测试时姿态精化模块，无需重新训练即可修正前馈预测、提升求解器精度并减少长序列漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DUSt3R、VGGT 等多视角 Transformer 以单次前馈方式取代传统 SfM/SLAM 流程，虽然提升了 3D 重建能力，但使对应关系、几何约束和相机姿态在内部高度耦合，缺少可解释的阶段接口，也难以在测试时利用几何证据修正错误预测，主要仍依赖监督微调改进。

Method: PosePlaner 恢复经典“对应关系—相机姿态”耦合，将姿态精化建模为条件生成问题：输入粗几何匹配和初始姿态，使用流匹配生成多个候选姿态假设，再将它们融合为精化姿态估计。该过程可视为 RANSAC 投票机制的连续、数据驱动替代方案。模型仅用两视图数据高效训练，但可作为流式多视图姿态估计器的周期性精化器，无需重新训练。

Result: 实验表明，PosePlaner 能够修正前馈多视角模型的失败预测，显著改善基于对应关系的求解器性能，并在长流式序列中降低累积漂移。

Conclusion: PosePlaner 为端到端多视角 Transformer 提供了测试时几何修正接口，在不依赖重新训练的情况下增强姿态估计的鲁棒性与可优化性。

Abstract: Recent multi-view Transformers (e.g., DUSt3R and VGGT) have advanced 3D reconstruction by scaling training data and model capacity from two views to many views and long sequences, subsuming the classical SfM/SLAM pipeline of feature extraction, matching, and pose estimation in a single feed-forward pass. Yet this subsumption hides the interfaces between pipeline stages---correspondences, geometry, and pose are entangled in a single forward pass, leaving supervised fine-tuning as the major path to improvement and offering no mechanism to correct predictions from geometric evidence at test time. We propose PosePlaner to close this gap by reviving the classical coupling between correspondences and pose via flow matching, treating pose refinement as a conditional generation problem that takes coarse geometric matches and initial pose as input condition and fuses the resulting pose hypotheses into a single refined estimate, providing a continuous, data-driven analog of RANSAC voting. Our method is trained efficiently on two-view data, yet serves as a periodic refiner for streaming n-view pose estimators with no retraining. Empirically, it corrects failures of feed-forward predictors, substantially improves correspondence-based solvers, and reduces accumulated drift in long streaming sequences.

</details>


### [113] [Seeing Before Naming: Context-Grounded Learning of Novel Visual Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34846)
*Gabriel Adineera,Jinoh Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该摘要提出SynerNet，通过上下文引导的语义锚定，将视觉证据与概念名称嵌入结合，使预训练视觉语言模型更好地学习新的视觉类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习陌生视觉类别不仅需要判别性图像特征，还需要将视觉证据连接到有用的语义名称。预训练视觉语言模型虽然能保留判别性图像特征，但为目标概念提供的文本锚点较弱，因此需要弥合视觉表征与语义名称之间的不平衡。

Method: 论文提出SynerNet框架，核心思想是context-grounded semantic anchoring。该方法学习面向具体概念的名称嵌入，将其暴露于兼容的语言上下文中，并根据视觉证据条件化其表征。同时，框架采用难度感知处理和自适应目标权重，以在保留预训练骨干的同时协调跨模态适配。

Result: 在四个VISTA-Beyond领域中，SynerNet的16-shot平均准确率为74.35%，高于few-shot name learning的72.45%。在14个领域的名称掩码迁移设置中，平均准确率从45.17%提升到46.74%。消融实验表明，概念特定的名称嵌入和跨模态上下文整合是关键组件。

Conclusion: 结果支持将概念获取视为构建视觉接地的语义锚点的过程。SynerNet通过让名称嵌入在语言上下文中并与视觉证据对齐，能够更有效地帮助模型学习新的视觉类别。

Abstract: Learning an unfamiliar visual category requires more than adapting a classifier: the model must connect its visual evidence to a useful semantic name. A pre-trained vision-language model can retain discriminative image features while providing a weak textual anchor for the target concept. We address this imbalance through context-grounded semantic anchoring. Our framework, SynerNet, learns concept-specific name embeddings, exposes them to compatible linguistic contexts, and conditions their representations on visual evidence. Difficulty-aware processing and adaptive objective weighting coordinate this adaptation while retaining the pre-trained backbone. On four VISTA-Beyond domains, SynerNet reaches a mean 16-shot accuracy of 74.35%, compared with 72.45% for few-shot name learning. Across 14 domains in the name-masked transfer setting, it improves mean accuracy from 45.17% to 46.74%. Ablations identify concept-specific name embeddings and cross-modal context integration as important components. These results support treating concept acquisition as the construction of visually grounded semantic anchors.

</details>


### [114] [Recovering Black-Box Point Cloud Models from Pose Shifts via Derivative-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34850)
*Seonghwan Park,Min-Gyu Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CanDO，一种仅依赖前向查询的测试时规范化方法，用于在未知训练分布外旋转扰动下恢复冻结的点云模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云模型常通过只输出分数的黑盒接口部署，无法访问模型权重或梯度；当测试阶段出现超出训练分布的旋转时，模型性能会显著下降，而现有缓解方法通常需要权重或梯度信息，因此不适用于严格的黑盒部署场景。

Method: CanDO 在测试时搜索一个输入变换，使模型的置信度最大化，从而将输入“规范化”回模型更易处理的分布。该方法不使用梯度，而是采用无导数优化进行搜索。论文还归纳了成功恢复性能的三个条件：训练数据增广存在覆盖缺口、置信度代理与准确率对齐、以及需要对多峰目标函数进行全局搜索。

Result: 在多个模型架构和基准任务上，CanDO 能够仅通过黑盒前向查询恢复旋转导致分布偏移带来的性能损失。对于物体部件分割任务，在方位角错误旋转下可恢复 oracle 性能差距的 76%，在任意 SO(3) 旋转下可恢复 51%。

Conclusion: 即使点云模型被冻结且仅暴露分数输出接口，也可以通过部署后的测试时规范化显著缓解旋转分布偏移带来的性能下降，说明黑盒场景下的自适应恢复是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Point cloud models are increasingly deployed through score-only black-box interfaces. Under this access model, test-time rotations outside the training distribution can substantially degrade performance. Although existing mitigations such as equivariant retraining and gradient-based adaptation are effective, they are infeasible without access to model weights or gradients. To address this limitation, we propose CanDO, a test-time canonicalization method that recovers lost performance using only forward queries. Specifically, CanDO searches for an input transformation that maximizes model confidence and uses derivative-free optimization to perform this search without gradients. We further identify three conditions for successful recovery: a training augmentation gap, an accuracy-aligned confidence surrogate, and global search over a multi-modal objective. Across multiple architectures and benchmarks, CanDO recovers 76% of the oracle gap under azimuthal misorientation and 51% under arbitrary SO(3) rotations for object part segmentation. These results show that frozen score-only point cloud models can recover from rotation-induced distribution shifts through post-deployment canonicalization alone.

</details>


### [115] [Learning Dual-Camera Smooth Zoom via Decoupled Geometric-Photometric Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34854)
*Junjie Fan,Zhilu Zhang,Renlong Wu,Dazhi Zhang,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出双摄平滑变焦任务，并通过ZoomGS v2合成训练数据和ZoomFI网络实现连续中间帧生成，缓解手机变焦时的几何与色彩跳变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机双摄在变焦时存在几何透视和色彩渲染不连续的问题，影响视觉体验；现有视频插帧模型直接用于该任务面临明显域差距，且难以采集真实世界连续变焦的高质量配对训练数据。

Method: 作者定义双摄平滑变焦（DCSZ）任务，目标是合成连续中间帧以实现平滑变焦。提出数据合成框架ZoomGS v2，将几何变换和色彩变换与3D场景表示建模解耦，从而利用易获取的单摄3D场景批量生成高保真DCSZ训练序列。同时提出面向DCSZ的视频插帧网络ZoomFI，利用互补的双向warping融合双摄信息，完成连续帧合成。

Result: 使用两部手机采集的测试数据集验证表明，基于构造的DCSZ数据训练能显著提升多种视频插帧模型的性能；ZoomFI在定量指标和视觉质量上均取得最优结果。数据集、代码和预训练模型已开放。

Conclusion: 该工作通过专门的3D解耦数据合成流程和面向双摄变焦的插帧网络，有效缓解了智能手机双摄平滑变焦中的几何与色彩不连续问题，为DCSZ任务提供了高质量训练范式和实用模型。

Abstract: When zooming between dual cameras on smartphones, prominent discontinuities in both geometric perspective and color rendition inevitably degrade the visual experience of users. To address this issue, we introduce the dual-camera smooth zoom (DCSZ) task, aiming to synthesize continuous intermediate frames for fluid zooming. However, directly applying existing video frame interpolation (VFI) models to this task is severely hindered by substantial domain gaps and the practical intractability of capturing real-world continuous ground-truth. To this end, we propose a novel data synthesis framework to generate high-fidelity DCSZ training sequences, named ZoomGS v2. It explicitly decouples geometric and color transformations from 3D scene representation modeling, thereby enabling extensive data generation from easily accessible single-camera 3D scenes. Furthermore, we develop ZoomFI, a specialized VFI network tailored to the DCSZ task, which leverages complementary bidirectional warping for comprehensive dual-camera information fusion. Extensive experiments on test datasets captured using two mobile phones demonstrate that training with the constructed DCSZ data significantly improves the performance of various VFI models. Moreover, ZoomFI achieves state-of-the-art results in both quantitative metrics and visual quality. The datasets, code, and pretrained models are available at this https URL

</details>


### [116] [LayoutMamba: Efficient Document Layout Analysis with Hybrid Mamba-Transformer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34879)
*Kehua Chen,Mingyi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 LayoutMamba，一种面向文档版面分析（DLA）的混合框架，结合稀疏注意力 Transformer 与 Mamba 状态空间机制，在低计算成本下同时实现局部细节建模和全局长距离依赖建模，并针对现有基准中的低重叠分布偏置提出几何重叠惩罚损失（GOPLoss）。在 PubLayNet 和 D4LA 上，该方法以显著更少参数取得领先精度与效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档版面分析方法长期存在“局部细节丰富但计算昂贵”与“全局建模高效但结构感知不足”的两难问题：Transformer 类方法通常具有二次复杂度，难以部署于资源受限场景；状态空间模型虽能高效建模全局依赖，却容易牺牲细粒度结构信息。该权衡阻碍了 DLA 在边缘计算和资源受限环境中的实际应用。

Method: 作者提出 LayoutMamba，采用两阶段混合范式。第一阶段使用轻量级稀疏注意力 Transformer 编码器进行局部特征提取，实现 O(n) 复杂度的层次化显著局部语义蒸馏；第二阶段利用 Mamba 的循环状态空间机制在二维文档画布上传播全局上下文依赖，避免注意力稀释并实现无稀释的长程建模。此外，作者发现现有 DLA 基准中版面目标固有低重叠分布偏置问题，并提出几何重叠惩罚损失（GOPLoss），通过双优化目标减少冗余边界框重叠并鼓励几何一致预测。

Result: 在 PubLayNet 和 D4LA 数据集上进行大量实验。结果显示，LayoutMamba 在 PubLayNet 上以约 8 倍更少参数量超越现有方法的最先进平均精度（AP），并在 D4LA 上保持领先性能，已发表方法中排名前三。结果表明该方法在精度、效率和可扩展性之间实现了前所未有的平衡。

Conclusion: LayoutMamba 为文档版面分析提供了认知启发的混合建模范式，将局部细粒度语义抽取与高效全局依赖传播相结合，并针对数据分布偏置设计了 GOPLoss。该工作有助于推动文档分析模型在边缘计算和资源受限环境中的实际部署。

Abstract: Document layout analysis (DLA) faces a long-standing dilemma: existing methods either capture rich local details at prohibitive computational cost (e.g., Transformer-based approaches with quadratic complexity) or achieve efficient global modeling but sacrifice fine-grained structural awareness (e.g., state-space models). This trade-off hinders deployment in resource-constrained scenarios. We present LayoutMamba, a hybrid framework that synergistically combines sparse-attention Transformers for localized feature extraction (O(n) complexity) and the recurrent state-space mechanism of Mamba for dilution-free long-range dependency modeling across the 2D document canvas. Our two-stage paradigm first uses a lightweight Transformer encoder to hierarchically distill salient local semantics; second, Mamba propagates global contextual dependencies without attention dilution, enabling precise, efficient, and scalable layout understanding. We further identify a critical yet overlooked issue in existing DLA benchmarks: the inherent low-overlap distribution bias of layout objects, which we mitigate through a novel Geometric Overlap Penalty Loss (GOPLoss), a dual-optimization objective that minimizes redundant bounding box overlaps and encourages geometrically consistent predictions. Extensive experiments on PubLayNet and D4LA show that LayoutMamba surpasses state-of-the-art AP on PubLayNet with 8x fewer parameters and maintains leading performance on D4LA, ranking top-3 among published methods, achieving an unprecedented balance between accuracy, efficiency, and scalability. Our work pioneers a cognition-inspired paradigm for document analysis, advancing practical deployment in edge computing and resource-limited environments.

</details>


### [117] [SGDB: Semantic-Geometric Decoupling and Evidence Binding for Logical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34897)
*Chenrui Ji,Jiafan Lu,Zilu Luo,Feng Wan,Qiuhui Chen,Weimin Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SGDB，将逻辑异常检测中的语义身份与几何测量解耦，并通过部分一一关联与证据绑定、支持性轴测量、可执行任务约束检查和可解释报警证书，提高异常定位与判定的可靠性，在 MVTec LOCO 和 VID-AD 上取得较高性能。</tldr
<parameter name="motivation">逻辑异常检测不仅要求识别场景组件，还要求其配置满足任务约束。现实中可能出现物体身份正确但几何位置/长度不准确的情况；同时，干净但碎片化的 mask 可能误导长度测量。因此，若语义识别与几何测量混在一起，容易导致错误关联、错误计数和不可解释的异常判定。需要一种机制将语义证据与几何证据分离、绑定并约束到可执行的任务检查中，同时生成可信、简洁且可追溯的异常解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑异常检测不仅要求识别场景组件，还要求其配置满足任务约束。现实中可能出现物体身份正确但几何位置/长度不准确的情况；同时，干净但碎片化的 mask 可能误导长度测量。因此，若语义识别与几何测量混在一起，容易导致错误关联、错误计数和不可解释的异常判定。需要一种机制将语义证据与几何证据分离、绑定并约束到可执行的任务检查中，同时生成可信、简洁且可追溯的异常解释。

Method: SGDB 包含语义-几何解耦与证据绑定：语义解码器基于组件标注正常样本和事实性编辑学习物体身份；独立测量阅读器输出最终几何量。采用部分一一关联，将几何证据绑定到语义身份，同时保留未匹配物体计入总数。支持性阅读器根据线质量和组件完整归属选择 mask 派生轴，确保测量证据可靠。任务约束和正常数据参考形成可执行检查，判定阈值由独立正常数据校准。系统还通过执行级解释追踪规则贡献到源物体与测量值，抽取紧凑充分报警证书；冻结语言模型仅将执行器选择的结论表达为带引用 claim，数值证据由执行器保留，报告保留偏差与缺失证据。

Result: 在五个 MVTec LOCO 逻辑类别上，SGDB 达到 98.19% AUROC 和 94.52% 固定阈值 F1；在全部 50 个 VID-AD 任务上达到 93.56% AUROC 和 92.76% F1。冻结输出实验进一步评估几何读出、解释充分性和解释紧凑性。

Conclusion: SGDB 的核心贡献在于将语义身份、几何测量、任务规则与解释生成进行层次化分离和可靠绑定。该方法能减少由碎片化 mask 或身份正确但几何错误导致的误判，并以可追溯、可校准和紧凑的证书支持逻辑异常决策，在工业图像异常检测基准上表现出较强的检测与解释能力。

Abstract: Logical anomaly detection requires recognizing scene components and measuring whether their configuration satisfies task constraints. Correct identities can coexist with inaccurate geometry; clean mask fragments can yield misleading lengths. We propose Semantic-Geometric Decoupling and Evidence Binding (SGDB). A semantic decoder learns object identities from component-annotated normal sources and factual edits; a separate reader supplies final measurements. Partial one-to-one association binds geometric witnesses to identities while retaining unmatched objects in the count. A support-certified reader selects mask-derived axes using line quality and complete component ownership. Explicit task requirements and normal-data references define executable checks, with decision thresholds fixed by independent normal calibration. Execution-grounded explanation traces rule contributions to source objects and measurements, extracting compact sufficient alarm certificates. A frozen language model expresses selected outcomes as cited claims, with numeric evidence retained by the executor. Reports preserve observed deviations and unavailable evidence. SGDB achieves 98.19% AUROC and 94.52% fixed-threshold F1 on five MVTec LOCO logical categories, and 93.56% AUROC and 92.76% F1 across all 50 VID-AD tasks. Frozen-output controls evaluate geometric readout and explanatory sufficiency and compactness.

</details>


### [118] [LE-GDA: Layer Ensemble Gaussian Discriminant Analysis for Training-Free Few-Shot CLIP Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34901)
*Pavodi Maniamfu,Vogel Kiketa,Zezina Kuzulu Kuetu,Jakin Mukendi Kabongo,David Mayoya Kutangila,Pescie Honore Kinkani Mampuya,L'Or Wolo Sieka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 LE-GDA（层集成高斯判别分析），无需训练即可融合 CLIP 的中间层与最终层表征，以提升视觉-语言模型的少样本分类性能；实验表明其能稳定优于仅使用最终特征的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练少样本适配 CLIP 的方法通常只使用最终投影图像表征构建分类器，忽略中间层表征。但直接合并不同表征存在问题：同一查询下不同层分类器得分的数值尺度差异极大，简单平均会按尺度而非预测信息分配权重，因此难以有效利用中间层信息。

Method: 提出 Layer-Ensemble Gaussian Discriminant Analysis（LE-GDA），在冻结 CLIP 编码器单次前向中提取中间层和最终层表征，并分别对每层拟合带正则化的共享协方差高斯判别分析模型。方法进一步对每个判别器输出的类得分按查询进行标准化，以消除不同层之间的尺度差异；随后利用带标签的验证数据选择少量有效表征组合，并与零样本分类器结合。整个适配过程不依赖梯度优化。

Result: 在 11 个数据集、4 种 CLIP 骨干、5 种样本数设定以及 3 组独立采样的 K -shot 训练集上，LE-GDA 在所有骨干中均取得所比较方法里的最高平均准确率。与仅使用最终特征的匹配 GDA-CLIP 基线相比，16-shot 平均准确率从 78.01% 提升至 79.54%，并且在每个骨干和样本数组合上均有改进。即使在一样本设定下协方差估计不可用、查询级标准化中性时，LE-GDA 仍保持比基线高 1.20% 的平均增益。控制实验显示，在单一表征内引入更丰富的类别结构并不能复现该增益，说明收益主要来自不同层表征的融合。

Conclusion: 中间层 CLIP 表征包含最终嵌入之外的有效信息。通过正则化 GDA、查询级得分标准化、验证集表征选择和零样本分类器融合，LE-GDA 能够在不训练模型的情况下显著改善少样本适配效果，证明跨层表征集成是一种有效且实用的免训练增强策略。

Abstract: Training-free few-shot adaptation of vision-language models typically constructs a classifier from CLIP's final projected image representation, leaving intermediate representations unused. Combining them is not straightforward. Class scores from different representations differ in spread by orders of magnitude for the same query. Direct averaging allocates influence by numerical scale rather than predictive information. We propose Layer-Ensemble Gaussian Discriminant Analysis (LE-GDA), a method that fits regularized shared-covariance discriminants independently to intermediate and final representations extracted in one frozen encoder pass. LE-GDA standardizes each discriminant's class scores per query, selects a small representation multiset on labeled validation data, and combines it with the zero-shot classifier without gradient-based optimization. Across 11 datasets, four CLIP backbones, five shot counts, and three independently sampled K-shot training sets, LE-GDA achieves the highest mean accuracy of all evaluated methods on every backbone, raising the 16-shot mean from 78.01% to 79.54% over a matched GDA-CLIP (final-feature) baseline and improving that baseline for every backbone-shot combination. At one shot, LE-GDA retains a 1.20-point mean gain over the baseline even though covariance estimation is unavailable and per-query standardization is neutral. A control with richer per-class structure inside a single representation does not reproduce the gain, indicating that the benefit comes from combining representations. These results show that intermediate CLIP representations carry information beyond the final embedding and can improve training-free few-shot adaptation. Code and evaluation resources are available at this https URL.

</details>


### [119] [DiffAuction: An Auction-Driven Framework for Generative Image Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34917)
*Yijie Su,Ruiyang Si,Tonghan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiffAuction 是一个面向共享生成图像中多广告主竞争曝光的框架，联合实现可控图像生成、点击意图评估与激励相容拍卖定价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散生成广告在面向不同目标曝光份额生成图像时计算成本高，且生成结果如何评估、如何为多个广告主定价仍不明确。本文旨在解决多广告主共享图像场景下的可控生成、效果度量与市场化定价问题。

Method: 方法包括三部分：1）可控生成：提出无需训练的 Residual Share Calibration（RSC），将联合注意力更新分解为各产品专属残差，并按目标份额重新缩放；同时引入 Share-Conditioned Prompting（SCP）以适配不同分配所需视角。2）效果评估：构建基于 VLM 提取视觉证据、LLM 评估 Click-Through Intent（CTI）的评价器，从产品突出性、品牌可识别性、场景融合度、上下文价值传达四个维度度量点击倾向，并在 1000 张图像上与 10 名训练标注员判断进行验证。3）拍卖设计：将广告位分配建模为联合可行生成配置上的拍卖，每个配置公开期望 CTI 向量，广告主对单位 CTI 拥有私有价值；采用可学习的仿射最大化器拍卖（AMA），联合选择生成配置并确定支付以最大化平台期望收入，同时保证占优策略激励相容性（DSIC）与个体理性（IR）。

Result: 实验表明，所提生成方法相比基线能增强目标份额与 CTI 之间的单调关系；在由该评估与生成设定构成的拍卖环境中，学习得到的 AMA 能带来更高的平台收入。

Conclusion: DiffAuction 为多广告主共享生成图像的广告场景提供了从可控生成、点击意图评估到拍卖定价的完整方案，使品牌曝光份额可量化、可比较，并通过激励相容机制为平台与广告主之间的商业化分配提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Diffusion models enable multiple advertisers to compete for exposure within a shared generated image. Yet existing approaches are computationally costly when generating images under varying target-share configurations, and it remains unclear how the resulting outcomes should be evaluated and priced. To address these gaps, we propose DiffAuction, a framework integrating controllable generation, click-intent evaluation, and auction design. First, our training-free Residual Share Calibration (RSC) decomposes joint-attention updates into product-specific residuals and rescales them according to target shares. We further introduce Share-Conditioned Prompting (SCP) to adapt the viewpoint to each desired allocation. Second, we develop an evaluator that uses a VLM to extract visual evidence and an LLM to assess Click-Through Intent (CTI), a proxy for click propensity, along four dimensions: Product Prominence, Brand Recognizability, Scene Integration, and Contextual Value Communication. We validate its assessments against judgments from 10 trained annotators on 1000 images. Third, we formulate this advertising setting as an auction over jointly feasible generation configurations. Each configuration has a public expected CTI vector, while advertisers hold configuration-specific private values per unit of CTI that map click intent into commercial value. We adopt a learnable affine maximizer auction (AMA), which jointly selects a shared generation configuration and determines advertiser-specific payments to maximize expected platform revenue. The resulting allocation and payment rules guarantee dominant-strategy incentive compatibility (DSIC) and individual rationality (IR). Experiments show that our generation method strengthens the monotonic relationship between target shares and CTI relative to baselines, while the learned AMA achieves higher platform revenue in the resulting auction setting.

</details>


### [120] [Supervising the Visible State: Separating Representation from View Selection in Streaming Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34926)
*Yihao Hu,Pan Wang,Yitong Lin,Zhihao Wen,Deyuan Liu,Hang Wang,Mingda Wan,Yiwen Zhao,Yutong Li,Yuchen Mao,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出“可见状态监督”（ViSS）与“测试时因果视图搜索”（TVS），分别解决流式视频理解中监督不足的问题和推理时证据访问不足的问题，从而在 OVO-Bench 和 StreamingBench 上取得更强表现，同时仅处理较少帧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在流式视频动作识别或时序问答中，如果同一动作只出现一次和出现三次都获得相同的识别标签，模型并不会被迫学习时间、计数、事件顺序、事件累积和关系变化等关键区分。作者将这种训练目标无法充分约束历史差异的状态称为“欠规定”（under-specification）。此外，推理时仅使用近期帧会限制模型对完整视频历史的访问，因此需要一种不重新训练模型、但能在测试阶段选择更有利证据视图的方法。

Method: ViSS 通过构造源数据支持的探针任务来监督事件身份、事件顺序、事件累积、关系变化和答案充分性，使模型在相同“近期帧接口”下学习更明确的时间状态。TVS 则不改变已训练模型，而在测试时动态扩大证据访问范围：从近期帧视图扩展到稀疏事件视图和紧凑历史视图，按需选择更适合回答问题的视图。两者分别对应表示学习与视图选择。

Result: ViSS 在严格匹配的通用时间监督基线上显著提升 OVO-Bench 表现，并在 StreamingBench 上保持可比性，其中最大的收益来自幻觉检测任务。TVS 在测试时改善证据访问，特别有助于事件回忆和前瞻推理，而 ViSS 更明显提升幻觉检测与因果推理。两者结合后，在被比较的已发表系统中取得了 StreamingBench 和 OVO-Bench 上的最佳报告准确率，同时处理帧数远低于穷举式帧选择。严格回放分析表明，模型与三视图 oracle 之间的剩余差距大多来自模型曾经给出正确答案但后来被替换的情况。

Conclusion: 流式视频理解的准确率不仅取决于模型表示什么，还取决于监督要求模型保留什么、推理时暴露哪些证据，以及模型是否能在获得正确答案后保持稳定保留。ViSS 和 TVS 将“表示学习”和“视图选择”解耦：前者通过更明确的时间状态监督提升因果与幻觉相关能力，后者通过测试时证据检索提升回忆和前瞻推理能力。

Abstract: An action seen once and the same action seen three times receive the same action-recognition label, so that supervision does not require a model to count. Answers shared across histories that differ in event order, accumulation, or relations leave those distinctions unconstrained; we call this state under-specification. Visible-State Supervision (ViSS) addresses it by training on source-grounded probes of event identity, order, accumulation, relation change, and answer sufficiency; under the same recent-frame interface it improves OVO-Bench over a strict-matched generic temporal supervision baseline while remaining comparable on StreamingBench, with the largest gain in hallucination detection. Test-Time Causal View Search (TVS) then adapts evidence access while keeping the learned model fixed, expanding from recent frames to sparse event and compact history views on demand. The two interventions separate representation from view selection: supervision particularly benefits hallucination detection and causal reasoning, access particularly benefits event recall and forward reasoning. Together they reach the best reported accuracy among the published systems compared here on StreamingBench and OVO-Bench at a fraction of the processed frames of exhaustive selection, and strict replay shows that most of the remaining gap to a three-view oracle is a correct answer that was later replaced. Streaming accuracy depends on what supervision asks the model to keep, what evidence inference exposes, and whether the model keeps a correct answer once it has one.

</details>


### [121] [Fed-MAPTrack: Federated Modality-aware Prompt Tuning for RGB-X Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34939)
*Xiangyu Liu,Yijie Shen,Yue Yin,Zhitong Yin,Yongqi Pan,Wentao Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Fed-MAPTrack，一种面向RGB-X目标跟踪的模态感知联邦提示微调框架，通过分离RGB共享参数与热红外、事件、深度等辅助模态参数，并让仅有RGB的客户端选择性参与聚合，缓解联邦训练中传感器异质性导致的参数共享域不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-X跟踪可利用热红外、深度或事件相机等信息弥补RGB外观不足，但传统集中式训练需要在同一地点采集多模态视频，存在隐私与部署障碍。联邦训练可让原始视频留在本地，但客户端拥有的传感器不同，尤其是RGB-only客户端能提供RGB观测，却无法贡献辅助传感器相关的融合与归一化交互学习。若直接共享所有参数，会导致参数所属模态域与实际贡献客户端不匹配，影响模型性能。

Method: 作者提出Fed-MAPTrack，一种模态感知联邦提示微调方法。其核心设计包括：1）冻结预训练RGB-X跟踪主干网络和预测头，仅训练和交换提示相关参数，降低通信和隐私开销；2）服务器对不同参数进行分组管理，全局聚合RGB共享参数，并分别维护热红外、事件和深度模态对应的辅助特定、融合和归一化参数库；3）引入选择性参与机制，允许RGB-only客户端贡献全局RGB参数更新，但排除其向模态特定融合和归一化参数的更新，避免其因缺少辅助传感器而引入噪声或偏差。

Result: 在固定客户端划分下，经过三次匹配训练重复实验，Fed-MAPTrack相比平均FedAvg基线在LasHeR上提升2.35 AUC点，在VisEvent上提升3.45 SR点，在DepthTrack上提升2.11 F-score点。在九种评测方法中，该方法取得最高平均热红外AUC和深度F-score，但平均事件SR略低于Independent FL，差距为0.07点。消融实验表明，如果只扩大参数分组范围而不采用与客户端模态能力匹配的参与规则，性能可能下降。

Conclusion: Fed-MAPTrack表明，在多模态联邦跟踪中，不仅要决定哪些客户端参与聚合，还必须明确每一组参数应由哪些客户端贡献，即共享域与合格贡献者需要联合定义。该方法通过提示微调和模态感知参数隔离，为隐私保护下的RGB-X跟踪提供了一种兼顾模态异质性和联邦有效性的解决思路。

Abstract: RGB-X tracking complements RGB appearance with thermal, depth, or event observations, but centralized training requires collecting multimodal videos at a single location. Federated training keeps raw videos local, but sensor heterogeneity can create a mismatch between parameter-sharing domains and the clients contributing to them. This mismatch is particularly relevant when RGB-only clients coexist with sensor-equipped clients: their RGB observations remain useful, but they provide no auxiliary measurements for learning sensor-dependent interactions. We propose Fed-MAPTrack, a modality-aware federated prompt tuning framework for RGB-X tracking. The pretrained tracking backbone and prediction head are frozen, and clients optimize and exchange only prompt-related parameters. The server aggregates RGB-shared parameters globally and maintains separate thermal, event, and depth banks for auxiliary-specific, fusion, and normalization parameters. Selective participation retains RGB-only clients' contributions to the global RGB bank while excluding their fusion and normalization updates from modality-specific aggregation. Across three matched training repetitions under a fixed client partition, Fed-MAPTrack improves the mean FedAvg scores by 2.35 AUC points on LasHeR, 3.45 SR points on VisEvent, and 2.11 F-score points on DepthTrack. Among the nine evaluated methods, it achieves the highest mean thermal AUC and depth F-score, while its mean event SR is 0.07 points below that of Independent FL. Controlled ablations show that extending the bucketing scope without a compatible participation rule can reduce performance. These results highlight the need to jointly define the sharing domain and eligible contributors for each parameter group.

</details>


### [122] [DimFlow: Bidirectional Grid-Expert Learning for Dynamic Gaussian Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34952)
*Gabriel Adineera,Jinoh Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DimFlow，一种用于动态高斯重建的特征学习框架，通过多分辨率网格与坐标式专家分支的双向交互，提升几何与外观属性在空间和时间上的一致性，最终在多个动态场景基准上取得优于 4D-GS 的重建质量，同时保持相近的渲染速度和模型大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态 Gaussian 重建需要紧凑、可协调变化的场景特征，以同时表达几何形变和外观变化。已有 factorized grid 能支持高效局部查询，但仅靠精确插值并不能保证动态属性在空间和时间上的相容性。因此，需要一种机制来学习更协调、更一致的动态场景特征。

Method: DimFlow 通过多分辨率网格与基于坐标的专家分支之间的双向交互来学习场景特征。具体包括：利用特征级对抗对齐连接两种特征的分布；通过自适应融合传递互补信息；利用激活掩码残差反馈在训练过程中优化网格表示；并设置四个属性专家分别组织形变、旋转、不透明度和时间信息，随后进行 Gaussian 渲染。

Result: 在 D-NeRF、HyperNeRF 和 DAVIS 的聚合比较中，DimFlow 达到 39.73 dB PSNR，比 4D-GS 高 1.92 dB，同时保持与其报告的大约 85 FPS 渲染速度相当。模型大小为 18.9 MB，略高于 4D-GS 的 18.3 MB。组件和属性专家消融实验表明，特征对齐、自适应信息交换和时间处理具有互补贡献。

Conclusion: DimFlow 证明动态高斯重建不仅依赖高效插值，还需要学习空间与时间上更相容的场景特征。通过多分辨率网格与坐标式专家分支的双向交互、特征对齐、自适应融合和属性专家组织，该方法能够在保持高渲染效率和较小模型规模的同时显著提升动态场景重建质量。

Abstract: Dynamic Gaussian reconstruction requires compact scene features to support coordinated changes in geometry and appearance. A factorized grid provides efficient local queries, but accurate interpolation alone does not ensure that the resulting dynamic attributes remain compatible across space and time. We introduce DimFlow, a framework that learns these features through bidirectional interaction between a multi-resolution grid and a coordinate-based expert branch. Feature-level adversarial alignment connects their distributions, adaptive fusion transfers complementary information, and activation-masked residual feedback refines the grid during training. Four attribute experts organize deformation, rotation, opacity, and temporal information before Gaussian rendering. In the aggregate comparison across D-NeRF, HyperNeRF, and DAVIS, DimFlow achieves 39.73 dB PSNR, improving on 4D-GS by 1.92 dB while matching its reported 85 FPS rendering speed. The model occupies 18.9 MB, compared with 18.3 MB for 4D-GS. Component and attribute-expert ablations reveal complementary contributions from feature alignment, adaptive exchange, and temporal processing.

</details>


### [123] [GUIFlow: Temporal Redundancy-Aware Incremental Inference for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34959)
*Ye Li,Haoran Ding,Huanan Liu,Yuan Meng,Yutian Shen,Yuansong Wang,Kangye Ji,Hanyun Cui,Hongbo Duan,Chen Tang,Jiawei Li,Shu-Tao Xia,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GUIFlow提出一种面向多图像GUI交互的全流程增量推理框架，通过跨步复用视觉编码、预填充和解码中的历史计算状态，在无需额外训练或改动模型可见输入的前提下实现最高约3.39x至3.49x加速，同时保持任务性能接近稠密基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视觉语言模型的GUI智能体需要连续处理高度重叠的界面观测与历史上下文，但传统推理每步都重复计算这些相似表示，导致多步交互成本很高；同时上下文、界面内容和动作参数不断变化，使简单缓存复用难以直接适用。

Method: GUIFlow将多步GUI交互视为可增量更新计算状态的过程：历史KV状态按位置对齐并注入当前上下文；利用变化信号选择性更新当前观测在视觉编码器和语言模型中的状态；并将较早动作响应作为推测草稿，由目标模型验证。由此实现视觉编码、预填充和解码阶段的跨步复用，且不需要额外训练、独立草稿模型或改变模型输入与动作接口。

Result: 在离线多图像基准上实现最高3.39x的几何平均加速，在OSWorld在线闭环交互中实现最高3.49x加速，同时任务性能大体与稠密基线相当。

Conclusion: GUIFlow表明，通过跨步选择性地维护和复用已有计算状态，可以显著降低多图像GUI智能体推理开销，并在保持交互能力的前提下提升端到端决策效率。

Abstract: GUI agents built on vision-language models interact with continuously evolving interfaces, yet conventional inference repeatedly recomputes representations of largely overlapping observations and histories. This mismatch makes multi-step interaction costly, while changes in context, interface content, and action arguments prevent straightforward cache reuse. We introduce GUIFlow, a framework for full-pipeline incremental inference in multi-image GUI interaction. Our central insight is that advancing a decision need not require rebuilding all supporting representations: each interaction step can instead selectively update computational states maintained across the trajectory. To support new decisions as the context evolves, GUIFlow adapts how past computation is reused: historical KV states are position-aligned and injected into the current context; change signals guide selective updates of current-observation states in the vision encoder and language model; and earlier action responses serve as speculative drafts verified by the target model. Together, these mechanisms enable cross-step reuse throughout visual encoding, prefill, and decoding without additional training, a separate draft model, or changes to the model-visible inputs and action interface. Experiments demonstrate up to 3.39x geometric-mean speedup across offline multi-image benchmarks and up to 3.49x in online, closed-loop interaction on OSWorld, while maintaining task performance broadly comparable to the dense baseline.

</details>


### [124] [Sparse Recovery Bird's-Eye View Model Soup for Robust Camera-Based 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34976)
*Jinwoo Chung,Sangho An,Hyunsung Park,Jangho Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SR-BEVS，通过稀疏恢复与模型汤平均提升Fast-BEV的干净精度与真实场景鲁棒性，且不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机BEV感知模型在实际部署中会受到传感器噪声、天气变化和光照变化等真实世界扰动影响，但其鲁棒性研究不足。

Method: 基于逐层权重幅值确定剪枝候选，随机生成多个稀疏模型，并使用随机学习率重新训练，再将多个恢复后的模型参数平均为一个稠密模型；同时通过模式连通性分析验证剪枝多样性仍能保持在低损失盆地内。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，相比Fast-BEV基线，腐蚀鲁棒性最高提升33.8%，干净精度提升1.8%，并在传感器噪声、天气畸变和光照变化下均有一致改进。

Conclusion: SR-BEVS是一种简单有效、无需额外数据或结构修改的稀疏恢复模型汤方法，可在不增加推理成本的前提下同时提升BEV模型精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Camera-based bird's-eye view (BEV) perception methods such as Fast-BEV have demonstrated promising results with fast inference speed and competitive accuracy. However, their robustness to real-world corruptions remains insufficiently explored, posing risks for autonomous driving deployment where environmental variations are inevitable. In this paper, we propose Sparse Recovery Bird's-Eye View Model Soup (SR-BEVS), a sparse recovery learning approach that simultaneously improves clean accuracy and corruption robustness without requiring additional data or architectural modifications. Our method identifies pruning candidates based on layer-wise weight magnitudes, applies random pruning to create diverse sparse models, and retrains each model with a randomly sampled learning rate. By averaging these diverse recovered models into a single dense model, we achieve enhanced robustness against various corruptions while introducing zero inference overhead. We further provide a mode connectivity analysis demonstrating that our pruning-based diversification preserves the connected low-loss basin necessary for stable weight averaging. Experiments on the nuScenes dataset demonstrate up to 33.8% improvement in corruption robustness and a 1.8% gain in clean accuracy over the Fast-BEV baseline, with consistent improvements across sensor noise, weather distortions, and illumination changes. Our simple yet effective approach may be applicable to other BEV models.

</details>


### [125] [Reason or Recite: Complementary Specialists with an LLM Judge for Traffic Anomaly Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34993)
*Mayur Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Reason-or-Recite（RoR）双专家框架，分别优化精确推理与参考答案风格匹配，并通过免训练 LLM 裁判逐题选择答案，在 2026 AI City Challenge 交通异常推理赛道取得域内榜首，平均得分 0.6788。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路异常视频理解不仅需要检测事件，还需验证发生了什么、描述场景并解释原因；而封闭问答奖励基于证据的精确推理，开放问答又受与参考文本风格相似度的影响，两类目标对视觉语言模型存在张力。

Method: RoR 包含三部分：首先将官方思维链标注重写为结构化、时间定位的 schema，并用 LoRA 微调 9B 视觉语言模型以增强证据推理；其次对 4-bit 量化的 27B 模型仅用答案进行微调，以吸收标注风格；最后使用无需训练的 LLM 裁判逐题在两个候选专家输出中进行选择。

Result: 在 2026 AI City Challenge 的 Traffic Anomaly Reasoning 赛道中，RoR 以 0.6788 的平均分位列 in-domain 排行榜第一。

Conclusion: 将“推理型专家”和“风格匹配型专家”分离并通过 LLM 裁判动态选择，可有效缓解视频异常理解中精确推理与开放文本评分风格之间的目标冲突。

Abstract: Road anomalies such as collisions, violations, and sudden hazards demand more from video understanding than detection: a practical system must verify what happened, describe the scene, and explain the cause. We find these competences pull in opposite directions for vision-language models: closed-form questions reward precise, evidence-grounded reasoning, while open-ended answers are scored by similarity to reference text, where matching the annotation style matters as much as reasoning correctly. We present Reason-or-Recite (RoR), a two-specialist framework for the Traffic Anomaly Reasoning track of the 2026 AI City Challenge. RoR (1) rewrites the official chain-of-thought annotations into a structured, temporally grounded schema and LoRA-tunes a 9B vision-language model on the enhanced traces; (2) fine-tunes a 4-bit-quantized 27B model on answers alone, absorbing the annotation style; and (3) selects between the two candidates per question with a training-free LLM judge. RoR ranks first on the in-domain leaderboard with mean score 0.6788.

</details>


### [126] [More Memory, Better Videos? A Controlled Study of Visual Memory in Long-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35001)
*Yuzhuo Chen,Huafeng Shi,Xinyu Wang,Yulong Xu,Yucheng Wang,Haoqin Hong,Guoxin Zhang,Zehua Ma,Weiming Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VMem-Bench，用于诊断长视频生成中的“视觉记忆/检索”是否真正有益：它能提高对历史实体的召回，但会降低精确性、增加错误引用，并可能在剧情已改变后强化过时外观；因此历史忠实度不等于当前故事正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频生成常通过检索过去视觉观察来缓解身份漂移，直觉上访问更多历史应提升一致性。但论文指出，一个图像可能“忠实于过去”却“错误于当前剧情”，因此需要系统检验视觉记忆是否总是改善生成叙事。

Method: 作者构建 VMem-Bench 两轨诊断实验。Track-A 在 92 部真实电影（16,548 段）中测量生成前从历史中选择哪些参考帧，避免生成器混淆；Track-B 在一个共享生成器上比较四种检索策略，与无记忆和“已见金标准最后出现”控制对照，生成 30 个因果故事（2,824 段）。此外还进行外观剥离分析，检查提示词本身是否已提供外观信息。

Result: 检索将实体召回率从 0.559 提升到 0.628–0.693，并提高与过去外观的相似度，但精确率从 0.977 降至 0.915–0.938，禁止实体规避率从 0.968 降至 0.845–0.898。检索还可能强化剧情变化后的过时外观。另一方面，即使没有记忆，提示词若提供外观也可实现高长间隔召回，形成混淆。

Conclusion: 视觉记忆带来的历史保真与当前故事正确性应被区分；仅凭检索相关性或最终视频一致性不足以证明记忆真正有用。评估长视频生成记忆机制时，需要专门控制提示词外观泄漏、剧情变更和错误历史引用等问题。

Abstract: Long-video generators increasingly retrieve past visual observations to prevent identity drift. The intuition is compelling: more access to history should make future video more consistent. But an image can be faithful to the past and wrong for the present. Does visual memory always improve the generated story? We investigate this question through VMem-Bench, a two-track diagnostic study. Track-A measures which references are selected from 92 real films (16,548 segments), before generation and without generator confounds. Track-B compares four retrieval policies with no-memory and gold-informed last-seen controls on 30 causally generated stories (2,824 segments) using a shared generator. Retrieval increases entity recall from 0.559 to 0.628-0.693 and improves similarity to past appearances, but decreases precision from 0.977 to 0.915-0.938 and forbidden-entity avoidance from 0.968 to 0.845-0.898. It can also reinforce obsolete appearances after the story changes them. Conversely, high long-gap recall can arise without memory when the prompt itself supplies the appearance; an appearance-stripping analysis exposes this confound. These results distinguish historical fidelity from current-story correctness and show why retrieval relevance or final-video consistency alone does not establish memory utility.

</details>


### [127] [DUET4D: Self-Corrective Coupled Guidance for Sparse-View 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35002)
*Beizhen Zhao,Nanjie Yao,Boyi Fu,Qingsong Yan,Haofan Ren,Kaiyong Zhao,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DUET4D 面向稀疏视角 4D Gaussian Splatting，提出自校正耦合引导框架，用当前 4D 高斯表示反过来引导生成先验，获得时空一致监督，并通过顺序保真重采样筛选可靠高斯基元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏相机条件下 4D 重建高度欠约束，生成先验可补全缺失外观；但视频生成模型计算代价高、难以迭代用于重建，单视角生成模型又会产生时序不一致伪监督；同时，直接吸收不确定生成结果会导致冗余高斯基元和漂浮伪影。

Method: 构建闭环自校正流程：首先由当前高斯证据构造序列对齐信息，引导生成先验在潜空间进行修复；随后使用 Sequential Fidelity Resampling 将生成监督提升回高斯空间，选择并区分被反复支持的高斯基元；修正后的 4D 表示再为下一轮生成修复提供更可靠的证据。

Result: 大量实验表明，DUET4D 能显著提升渲染质量，并改善渲染效率。

Conclusion: 通过让稀疏视角 4D 高斯表示与生成先验相互校正、耦合引导，可在缓解欠约束和时序不一致的同时降低不可靠生成监督带来的伪影，从而提升稀疏视角动态场景重建效果。

Abstract: Sparse-view 4D reconstruction is highly under-constrained when only a few cameras are available. Generative priors can recover missing appearances, but video models are computationally impractical for iterative reconstruction, whereas single-view models produce temporally inconsistent pseudo-observations. Moreover, directly absorbing uncertain generative outputs into Gaussian optimization often creates redundant primitives and floating artifacts. To address these challenges, we propose DUET4D, a self-corrective coupled guidance framework for sparse-view 4D Gaussian splatting. The core of our approach is a self-corrective framework where the 4D representation guides generative prior for spatio-temporal consistent supervisions. We first construct sequential alignments from current Gaussian evidence to steer the latent restoration. Then we use Sequential Fidelity Resampling to lift generative supervisions into Gaussian space to selectively distinguish repeatedly supported primitives. The corrected representation then provides more reliable Gaussian evidence for the next restoration cycle. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DUET4D significantly improves rendering quality and rendering efficiency.

</details>


### [128] [When Pointing Becomes Profiling: Social Bias and Refusal in Text-Steerable Visual Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35017)
*Seyedarmin Azizi,Parsa Razmara,Sajjad Ghiasvand,Ardalan Aryashad,Tina Khezresmaeilzadeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文审计一种文本引导视觉定位模型 SteerViT，发现其语言提示接口会把“指向相关区域”变成带有种族/性别刻板印象的人群画像，并进一步提出带拒绝输出和反事实不变性训练的安全方法 SafeSteer，在几乎不损失定位性能的情况下显著降低有害偏向和无法拒绝的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本条件化的视觉编码器/多模态定位模型可以通过自然语言提示生成注意力热图并定位图像区域。若提示中的社会语义（如性别、种族、犯罪相关词）被模型错误地映射到某些人群，就会把普通视觉定位转化为敏感属性推断或歧视性路由。本文需要系统审计这种风险是否真实存在、来自哪里，以及如何在不破坏模型定位能力的前提下引入安全机制。

Method: 作者首先对公开的 SteerViT 模型进行安全审计，在反事实人脸拼图、真实多人照片和 SocialCounterfactuals 等数据上测试不同文本提示（如“the person”、犯罪词、职业词、“the man”、“the monkey”等）对图像区域选择的影响。随后分析偏向来源，比较骨干模型的无提示显著性、冻结文本编码器，并定位到跨注意力层的作用，尤其是最后两层。再将结果与 CLIPSeg、Grounding-DINO、SAM 3、Qwen2.5-VL 等其他定位/视觉语言模型对比。最后提出 SafeSteer：在相同规模的 23M 可训练 steering 参数上新增一个可学习的 abstention/拒绝输出，并使用反事实不变性训练，使模型在应当指出的可见对象上保持定位，在不应回答的缺失对象或敏感刻板印象提示上选择拒绝。评价指标包括 should-point 与 should-abstain 提示分离能力（AUROC）、对敏感词和缺失词的拒绝率、对真实可见人物的误拒率，以及定位/检索性能相对原模型的下降幅度。

Result: 审计结果显示 SteerViT 存在明显的种族相关有害路由：例如在多人场景中，“the person”比基准概率更常选中男性，犯罪相关词比职业词更常选中黑人；在 SocialCounterfactuals 的四个女性图像中，提示“the man”有 75.6% 选中黑人女性；“the monkey”也会选择黑人。作者指出这些现象不是简单来自骨干模型的无提示显著性，也不只是来自冻结文本编码器，而是随 steering 强度增长，并由最后两层跨注意力进入；仅向这些层提供提示即可复现偏向。其他四个模型未观察到犯罪词路由，种族路由也较弱。原模型无法拒绝：patch softmax 必须把注意力质量分配到某处，导致它难以区分“the police officer”这类可见可定位对象、“the pizza”这类缺失对象，以及“the criminal”这类需要刻板印象推断的请求，AUROC 仅 0.52。引入 SafeSteer 后，模型在 should-point 与 should-abstain 提示分离上达到 AUROC 0.997，对未见过的敏感词和缺失词泛化达到 0.998；在 0.5 阈值下可拒绝训练集中 99.8% 的敏感词请求，以及保留集中 62% 的未见敏感词请求，同时不会过度拒绝真实可见人物。定位和检索性能相比公开模型仅下降不超过 1 个点。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，文本引导的视觉定位接口可能成为敏感属性推断和歧视性路由的入口，即使底层模型未表现出相应显著性，提示条件和可训练的跨注意力模块也可能放大社会偏见。作者认为安全机制不能只依赖事后过滤，而应在视觉 steering 模型中显式学习“何时不该定位”。SafeSteer 通过 abstention 输出和反事实不变性训练，在保持定位/检索效用的同时显著降低种族相关有害行为和无法拒绝问题。代码、基准和整理的人脸拼图数据集将被发布，便于复现和后续安全评估。

Abstract: Text-steerable visual encoders condition a frozen Vision Transformer on a natural-language prompt and point at the matching image region, producing prompt-conditioned heatmaps and features. We audit the released SteerViT model and find that its steering interface turns language-driven pointing into demographic profiling. On counterfactual face mosaics and real multi-person photographs, "the person" selects a man more often than the base rate, crime words select Black people more often than occupation words do, "the man" among four women selects the Black woman 75.6% of the time on SocialCounterfactuals, and "the monkey" selects Black people. None of them is in the backbone's unsteered saliency, and the crime association is not in the frozen text encoder; the race effects grow with steering strength and enter through the last two cross-attention layers: giving the prompt to those layers alone reproduces them. Four other grounding models, CLIPSeg, Grounding-DINO, SAM 3, and Qwen2.5-VL, show no crime routing and weaker race routing. The model cannot refuse. Its patch softmax must place its mass somewhere, and its heatmap separates a groundable request ("the police officer") from one that is absent ("the pizza") or that would require a stereotyped demographic inference ("the criminal") only at chance (AUROC 0.52). We introduce SafeSteer, which adds a learned abstention output and counterfactual-invariance training on the same 23M steering parameters. SafeSteer separates should-point from should-abstain prompts at AUROC 0.997, generalizes to held-out sensitive and absent words (0.998; at a 0.5 threshold it refuses 99.8% of trained and 62% of held-out sensitive words) with no over-refusal of visible people on the benchmark, and removes most race routing while keeping localization and retrieval within 1 point of the released model. Code, benchmarks, and the curated mosaic sets will be released.

</details>


### [129] [DriveUniTok: A Unified Multimodal Tokenizer for Driving World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35044)
*Jingbo Xu,Hanchao Leng,JiaHui Huang,Hanbing Li,Jingwei Zhao,Kuiyuan Yang,Chen Gao,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 DriveUniTok 统一多模态 token 化和 DriveUniWAM 世界-动作模型，将驾驶场景中的外观、投影 LiDAR 深度和自车运动编码到可解码的共享潜空间中，并基于 NAVSIM 验证其在预测、规划和计算成本方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型常将外观、度量几何和自车运动分别建模或部分建模，忽视它们本质上来自同一个底层世界状态的不同投影，因此可能造成信息损失和跨模态不一致，限制下游预测与规划性能。

Method: 作者提出 DriveUniTok，一种面向驾驶世界-动作模型的多模态统一 tokenizer，将未来外观、投影 LiDAR 深度和自车轨迹编码为单一可解码连续 latent。随后冻结 tokenizer，微调预训练扩散模型 DriveUniWAM，使其预测未来统一 latent，再由 tokenizer 解码回各模态。进一步使用强化学习进行后训练以提升规划性能。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 基准上，统一设计比分离流方法在预测和规划性能上更优，同时下游计算成本更低。经强化学习后训练，DriveUniWAM 的 PDMS 达到 91.54，在所评测的世界-动作模型规划器中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 将外观、度量几何和自车运动纳入统一 latent 表示，有助于减少跨模态不一致并提升信息利用效率，证明统一 tokenizer 与世界模型架构对自动驾驶未来预测和规划具有实际价值。

Abstract: World models are emerging as a fundamental component of policy learning for real-world agents, enabling agents to predict future world states and plan actions within a learned representation of the dynamic environment. Existing approaches, however, often model appearance, metric geometry represented by projected LiDAR depth, and ego motion either partially or independently, overlooking the fact that these modalities are different projections of a shared underlying world state. This separation can lead to information loss and cross-modal inconsistency, ultimately limiting downstream performance. Motivated by this unified view of the world, we propose DriveUniTok, a unified multimodal tokenizer for driving world-action models (WAMs) that encodes these modalities in a single decodable continuous latent. We freeze DriveUniTok and adapt a pretrained diffusion model, DriveUniWAM, to predict future unified latents; the tokenizer then decodes them into future appearance, projected LiDAR depth, and ego trajectories. Experiments on NAVSIM demonstrate that our unified design achieves better future prediction and planning performance at lower downstream computational cost than separate-stream alternatives. With reinforcement learning post-training, DriveUniWAM achieves a PDMS of 91.54, reaching state-of-the-art performance among the evaluated WAM-based planners.

</details>


### [130] [LOCUS: Locate Once, Check Until Settled. Draft-and-Verify Visual Grounding with a Box Head and Coordinate-Token Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35053)
*Muhammad Talha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Locus提出用视觉语言模型中的回归定位头作为坐标token解码的“投机起草器”，并用同一主干通过共享图像前缀上的Jacobi迭代进行验证，使模型以更少顺序解码步数得到与逐token解码一致的精确定位结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型定位通常有两种方式：一是将边界框解码为坐标token，精度较高但需要逐token顺序生成，速度较慢；二是用小型回归头从隐藏状态一步回归框，速度快但在高IoU指标上精度不足。作者观察到两者的失效模式不同：回归头通常能找到正确目标但边缘定位较松，而坐标token能够更精确地放置边界，因此希望结合两者优势。

Method: Locus首先使用回归头快速输出一个候选框，并将其转换为坐标token作为草稿；随后由同一视觉语言模型主干在共享图像前缀上通过Jacobi迭代并行验证这些草稿token，迭代不动点对应模型自身的贪婪坐标token答案。回归头与验证器共享同一套权重并联合训练，无需额外独立的起草器或验证模型。

Result: 在RefCOCO、RefCOCO+和RefCOCOg共8个评测划分上，验证使Locus-2B的Acc@0.9从回归头的54.87提升到59.13；其单步回归头在Acc@0.5上超过参数量不超过3B的多数已发表模型，仅Florence-2-L除外。与同规模和同训练预算下较强的开放回归头模型Jag相比，Locus在Acc@0.5上持平，并在典型接受率下使Acc@0.9提升4.52个百分点，且通过配对显著性检验验证。平均顺序解码从18.46步降至约5.60个验证pass；相比vLLM对相同坐标token进行贪婪解码，精确答案快1.5倍，但仍慢于纯回归头。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将快速回归头作为坐标token解码的投机起草器，并由同一模型自身验证，可以在不引入额外大模型的情况下显著提升视觉语言模型定位的高IoU精度，同时减少顺序解码开销。它适合对定位精度要求高但又希望比逐坐标token解码更快的场景；不过若追求极致推理延迟，纯回归头仍具有速度优势。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) ground a referring expression in one of two ways. They decode the box as coordinate tokens, which is precise but takes one sequential step per token, or they regress it from a hidden state with a small head, which takes one step but loses accuracy at high IoU. The two fail differently: a head usually finds the right object but places its edges loosely, while coordinate tokens place edges precisely. We therefore use the regression head as a speculative drafter for the model's own coordinate tokens. Locus writes the head's box as coordinate tokens and checks them with the same backbone by Jacobi iteration over a shared image prefix. The fixed point is the model's greedy coordinate-token answer, reached in about 5.60 sequential passes on average instead of 18.46 decoding steps; head and verifier are one set of weights, trained jointly. On the 8 RefCOCO, RefCOCO+ and RefCOCOg splits, verification raises Locus-2B's Acc@0.9 from 54.87 for its own head to 59.13, and the one-pass head is ahead at Acc@0.5 of every published model with at most 3B parameters except Florence-2-L. Against Jag, the strongest open regression-head model, at its own size and training budget, Locus ties at Acc@0.5 and, with typical acceptance, gains +4.52 points at Acc@0.9, with paired significance tests. Exact answers come 1.5x faster than vLLM greedy-decoding the same coordinate tokens with the same weights; a pure regression head remains faster still.

</details>


### [131] [Kronecker-Structured Video Completion with Cross-Fitted Self-Supervised Residual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35065)
*Qi Qi,Yanfei Wang,Yanjun Cui,Jun-e Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对逐管视频补全问题，提出一个“结构化模型+自监督残差校正”的两阶段方法，利用正则化掩码克罗内克积分解估计时空低秩骨架，再通过交叉拟合残差学习和轻量3D卷积网络预测缺失轨迹处的修正，从而提高观测与缺失区域的预测一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频补全或张量/矩阵补全方法常以帧内空间像素或低维缺失模式为对象，而“tube-wise video completion”要求重建每个空间位置在每一帧均缺失的整条时间轨迹。此类任务更关注时间连续性与空间相关性，同时受训练/验证目标不一致、掩码稀疏、缺失模式结构化等因素影响。仅依赖低秩分解得到的结构骨架往往只能拟合已观测区域，其样本内拟合误差不能准确反映缺失轨迹上的预测误差，因此需要引入面向缺失管段的残差校正机制。

Method: 方法分为两个阶段：第一，使用正则化掩码克罗内克积分解（KPD）构建紧凑的结构骨架，仅从已观测数据中估计时空因子，采用循环分量式最小二乘更新、空间与时间因子正则化以及正则器最优尺度平衡，保证更新具有不增目标函数性质；第二，针对结构骨架在缺失管段上的预测偏差，采用交叉拟合的自监督残差学习：将已观测管段划分为多折，利用不包含目标管段的骨架模型生成残差目标，再训练轻量3D卷积神经网络，从骨架输出、可见残差上下文、观测掩码和局部细节特征中预测修正量，最终通过精确数据一致性约束保留观测值。

Result: 实验包括消融分析、敏感性分析、灰度与RGB视频补全以及结构化缺失模式。灰度基准上，在较高测试采样率下获得最高平均PSNR和SSIM；在8段DAVIS RGB序列上，在所有评估采样率下均取得最高缺失区域平均PSNR、最高全帧SSIM，以及最低的缺失管段时间梯度平均绝对误差。

Conclusion: 该研究的核心贡献在于将可优化的结构化低秩分解与面向缺失区域的自监督残差学习相结合：KPD提供可解释且稳定的时空结构先验，交叉拟合残差学习则弥补其样本内拟合与缺失轨迹预测之间的误差失配。结果表明该方法在高稀疏采样率、结构化缺失和RGB复杂场景下具有较优的重建质量和时间一致性，适合对时序完整性要求较高的视频补全任务。

Abstract: Tube-wise video completion reconstructs temporal trajectories at spatial locations unobserved in every frame. We propose a two-stage framework combining regularized masked Kronecker product decomposition (KPD) with cross-fitted self-supervised residual learning.
