First, we estimate a compact structural backbone directly from observed entries using cyclic component-wise least-squares updates, spatial and temporal factor regularization, and regularizer-optimal scale balancing. The exact updates yield a nonincreasing objective. Second, we address the mismatch between in-sample fitting errors and the prediction errors encountered at missing tubes. We partition observed tubes into folds and construct residual targets using backbone models fitted without the corresponding target tubes. A lightweight 3-D convolutional neural network then predicts corrections from the backbone, visible residual context, observation mask, and local detail features. Exact data consistency preserves observed measurements. Experiments include ablations, sensitivity analysis, grayscale and RGB video completion, and structured missing patterns. Our method achieves the highest mean PSNR and SSIM at the higher tested sampling rates on the grayscale benchmark. On eight selected DAVIS RGB sequences, it achieves the highest mean missing-region PSNR and full-frame SSIM and the lowest mean missing-tube temporal-gradient MAE at every evaluated sampling rate.

</details>


### [132] [MemVLN: Streaming Episodic Memory for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35066)
*Yan Wang,Jiangyong Huang,Baoxiong Jia,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MEMVLN，一种面向视觉语言导航（VLN）的流式情景记忆框架，通过压缩感知特征、时间戳和相对位姿构建有界记忆库，并生成紧凑记忆 token，从而提升长时序导航能力；在标准 VLN-CE 基准上取得多数指标最优，并在真实机器人实验中明显提高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言导航要求具身智能体根据语言指令在三维环境中移动。基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的流式智能体在长时序导航中难以维持持久的情景记忆：过去观测容易被当前视频流稀释，导致智能体忘记已访问路径、错过指令线索或难以进行长距离定位。因此，需要一种可在线更新、存储开销可控、并能与预训练几何理解能力协同的情景记忆机制。

Method: MEMVLN 构建一个有界的情景记忆库，保存经过压缩的感知特征，并标注时间步和由动作序列得到的相对位姿。记忆模块通过交叉注意力将当前查询与历史记忆 token 交互，生成紧凑记忆表示。框架将记忆模块与冻结的预训练几何编码器配对，以保留几何感知能力并降低学习负担。为训练记忆模块，作者设计基于掩码的轨迹摘要任务，采用多粒度子轨迹分解，迫使模型仅依靠情景记忆重建路径。导航训练仅使用官方 R2R/RxR 轨迹，不进行额外数据泄漏。长时序分析进一步考察情景记忆何时有效。物理评估中，作者采集一个真实场景中的 30 个指令条件化 episode，其中 24 个用于后训练演示，6 个作为保留测试集。

Result: 在标准 VLN-CE 基准上，MEMVLN 在大多数已报告指标上达到最先进性能，并超过现有 VLM-based streaming agents。长时序分析表明情景记忆对依赖历史路径、重复场景或远距离指令定位的导航任务更有帮助。在真实机器人实验中，相比 VLM 基线，MEMVLN 将保留测试 episodes 的成功率从 17% 提升到 40%；进一步使用后训练演示将成功率提高到 47%。

Conclusion: MEMVLN 证明了面向长时序视觉语言导航的显式、有界、动作对齐情景记忆的重要性。通过压缩历史感知特征、结合时间戳和相对位姿，并以掩码轨迹摘要任务进行自监督学习，该方法能够增强流式智能体的路径重建和导航记忆能力。其标准仿真结果与真实机器人结果一致表明，记忆机制能显著改善长期任务成功率和真实场景鲁棒性。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to follow language instructions through 3D environments. Maintaining persistent episodic memory remains a challenge for VLM-based streaming agents in long-horizon navigation. We present MEMVLN, a streaming episodic memory framework that maintains a bounded bank of compressed perceptual features annotated with timesteps and action-derived relative poses, and produces compact memory tokens via cross-attention. We pair the proposed memory with a frozen pretrained geometry encoder. To learn the memory module, we design a masking-based trajectory summarization task with multi-granularity sub-trajectory decomposition, encouraging route reconstruction exclusively from episodic memory. Under navigation training restricted to the official R2R/RxR trajectories, MEMVLN achieves state-of-the-art performance on most reported metrics on standard VLN-CE benchmarks. Long-horizon analyses further characterize when episodic memory is useful. For physical evaluation, we collect 30 instruction-conditioned episodes in one physical scene, use 24 as demonstrations for post-training, and hold out six for testing. On the held-out episodes, MEMVLN improves success over the VLM baseline from 17% to 40%, and post-training further raises it to 47%.

</details>


### [133] [Blendshape Generation for Models with Disconnected Parts: Adding ARKit/MediaPipe Blendshapes to Anime-Style Character Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35076)
*Pramook Khungurn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向商业动漫风格3D角色模型的面部表情Blendshape生成方法，能够处理每张脸最多11个分离部件的模型；通过神经网络生成以输入网格为中心的形变场，并用锚定部件协调各部件输出，从而获得不依赖拓扑结构的变形结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数Blendshape生成研究通常假设模型为单一连续部件，但实际动漫角色模型常由头发、眼睛、眉毛、牙齿、配饰等多个分离部件组成。用户社区需要为这类多部件模型快速生成固定表情Blendshape，而传统方法和已有神经网络方法难以简单、通用地解决这一问题。

Method: 作者训练神经网络生成围绕输入网格的形变场，而非直接输出部件间硬编码的网格变形。每个分离部件由独立网络处理，同时每个网络还处理一个“锚定部件”的几何信息，以协调不同部件输出之间的相对位置。为提高精度，引入两个机制：一是预测输出位移是否为零，二是在不同尺寸级别的Blendshape上为每组分别配置网络。另外提出一种增强左右对称性的方法来提升Blendshape质量。

Result: 论文以案例研究形式展示该方法能够生成商业动漫风格3D角色模型的面部表情Blendshape，并处理多部件模型。作者强调该方法相比近期基于神经网络的工作更简单，同时能解决目标模型用户社区中的实际问题。

Conclusion: 通过部件级形变场生成与锚定部件协调，该方法实现了拓扑无关、可处理多分离部件的面部Blendshape自动生成。其设计简洁，并通过零位移预测、尺度分组学习和对称性增强提升了生成质量，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Blendshape generation for 3D models has been extensively researched, but very few works address generating blendshapes for models with multiple disconnected parts. We present a case study of generating fixed facial expression blendshapes for commercial anime-style 3D character models with up to 11 parts per face. To make sure that our system can handle models in a way that is agnostic to topology, we train neural networks to generate deformation fields around the input meshes. We employ at least one network to independently process each part, and the relative positions of their outputs are coordinated by having each network also process the geometry of an anchor part. We use two mechanisms to improve accuracy: having the networks predict whether the output displacement is zero or not, and classifying blendshapes into different size groups and dedicating a network to each group. We also introduce a method to improve blendshape quality by enhancing bilateral symmetry. Our method is simpler than recent neural-network-based works and solves a real problem of the user community of these models.

</details>


### [134] [Improving VLMs on Scientific Chart Analysis via Autonomous Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35077)
*Abhinav Sudhakar Dubey,Vidushi Goel,Kiran Rana,Shikhar Mathur,Vaggos Chatziafratis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在模型完全冻结、不重新训练的前提下，通过自主进化搜索并优化VLM科学图表问答推理程序及外围代码，显著提升答案精确匹配准确率并提高推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放视觉语言模型在科学图表问答任务中常因回答冗长或不够精确而失败，作者希望探究在完全不改变模型权重、仅优化推理流程与代码的情况下，可以恢复多少模型潜在准确率。

Method: 方法从一个基线推理程序出发，采用类AlphaEvolve的自主进化流程：每轮将候选程序代码和训练集中的少量失败样例交给LLM judge，要求生成改进性演化变异；候选程序放入小型池并依据训练准确率排序，后续编辑优先基于高分候选进行。实验覆盖3个VLM和4个科学图表基准，主要测量描述型问答中答案与真实标注完全一致的比例。

Result: 在全部实验设置中准确率均提升，平均精确匹配准确率从起始程序的16.6%提升到进化后程序的41.3%，最大单项提升达55.2个百分点。由于进化程序倾向生成更短答案，答案更易判分且生成更快，最终程序相比起始程序提速3.8倍。

Conclusion: 科学图表VLM问答中的大量错误并非完全源于模型视觉理解能力不足，而是显著受推理管线和答案生成格式影响。仅通过进化式优化外围代码与推理程序，即可在不重新训练模型的情况下大幅提升冻结VLM的图表问答精确率和效率。

Abstract: Understanding and operating with scientific charts is a standard approach in data analysis, and while humans are good at interpreting such charts, open vision-language models (VLMs) are known to perform poorly when it comes to question answering based on such data types. Motivated by an initial observation that many of their errors are due to inexact answering, we raise the following natural question: how much accuracy can we recover without changing the model at all? In this paper, we study this question by searching over inference programs and the code around a "frozen" version of the model. Using ideas from autonomous evolution (famously used in AlphaEvolve), we first start from a baseline inference program, and in each round we show an LLM judge the code together with a few failures on training questions, and ask for an improving evolutionary move. As usual in autonomous evolution approaches, the candidate programs are kept in a small pool ranked by training accuracy, and the next edit is performed on top-scorers from this pool. We run this approach on three VLMs and four popular benchmarks on scientific charts, and we measure the fraction of descriptive questions whose answer exactly matches the ground truth. The accuracy improves in all experimental settings. On average, it rises from 16.6% with the starting program to 41.3% with the evolved one, and the largest single increase is 55.2 percentage points. As a side benefit, our evolved program is 3.8x faster than the starting program, because short answers are easier to grade and faster to generate. Our results demonstrate that a large part of the observed errors arise due to the inference pipeline, and that optimizing it can improve the "frozen" VLM without the need to retrain it.

</details>


### [135] [Test-Time Prompt-Agnostic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35090)
*Junze Wang,Lei Fan,Dezheng Zhang,Donglin Di,Yang Song,Sidong Liu,Cong Cong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TPD：一种测试时提示无关的分解与控制框架，将提示更新轨迹建模为动力系统，并通过 AKC 和 HUR 分别调控更新幅度与方向，提升 TPT 稳定性；在 15 个数据集上改善 10 个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时提示微调（TPT）主要对每步提示更新进行局部优化，未受控的提示更新轨迹容易因噪声或偏置的无监督目标而累积误差。作者识别出两类失败模式：幅度扩张（更新过大）和方向漂移（连续更新方向不一致），导致提示优化不稳定。

Method: 提出 Test-Time Prompt-Agnostic Decomposition（TPD），从动力系统视角分析提示更新轨迹，并将基础提示更新分解为幅度与方向。TPD 通过谱半径度量更新扩张，并将方向分解为持续分量、振荡分量和残差分量。基于该分解，自适应 Koopman 控制（AKC）在近期动力学具有扩张性时收缩更新幅度；Hankel 更新路由器（HUR）保留持续方向分量并抑制振荡与残差分量，从而稳定提示更新。该框架为提示无关设计，可接入视觉和文本 TPT 方法，无需改动主干网络、提示架构或适配损失。

Result: 在 15 个数据集上的实验表明，TPD 一致提升 10 个基线方法：视觉提示准确率提高 3–8%，文本提示准确率提高超过 2%。

Conclusion: TPD 将测试时提示更新轨迹从局部逐步优化视角提升为动力学可控问题，通过幅度控制和方向滤波有效缓解 TPT 的不稳定现象，为视觉和文本测试时提示微调提供了通用、可插拔的稳定化框架。

Abstract: Test-Time Prompt Tuning (TPT) adapts large pretrained models to unseen target distribution shifts by updating only lightweight prompt tokens. Existing TPT methods are effective, but they mainly optimize each prompt update locally. Across test-time steps, prompts updated at the current step are reused for the corresponding predictions and updates, so noisy or biased unlabeled objectives can accumulate and destabilize the prompt-update trajectory. We identify two failure modes: magnitude expansion, where updates move the prompt too far, and directional drift, where consecutive updates point in inconsistent directions. To analyze and control these failures, we propose Test-Time Prompt-Agnostic Decomposition ($\mathtt{TPD}$), which characterizes observed prompt-update trajectories from a dynamical-systems view and decomposes base prompt updates into magnitude and direction. $\mathtt{TPD}$ computes a spectral radius to measure update expansion and decomposes update directions into persistent, oscillatory, and residual components. Building on this decomposition, Adaptive Koopman Control ($\mathtt{AKC}$) regulates update magnitude by shrinking updates under expansive recent dynamics, while Hankel Update Router ($\mathtt{HUR}$) refines update direction by preserving persistent components and suppressing oscillatory and residual components. Together, $\mathtt{AKC}$ and $\mathtt{HUR}$ produce a stabilized prompt update. As a prompt-agnostic framework, $\mathtt{TPD}$ can be plugged into visual and text TPT methods without modifying the backbone, prompt architecture, or adaptation loss. Experiments on 15 datasets show that $\mathtt{TPD}$ consistently improves 10 baselines, with accuracy gains of $3$--$8\%$ for visual prompts and over $2\%$ for text prompts.

</details>


### [136] [Uncertainty Evaluation to Support Safety of the Intended Functionality Analysis for Determining the Applicability of Performance Targets Across Triggering Conditions in ML-Based LiDAR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35092)
*Milin Patel,Rolf Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究针对 ISO 21448 预期功能安全（SOTIF）分析，提出一种不确定性阈值跨触发条件迁移与统计检验框架，用于评估基于点云的目标检测器在不同工况下是否仍满足错误接受率上限；实验表明，单条件下确定的阈值不能直接外推到所有触发条件，必须结合独立序列数量、错误接受率、正确检测保留率等进行条件内验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶感知系统的安全评估中，机器学习检测器的性能指标常依赖训练或验证数据的特定条件分布。若某一条件下选择的置信度或不确定性阈值能够满足目标性能上限，该统计保证通常只对类似分布的数据有效。然而，SOTIF 分析要求覆盖各种可能触发系统失效的工况，因此需要回答：一个条件下的阈值和性能证据能否迁移到另一触发条件？如何以可量化的误差概率判断该性能目标在目标条件下是否适用、不适用或尚无法判定？

Method: 论文提出一种不确定性评估与阈值迁移方法。首先，将 LiDAR 点云目标检测器（如 PointPillars 集成模型）在源条件下选定的不确定性阈值迁移到目标触发条件；其次，设计统计检验，在给定错误概率下判断目标性能指标在迁移后条件中是否“适用”“不适用”或“未确定”；再次，给出达到“适用”结论所需的独立样本数量下限。实验使用 KITTI 和 nuScenes 数据集，并以两种计数单元评估：帧和录制序列。由于相邻帧存在依赖关系，论文强调应以录制序列作为独立单位进行统计推断，同时报告被接受的正确检测比例等指标。

Result: 当以录制序列为独立单位时，KITTI 和 nuScenes 数据均没有足够的独立序列来支持任何目标性能上限，且没有迁移条件得到“不适用”的明确判定。当改为以帧计数时，在预先设定的性能上限下，迁移至远距离、雨天，以及在两个独立训练的检测器之间进行阈值迁移时，均出现“不适用”结果；同时，那些在迁移后仍满足目标上限的阈值会拒绝大量正确检测，导致正确检测保留率下降。此外，在雨天场景中，接收者操作特征曲线下面积（AUC）并未必然下降。

Conclusion: 单一下游条件下获得的性能达标证据不能直接视为其他触发条件下的安全证据。阈值和不确定性方法在跨条件使用前，必须在目标触发条件中进行独立统计检验，并明确报告独立录制序列数量、被接受检测中的正确检测比例等关键信息。该工作为 SOTIF 场景下的感知模块验证提供了更严谨的不确定性阈值迁移与统计判定框架。

Abstract: For safety of the intended functionality (SOTIF) analysis according to ISO 21448, a machine learning object detector for light detection and ranging (LiDAR) point clouds can meet a performance target, a limit on the mean per-frame share of wrong detections among accepted detections, by rejecting detections above an uncertainty threshold. A threshold selected on data of one condition carries a statistical guarantee only for data distributed like its selection data, whereas SOTIF analysis covers every triggering condition. This paper presents an uncertainty evaluation in which the threshold is transferred into a triggering condition, a test decides at a stated error probability whether the target is applicable there, not applicable or not determined, and a lower bound counts the independent units an applicable outcome requires. For PointPillars ensembles on KITTI and nuScenes, counted in recorded sequences, as the dependence between frames requires, neither dataset held enough independent units for any target limit, and no transfer had a not applicable outcome. Counted in frames, the same data gave not applicable outcomes at limits fixed in advance after transfers into far distance, into rain and between two separately trained detectors, thresholds that met the target limit after a transfer rejected most correct detections, and in rain the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve did not decrease. A performance target met under one condition therefore counts as evidence under a triggering condition only after a test there, reported with its share of accepted correct detections and its number of independent recorded sequences.

</details>


### [137] [Understanding and Repairing Cooperative Perception under Adverse Weather](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35110)
*Mengxu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究恶劣天气导致协同3D检测失效的“天气诱导上下文干扰”现象，并提出Adverse-Weather Cooperative Repair（AWCR）框架，通过恢复目标邻域特征、校正候选和基于当前测量质量筛选来修复协同检测性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同3D检测通常依赖路侧或车端传感器之间的互补信息，但恶劣天气会改变目标外部上下文，使协同收益消失，即使标注对象内部的LiDAR点保持不变。作者希望系统研究这种非显性的天气诱导失效机制，并设计可针对不同监督条件工作的修复方法。

Method: 首先，在模拟和真实路侧输入上进行受控实验，保持标注对象内部点不变，仅移除或替换外部上下文，以验证天气相关外部变化如何破坏融合前的目标表示。随后提出AWCR，根据可用监督信号采用不同实现：在模拟环境中通过Point Classifier过滤新增天气回波；在真实输入中，通过Local Correction细化候选目标，并通过Quality Selection依据当前测量对候选进行质量检查。方法应用于V2X-ViT、AttFuse以及V2X-R雾天等协同检测设置。

Result: 实验显示，外部上下文丢失会在融合前改变目标表示，并移除原本有效的协同辅助。在真实输入中，恢复目标邻域特征可修复31个天气诱导失效案例中的30个，而三组等面积外部对照仅恢复0个。AWCR在自然恶劣天气CoInfra开发数据上分别使V2X-ViT和AttFuse的car BEV AP@0.7提升4.66和5.56个百分点；在模拟V2X-R雾天设置下，于固定4096候选预算时使vehicle BEV AP@0.7提升4.47个百分点。候选追踪还表明，校正模块可为部分原候选无法在评测阈值下定位的目标提供准确几何信息。

Conclusion: 该研究首次以受控方式揭示恶劣天气通过外部上下文变化削弱协同3D检测收益的失效机制，证明恢复目标邻域特征和基于当前测量进行候选校正与筛选能有效修复协同检测。AWCR为恶劣天气下鲁棒协同3D检测提供了可操作的方法方向。

Abstract: A cooperative 3D detector can miss a vehicle even when the LiDAR points on it remain unchanged. We study this failure under adverse weather as Weather-Induced Context Interference. To our knowledge, this is the first controlled study of how weather-related exterior changes remove cooperative benefits while preserving all points inside annotated objects. Controlled experiments on simulated and real roadside inputs show that exterior loss changes object representations before fusion and removes previously useful assistance. On real inputs, restoring object-neighborhood features recovers 30 of 31 induced failures; three equal-area exterior controls recover none. The failure condition also appears with a second fusion model. These findings motivate Adverse-Weather Cooperative Repair (AWCR), with separate implementations for the available supervision. Point Classifier filters added weather returns in simulation. On real inputs, Local Correction refines candidates and Quality Selection checks them against current measurements, without naturally paired clear/adverse-weather recordings. Candidate tracing shows that correction supplies accurate geometry for some targets that original candidates cannot localize at the evaluation threshold. On natural adverse-weather CoInfra development data, AWCR improves car bird's-eye-view (BEV) AP@0.7 by 4.66 points with V2X-ViT and 5.56 with AttFuse. On simulated V2X-R fog, it improves vehicle BEV AP@0.7 by 4.47 points at a fixed 4,096-candidate budget.

</details>


### [138] [Temporal Slice Learning for AI-Generated Video Detection with 400$\times$ Fewer FLOPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35148)
*Wei Shang,Zhiyuan Li,Shengfeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向AI生成视频检测的轻量化频率感知时间切片一致性框架，将完整视频重构为紧凑的时间切片表示，以更低计算成本捕捉微弱且局部化的时间伪造痕迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成视频模型逼真度提升，AI生成视频的判别信号越来越依赖细微、微弱且空间局部化的时间不一致性；但现有检测器通常需要建模整段视频并依赖重量级骨干网络，造成高计算成本、扩展性不足，且大量冗余视频内容会稀释关键时间线索。

Method: 作者从表征角度重新设计AI生成视频检测流程：不直接对完整视频建模，而是将视频重组为紧凑的时间切片表示。该方法通过聚合随时间变化的信息量较大的空间行和列形成切片域输入，抑制冗余外观信息并保留可区分的时间动态。同时，从高区和低频区构造互补切片，以暴露不稳定细节和异常运动平滑性等伪影。框架具体实例化为时间切片一致性网络，包含任务驱动切片定位、分层位置编码和轻量Transformer，用于建模跨切片与跨频率依赖。此外，作者构建了AI-Artist基准，包含来自Seedance、Kling等高保真生成器并由艺术家筛选的视频。

Result: 在GenVideo和AI-Artist上的实验表明，该方法取得较强的AI生成视频检测性能，同时将模型前向计算量降低超过400倍，相比强基线ReStraV实现3.6倍模型推理加速；包含预处理在内的端到端速度也快2.9倍。

Conclusion: 将AI生成视频检测从整视频建模转向紧凑、频率感知的时间切片一致性表示，可更有效利用局部时间动态伪影，显著降低计算开销，推动面向真实部署的可扩展AI生成视频检测。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative video models have improved visual realism, making detection increasingly dependent on subtle temporal inconsistencies. However, these signals are weak and spatially localized, while most of the video content is redundant. This creates a fundamental mismatch: existing detectors rely on heavy backbones to model full videos, which leads to high computational cost and limited scalability. In this work, we rethink AI-generated video detection from a representation perspective. Instead of modeling entire videos, we restructure them into compact temporal slice representations that isolate informative temporal evolution. We introduce a lightweight framework using frequency-aware temporal slice consistency learning, where informative spatial rows and columns are aggregated over time to form slice-domain inputs. This reparameterization suppresses redundant appearance while preserving discriminative temporal dynamics, enabling efficient and targeted detection. By constructing slices from both high- and low-frequency regions, our method exposes complementary temporal artifacts, including unstable details and abnormal motion smoothness. We instantiate this idea in a Temporal Slice Consistency Network, which integrates task-driven slice localization, hierarchical positional encoding, and a lightweight Transformer to model cross-slice and cross-frequency dependencies. We further introduce AI-Artist, a new benchmark with artist-curated videos from recent high-fidelity generators, including Seedance and Kling. Experiments on GenVideo and AI-Artist show that our method achieves strong performance while requiring over 400$\times$ fewer model-forward FLOPs and 3.6$\times$ faster model inference than the strong video-based baseline ReStraV (2.9$\times$ faster end to end, including preprocessing), enabling scalable and practical deployment.

</details>


### [139] [OrthoRF: Exploring Orthogonality in Object-Centric Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35203)
*Despoina Touska,Bastiaan Onne Fagginger Auer,Alexandru Onose,Tejaswi Kasarla,Luis Armando Pérez Rey,Maximilian Lipp,Lyubov Amitonova,Martin R. Oswald,Pascal Cerfontaine*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出正交旋转特征（OrthoRF），通过在旋转特征的相位/方向空间中引入正交约束，提升基于神经同步的物体中心学习中的特征绑定、分组和遮挡物恢复能力，从而得到更清晰、更可解释的无监督物体发现表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经同步被认为是大脑将视觉场景组织成多物体结构化表示的重要机制。在机器学习中，基于同步的模型可通过复值特征的相位存储绑定关系来学习物体中心表示；旋转特征进一步用向量激活的幅值表示物体存在、方向表示归属关系。但现有方法在重叠物体、噪声和遮挡等复杂场景中的相位对齐与分组仍不够稳定，且许多同步模型仍需后处理聚类。因此需要一种简单有效的归纳偏置来增强特征绑定和可解释性。

Method: 作者提出 OrthoRF，其核心是在 RF 的方向/相位空间中施加正交性约束。具体包括设计内积损失以鼓励不同物体或特征之间的方向正交，并配合架构改进实现更稳定的旋转特征表示。该机制使不同物体的相位对齐更尖锐、归属绑定更可靠，从而改善无监督物体发现中的分组性能，并减少对后处理聚类的依赖。方法在包含重叠物体、噪声和分布外测试的场景中进行评估。

Result: 在无监督物体发现任务中，OrthoRF 在多个设置下与现有模型相比性能持平或更优，包括重叠物体、噪声干扰和分布外测试。其学习到的表示更具可解释性，相位对齐更清晰，分组更可靠，并且不需要许多同步模型所依赖的后处理聚类。尤为重要的是，OrthoRF 相比当前模型能够恢复被遮挡的物体部分，说明其在遮挡条件下具有更强的物体分组能力。

Conclusion: 正交性是一种简单而有效的归纳偏置，可显著增强基于神经同步的物体中心学习中特征绑定、场景分解和遮挡恢复能力。OrthoRF 通过方向空间中的正交约束改善了旋转特征的相位对齐与表示质量，为可解释、鲁棒的多物体无监督学习提供了新路径。

Abstract: Neural synchrony is hypothesized to help the brain organize visual scenes into structured multi-object representations. In machine learning, synchrony-based models analogously learn object-centric representations by storing binding in the phase of complex-valued features. Rotating Features (RF) instantiate this idea with vector-valued activations, encoding object presence in magnitudes and affiliation in orientations. We propose Orthogonal Rotating Features (OrthoRF), which enforces orthogonality in RF's orientation space via an inner-product loss and architectural modifications. This yields sharper phase alignment and more reliable grouping. In evaluations of unsupervised object discovery, including settings with overlapping objects, noise, and out-of-distribution tests, OrthoRF matches or outperforms current models while producing more interpretable representations, and it eliminates the post-hoc clustering required by many synchrony-based approaches. Unlike current models, OrthoRF also recovers occluded object parts, indicating stronger grouping under occlusion. Overall, orthogonality emerges as a simple, effective inductive bias for synchrony-based object-centric learning.

</details>


### [140] [Multi-Agent Relay Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35219)
*Xu Liu,Jungang Qiao,Guikun Chen,Xiaojuan Qi,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MACR 多智能体置信度引导接力框架，通过动态转移推理所有权，让后续智能体继承并改进逐步演化的推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体协作方法多采用群体思考拓扑，即各智能体独立生成答案后再聚合输出，但这种方式难以让智能体在推理过程中持续互补地修正和深化中间推理。作者希望探索一种接力思考拓扑，使推理责任可以动态转移，从而让不同能力的智能体在不同推理阶段逐步接力完善答案。

Method: 提出 Multi-Agent Confidence-guided Relay（MACR）框架。其关键问题包括何时将推理传递给下一个智能体，以及由哪个智能体继续推理。MACR 通过置信度引导的接力触发机制判断传递时机，并设计竞标机制，根据各智能体的置信度选择最合适的下一个接力者，从而实现多智能体之间的动态接力协作。

Result: 在四个基准测试上，该方法相比最强单模型策略在准确率上取得提升，例如在 MathVista 上提升 3.3%，在 MMStar 上提升 3.9%。同时，其效果与多智能体策略相当，但在 HallusionBench 上 token 开销减少至约六分之一。分析表明，接力协作能使异构智能体在不同推理阶段逐步整合互补信息并融合不同能力。

Conclusion: MACR 证明了动态接力式多智能体协作相比传统群体聚合方式更具效率与扩展性。它能够在保持多智能体协同效果的同时显著降低 token 成本，并为异构智能体协作提供了新的拓扑范式。

Abstract: Existing multi-agent collaboration (MAC) methods have primarily explored a group thinking collaboration topology, where agents independently generate solutions and aggregate their outputs. In this work, we investigate an alternative relay thinking topology, where reasoning ownership is dynamically transferred across agents, allowing subsequent agents to inherit and refine an evolving reasoning trajectory. We introduce Multi-Agent Confidence-guided Relay (MACR), a framework that enables dynamical relay collaboration among agents. The key challenge is determining when reasoning should be relayed and which agent should continue the process. method addresses this challenge through confidence-guided relay triggering and a bidding mechanism that selects the next agent based on its confidence. Experiments on four benchmarks show that method outperforms the strongest single-model strategies in accuracy, e.g., +3.3% on MathVista and +3.9% on MMStar, and remains on par with multi-agent strategies while consuming 6.0x less token overhead on HallusionBench and this http URL analysis demonstrates that relay collaboration enables heterogeneous agents to progressively integrate complementary insights, combining heterogeneous capabilities across reasoning stages. Our source code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [141] [Joint Latent Reasoning and Observation for Agentic Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35229)
*Junzhang Liu,Hao Yuan,Qi Zhang,Yiming Wu,Long Chen,Zhecan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 LARVA，一种面向视频理解的潜在智能体推理框架，通过连续潜变量表示而非文本描述在观察器与推理器之间传递信息，并用动作与答案监督优化表示，以提升小模型蒸馏多轮视频智能体的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效的视频智能体不仅需要知道如何回答，还需要决定下一步观察哪里。将这种能力迁移到较小模型很困难，因为每次观察既要支持当前答案，也要指导后续证据搜索。纯文本描述可能只记录当前可见内容，却遗漏对下一步决策有用的视觉线索。

Method: LARVA 框架联合学习潜在推理表示与视觉观察：观察器将紧凑的连续表示传给推理器，推理器据此选择下一个采集动作或给出最终答案。由于动作和答案的监督信号会穿过观察器—推理器接口，观察器被训练为传递对下游决策有用的信息。同时，利用教师轨迹进行观察重构和推理分布对齐，作为辅助监督。实验采用 4B 和 7B 主干模型，在五个视频理解基准上评估。

Result: LARVA 在多个视频理解基准上表现较强。在 VideoHolmes、Video-MME 和 LVBench 上，7B 模型相比使用相同教师轨迹训练的文本思维链学生模型分别提升 5.1、2.5 和 3.7 个百分点，说明潜在表示在蒸馏视频智能体能力时优于仅用文本中间表示。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在将多轮视频智能体能力蒸馏到小模型时，联合学习证据表示方式与基于该表示的决策行为非常重要。用连续潜变量承载视觉证据并受下游动作/答案监督，比依赖文本描述更利于指导后续观察选择，从而提升视频理解性能。

Abstract: Effective video agents must learn not only how to answer, but also where to look next. Transferring this capability to smaller models is challenging because each observation serves two purposes: supporting an answer and guiding the search for further evidence. Textual descriptions may capture what is currently visible while omitting cues needed for subsequent acquisition. We introduce Latent Agentic Reasoning for Video Analysis (LARVA), a framework that distills multi-round video agents through jointly learned latent reasoning and visual observations. Rather than communicating through captions, an observer passes compact continuous representations to a reasoner that selects the next acquisition action or produces the final answer. Crucially, action and answer supervision propagate through this interface, training the observer to communicate information useful for downstream decisions. Observation reconstruction and reasoning-distribution alignment provide auxiliary supervision from textual teacher trajectories. Across five video-understanding benchmarks, LARVA achieves strong performance with 4B and 7B backbones. On VideoHolmes, Video-MME, and LVBench, the 7B model outperforms a textual CoT student trained on the same teacher trajectories by 5.1, 2.5, and 3.7 percentage points, respectively. These results highlight the value of jointly learning how agents represent evidence and act on it when distilling video-understanding capabilities.

</details>


### [142] [OVM-Occ: Open-Vocabulary Monocular Occupancy Prediction via Geometry-Aware Vision-Language Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35312)
*Shuyang Yu,Haibo Qin,Shaohan Hu,Wei Shan,Xiaoqing Guan,You Wang,Guang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出OVM-Occ，一种基于高斯的开放词汇室内单目占用预测框架，通过语言嵌入高斯表示、几何感知可变形注意、实例级视觉语言监督和几何/语义解耦蒸馏，实现更泛化的3D几何与细粒度语义预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目室内占用预测方法通常受限于预定义封闭类别集合，难以泛化到未见类别；而室内场景物体语义高度多样且细粒度强，因此需要支持开放词汇识别的占用预测框架。

Method: 方法核心包括：1）采用语言嵌入高斯作为中间场景表示，以连接3D占用几何与视觉语言特征；2）设计几何感知可变形注意模块，利用几何信息优化高斯语义特征；3）构造实例级视觉语言嵌入，为语义学习提供更可靠的监督；4）提出几何感知解耦语义蒸馏，缓解几何学习与语义学习之间的相互干扰；5）扩展OpenOcc-ScanNet和OpenOcc-Replica两个开放词汇占用预测基准，支持更丰富、更细粒度的类别评测。

Result: 实验表明，OVM-Occ在室内开放词汇单目占用预测任务上达到最新最优性能。在Occ-ScanNet数据集上，相比此前最优方法取得12.2 mIoU和0.6 IoU的提升，并在新提出的开放词汇评测协议下保持一致增益。

Conclusion: 该工作证明将高斯场景表示与实例感知的视觉语言教师相结合，可有效提升室内单目占用预测在开放词汇类别上的泛化能力，同时缓解几何和语义学习的冲突，为细粒度室内场景理解提供了新的方法框架与评测基准。

Abstract: Monocular occupancy prediction in indoor scenes aims to recover a scene's 3D geometry and semantics from a single RGB image. Existing methods are restricted to a closed set of predefined categories, limiting their ability to generalize to unseen categories. This limitation is particularly critical in indoor scenes, where objects exhibit highly diverse and fine-grained semantic categories. We study open-vocabulary monocular occupancy prediction for indoor scenes and present OVM-Occ, a Gaussian-based occupancy framework with an instance-aware vision-language teacher. Specifically, we adopt Language-Embedded Gaussians as the intermediate scene representation, propose a geometry-aware deformable attention module to refine Gaussian semantic features, and construct instance-level vision-language embeddings to provide reliable semantic supervision. We further introduce geometry-aware decoupled semantic distillation to mitigate the interference between geometry learning and semantic learning. To evaluate open-vocabulary occupancy prediction performance, we introduce OpenOcc-ScanNet and OpenOcc-Replica, two extended benchmarks with more diverse and fine-grained semantic categories. Experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in open-vocabulary monocular occupancy prediction for indoor scenes, outperforming the previous state-of-the-art approach by 12.2 mIoU and 0.6 IoU on the Occ-ScanNet dataset, with consistent gains under new open-vocabulary evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [143] [CVTSplat: Multi-view Reconstruction Using an Adaptive Centroidal Voronoi Tessellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35347)
*Briac Toussaint,Diego Thomas,Jean-Sébastien Franco,Edmond Boyer,Christian Theobalt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CVTSplat 将自适应质心 Voronoi 剖分用于多视图图像形状与外观重建，通过符号距离场调节 cell 大小，并设计专用 GPU 流程和光栅器，实现比通用方法更快的 Voronoi 构造和具有竞争力的几何重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Voronoi 剖分可作为自适应空间离散化表示，用于形状和外观重建；但其不规则 cell 会在优化过程中导致频繁重建剖分的高昂开销。标准质心 Voronoi 剖分具有规则、形状良好的 cell，计算高效，但原始 CVT 主要支持较均匀的 cell 尺寸，难以适应重建表面附近的局部复杂度变化。因此需要一种既能保持 CVT 规则性和计算效率，又能实现非均匀自适应 cell 尺寸的剖分方法。

Method: 该方法提出 adaptive centroidal Voronoi tessellations，即自适应质心 Voronoi 剖分。它扩展标准 CVT，使 cell 尺寸可以非均匀变化，同时尽量保持质心性和规则形状。具体地，使用演化中的符号距离场来调节 cell 大小，将生成点集中到零水平集附近，从而在表面附近提供更精细的离散化。为利用这种规则性，作者设计了整体加速流程，包括面向最近邻查询和近似 Voronoi 构造的 GPU 算法，以及专门适配小型、近似各向同性 cell 的光栅器。

Result: 在该设定下，所提出的 Voronoi 构造比通用替代方法快 5 到 10 倍。在 DTU 和 BlendedMVS 数据集上，CVTSplat 的几何重建精度可与当前基于 Gaussian splatting 的最优表面重建方法竞争，同时在单 GPU 上约 2.5 分钟即可完成训练。

Conclusion: CVTSplat 表明，自适应质心 Voronoi 剖分可以在保持剖分规则性和计算效率的同时，根据重建表面进行自适应空间离散。这种平衡使 Voronoi-based 表示更适合多视图形状与外观重建，并在速度与精度之间取得良好折中。

Abstract: Voronoi tessellations have recently emerged as an adaptive, space-partitioning discretization for shape and appearance reconstruction from multi-view images. However, the irregular cells they produce make the repeated reconstruction of the tessellation during optimization costly. We therefore explore adaptive centroidal Voronoi tessellations (CVTs), which extend standard CVTs with non-uniform cell sizes while preserving the regular, well-shaped cells that make them efficient to compute. In CVTSplat, cell size is modulated by the evolving signed distance field, concentrating sites near the zero level set while keeping cells nearly centroidal. This regularity lets us specialize the whole pipeline, with GPU algorithms for nearest-neighbor queries and approximate Voronoi construction, and a rasterizer tailored to small, nearly isotropic cells. Our Voronoi construction is 5 to 10 times faster than general-purpose alternatives in this regime. On DTU and BlendedMVS, CVTSplat reaches geometric accuracy competitive with the best Gaussian-splatting surface methods while training in about 2.5 minutes on a single GPU.

</details>


### [144] [SG-Splat: Stable Geometry Learning under Pose Uncertainty for Pose-Free 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35361)
*Xiaohan Lu,Jiaqi Zhang,Weijia Jiang,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SG-Splat，一种面向无姿态前馈 3D Gaussian Splatting 的鲁棒框架，通过解耦几何与属性、位姿/几何精修和双路径训练，降低残差位姿误差对 Gaussian 学习的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 适合实时新视角合成与三维重建，但现有无姿态前馈方法对残差相机位姿误差敏感。位姿误差会扰动 canonical-space Gaussian 变换，使几何和属性学习受到噪声监督，从而降低重建稳定性与精度。

Method: SG-Splat 将几何估计与 Gaussian 属性预测分为两个专用分支，以减弱位姿敏感的几何与属性之间的误差耦合；并采用 coarse-to-fine 精修流程：Flow-Guided Pose Refinement 利用光流相关特征和可微 bundle adjustment 精修相机位姿；Residual-Guided Geometry Correction 根据位姿精修产生的深度残差修正初始点图，得到更可靠的 Gaussian 中心。训练阶段还提出双路径策略，混合真实位姿与预测位姿进行 canonical transformation，为属性学习提供干净监督并提升几何对残差位姿误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 在 DL3DV 和 RealEstate10K 上的大量实验表明，SG-Splat 在新视角合成和相机位姿估计任务中均取得 state-of-the-art 性能。

Conclusion: SG-Splat 通过分支解耦、位姿与几何级联精修以及双路径训练，有效缓解了无姿态前馈 3DGS 对残差位姿误差的敏感性，提升了 Gaussian 表示学习的稳定性与重建质量。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become a promising representation for real-time novel view synthesis and 3D scene reconstruction. For practical deployment, recent studies have explored pose-free feed-forward 3DGS, which reconstructs Gaussian representations directly from unposed input images. However, these methods remain sensitive to residual pose errors, which perturb canonical-space Gaussian transformation, introducing noisy supervision for geometry and attribute learning. To address this problem, we present SG-Splat, a pose-free feed-forward 3DGS framework that improves robustness to pose uncertainty and stabilizes the learning of Gaussian primitives. SG-Splat decouples geometry estimation and Gaussian attribute prediction into two dedicated branches, reducing error coupling between pose-sensitive geometry and Gaussian attributes. We further introduce a coarse-to-fine refinement pipeline, consisting of Flow-Guided Pose Refinement (FGPR) and Residual-Guided Geometry Correction (RGC), to improve geometric alignment before Gaussian attribute reconstruction. Specifically, FGPR refines camera poses with flow-based correlation features and differentiable bundle adjustment. RGC then corrects initial point maps using depth residuals derived from FGPR, producing more reliable Gaussian centers. To stabilize training, we introduce a dual-path strategy that mixes ground-truth and predicted poses for canonical transformation. It provides clean supervision for Gaussian attributes while improving geometry robustness to residual pose errors. Extensive experiments on DL3DV and RealEstate10K show that SG-Splat achieves state-of-the-art performance in both novel view synthesis and pose estimation.

</details>


### [145] [Shared-Factor-Conditioned Diffusion for Low-Rank Tensor Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35368)
*Zhengyun Cheng,Guanwen Zhang,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DiffTR：一种无监督张量恢复框架，通过坐标条件扩散模型统一张量补全、去噪和高光谱超分辨率，在多种张量模态和恢复任务中获得有竞争力的重建质量与统一建模灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量恢复涉及不同数据结构与观测过程，如表格、图像、视频和高光谱数据，且任务包括补全、去噪和超分辨率。现有方法往往依赖特定任务或观测模型，缺乏一种能够跨模态、跨任务适应的统一表示学习框架。

Method: DiffTR采用无监督坐标条件扩散建模：以张量坐标为条件，由共享因子索引并引导紧凑score网络；通过score matching从可用观测中联合学习共享因子和隐式张量表示。标量与向量输出可统一处理单个元素和完整tube。框架将表示学习与任务特定观测建模分离：反向扩散用于缺失值恢复；Tweedie公式、软阈值和可选空间一致性用于混合噪声去噪；测量引导采样将低分辨率高光谱先验迁移到高分辨率重建，并通过数据一致性与光谱子空间正则进行后采样优化。

Result: 在多种张量模态和恢复任务上的实验表明，DiffTR具有有竞争力的重建质量，并体现统一条件生成表示在不同损坏模型和任务约束下的灵活性与适应性。

Conclusion: DiffTR通过坐标条件扩散和共享因子表示，将张量补全、去噪和高光谱超分辨率统一到同一框架中，实现了表示学习与任务特定观测建模的解耦，为多模态、多任务张量恢复提供了通用生成式解决方案。

Abstract: Tensor recovery spans heterogeneous data structures and observation processes, calling for a representation that can adapt across modalities and tasks. We propose DiffTR, an unsupervised framework that unifies tensor completion, denoising, and hyperspectral super-resolution through coordinate-conditioned diffusion. Shared factors indexed by tensor coordinates condition a compact score network, and score matching jointly learns the factors and an implicit tensor representation from the available observations. Scalar and vector-valued outputs accommodate individual entries and entire tubes, enabling a common formulation for tabular tensors, images, and videos. The framework separates representation learning from task-specific observation modeling: reverse diffusion recovers missing values; estimation via Tweedie's formula, soft thresholding, and optional spatial consistency support mixed-noise denoising; and measurement-guided sampling transfers a prior learned from low-resolution hyperspectral observations to high-resolution reconstruction, followed by post-sampling refinement with data consistency and spectral subspace regularization. This separation makes the representation compatible with different corruption models and permits task-specific constraints to be incorporated within the same recovery framework. Experiments across multiple tensor modalities and recovery tasks demonstrate competitive reconstruction quality and the flexibility of a unified conditional generative representation. The code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [146] [ACSAN: All-Convolutional Self-Attention Network Integrating Convolutional Operations with Vision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35375)
*Zimian Wei,Tianqi Zhang,Hengyue Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出全卷积自注意力网络（ACSAN），以多尺度扩张卷积替代ViT中的全连接矩阵乘法生成QKV，并融合空间自注意力与通道自注意力（DCSA模块），在CIFAR-10/100和ImageNet-1k上从头训练，以更少或相当参数量取得优于标准ViT的分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT虽在计算机视觉中表现优异，但因其包含大量可训练参数且对全局自注意力机制的全连接运算依赖较强，导致对大规模训练数据需求高、学习效率低，难以在资源受限场景中应用。

Method: 1. 设计核心模块——动态卷积自注意力（DCSA）：采用多尺度扩张卷积生成Query、Key、Value；分别定义空间自注意力和通道自注意力产生注意力特征，再将二者合并为DCSA输出。
2. 构建基本ACSAN块：以DCSA替代ViT中的标准自注意力操作，以卷积层替代多层感知机（MLP），从而将全连接矩阵乘法全部替换为卷积运算。
3. 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1k上从头训练ACSAN，并与标准ViT及其他SOTA方法进行对比；同时通过消融实验验证各组件的贡献。

Result: 实验表明，ACSAN在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet-1k上的分类精度优于参数量相当甚至更少的标准ViT及其他SOTA方法；消融实验证实各模块均对最终性能有正向贡献。

Conclusion: ACSAN通过全卷积架构有效降低了ViT对大规模训练数据和全连接运算的依赖，在保持优异分类性能的同时提升了参数效率与学习速度，为资源受限场景下的视觉Transformer应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In the past few years, Vision Transformer (ViT) has achieved excellent performance in the field of computer vision. However, there still some problems remain. For instance, ViTs heavily rely on large-scale training data since they simply contain a large number of trainable parameters, which slows down their learning efficiency and limits their applicability in resource-constrained scenarios. In this paper, we propose a novel deep learning model, namely All-Convolutional Self-Attention Network (ACSAN), which applies convolution operations to replace fully-connected matrices multiplications in the ViT framework. To implement ACSAN, we firstly design Dynamic Convolutional Self-Attention (DCSA) as the basic module, which employs multi-scale dilated convolutions to generate Query, Key, and Value. We defined spatial self-attention and channel self-attention to generate attention features respectively. The two attention features are finally merged as the output of the DCSA module. Based on DCSA, we then construct the basic ACSAN block. Comparing with ViT, we use DCSA to replace the regular self-attention operations, and use convolution layers to replace MLPs. To evaluate the performance of the proposed method, we train ACSAN on three widely-used image classification databases, i.e. CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-1k, from scratch. Experimental results show that ACSAN achieves excellent classification accuracy comparing with standard ViT with comparable or fewer parameters, as well as other state-of-the-art methods. Ablation studies verify that each component contributes positively to the final performance.

</details>


### [147] [Unveil the Mask: Jointly-Optimized Diffusion Masked Pretraining for Appearance-Agnostic Operator Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35414)
*Katsuya Ogata,Ryoya Yoshiyama,Kawachi Hodaka,Yuta Nakashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需外部预训练模型的动作/身份验证框架，利用面部关键点序列和视频帧学习运动表征，识别驱动虚拟人视频的操作者，并在WBA2026基准上达到0.769的AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 照片级真实感的虚拟人视频使被渲染面部外观与实际操作者解耦，传统人脸识别难以验证操作者身份；验证需要依赖对面部运动方式的表征，同时降低对虚拟人外观身份的敏感性。

Method: 框架融合两种互补模态。对关键点模态，使用分组关键点的扩散掩码预训练学习运动表征，并用预测运动强度、眼睛开合等行为线索的轻量辅助任务引导，再通过对比线性探针生成操作者嵌入；输入随机变换用于模拟不同虚拟人生成器可能改变操作者运动的方式，从而提升对未见生成器的泛化。对视频模态，通过相邻帧差异捕捉细粒度面部运动。最终将两种模态的嵌入进行融合。

Result: 在WBA2026基准上取得整体0.769的AUC。线性探针分析表明，视频嵌入几乎不保留虚拟人身份信息，关键点嵌入仍保留部分身份信息；融合两种模态后验证性能提升，尤其在说话和倾听两种交互模式中更明显。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，面向虚拟人操作者验证时，应重点利用与外观解耦的面部运动表征；结合关键点运动建模与视频帧间细粒度运动差异，可在不依赖外部预训练模型的条件下实现更鲁棒的操作者身份验证。

Abstract: Photorealistic avatar videos decouple the appearance of a rendered face from the person who operates it, which makes conventional face recognition unsuitable for identifying the operator. Verifying the operator therefore requires representations of how a face moves that are insensitive to what the avatar looks like. We propose a verification framework built without any external pretrained model. It uses two complementary modalities provided for each sample: facial landmark sequences and video frames. From the landmark modality, it learns motion representations by diffusion masked pretraining over grouped landmarks, guided by lightweight auxiliary tasks that predict behavioral cues such as motion intensity and eye openness, and turns them into operator embeddings with a contrastive linear probe. To generalize to unseen avatar generators, the pretraining input is randomly transformed to simulate how a generator may alter the operator's motion. From the video modality, it captures fine facial motion through differences between consecutive frames. The embeddings of the two modalities are combined into one. Our approach reaches an overall AUC of 0.769 on the WBA2026 benchmark. Linear probing shows that the video embedding retains little information about the avatar's identity, whereas the landmark embedding retains some, and fusing the two modalities improves verification in particular across speaking and listening modes.

</details>


### [148] [TP-JSCC: Efficient Token Pruning and Joint Source-Channel Coding for LVLMs-based 3D-VQA in Cloud-Edge Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35489)
*Zhengxin Li,Chongzhen Tian,Hui Yuan,Hao Liu,Sam Kwong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 TP-JSCC，面向云边协同中的 LVLM-based 3D-VQA 任务，通过视觉 token 剪枝与联合信源信道编码降低传输开销并提升信道鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限的具身智能体需要借助云端大视觉语言模型完成 3D 视觉问答，但向云端卸载大规模视觉数据会带来高延迟和噪声信道下的传输失真，亟需在语义保留与高效鲁棒传输之间取得平衡。

Method: 首先设计局部与全局语义混合 token 剪枝模块 LGS-HTP，通过解耦前景/背景 token 并联合选择局部与全局语义来压缩视觉 token；随后提出轻量 token 联合信源信道编码模块 T-JSCC，采用级联轻量线性层和信噪比自适应机制，在压缩 token 的同时适配波动信道条件并降低计算开销。

Result: 在 ScanQA 和 SQA3D 数据集上的实验表明该方法具有高效性和鲁棒性：LGS-HTP 在 ScanQA 验证集上可实现 90% 的 token 剪枝率，同时 CIDEr 损失小于 1%；在此基础上，T-JSCC 进一步将 token 压缩 18 倍，使总体数据量下降 180 倍，并节省 90% 的 LVLM 部署计算复杂度。

Conclusion: TP-JSCC 通过语义保留型 token 剪枝与轻量联合信源信道编码，有效缓解云边协同 3D-VQA 中的传输延迟与失真问题，为资源受限具身智能体部署 LVLM 提供了高效且鲁棒的压缩传输方案。

Abstract: Deploying large vision-language models (LVLMs) on resource-constrained embodied agents for 3D visual question answering necessitates cloud-edge collaboration. However, offloading massive visual data to the cloud over bandwidth-limited and noisy channels introduces severe latency and transmission distortion. To address this, we propose TP-JSCC, an efficient token pruning and joint source-channel coding method for LVLMs-based 3D-VQA in Cloud-Edge Collaboration. First, we develop a Local and Global Semantics-based Hybrid Token Pruning (LGS-HTP) module to significantly reduce the visual token count. By decoupling foreground-background tokens and jointly selecting local and global semantics, LGS-HTP effectively reduces redundancy while preserving reasoning-critical tokens. Second, we introduce a lightweight Token Joint Source-Channel Coding (T-JSCC) module to further compress the pruned tokens and ensure their robust transmission over error-prone channels. By employing lightweight cascaded linear layers and Signal-to-Noise Ratio (SNR)-adaptive mechanism, this module adapts to fluctuating channel conditions while maintaining minimal computational overhead. Extensive experiments on ScanQA and SQA3D datasets indicate the exceptional efficiency and robustness of our approach. Specifically, on ScanQA validation set, the LGS-HTP module could achieve 90% pruning ratio while incurring less than a 1% CIDEr loss. Building on this, the T-JSCC further compresses the tokens by 18 times, resulting in an overall 180 times data volume reduction for robust transmission, while saving 90% in computational complexity for LVLM deployment. Our code is available at this https URL.

</details>


### [149] [S$^2$-RL: Sample-Set Dual Reinforcement Learning for Generative Semantic Segmentation Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35491)
*Haoyu Wang,Fei Zhou,Qingqing Qiu,Lei Zhang,Wei Wei,Chen Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S²-RL，将语义分割数据集蒸馏建模为基于扩散模型的文本生成图像过程，并通过GRPO和样本—样本集双重奖励优化，无需像素级梯度展开即可生成紧凑、多样且富含语义信息的合成分割数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义分割数据集蒸馏存在两个瓶颈：一是双层优化方法需逐像素梯度展开，计算成本高昂；二是代理式生成方法仅在真实固定语义掩码上合成纹理，难以将丰富语义知识压缩为具有新颖语义分布的生成样本。

Method: 提出S²-RL框架，将生成式语义分割数据集蒸馏转化为扩散模型主导的文本到图像生成范式，通过简洁文本提示引导生成信息丰富且语义分布新颖的图像。进一步采用GRPO微调扩散策略，设计样本—样本集双重奖励：单个样本层面鼓励与文本提示语义对齐并提升类内特征多样性；样本集层面通过上下文多臂赌博机最大化跨样本语义分布多样性。

Result: 在ADE20K和COCO数据集上取得显著且一致的性能提升，优于现有最先进基线，建立了语义分割数据集蒸馏的新基准。

Conclusion: S²-RL能够以较低计算开销将大规模语义分割数据集蒸馏为少量生成样本，同时保留多样且有价值的语义知识，为生成式密集预测任务数据集蒸馏提供了有效范式。

Abstract: While dataset distillation has witnessed significant advances in image classification, its extension to dense prediction tasks such as semantic segmentation is plagued by two core bottlenecks: bi-level optimization-based approaches suffer from prohibitive computational costs stemming from pixel-wise gradient unrolling, whereas proxy-based generative methods merely infuse visual textures into fixed semantic masks derived from real data, thus failing to condense rich semantic knowledge into a compact set of informative, novel scene generations. To address both bottlenecks simultaneously, we propose a novel Sample-Set Reinforcement Learning (S$^2$-RL) framework for generative semantic segmentation dataset distillation (SSDD). Specifically, S$^2$-RL formulates SSDD as a diffusion model based text-to-image generation paradigm, enabling flexible generation of images with informative, novel semantic distributions via a concise text prompt. Furthermore, we fine-tune a diffusion policy via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), leveraging a sample-set dual reward paradigm: for individual samples, it enforces semantic alignment with input text prompts and intra-class feature diversity; for sample sets, it maximizes inter-sample semantic distribution diversity by solving a contextual multi-armed bandit problem. These design choices enable S$^2$-RL to distill large-scale semantic segmentation datasets into a compact set of generated samples that encapsulate diverse, informative semantic knowledge without the need of pixel-wise gradient unrolling. Extensive experiments on the ADE20K and COCO datasets demonstrate that S$^2$-RL achieves substantial and consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines, establishing a new benchmark for SSDD.

</details>


### [150] [Learning Scene Coordinate Reconstruction from Unposed Images via Pose Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35501)
*Tze Ho Elden Tse,Jizong Peng,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种混合式学习结构恢复运动框架，将位姿图优化融入ACE-Zero，并利用几何先验进行不确定性感知优化，以提升无监督相机位姿估计的全局一致性、鲁棒性和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的结构恢复运动方法（如ACE-Zero）虽能在无真值监督下从无序图像集合估计相机位姿和场景坐标，但缺乏全局与多视角一致性约束，在复杂或歧义场景中易出现位姿漂移和未对齐问题。因此需要引入全局几何推理来修正局部学习输出。

Method: 提出将位姿图优化与ACE-Zero结合的混合框架。首先直接从ACE-Zero输出构建位姿图，并通过预测的场景坐标提取相对位姿约束；其次引入不确定性感知优化，基于几何先验估计置信度，包括跨视角的极线一致性和光流一致性，用于筛选或加权约束，抑制错误优化。

Result: 所提方法提高了位姿估计的鲁棒性和准确性，表明全局几何推理能够有效补充基于学习的推理过程，缓解ACE-Zero在多视图一致性不足导致的位姿漂移和未对齐问题。

Conclusion: 在基于学习的结构恢复运动任务中，引入位姿图优化和几何先验驱动的不确定性建模，可增强相机位姿估计的全局一致性与可靠性，为无监督SfM提供学习先验与几何约束协同的有效路径。

Abstract: Learning-based structure-from-motion methods such as ACE-Zero have demonstrated strong performance in estimating cameraposes and scene coordinates from unordered image collections without requiring ground truth supervision. However, the lack of global and multi-view consistency constraints in ACE-Zero can lead to pose drift and misalignment, particularly in complex or ambiguous scenes. In this work, we propose a hybrid framework that integrates pose graph optimization (PGO) into ACE-Zero to refine camera poses and suppress incorrect refinements. We construct pose graphs directly from ACE-Zero outputs by extracting relative pose constraints from predicted scene coordinates. Furthermore, we introduce an uncertainty-aware optimization strategy by estimating confidence scores using geometric priors, including epipolar and optical flow consistencies across views. Our approach improves the robustness and accuracy of pose estimation, demonstrating that global geometric reasoning can effectively complement learning-based inference in structure-from-motion.

</details>


### [151] [HiFiPix: Bringing High-Fidelity Real-World Super-Resolution Back to Pixel Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35515)
*Wenjie Li,Du Chen,Hui Wang,Linlong Fan,Youwei Lv,Zeyi Chen,Zehan Chen,Jinwei Chen,Heng Guo,Zhanyu Ma,Qingnan Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiFiPix 是一种面向真实世界图像超分辨率（Real-ISR）的像素空间扩散框架，通过波频判别、混合对比自蒸馏和选择性 LoRA 剪枝，抑制块边界伪影并提升生成能力，实现更高重建保真度与感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 VAE 潜空间的扩散超分辨率方法容易因潜变量压缩造成信息损失，限制重建保真度；像素空间扩散虽能避免潜空间压缩，但在 Real-ISR 中面临 patch 边界伪影和生成能力不足的问题。

Method: 提出 HiFiPix：1）Cross-Scale Wavelet Discriminator（CWSD）在图像级别惩罚不真实的高频周期成分，缓解块边界伪影；2）Mixup Contrastive Self-Distillation（MixCSD）对中间表示进行正则化，提升一致性；3）发现 LoRA 层存在生成—保真度权衡异质性，提出 Selective LoRA Pruning（LoRA-SP）自适应剪枝 LoRA 层，以增强生成能力并最小化重建质量损失。

Result: 实验表明，HiFiPix 在恢复准确性和感知质量上均大幅优于现有潜空间扩散超分辨率方法，并能实现超高分辨率重建和更细粒度的视觉效果生成。代码与模型将公开。

Conclusion: HiFiPix 验证了像素空间扩散模型在真实世界图像超分辨率中的潜力：通过图像级频域约束、中间表示正则化与 LoRA 选择性剪枝，可有效克服块边界伪影和生成能力不足，在保真度与感知质量之间取得更好平衡。

Abstract: Diffusion-based real-world image super-resolution suffers from limited reconstruction fidelity due to information loss in VAE-based latent representations. Recently, pixel-space diffusion models offer a promising high-faithfulness alternative by avoiding latent compression, but adapting them to Real-ISR remains challenging due to inherent patch boundary artifacts and degraded generative capability. To address the issues and maximize the potential of pretrained pixel-space diffusion models, we propose HiFiPix, a pixel-space Real-ISR framework capable of ultra high-fidelity reconstruction and fine-grained visual generation effects. To suppress patch boundary artifacts, we penalize unrealistic high-frequency periodic components with a Cross-Scale Wavelet Discriminator (CWSD) for image-level supervision, and Mixup Contrastive Self-Distillation (MixCSD) for intermediate representation regularization. Furthermore, we uncover a heterogeneous generation-fidelity trade-off across low-rank adapters (LoRA) and propose Selective LoRA Pruning (LoRA-SP) to adaptively prune LoRA layers, enhancing generative capability with minimal deterioration of reconstruction fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HiFiPix consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art latent space methods in terms of both restoration accuracy and perceptual quality by a large margin. Code and models will be released.

</details>


### [152] [Learning High-Quality Pseudo-Labels for Point-Supervised Temporal Action Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35516)
*Zhenying Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对点监督时序动作定位（PTAL）中伪标签初始化选择偏倚、训练过程中伪标签利用不充分的问题，提出结合先验引导初始化与共识引导精化的框架，通过整合提案置信度、点级先验和候选边界证据提升伪标签质量与检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于伪标签的 PTAL 方法依赖置信度进行初始化，容易忽略边界定位良好但置信度不高的提案；同时，伪标签在检测器训练期间要么固定不变，要么更新时未充分利用原始提案池中其他边界候选提供的补充证据，导致监督信号质量不足，进而限制检测性能。因此，需要在学习高质量伪标签的同时，更充分地利用原始候选边界证据。

Method: 提出一个整合先验引导初始化与共识引导精化的 PTAL 框架，包含两个核心模块。首先，先验引导初始化（PGI）将提案置信度与点级标注推导出的先验相结合，为实例级可靠性建模，从而完成初始伪标签选择和候选提案保留，避免仅依赖置信度造成的定位良好提案被误丢弃。其次，共识引导精化（CGR）在检测器训练过程中持续利用保留下来的原始候选提案，构建边界目标，并将这些候选之间的共识与检测器不断演化的预测结果相结合，逐步优化伪标签，使伪标签随训练过程自适应更新。整体而言，该框架通过初始化阶段引入实例特定先验、训练阶段引入候选边界共识，提升伪标签质量和监督有效性。

Result: 在四个 PTAL 基准数据集上取得具有竞争力的检测性能。具体而言，方法在 THUMOS14 上达到 tIoU 0.1 到 0.7 范围内的平均 mAP 68.5%，较 PseR 提升 3.7 个百分点，验证了先验引导初始化和共识引导精化在提升伪标签质量及检测性能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过融合点级先验与原始提案池中的边界共识证据，所提出的 PGI 和 CGR 框架能够缓解置信度初始化导致的定位良好提案被忽略问题，并在训练过程中持续修正伪标签，从而提升点监督时序动作定位的性能。实验结果表明该方法在多个基准上具有竞争力，并在 THUMOS14 上显著优于代表性方法 PseR。

Abstract: Point-supervised temporal action localization (PTAL) aims to recognize and localize action instances in untrimmed videos using only point-level annotations. Existing pseudo-label-based methods have made substantial progress in PTAL. However, confidence-based initialization can overlook well-localized proposals. Meanwhile, existing methods either keep pseudo-labels fixed during detector training or update them without fully exploiting alternative boundary evidence from the original proposal pool. These limitations constrain supervision quality, thereby hindering detection performance. To learn high-quality pseudo-labels, we propose a framework that integrates prior-guided initialization with consensus-guided refinement during detector training. Our framework comprises two modules: Prior-Guided Initialization (PGI) and Consensus-Guided Refinement (CGR). PGI combines proposal confidence with point-derived priors to establish instance-specific reliability for initial pseudo-label selection and candidate retention. CGR continually uses the retained original candidates to construct boundary targets, combining their consensus with evolving detector predictions to refine pseudo-labels throughout training. Experiments on four benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves competitive detection performance compared with existing methods. In particular, our method achieves 68.5\% average mAP over tIoU thresholds 0.1--0.7 on THUMOS14, surpassing PseR by 3.7 percentage points. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [153] [Generative Structure from Motion with Native 3D Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35522)
*Haobin Duan,Binbin Huang,Mochu Xiang,Yiqun Zhao,Zibo Zhao,Yi Ma,Shenghua Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GenSfM，将图像化 3D 重建与原生 3D 扩散生成统一在一个联合去噪框架中，从少量未标定视角同时恢复物体几何、纹理和相机位姿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 SfM 只能恢复与输入图像一致的可见区域，难以补全未见区域；原生 3D 生成能产出合理物体但不一定忠实于输入；简单串联重建与生成会导致误差跨阶段级联。因此需要在同一框架中同时利用观测约束和物体先验。

Method: GenSfM 使用多模态扩散 Transformer，在共享自注意力机制下联合去噪形状潜变量和逐视角位姿潜变量，使生成几何被观测视角约束并利用先验推断未观测区域；同时采用紧凑的 3D-native UV-volume tokenization 控制随视角增加而膨胀的序列长度，并通过位姿感知阶段在恢复的相机位姿下采样逐体素图像特征，进一步优化几何与纹理。

Result: 在 Toys4k 和 GSO 数据集上，针对 4 个未标定视角，相比强原生 3D 生成基线，新视角渲染 PSNR 提升 3.2–4.5 dB，中位 Chamfer 距离降低 2.8–3.8 倍；在不使用外部位姿估计主干、事后优化或已知内参的情况下，输入视角位姿恢复达到 96% 以上 Acc@5°。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将重建与生成在扩散过程中耦合建模，能够更好平衡对输入图像的忠实性与对未观测区域的合理补全，为少视角、未标定图像下的物体 3D 结构和相机运动联合恢复提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recovering an object's 3D structure and camera motion from a few uncalibrated views is a fundamental problem in computer vision. Classical structure from motion methods recover 3D geometry consistent with the input images but cannot recover unseen regions; native generation methods produce plausible 3D objects which are related but not necessarily faithful to the input images. Methods simply concatenating reconstruction and generation allow error to cascade across the two stages, instead of producing a 3D object consistent with both the distribution and the input. We propose GenSfM, which integrates image-based 3D reconstruction with a native 3D diffusion model. In particular, we use a multi-modal diffusion transformer to jointly denoise the shape latent and per-view pose latents under shared self-attention, grounding generated geometry in the observed views while using object-level priors to infer unobserved regions. A compact 3D-native UV-volume tokenization keeps the joint sequence small as views are added, and a pose-aware stage refines geometry and texture by sampling per-voxel image features at the recovered camera poses. On Toys4k and GSO, for $4$ uncalibrated views, GenSfM improves novel-view PSNR by $3.2$-$4.5$ dB and reduces median Chamfer distance by $2.8$-$3.8\times$ over a strong native-3D generation baseline, while recovering input-view poses over $96\%$ Acc@$5^\circ$ without any external pose-estimation backbone, post-hoc optimization, or known intrinsics. Project page: this https URL.

</details>


### [154] [EARS: Evidence-Aware Retrieval via Sound and Motion for Budgeted Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35536)
*Shih-Yao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出EARS，一种训练无关、查询无关的视频帧采样前端，利用光流运动与音频能量排序帧，并结合时序非极大值抑制提升多样性，在低预算稀疏事件视频中优于均匀采样，但不适用于静音或隐蔽事件场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉语言模型只能观察少量帧时，均匀帧采样容易遗漏稀疏事件视频中的短暂关键证据。现有方法需要训练或查询信息，而低成本视频理解需要无需额外模型且与查询无关的证据感知帧选择方式。

Method: 作者提出EARS：首先利用光流运动信号和音频能量为视频帧计算证据得分；随后使用时序非极大值抑制进行去冗余，保证所选帧在时间分布上的多样性；最后仅将选中帧输入视觉语言模型进行后续定位或视频理解，不使用训练过程，也不依赖查询文本。

Result: 实验表明，单独的EARS证据得分达到帧级AP 0.39；当证据得分仅用于选择输入VLM的帧时，结合时序多样性的EARS达到定位AP 0.94，高于均匀采样的0.88。在更新后的匹配实验中，K=1时EARS在XD子集上F1为0.660，高于以中心均匀采样的0.614，但配对置信区间包含零。在以静音为主的UCF-Crime数据集上，EARS略低于均匀采样。

Conclusion: EARS适用于低预算视频理解场景中显著证据与运动或音频线索相关的情况，尤其是稀疏事件视频。对于密集叙事、隐蔽事件或音频运动线索较弱的场景，均匀采样或语义选择可能更优；仅依赖低成本模态统计无法可靠判断何时采用哪种帧选择策略。

Abstract: Uniform frame sampling can miss brief evidence in sparse-event videos when the VLM sees only K frames. We propose EARS (Evidence-Aware Retrieval via Sound and motion), a training-free, query-free front-end that ranks frames using optical-flow motion and audio energy, then applies temporal non-maximum suppression for diversity. The evidence score alone has frame-level AP 0.39; used only to select frames for the VLM, EARS with temporal diversity reaches localization AP 0.94, compared with 0.88 under Uniform sampling. In the updated matched study, at K = 1 EARS reaches XD-subset F1 0.660 versus 0.614 for centered Uniform (the paired interval includes zero); earlier backbone studies use separate protocols. On mostly-silent UCF-Crime, however, EARS slightly trails Uniform. A single configuration is used across both benchmarks; the new controls do not repeat the original held-out search. EARS therefore targets low-budget videos whose salient evidence aligns with motion or audio; on dense narratives or stealthy events, uniform or semantic selection remains preferable, and cheap modality statistics cannot reliably route between them. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [155] [Metric Scale for Insta360 Structure-from-Motion from the Camera's Embedded IMU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35554)
*Davide Cuttini,Diego Di Tommaso,Alessio Antoniacomi,Luca Simonetti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种利用消费级全景相机内置IMU进行尺度估计的方法，替代依赖手机ARKit/ARCore位姿的传统做法，并通过视觉-惯性对齐从SfM位姿中恢复米制尺度。实验表明，该方法在多种场景下具有更高或可比的精度，且更具自包含性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级360相机双鱼眼视频进行SfM重建时只能恢复相似变换意义下的几何结构，米制尺度通常需要借助手机位姿。然而，手机位姿依赖外部设备、同步和平台信息，不总是稳定或可用。因此需要一种不依赖外部参考、利用相机自身IMU获取米制尺度的方法。

Method: 使用Insta360 X5/X6的内置IMU并以1 kHz采样，先对传感器噪声密度、相机-IMU同步、加速度计尺度和零偏稳定性进行标定，并从数据中估计相机-IMU旋转和帧时序。随后在完整测区的SfM位姿上进行线性视觉-惯性对齐以恢复米制尺度。针对共享重力和加速度计零偏时出现约3.4%尺度高估的问题，改为按连续分段独立估计尺度并取平均。实验使用LiDAR、印刷标志物、公开基准轨迹和生产数据评估，并对窗口构造和单目米制深度网络进行消融与比较。

Result: 在9次配备LiDAR的测区中，惯性尺度相对于LiDAR的平均绝对误差为0.74%，优于手机尺度的1.94%；在印刷标志物上二者均在目标1%不确定度内一致。在公开基准30条轨迹上，将尺度归一化到1/40、1和40后，估计米制尺度的平均误差为-0.07%，标准差为1.28%。在50次仅有手机参考的生产测区中，惯性尺度与手机尺度标准差为2.6%，惯性尺度平均低0.9%；该差异不依赖窗口构造，随手机锚点范围增大而增大，且无法明确归因于某一参考。

Conclusion: 相机内置IMU可以作为消费级360相机SfM流水线中手机位姿的自包含替代方案，用于恢复米制尺度。所提出的分段线性视觉-惯性对齐能消除标准共享参数公式的尺度偏置，并在LiDAR和公开基准验证下达到优于0.74%的平均绝对误差或约1%级一致性。对于缺少外部真值的生产场景，惯性尺度与手机尺度存在系统性小偏差，需进一步研究其来源。

Abstract: Structure-from-motion (SfM) from the dual-fisheye video of a consumer 360-degree camera recovers geometry only up to a similarity transform; in the pipeline considered here, metric scale is obtained from smartphone ARKit or ARCore poses captured alongside the camera. This paper evaluates a self-contained alternative: the IMU embedded in the Insta360 X5 and X6, recorded at 1 kHz. The sensor is characterised (noise density, camera-IMU synchronisation, accelerometer scale factor, bias stability), the camera-IMU rotation and frame timing are estimated from the data, and the scale is recovered by a linear visual-inertial alignment over the SfM poses of a whole survey. The standard formulation, with gravity and accelerometer bias shared across the survey, overestimates the scale by 3.4% on average; estimating each contiguous segment independently and averaging removes the bias. On nine surveys with a co-mounted LiDAR scanner the inertial scale deviates from the LiDAR by 0.74% in mean absolute error, against 1.94% for the smartphone scale; on printed fiducial markers both agree within the 1% uncertainty of the target. On the reference trajectories of thirty sequences of a public benchmark, brought to scales of 1/40, 1 and 40, the estimator recovers the metric scale with a mean error of -0.07% and a standard deviation of 1.28%. On fifty production surveys where the smartphone is the only reference, the two scales agree to a standard deviation of 2.6%, the inertial scale being 0.9% lower on average; the difference does not depend on window construction, grows with the extent of the smartphone anchor, and cannot be attributed to either reference. An ablation over window construction and a comparison with monocular metric-depth networks complete the study.

</details>


### [156] [Copying As Defense: Protecting Images from Editing By Eliciting Model Copying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35566)
*Salma Abdel Magid,Sanghyuk Chun,Eric Slyman,Kushal Kafle,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Copying-as-Defense（CaD）：通过在扩散 Transformer 图像编辑器的早期注意力层中强化目标 token 对受保护参考图像的注意力，迫使模型倾向复制输入而非执行编辑，从而低成本地降低恶意图像篡改成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 DiT 的图像编辑模型（如 Flux Kontext）可将一张照片转成逼真但有害的非自愿编辑图像。现有主动防护通常给图像加入人眼不可见扰动，并试图降低模型对参考图像的注意力，但这并不能真正阻止编辑；模型仍会依据文本指令和自身生成内容生成一张相关性较弱的新图像。因此需要更有效的预防性防御思路。

Method: 论文通过受控注意力干预发现，只需在少数早期 transformer block 中让目标 token 的注意力集中到参考图像上，就足以抑制编辑行为，使模型倾向于复制输入图像（常表现为有损复制），而不是完成所要求的变换。基于此，提出 CaD：最大化每个 token 在早期层中从目标端到参考图像的注意力质量。由于注意力权重经过归一化，提高参考图像注意力会同时压低模型对文本指令和生成画布的注意力。同时，将优化目标限制在早期 block 可截断反向传播路径，从而降低优化开销。

Result: 在多种图像编辑器和基准上，CaD 将未保护图像上的平均编辑成功率从 81.4% 降低到 24.0%，相对降低 70.6%；相比最强基线 DeContext 的 43.0% 成功率，进一步相对降低 44.3%。此外，每个优化步骤的速度约为同类方法 1.8–2.0 倍。

Conclusion: 与试图让模型忽略参考图像的防御方式相反，鼓励模型“复制参考图像”是一种更有效且高效的主动防御策略。CaD 通过早期注意力干预削弱文本驱动编辑能力，可显著降低 DiT 图像编辑器带来的恶意篡改风险，同时保持较低优化成本；其局限在于模型可能退化为对输入的有损复制，因此实际部署仍需权衡视觉保真度与不可见扰动的有效性。

Abstract: Image editors built on diffusion transformers (DiTs), such as Flux Kontext, make it possible to turn a single photograph into a convincing manipulated image, including harmful and non-consensual edits. Proactive defenses aim to prevent such manipulations by adding an imperceptible perturbation before an image is shared. Existing defenses for DiT-based in-context editors make the output attend less to the protected reference image. But this leaves the model free to follow the edit instruction using the text and its own generated tokens, often producing a new, weakly grounded image rather than preventing the edit. We find that the opposite strategy is more effective: instead of leading the model to ignore the reference, we encourage it to copy it. Through controlled attention interventions, we show that concentrating target-token attention on the reference image in only a few early blocks is sufficient to collapse the editing behavior, causing the model to reproduce its input, often as a lossy copy, rather than execute the requested transformation. Based on this insight, we introduce Copying-as-Defense (CaD), which maximizes per-token target-to-reference attention mass in early transformer blocks. Since attention rows are normalized, increasing reference attention suppresses the attention allocated to the text and generated canvas. Restricting the objective to early blocks also lets us truncate the backpropagation path, keeping optimization cheap. Across editors and benchmarks, CaD reduces average edit success from 81.4% on unprotected images to 24.0%, a 70.6% relative reduction, and by a further 44.3% relative to DeContext, the strongest baseline (43.0%). CaD is also roughly 1.8-2.0x faster per optimization step than comparable methods.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Distribution Matching: Self-Supervised Representation Forcing for Few-Step Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35594)
*Junyi Chen,Haiyu Zhang,Zhoujie Fu,Tengfei Wang,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Self Flow Forcing（SF²），在 Distribution Matching Distillation（DMD）框架中引入内部表示预测损失，使低步数蒸馏的视频扩散模型在保持输出空间保真度的同时，提升空间结构和时序运动质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DMD 方法主要依赖输出空间目标，缺少对模型内部表示的显式正则化。随着蒸馏步数进一步减少，仅靠输出监督不足以保证学生模型学习精细空间结构与连贯时序动态，导致视频生成出现闪烁、结构退化和运动不一致。

Method: SF² 在不改变 DMD 输出目标的基础上，加入自监督表示对齐损失。具体地，给学生模型和指数移动平均教师模型施加不同噪声水平，形成信息不对称，迫使学生从更嘈杂的输入中预测教师的内部表示，从而将表示层面的监督整合进分布匹配蒸馏过程。该方法不依赖外部特征提取器，并兼容双向和因果模型架构。

Result: 实验表明，SF² 能有效缓解视频蒸馏模型中的时序闪烁问题，并持续提升空间保真度与运动质量。该机制具有架构无关性，可适配不同学生模型，同时计算开销较小。

Conclusion: SF² 通过引入内部表示预测约束，弥补了 DMD 仅监督输出空间的不足，为视频扩散模型的低步数蒸馏提供了更稳定、可扩展且低开销的改进方案。

Abstract: Few-step distillation is essential for deploying modern video diffusion models. Among existing approaches, Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) has emerged as a highly promising paradigm by achieving strong output-space fidelity without adversarial training, while remaining flexible across various student architectures. We observe, however, that DMD fundamentally relies on an output-space objective, lacking explicit regularization on the model's internal representations. As the distillation step count aggressively shrinks, this output-only supervision becomes insufficient, causing the distilled student to degrade in both fine-grained spatial structures and coherent temporal dynamics. To address this limitation, we introduce Self Flow Forcing (SF$^2$), which incorporates a predictive representation loss into the DMD framework without altering its output objective. By providing the student and an Exponential Moving Average (EMA) teacher with heterogeneous noise levels, we establish an information asymmetry. This formulation forces the student to predict the teacher's internal representations from a more corrupted input, effectively integrating self-supervised representation alignment into distribution matching. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SF$^2$ effectively mitigates temporal flickering and consistently improves both spatial fidelity and motion quality. Furthermore, the proposed mechanism is architecture-agnostic - seamlessly supporting both bidirectional and causal models - and requires no external feature extractors, introducing minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [158] [Synthetic Deformable Tubular Benchmark: A mechanically annotated dataset for evaluating deformable 3D reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35621)
*Íñigo Cirauqui-Viloria*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出并发布了基于LumenMorph生成的可变形管状场景合成基准数据集，用于系统评估可变形三维重建方法在相机运动、表面形变、材质对应和图像外观之间的综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形三维重建评估需要可靠的ground truth，以关联相机运动、表面形变、材质对应关系和图像外观。真实场景难以同时提供精确的运动、几何和物理标注，因此需要一个受控、可复现且接口明确的合成基准来区分重建覆盖度、几何精度和机械先验的有效性。

Method: 作者使用开源工具包LumenMorph生成Synthetic Deformable Tubular Benchmark，包含5,580张RGB图像，覆盖三种表面运动条件、移动与静止相机、六个场景家族，并提供匹配的表观变体。基准开放了相机位姿、几何、深度、形变场、持久材质对应关系和有限元记录等标注，并通过持久三角形标识和材质权重支持跨帧追踪同一表面位置及其三维轨迹。另设几何和机械一致性检查，并用SfM、光流和给定几何下的位移插值实验验证评估能力。

Result: 该资源能够区分配准覆盖度与几何精度，暴露相机运动和表面运动对重建的影响，并检验机械先验的收益及模型失配问题。公开的数据读取接口、评估协议和可配置生成器支持复用与扩展，同时数据完整性、固定环境重复性和保留预测结果的可重放评分具有明确验证范围。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个受控、可追溯、可扩展的合成参考基准，适用于可变形管状表面重建评估。它强调算法验证而非解剖或临床真实性，为研究相机运动、形变恢复、材质对应和有限元先验提供了标准化测试环境。

Abstract: Evaluating deformable 3D reconstruction requires ground truth linking camera motion, surface deformation, material correspondence and image appearance. We introduce the Synthetic Deformable Tubular Benchmark, generated with LumenMorph, an open-source toolkit for creating, annotating and evaluating tubular scenes. The release contains 5,580 RGB images. Its baseline crosses three surface-motion conditions with moving and stationary cameras in six scene families; matched appearance variants cover only moving-camera finite-element actions in four families. Documented public and evaluation interfaces expose camera poses, geometry, depth, deformation fields, persistent material correspondences and discrete finite-element records. Persistent triangle identities and material weights let users track the same surface location across frames and query its three-dimensional trajectory. Separately implemented geometric and mechanical checks assess annotation consistency with the generating models. Structure-from-motion, optical-flow and supplied-geometry displacement-interpolation studies demonstrate how the resource distinguishes registration coverage from geometric accuracy, exposes camera- and surface-motion effects, and tests the benefits and mismatch of mechanical priors. Public readers, evaluation contracts and the configurable generator support reuse and extension. Data integrity, fixed-environment repeatability and retained-prediction score replay have distinct verification scopes. The benchmark provides a controlled synthetic reference without claiming anatomical or clinical realism.

</details>


### [159] [SeeRF: Learning to See Beyond Fields of View via Generative Image Registration and Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35637)
*Xunpeng Yi,Yibing Zhang,Qinglong Yan,Yifan Lu,Han Xu,Jiayi Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 SeeRF，一种面向多模态图像配准与融合的生成式框架，通过跨模态光流估计、有效视场掩码和扩散生成补全，突破共享视场限制，实现更完整的场景级融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态传感器在成像视角和视场上常存在不一致，导致传统融合方法只能在共享视场内工作，难以完成完整场景级信息整合，融合结果容易出现模态特有区域和融合伪影，影响场景结构与语义信息的全面表示。

Method: 提出生成式配准-融合框架 SeeRF：首先基于微分同胚稳态速度场（diffeomorphic stationary velocity field）构造形变生成策略，并结合视觉基础模型引导的特征提取与由粗到细的渐进式多模态图像配准模块，实现准确的跨模态流估计并生成有效视场掩码；随后将配准模块与生成扩散模块耦合，通过跨模态控制编码模块和控制残差特征编解码机制引导多模态补全与融合。

Result: 在多个公开数据集和真实场景实验中，SeeRF 相比现有方法表现出更优性能，验证了其在跨模态配准、非重叠视场补全和自然融合方面的有效性、优势与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SeeRF 利用扩散模型的先验建模能力，克服了多模态融合中共享视场的限制，能够补全非重叠区域并生成更完整、自然的融合图像，为多模态图像融合提供了新的生成式配准框架。

Abstract: Due to the inherent discrepancies in imaging viewpoints and field-of-view mismatches among multi-modal sensors, existing fusion methods have long struggled to achieve complete scene-level information integration beyond the limitation in sensor fields of view. Consequently, the generated fusion results often contain modality-specific regions and fusion artifacts, limiting the comprehensive representation of scene structures and semantic information. To address this challenge, we propose a generative registration-fusion framework, termed SeeRF. Specifically, it first introduces a deformation generation strategy based on the diffeomorphic stationary velocity field, combined with visual foundation model-guided feature extraction and a coarse-to-fine progressive multi-modal image registration module, to achieve accurate cross-modal flow estimation and generate valid field-of-view masks. Subsequently, the registration module is tightly coupled with the generative diffusion module for multi-modal completion and fusion, where the cross-modal control encoding module and the control residual feature encoder-decoder mechanism provide effective guidance. By leveraging the powerful prior modeling capability of generative diffusion models, the proposed framework overcomes the limitations imposed by shared fields of view and enables effective completion and natural fusion of non-overlapping field-of-view regions. Extensive experiments on multiple public datasets and real-world scenarios demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior performance, validating its effectiveness, superiority, and robustness. The code will be available at this https URL.

</details>


### [160] [Printed and Scanned: Exposing the Fragility of Single-Image Morphing Attack Detectors under Print-Scan Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35682)
*Diogo J. Paulo,Hugo Proença,João C. Neves*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究针对人脸融合攻击检测在数字域与真实证件签发流程之间的性能差距，构建了SYN-MAD22的打印-扫描版本，并系统评估了8种先进单图像融合攻击检测模型在打印扫描退化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像人脸融合攻击检测方法大多在数字域数据集上评测，但许多国家的护照签发流程要求申请人提供的照片先打印再扫描，这一过程引入半色调、重采样、色彩空间变化和传感器噪声，可能掩盖模型依赖的高频伪影，因此需要评估检测器在真实打印扫描条件下的可靠性。

Method: 作者对SYN-MAD22数据集中的真实人脸图像和融合攻击图像分别进行打印和重新扫描，在300 DPI和600 DPI下构建新的打印扫描基准数据集，并使用8种仅在数字数据上训练的最先进S-MAD模型进行测试；进一步用第二台扫描仪采集数据，以检验跨设备鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明，在数字域中表现接近最先进水平的检测模型在打印扫描版本上的平均等错误率上升超过三倍，且两台扫描仪上的退化趋势一致，说明打印扫描显著削弱了现有单图像融合攻击检测模型的性能。

Conclusion: 现有基于数字域基准的S-MAD模型与真实证件签发或自动化边控中的打印扫描场景之间存在明显且此前被低估的性能鸿沟，未来融合攻击检测研究需要将打印扫描退化纳入数据构建、评测和鲁棒性改进。

Abstract: Face morphing attacks, in which the biometric traits of two or more identities are blended into a single face image, pose a severe threat to identity document issuance and automated border control. Consequently, a large body of work has proposed Single-image Morphing Attack Detection (S-MAD) algorithms and benchmarked them on datasets such as SYN-MAD22. However, the majority of these benchmarks operate exclusively in the digital domain, which contrasts with the real-world passport issuance pipeline in some countries, which still requires an applicant-supplied photograph to be printed and subsequently re-scanned before it is embedded in the identity document or fed into an automated verification system. This print-scan step introduces halftoning, resampling, color-space, and sensor noise that are absent from digital pipelines, and may mask the high-frequency artifacts that S-MAD models rely on. In this work, we introduce a print-scan version of the SYN-MAD22 dataset, obtained by printing and re-scanning all bona fide and morphed images at 300 and 600 DPI, and we use it to evaluate eight state-of-the-art S-MAD models trained exclusively on digital data. We additionally repeat the acquisition with a second scanner to assess cross-device robustness. Our results show that detectors that achieve near state-of-the-art performance in the digital domain suffer a more than threefold increase in average equal error rate, with the same qualitative degradation observed across both scanning devices, exposing a critical, previously under-reported gap between digital-benchmark performance and robustness to print-scan degradation.

</details>


### [161] [OmniShowcase: Omnidirectional Subject-Consistent Video Generation from Multi-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35684)
*Junyeong Ahn,Geonwoo Kim,Kinam Kim,Gihwi Kim,Hoonjin Jung,Hyojin Jang,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniShowcase提出面向主体到视频（S2V）的多视角生成方法，通过自动合成的几何一致训练数据和基于表面法线图的条件建模，提升相机变化时主体身份与3D一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量参考图生成特定主体的视频时，如果相机揭示输入中不可见的表面，模型容易无法保持主体外观和几何一致。早期方法只依赖单张参考图，难以推断未观测表面；多参考图方法虽提供更多视角，却未显式连接不同视角下的同一3D结构，且现有数据不保证视角变化始终围绕同一主体。

Method: 该方法包含两个核心组件：一是全自动数据合成流水线，基于3D资产生成多样相机轨迹的视频，并使视角变化由主体几何驱动，可覆盖360度环绕等运动；二是几何条件化生成方式，从参考视角重建一组转盘式表面法线图，用作视频模型条件，但不要求与生成帧进行严格时间对齐。作者进一步构建Showcase-4K数据集和ShowcaseBench评测基准，包含十二类相机运动、88种轨迹配置，以及可用于评测的源网格和相机参数。

Result: 实验表明，OmniShowcase在主体身份保持和3D一致性方面达到先进效果，同时保持有竞争力的视频质量与运动能力，说明显式几何条件化和几何一致合成数据有助于多视角主体视频生成。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在主体到视频生成中引入可重建的3D几何线索，并构建视角变化与主体几何严格对应的训练数据，是提升跨视角身份一致性和3D一致性的有效路径，为少图主体视频生成提供了新的数据、条件表示和评测方案。

Abstract: Generating videos of a specific subject from a few reference images remains challenging when the camera reveals surfaces that are not visible in the input. Early subject-to-video (S2V) methods condition on a single reference image and must therefore infer the subject's unseen appearance and geometry as the viewpoint changes. To address this limitation, recent methods extend S2V to multiple reference views, providing more information about the subject from different viewpoints. However, they do not explicitly specify the 3D structure that connects these observations. In addition, existing training data do not guarantee that viewpoint changes preserve the same underlying 3D subject. We present OmniShowcase, built on two components: (i) a fully automated data synthesis pipeline that produces training videos along diverse camera trajectories, with viewpoint variation driven by underlying subject geometry, even spanning full 360-degree orbits; and (ii) a geometry-conditioning method that provides the video model with a turntable of surface-normal maps reconstructed from the reference views without requiring temporal alignment with the generated frames. We construct Showcase-4K, a corpus of 3D-asset-based videos covering twelve camera motions across 88 trajectory configurations with reconstruction-derived surface-normal conditioning, and ShowcaseBench, a held-out evaluation set with the source meshes and camera parameters used to render each video. Experiments show state-of-the-art subject identity and 3D consistency while maintaining competitive video quality and motion. Project page: this http URL

</details>


### [162] [MatAnyone-E: Sparse Memory Propagation for Efficient High-Resolution Video Matting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35724)
*Shuyun Wang,Shangchen Zhou,Peiqing Yang,Yuekun Dai,Xin Yu,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MatAnyone-E 是一种高效的高分辨率视频抠图方法，通过低分辨率先验引导高分辨率局部匹配和轻量解码器，在保持接近 MatAnyone 2 质量的同时显著降低推理时间与显存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率视频抠图需要在保留发丝、半透明边缘等细粒度信息的同时维持帧间一致性。现有基于记忆的抠图方法通过当前帧特征与早期帧信息匹配获得高质量结果，但随着分辨率升高，特征匹配和内存检索的计算成本急剧增加，难以满足高效推理需求。

Method: 论文提出 MatAnyone-E，作为 MatAnyone 2 的高效扩展，核心思想是把高分辨率计算集中在最需要的区域。首先采用低分辨率传播来识别不确定区域以及与之相关的记忆候选；然后仅对这些区域使用所选记忆进行高分辨率匹配，其他区域复用低分辨率匹配结果。该策略同时减少了需要细粒度匹配的位置数量和需要搜索的记忆量。此外，根据实测运行时瓶颈设计轻量解码器，并训练其复现原解码器表示，以降低生成高分辨率 matte 的成本。

Result: 实验表明，MatAnyone-E 在保持与 MatAnyone 2 相近抠图质量的同时，实现约 7.4 倍推理加速，并在 4K 分辨率下将峰值 GPU 显存降至 MatAnyone 2 的 7.6%。在单张 NVIDIA H200 上启用编译优化推理时，1080p 可达 73.07 FPS，4K 可达 28.62 FPS。

Conclusion: MatAnyone-E 证明了通过低分辨率不确定性引导、选择性高分辨率记忆匹配和轻量解码器设计，可以显著缓解高分辨率视频抠图的计算与显存瓶颈。该方法在质量、速度和资源开销之间取得良好平衡，适合高分辨率实时或近实时视频抠图应用。

Abstract: High-resolution video matting requires preserving fine details, such as hair strands and soft transparency, while maintaining consistency across frames. Memory-based methods achieve strong matting quality by matching current-frame features with information stored from earlier frames, but this process becomes increasingly costly as resolution grows. We present MatAnyone-E, an efficient extension of MatAnyone 2 that focuses computation where it is most needed. Our approach first performs low-resolution propagation to identify uncertain regions that require closer inspection and the memory candidates most relevant to them. It then performs high-resolution matching only for these regions using the selected memory, while reusing low-resolution matching results elsewhere. This strategy reduces both the number of locations requiring detailed matching and the amount of memory searched, while preserving the information needed to produce a complete matte. We further reduce the cost of generating high-resolution mattes with a lightweight decoder designed around measured runtime bottlenecks and trained to reproduce the original decoder's representations. Experiments show that MatAnyone-E maintains matting quality comparable to MatAnyone 2 while achieving an estimated 7.4x inference speedup and reducing peak GPU memory usage to 7.6% of the usage of MatAnyone 2 at 4K. With compilation-enabled inference on a single NVIDIA H200 GPU, it reaches 73.07 FPS at 1080p and 28.62 FPS at 4K.

</details>


### [163] [VidEdit3D: Flexible 3D Gaussian Editing with Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35756)
*Yanming Yang,Chenxi Song,Tong Zhao,Juntao Xu,Wenhao Rao,Yiwen Chen,Yiwei Wang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VidEdit3D，将基于文本的 3D 场景编辑重新表述为轨迹式视频编辑任务，利用预训练视频扩散模型（VDM）的时空先验提升多视角一致性与指令对齐能力，并通过区域选择式 3DGS 优化限制编辑范围，避免背景属性泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导 3D 编辑方法，尤其是基于 3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）的方法，仍然难以兼顾灵活编辑与多视角一致性：一方面，图像编辑方法常难以将编辑准确应用到非典型视角或结构变化较大的场景中；另一方面，许多方法在保持视角一致时会抑制必要的几何或外观修改。因此，需要一种能够同时满足语义一致、结构可编辑和局部精确更新的 3D 编辑框架。

Method: 论文提出 VidEdit3D，其核心思想是：静态 3D 场景中沿相机轨迹变化的多视角图像与视频中沿时间轴演化的帧具有相似性，因此将 3D 场景编辑转化为轨迹式视频编辑。该方法包含三个关键模块：1）Trajectory-Guided Rendering（TGR），通过相机轨迹选择信息量高的关键帧，并构造符合 VDM 输入要求的平滑视频片段；2）Flow-Aligned Video Editing（FAVE），一种免训练的视频编辑框架，引入 Editing Consistency Guidance（ECG），通过在共享潜在空间中对比源图像与编辑图像条件，并使用调度的源图像放大机制，强化对编辑参考的遵循同时保留原始场景上下文；3）Region-Selective 3DGS Refinement（RS-GS），仅对目标 primitive 的几何与外观进行更新，从而约束编辑区域并减少背景污染。
