Result: 实验表明，VidEdit3D 相比基线方法具有更强的指令对齐能力和更好的多视角一致性，能够在保留场景上下文的同时实现更灵活、更一致的 3D Gaussian Splatting 场景编辑。

Conclusion: VidEdit3D 通过把 3D 场景编辑重构为轨迹式视频编辑，有效利用了视频扩散模型的时空先验，缓解了多视角一致性与结构编辑之间的冲突；其关键帧引导、编辑一致性引导和区域选择式 3DGS 优化共同提升了编辑的可控性、鲁棒性与最终质量。

Abstract: Recent advancements in text-guided 3D editing, particularly with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), have shown promise but still lag behind the flexibility of 2D editing tools. Existing methods face a critical trade-off between preserving multi-view consistency and allowing structural modifications. Image-based editors can also struggle to apply the intended edit to objects viewed from unusual angles. To address these limitations, we introduce VidEdit3D, a framework that reformulates 3D scene editing as a trajectory-based video editing task. By drawing a parallel between camera movement in static 3D scenes and temporal dynamics in video, we leverage the robust spatiotemporal priors of pre-trained Video Diffusion Models (VDMs) to encourage semantic and structural coherence across views. Specifically, we propose Trajectory-Guided Rendering (TGR) to select informative keyframes and construct smooth video clips aligned with VDM input requirements. To propagate edits from an anchor view to these clips, we develop Flow-Aligned Video Editing (FAVE), a training-free framework incorporating an Editing Consistency Guidance (ECG) mechanism. ECG contrasts source and edited image conditions at a shared latent state, using scheduled source-image amplification to strengthen adherence to the edited reference while maintaining original scene context. Finally, we introduce Region-Selective 3DGS Refinement (RS-GS) to strictly confine geometric and appearance updates to target primitives, reducing background attribute leakage. Experiments demonstrate stronger instruction alignment and better multi-view consistency than the baselines.

</details>


### [164] [Does VGGT Know What It Does Not Know?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35806)
*Adityan Thirumani Srinivasan,David Nordström*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文发现：以 VGGT 为代表的前馈式 3D 重建模型虽不输出相机位姿不确定性，但其冻结的中间特征中已经编码了“位姿是否可能出错”的信息；用简单线性探针即可高精度预测失败，并可用于下游弃权与跨数据集/跨失败类型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈 3D 重建模型在单次前向中回归相机与几何，却不提供位姿不确定性，导致错误相对位姿在输出形式上与正确位姿难以区分。作者研究这些冻结模型是否在其内部表征中隐含了对自身位姿失败的“自我认知”，以便构建更可靠的 3D 感知与匹配系统。

Method: 作者构建 HardMatch 基准，包含 1,000 组刻意设计的困难图像对，其中 VGGT 在约 35% 图像对上失败。随后在 VGGT 的冻结特征上训练线性探针，预测相对位姿是否失败，并与 VGGT 自身点图置信度、基于 VGGT 全部输出训练的分类器进行 ROC-AUC 比较。进一步检验探针是否能识别 VGGT 高置信但错误的情况、是否支持下游系统弃权，以及是否能迁移到 PhotoTourism 和 ScanNet-1500 上的旋转失败，并通过层位分析判断失败信息来自单帧编码器还是跨视角融合层。还对另一模型 π^3 进行了类似分析。

Result: 在 HardMatch 的 held-out 类别上，基于 VGGT 冻结特征的线性探针预测位姿失败的 ROC-AUC 达 93.5%，显著高于 VGGT 自身点图置信度的 79.7% 和基于全部 VGGT 输出训练的分类器的 77.7%。该探针能够捕获 VGGT 自认为置信但实际失败的位姿，允许下游系统对不确定样本选择弃权，并可迁移到旋转失败场景，其解释能力超过仅由图像共视区域所能解释的范围。进一步分析表明，这种失败知识主要不在逐图像编码器中，而是在模型中途融合两个视角的层中产生，并在 π^3 上同样成立。

Conclusion: VGGT 等前馈 3D 重建模型虽然表面上不报告位姿不确定性，但其内部表征已经包含更丰富的自我失败信息。简单探针即可从冻结特征中恢复该信息，从而提升位姿可靠性、支持安全弃权，并具有一定跨模型与跨失败类型迁移能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models such as VGGT regress cameras and geometry in a single forward pass, but they report no uncertainty for the camera poses they predict: an incorrect relative pose looks no different from a correct one. We ask whether the frozen model nonetheless knows when its pose is wrong. On HardMatch, a benchmark of 1,000 deliberately difficult image pairs on which VGGT fails on 35% of pairs, a linear probe on VGGT's frozen features predicts pose failure on held-out categories with ROC-AUC 93.5%, compared with 79.7% for VGGT's own point-map confidence and 77.7% for a classifier trained on all of VGGT's outputs. The probe catches failures VGGT is confident about, lets a downstream system abstain on the pairs it distrusts, and transfers without retraining to rotation failures on PhotoTourism and ScanNet-1500, beyond what co-visibility explains. This knowledge is absent from the per-image encoder: it is computed partway through the layers that fuse the two views, and the same holds for a second model, $\pi^3$. In essence, we find that VGGT knows more about its own failures than it reports.

</details>


### [165] [Back to Basics: A Skill-Centric Approach to Generalist Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35823)
*Minh Pham-Dinh,Md Farhan Ishmam,Hernan Yupanqui,Reaz Hassan Joarder,Kenneth Marino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 MiniWeb、MiniWebGen 和 MiniWebAgent，强调浏览器智能体不应只增加练习量，而应针对可复用交互技能进行精准训练与组合规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浏览器智能体训练往往以任务成功与否为评价终点，难以诊断具体失败在哪类交互能力上；真实网页环境规模大、成本高且训练目标不够聚焦，导致智能体在可复用底层技能上存在系统性短板。

Method: 论文构建 MiniWeb：一个包含 65 个互联网站、15 个领域的微型真实网页环境，并设计 369 个人工标注任务，覆盖日常网页浏览、搜索与交易流程，同时标注 37 个可复用交互原语并归为 7 个技能族。随后提出 MiniWebGen，用于自动从 MiniWeb 生成经过验证的、以技能为中心的短程训练轨迹，对 Qwen3.5-4B 进行微调。进一步提出 MiniWebAgent，将训练后的执行器与规划器结合，规划器将完整任务分解为学习过的技能级步骤。

Result: 对十种开源权重智能体的基准测试发现，拖拽与手势类技能整体最弱，没有任何智能体能解决超过 24% 的相关实例。使用仅 3.1K 条已验证轨迹微调 Qwen3.5-4B 后，模型在 13 个未见网站上的单原语成功率提升 24.9 个百分点，且在所有技能族均有提升。但这些短程轨迹不能直接带来完整任务成功；MiniWebAgent 通过“先分解技能、再组合执行”的方式，在未见 MiniWeb 网站上将任务成功率提升 19.4 个百分点，并在外部基准上实现无训练迁移：WebArena-Lite 提升 9.3 个百分点，Online-Mind2Web 提升 16.1 个百分点，WebVoyager 提升 20.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 浏览器智能体的关键不仅是更多实践，而是练习正确的可复用技能。该工作提出一种紧凑训练范式：先直接训练原子化网页交互技能，再通过规划器将复杂任务分解为这些技能组合执行，从而提升未见任务和外部的泛化能力。

Abstract: Browser agents do not just need more practice. They need to practice the right things. We introduce MiniWeb, a realistic miniature of the web comprising 65 interconnected websites across 15 domains, where agents navigate, search, and transact as people do. Its 369 human-annotated tasks cover everyday workflows and label 37 reusable interaction primitives grouped into 7 skill families, allowing us to diagnose which skills fail rather than only whether a task succeeds. Benchmarking ten open-weight agents, we find that drag & gesture is consistently the weakest family, with no agent solving more than 24% of its instances. To address this gap, we introduce MiniWebGen, a framework that autonomously generates verified, skill-centric training data from MiniWeb. Fine-tuning Qwen3.5-4B on just 3.1K verified trajectories improves single-primitive success by 24.9pp across 13 unseen websites, with gains across every skill family. However, these trajectories teach short-horizon interactions, which do not directly translate to full-task success. We build on this result with MiniWebAgent, which pairs the trained executor with a planner that decomposes full tasks into the skill-level steps it learned to perform. This approach improves task success by 19.4pp points on unseen MiniWeb sites and transfers to external benchmarks, with gains of 9.3pp on WebArena-Lite, 16.1pp on Online-Mind2Web, and 20.0pp on WebVoyager, without training on their trajectories. We provide a simple recipe for compact browser agents: practice reusable skills directly, then compose them to solve unfamiliar tasks.

</details>


### [166] [Seeing Through Distractions: Context-Stable Attribution via the Least Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35827)
*Sai Ganesh Nagarajan,Toshinori Yamauchi,Hiroshi Kera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究视觉模型中“语境干扰项”在 Shapley 值类归因与 least core 之间的差异，证明弱平均边际单调性下的量化间隙界，并说明将 semivalue 权重移向更大上下文可减小有符号间隙，实验支持该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shapley 值类归因广泛用于机器学习解释，但在计算机视觉中，某些特征加入完整真实图像后反而降低目标类得分，尽管预测标签不变。此类“语境干扰项”揭示了传统加权和归因可能无法充分反映模型真实决策依赖，因此需要比较 semivalue 与 least core 的归因差异并给出理论刻画。

Method: 作者形式化定义 contextual distractors，并在 cooperative game 视角下分析不同 semivalues 与 least core 对特征归因的影响。通过引入弱平均边际单调性假设，推导 semivalue 归因与 least core 归因之间的量化差距上界，并给出基于“采样更小上下文概率”的充分条件。进一步分析权重移动策略，证明将 semivalue 权重从较小上下文移向较大上下文会降低有符号间隙。最后通过受控图像分类实验和 Salient ImageNet 上的评估验证理论结论。

Result: 理论结果建立了 semivalue 与 least core 归因之间的可计算差距界，并给出影响间隙大小和符号的充分条件。实验结果显示，语境干扰项确实导致 Shapley 类归因与 least core 之间存在可观测差异，且权重向更大上下文偏移能够减小有符号间隙，与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 在视觉特征归因中，传统 Shapley 值或 semivalue 解释可能因上下文依赖而产生偏差，尤其对语境干扰项的归因可能不稳定。least core 可视为一种更鲁棒的归因目标或校正基准。通过控制上下文采样权重和增大上下文规模，可以缩小 semivalue 与 least core 之间的差距。该工作为模型解释中的公平性、稳定性和语境敏感性提供了理论依据与实践指导。

Abstract: Shapley-value-based explanations are widely used for feature attribution in machine learning. However, in computer vision, a feature may reduce the target-class score when added to the complete genuine image, even when the predicted label remains unchanged. We formalize such features as contextual distractors and investigate their attributions under semivalues and the least core. Under a weak average marginal monotonicity assumption, we derive quantitative semivalue--least-core gap bounds and sufficient conditions based on the probability of sampling smaller contexts. We further show that shifting semivalue weights toward larger contexts decreases the signed gap. Finally, controlled image-classification experiments and evaluations on Salient ImageNet provide strong empirical support for our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [167] [OSWAM: An Effective World Action Model with One-Step Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35850)
*Guowen Zhang,Liyi Chen,Xu Hu,Pengfei Wang,Jingfan Chen,Chenhang He,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OSWAM，通过想象令牌初始化和特权信息蒸馏，实现一步扩散动作生成，显著降低World Action Models推理延迟并保持动作质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接策略世界动作模型将动作生成与显式未来视觉预测解耦，推理更高效，但仍需数十步去噪才能获得高质量动作，导致延迟大、难以实时部署；直接减少去噪步数会使非线性多步去噪轨迹退化，严重影响动作质量。

Method: 作者认为随机噪声初始化缺少结构化动作先验迫使模型迭代去噪以恢复连贯动作序列。因此构造想象令牌用于初始化扩散过程，提供物理先验和动作条件化表示；并引入带未来条件的特权信息蒸馏，增强面向未来的动作生成。最终实现一步扩散动作生成。

Result: 在自动驾驶和具身控制实验中，OSWAM仅需一步扩散即可生成高质量动作，性能与需要数十步的直接策略WAM和Joint-WAM相当甚至更优。相比直接策略WAM基线约获得3倍加速，在NVIDIA RTX 5090上支持实时编译推理，延迟低至40毫秒。

Conclusion: 通过引入结构化动作先验和未来感知蒸馏，OSWAM有效突破直接策略世界动作模型对多步去噪的依赖，为自动驾驶和具身控制提供了低延迟、可实时部署的一步动作生成方案。

Abstract: Direct-policy World Action Models (WAM) have demonstrated the benefits of decoupling action generation from explicit future visual prediction for efficient inference in autonomous driving and embodied control. However, they still require tens of denoising steps to achieve competitive action quality, incurring substantial latency and hindering real-time deployment. Simply reducing the number of denoising steps will collapse the nonlinear multi-step denoising trajectory into coarse updates, substantially degrading action quality. To reduce the denoising steps while enabling real-time action generation, we present an effective world action model with one-step action generation, namely OSWAM, for autonomous driving and embodied control. We argue that it is the absence of structured action priors in random noise initialization that forces direct-policy WAMs to recover coherent action sequences through iterative denoising. Hence, we construct imagination tokens to initialize the diffusion process, providing physical priors and action-conditioned representations. We further introduce privileged information distillation with future conditioning to enhance future-aware action generation. As a result, OSWAM can efficiently and effectively generate high quality actions in just one diffusion step. Our experiments demonstrate that OSWAM achieves comparable or even better results in both autonomous driving and embodied control than direct-policy WAM and Joint-WAM that require tens of steps. It obtains approximately $3\times$ speedup over direct-policy WAM baselines and supports real-time compiled inference with latency as low as 40ms on an NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPU. The project page is available at this https URL

</details>


### [168] [FL4N: Feed-forward Latent Model for 4D Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35854)
*Eunbeen Hong,Hyeonseo Yu,Honggyu An,Jooho Son,Hyunah Ko,Soowon Son,Chaehyun Kim,Jahyeok Koo,Jisoo Kim,Junhwa Hur,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FL4N提出了一种无需显式4D基元的单目前馈式4D新视角合成模型，通过统一潜变量表示联合建模空间结构与时间动态，实现高质量、实时且更稳定的动态场景渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单目前馈方法通常依赖显式4D高斯表示，容易在时间上出现不一致，并产生严重渲染伪影；而动态数据有限也增加了直接学习4D场景动态的难度。

Method: FL4N不构建显式4D图元，而是将4D场景先验编码为跨空间与时间的统一潜在表示。其核心思路是在预训练静态场景潜在先验之上学习时间残差，从而以较少的动态训练数据建模4D变化。同时引入轻量动态编码器，利用静态场景特征将物体运动与相机视差引起的表观变化解耦，并仅通过图像合成目标进行训练。

Result: FL4N在4D新视角合成质量上达到先进水平，同时具备实时渲染能力，并能泛化到真实世界视频。其学习到的潜在表示能够较好地将视角变化与时间变化解耦。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，相比显式4D基元表示，基于静态潜先验和残差式动态建模的统一潜在表示是一种更有效、更稳定的单目4D NVS范式，为数据受限条件下的动态场景渲染提供了新思路。

Abstract: 4D novel view synthesis (NVS) aims to render dynamic scenes across viewpoints and timestamps, which requires jointly inferring 3D structure and temporal dynamics. Existing monocular feed-forward methods often rely on explicit 4D Gaussian representations, which are prone to temporal inconsistency and severe rendering artifacts. We present FL4N, the first feed-forward latent 4D NVS model. Instead of relying on explicit 4D primitives, FL4N encodes a 4D scene prior into a unified latent representation across space and time. Our core insight is that learning temporal dynamics as residuals on top of a pretrained static latent prior enables effective 4D modeling with limited dynamic training data. To this end, we introduce a lightweight dynamic encoder that leverages static scene features to disentangle object motion from camera-induced parallax. Trained solely with an image synthesis objective, FL4N achieves state-of-the-art 4D NVS quality while rendering in real time. Its learned latent representation generalizes to real-world videos and disentangles viewpoint from time.

</details>


### [169] [VibeVideoBench:Evaluating Multimodal Agents on Code-Driven Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35864)
*Muxi Diao,Wenbin Xu,Lele Yang,Xuyang Ma,Yuzhe Chen,Jiankang Chen,Kaiyu Jiang,Changyi Liu,Haoyu Zhang,Hongyi Fu,Jianxiong Wang,Tianke Zhang,Haonan Fan,Yingxin Li,Bingyi Li,Xu Wang,Xuanyu Zheng,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Wentao Zhang,Weiran Xu,Kongming Liang,Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 VibeVideoBench，一个评估多模态智能体通过编写代码进行视频生成与合成的基准，包含 239 个中英任务、多层级证据化评测，并发现现有前沿智能体系统仍无法可靠完成高质量视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散视频模型虽然能够生成视觉效果吸引的短视频，但在需要精确公式、正确图表、规则一致的游戏、音画同步叙事等高约束任务中表现不可靠。相比之下，多模态智能体可以通过编写代码渲染视频帧并合成音频，提供更可控、可验证的视频生成路径。但目前缺乏系统评测此类代码驱动视频生成智能体能力的基准，因此作者提出 VibeVideoBench，以衡量智能体在真实任务约束下交付完整带声 MP4 视频的综合能力。

Method: 论文构建了一个面向代码驱动视频生成智能体的基准 VibeVideoBench。该基准包含 239 个自包含任务，覆盖中英文两种语言、12 个类别，例如数据叙事、音乐驱动视频、课堂教学和论文解释等。每个任务要求智能体在其原生运行框架和沙盒环境中，于 1 小时内完成一个带声音的最终 MP4 视频。评测采用分层且基于证据的方式：首先检查交付规范是否满足；其次使用约 1.3 万个与任务相关、可追溯至具体需求的 rubric 检查项；最后通过三组 VLM 评审面板评估五个任务锚定的质量维度，并用盲测人类偏好进行验证。作者进一步在 9 个前沿智能体系统上进行了 2151 次评分实验，以分析系统在视频生成、交付可靠性、任务完成度和质量方面的表现。

Result: 实验表明 VibeVideoBench 仍未被现有智能体系统充分解决。GPT-6 Astra 和 Claude Opus 5.5 表现相对领先，但即使最好的系统也在约四分之一任务上未能满足核心要求。除视频质量外，视频能否成功交付本身同样关键；约四分之三的失败视频源于最终渲染开始过晚、渲染速度过慢或从未开始渲染。此外，多个 VLM 评审系统之间在整体系统排名上具有较高一致性，也与人类偏好排序较为一致，但在单个视频层面的评价一致性仅为中等。

Conclusion: 代码驱动的多模态视频生成智能体相比传统扩散视频模型在可控性、可验证性和复杂内容合成方面具有潜力，但当前前沿系统在任务执行、渲染效率、交付可靠性和细节质量方面仍存在显著不足。VibeVideoBench 揭示了智能体视频生成中的关键瓶颈：不仅要看生成质量，还要看是否能按时、完整、可靠地交付可验收的视频。该基准可作为评估和改进智能体代码化媒体生成能力的重要测试平台。

Abstract: Diffusion video models produce striking clips, but they cannot reliably render an exact formula, a correct chart, a ruleconsistent game or narration synchronized to on-screen events. A different route is emerging: multimodal agents that write code to render every frame and synthesize every sound. We introduce VibeVideoBench, a benchmark for evaluating agents on code-driven video generation. Its 239 self-contained tasks in English and Chinese span 12 categories, from data stories and music-driven pieces to classroom lessons and paper explainers; each asks an agent, running in its native harness inside a sandbox, to deliver a finished MP4 with sound within one hour. Evaluation is hierarchical and evidence-based: program checks of the delivery spec, about 13k task-specific rubric checks traced to the requirements they enforce, and five quality dimensions anchored per task, judged by three VLM judge panels and validated with blind human preferences. Across nine frontier agent systems and 2,151 scored rollouts, the benchmark is far from solved: GPT-6 Astra and Claude Opus 5.5 lead clearly, yet even the best system fails a core requirement on about a quarter of tasks. Whether a video arrives at all matters as much as its quality, and three quarters of the lost videos are final renders started too late, run too slowly or never started. The judges agree with each other and with human preferences on system rankings, but only moderately on individual videos.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [TinyFlow: Breaking the Warping Bottleneck for Edge Optical Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33338)
*Xiao'ao Song,Chahat Deep Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TinyFlow 提出一种仅 1.26M 参数的无 warp 光流网络，用固定窗口相关性替代依赖光流的特征重采样，使整个网络能够以单一静态 INT8 计算图部署到资源受限边缘加速器，同时在 Sintel 等数据集上保持较高精度，并在单目视觉里程计任务中展现出良好实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能光流网络通常包含迭代式 coarse-to-fine refinement，并使用当前估计的光流对特征进行 warp。这种机制依赖 flow-dependent resampling 和 data-dependent memory access，导致计算图难以静态化、内存访问模式不固定，因而不适合部署在资源受限的边缘加速器上。

Method: 该方法保留迭代式 coarse-to-fine 光流估计结构，但将传统的依赖光流 warp 替换为在固定偏移集合上进行的相关性计算，从而消除动态重采样操作。配合量化感知微调、warp-teacher 知识蒸馏和 model souping，进一步降低 Float32 到 INT8 的精度损失，实现单一静态 INT8 图的端到端部署。

Result: TinyFlow 在 Sintel Clean、Sintel Final 和 FlyingChairs 上的 Float32 与 INT8 结果均优于 EdgeFlowNet 和 NanoFlowNet，且参数量比 EdgeFlowNet 少 54%。其 INT8 模型在 Sintel Clean/Final 上达到 6.29/7.13 EPE，量化造成的精度损失在 MPI-Sintel 上低于 0.2 EPE。在仅使用光流的单目视觉里程计实验中，该 INT8 模型在 TartanAir 和 UZH-FPV 的 11 个序列中有 7 个取得最低轨迹误差。

Conclusion: 通过用固定窗口相关性替代光流依赖的 warp，TinyFlow 将高精度光流网络转化为适合边缘加速器部署的静态 INT8 模型，在显著降低参数量和部署复杂度的同时保持了有竞争力的光流精度，并能够支持低误差的单目视觉里程计任务。

Abstract: High-performance optical flow networks are difficult to deploy on resource-constrained edge accelerators because their iterative refinement warps features by the current flow, which relies on flow-dependent resampling and data-dependent memory access. We present TinyFlow, a 1.26M-parameter warp-free optical flow network that preserves iterative coarse-to-fine refinement by replacing the warp with a fixed-window correlation over constant offsets, so the whole network compiles as a single static INT8 graph. Quantization-aware fine-tuning, warp-teacher distillation, and model souping reduce the Float32-to-INT8 penalty to below 0.2 end-point error (EPE) on MPI-Sintel. TinyFlow outperforms EdgeFlowNet and NanoFlowNet on Sintel Clean, Sintel Final, and FlyingChairs in both Float32 and INT8, while using 54% fewer parameters than EdgeFlowNet, and our INT8 model achieves 6.29/7.13 EPE on Sintel Clean/Final. In flow-only monocular visual odometry on TartanAir and UZH-FPV, the same INT8 model yields the lowest trajectory error on seven of eleven sequences.

</details>


### [171] [ReSight: Task-Driven Self-Improving Memory for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33487)
*Lirong Che,Yang Chen,Yimeng Su,Daqi Liu,Zhenyu Huang,Jiong Wang,Yuzhe Yang,Xu Cao,Chuang Wang,Jian Su,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ReSight：一种面向具身智能体的任务驱动型自改进记忆系统，可在任务需求出现时动态扩展历史观察的处理与访问能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在熟悉环境中已有大量历史观测，但“存在相关证据”并不等于“现有工具能完成任务所需操作”；传统记忆系统往往固定处理与查询能力，难以适应新任务带来的能力缺口。

Method: ReSight 保留关键帧及其时空关联，并将原始观测证据与可扩展的派生字段分离，使历史观测可被重新处理。系统采用双智能体架构：执行智能体在受限在线预算内调用注册工具完成任务，编码智能体在可编程工作区和更大探索预算下检查证据、编写程序、获取模型。通过 Explore-Distill-Mount（EDM）机制，从执行与探索记录中自动识别能力缺口，将探索得到的解决方案蒸馏为可复用的记忆构建器、查询工具和技能，从而扩展记忆表示与访问能力。

Result: 在三个公开基准上，ReSight 展示了有效的基础记忆访问与能力扩展。固定执行模型与在线预算后，EDM 将 OC-NaVQA 的平均位置误差从 33.10 m 降至 24.72 m，并将 FindingDory 的高层成功率从 38.3% 提升至 63.8%。在冻结工具与技能后，对未参与探索与蒸馏的 FindingDory episode，成功率仍提升 28.3 个百分点。真实机器人巡逻记忆实验进一步表明，该能力可被复用于后续查询与新记忆，并平均减少在线工具调用。

Conclusion: ReSight 通过将记忆表示、执行接口与离线自改进闭环结合，使具身智能体能从历史观测中按需扩展处理能力，显著提升了时空任务表现与跨任务、跨记忆的可复用性。

Abstract: Robots can use historical observations to answer spatiotemporal queries in familiar environments, but the presence of relevant evidence does not imply that existing tools support the operations a task requires. We introduce ReSight, a task-driven self-improving memory system for embodied agents that expands the capabilities for processing and accessing historical observations as task demands arise. ReSight retains keyframes and their spatiotemporal associations, separating observation evidence from extensible derived fields so that stored observations can be reprocessed for new needs. An execution agent and a coding agent share this memory through different interfaces: the former calls registered tools within a bounded online budget, while the latter uses a programmable workspace and a larger exploration budget to inspect evidence, write programs, and acquire models as needed. To realize capability expansion in ReSight, we develop Explore-Distill-Mount (EDM), which identifies capability gaps from execution and exploration records without task reference answers and converts exploration-derived solutions into reusable memory builders, query tools, and skills, extending memory representations and access capabilities as needed. Experiments on three public benchmarks demonstrate effective base memory access and capability expansion. With the execution model and online budget held fixed within each benchmark, EDM reduces mean position error on OC-NaVQA from 33.10 m to 24.72 m and improves high-level success on FindingDory from 38.3% to 63.8%. With the tools and skill frozen, success still improves by 28.3 percentage points on FindingDory episodes excluded from exploration and distillation. Experiments on real robot patrol memories further demonstrate reuse of these capabilities for subsequent queries and new memories, with fewer online tool calls on average.

</details>


### [172] [EXE: Egocentric Cross-Embodiment Pretraining via Factorized Latent Actions for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33685)
*Qingyuan Cai,Bo Zhang,Yongzhen Huang,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EXE 跨具身 egocentric 视频预训练框架，通过分解潜在动作表征分离共享操作先验与人类特定运动信号，从而提升机器人策略泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision-Language-Action 模型受限于机器人数据稀缺。人类第一视角操作视频数据丰富且语义多样，但存在 embodiment gap，难以直接提取可用于机器人策略学习的动作相关表征；需要在利用人类操作技能的同时避免人类特定运动学干扰机器人策略。

Method: 构建 Egocentric Cross(X)-Embodiment 预训练框架 EXE，从 egocentric 人类视频中学习 factorized latent action representation，将共享操作语义/先验与 embodiment-specific 的人类运动信号解耦，使机器人策略利用共享动作空间学习可泛化操作技能。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0、RoboCasa GR1 和真实世界操作任务中，相较于无预训练基线取得一致性能提升。该预训练知识可跨不同运动结构的具身形式迁移，同时惠及 parallel gripper 和 dexterous hand。

Conclusion: egocentric 人类视频可以作为高质量但需解耦的预训练来源；通过分离共享操作表征与 embodiment-specific 运动模式，机器人策略能够从人类演示中获得可迁移操作语义，缓解机器人数据稀缺并提升跨具身泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are bottlenecked by the scarcity of robot data. Egocentric human manipulation videos offer an abundant, diverse, and semantically-rich alternative that inherently captures manipulation intent. However, the embodiment gap makes it difficult to extract action-relevant representations from these videos. From the perspective of action execution, human hands and robots share analogous manipulation primitives---reaching, grasping, placing---yet exhibit distinct kinematics tied to their embodiments. This might motivate our key approach: egocentric video pretraining can yield shared action representations capturing transferable manipulation skills, while human-specific motion patterns should be disentangled to avoid interfering with robot policy learning. To this end, we propose EXE, an Egocentric Cross(X)-Embodiment pretraining framework that learns a factorized latent action representation from egocentric human videos, separating shared manipulation priors from embodiment-specific signals. By leveraging the shared action space, the robot policy acquires generalizable manipulation semantics from diverse human demonstrations without being biased by human-specific kinematics. This shared knowledge transfers across embodiments with different kinematic structures, enabling the same pretraining to benefit both parallel grippers and dexterous hands. EXE yields consistent gains over baseline without pretraining on RoboTwin 2.0, RoboCasa GR1, and real-world manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [173] [RoboReason: Boosting Robotics-Relevant VLM Reasoning Through Scalable Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33778)
*Sagnik Anupam,Utkarsh Lal,Aneesh Edara,Botong Zhang,Yao Tang,Jiatao Gu,Dinesh Jayaraman,Osbert Bastani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于空间与时间排序任务的视觉推理评估方法，用于测试机器人场景中的多模态大模型；并通过自动生成训练数据进行有监督微调与强化学习，显著提升模型在排序任务及下游机器人任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人操作场景中，Vision-Language Models（VLM）的能力评估困难且难以规模化。作者认为，与机器人感知密切相关的空间深度排序、像素左右排序以及时序帧排序任务，能够直接反映VLM是否具备可迁移到机器人下游任务的视觉推理能力。

Method: 使用来自立体相机和腕部相机的真实机器人图像，构建三类排序任务：1）根据语言描述对打乱的视频帧进行排序；2）根据距离相机的深度对物体排序；3）根据图像中的像素坐标从左到右对物体排序。同时通过改变待排序集合大小调节任务难度。进一步提出可扩展的自动训练数据生成流程，分别采用有监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）对VLM进行训练。

Result: 实验表明，多个先进VLM在这些空间和时间排序任务上表现不佳，说明其在真实机器人视觉推理任务中存在明显不足。经过自动生成的数据训练后，模型在排序任务上的表现显著提升，并且迁移到下游机器人任务和视觉推理任务时也有明显改进。

Conclusion: 基于真实机器人图像的空间与时间排序任务是一种有效、可扩大的VLM视觉推理评估方式。自动数据生成结合SFT和RL能够缓解VLM在机器人相关排序推理上的缺陷，提升其在机器人操作与视觉推理场景中的实际能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance when integrated into robot manipulation pipelines, but real-world evaluation of VLM capabilities on downstream robotics tasks is both difficult and expensive to scale. We propose that spatial and temporal ordering tasks using images from stereo and wrist cameras attached to real-world robots are particularly well-suited for evaluating visual reasoning capabilities, as they assess skills directly applicable to robots. We evaluate VLM reasoning abilities on three questions: ordering shuffled frames conditioned on a language description of the task, ordering objects depthwise based on distance from the camera, and ordering objects from left-to-right in pixel coordinates. The complexity of these tasks can also be modulated by changing the size of the set to sort. Our experiments show that several state-of-the-art vision-language models struggle on ordering tasks. To fix this, we propose a scalable autonomous training data generation pipeline, and show that both supervised finetuning and reinforcement-learning on the generated data can significantly improve VLM reasoning performance both on spatiotemporal ordering tasks as well as downstream robotics and visual reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [174] [One Scan is Enough: Demonstration-Free Adaptation for Language-Guided Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33794)
*Zhuoyuan Yu,Yuxing Long,Du Wang,Junyang Wang,Hongwei Fan,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出用一次短时 iPhone 场景扫描替代人工远程示范，自动构建语言引导导航的训练环境与指令，并通过免学习的 Pareto 效用适配策略，使真实机器人导航成功率大幅提升，实现无需人工示范的场景自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导视觉-语言-动作模型在新建筑或新场景中部署时，会因空间布局、视觉外观和语言描述分布的变化而逐步失效；传统解决方式需要在现场远程操控采集新轨迹或人工示范，成本高且难以规模化。因此，作者希望探索一种无需人工示范、可快速完成场景自适应的部署方式。

Method: 方法包含两个关键设计。首先，Scan2Instruction 将一次简短的 iPhone 扫描重建结果编译为完整训练环境，自动合成可执行的导航 episode，并生成与场景对齐的 grounded instructions。其次，PRPT-KS 利用重建场景中的 Pareto 效用以闭式形式适配策略，不依赖可学习的 critic，也不依赖人工标注，从而形成从扫描、数据合成到策略更新的闭环适配框架。

Result: 在五个真实环境中，所提出的 RealNav 达到 85% 的真实机器人导航成功率，而基于人工示范后训练的 RealNav baseline 仅为 49%。此外，在同一建筑中未扫描区域，RealNav 仍达到 82% 成功率，而相同 baseline 只有 41%，表明该方法不仅在已扫描场景有效，也具有一定区域外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作证明，仅凭一次低成本 iPhone 扫描即可替代大规模人工远程示范，构建可用于语言引导导航策略自适应的训练闭环。相比依赖人工示范的基线，该方法显著提高真实机器人部署成功率，使“无需人工示范的场景自适应”从理想目标变为可实际运行的方案。

Abstract: Deploying a language-guided navigation policy in a new building usually means watching it fail. Public-benchmark VLAs degrade scene by scene as layout, appearance, and language drift, and the standard fix of teleoperating fresh trajectories on site does not scale. We replace human demonstration with a single brief iPhone scan. Scan2Instruction compiles that scan into a complete training environment, automatically synthesizing executable navigation episodes with grounded instructions. PRPT-KS then adapts the policy through a closed-form Pareto utility read directly off the reconstructed scene, with no learned critic and no human labels. Across five real environments, the two designs together drive our RealNav to an 85% real-robot success rate, compared with 49% for a RealNav baseline post-trained on human demonstrations, and still achieve 82% in unscanned regions of the same buildings, compared with 41% for the same baseline. Our work turns demonstration-free scene adaptation for language-guided navigation from an aspiration into a working reality.

</details>


### [175] [Behavior Spatial Benchmark: Evaluating Spatial Understanding for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34051)
*Sirui Chen,Huang Huang,Qineng Wang,Wenlong Huang,Manling Li,Ruohan Zhang,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于 BEHAVIOR-1K 的 BEHAVIOR-Spatial 基准，用于评估 VLM/VLA 在机器人任务中的空间问答、目标定位和动作执行能力，并发现模型即便完成任务也可能未真正遵循空间指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务需要将空间语言映射到真实物理场景，但仅用任务成功率无法判断模型是否理解并遵循了具体空间指令，因此需要专门衡量空间理解如何转化为机器人行为。

Method: 构建包含 3,256 个空间问题、1,240 个定位查询以及 14 个操作任务和 10 个导航任务动作评估的基准；在保留配置以及固定训练布局下改变目标指令的场景中评估 VLM 空间问答、点级目标定位和 VLA 操作性能。

Result: 基线模型在保留配置上达到 41.6%，但在目标指令变化后降至 16.1%；VLM 适配和机器人数据预训练提升有限，而提供 oracle 视觉目标线索可将性能提升到 60.3% 和 38.0%。感知与操作之间的相关性依赖具体策略。

Conclusion: 除任务完成率外，还应单独评估空间指令遵循能力，并进一步研究空间感知性能何时、以及如何转化为实际机器人行为。

Abstract: Robot tasks require grounding spatial language in physical scenes, but task success alone cannot establish whether spatial instructions are followed. We present BEHAVIOR-Spatial, a benchmark built on BEHAVIOR-1K that measures spatial question answering, point-based target grounding, and robot action to examine how VLM spatial understanding relates to VLA behavior. It comprises 3,256 spatial questions across 49 activities, 1,240 grounding queries across 14 manipulation tasks, and action evaluations covering those 14 tasks and 10 navigation tasks. Our manipulation protocol evaluates held-out configurations and instruction following when the requested target changes within a fixed training layout. The baseline achieves 41.6% on held-out configurations but only 16.1% under changed instructions. VLM adaptation and robot-data pretraining provide limited improvements, whereas oracle visual target cues raise these rates to 60.3% and 38.0%, respectively. Perception evaluations reveal policy-dependent correlations between VLM question-answering and grounding accuracy and VLA manipulation performance. Together, these findings motivate evaluating spatial instruction following alongside task completion and examining when spatial perception performance translates into robot behavior. Project website: this https URL.

</details>


### [176] [Doppler-aided Position-Velocity Tracking of Hidden Objects using mmWave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34085)
*Yerin Cheon,Kush Garg,Akshat Dave*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出首个室内非视距毫米波雷达 Range-Azimuth-Doppler 数据集，并设计基于连续 RAD 序列的位置-速度联合估计跟踪器，实现隐藏移动目标低成本鲁棒追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内安全应用需跟踪机器人不可直接观测的目标；光学非视距方法受光照、几何和多径弱回波限制，而毫米波雷达可借墙面反射快速测距、测角和测速，但低分辨率造成空间歧义，且 Doppler 信息在现有室内 NLoS 定位中未被充分利用。

Method: 采集包含 576 个序列、720,856 帧的室内 NLoS 雷达数据集，同步记录位置与速度真值；将 NLoS 跟踪建模为序列位置-速度估计问题，用共享 3D 编码器处理连续帧完整 RAD 表征，并通过分支循环网络分别估计位置与速度，最后结合二者进行运动一致性轨迹精化。

Result: 在未见轨迹上取得 8.95 cm 位置误差和 1.75 cm/s 速度误差，并能泛化到包含移动人类目标的新场景，建立低成本雷达隐藏目标跟踪的基准。

Conclusion: 该工作证明保留并联合利用完整 Range-Azimuth-Doppler 动态信息可显著改善室内非视距低分辨率雷达跟踪，数据集与跟踪方法为隐藏移动目标感知提供了可复现基准。

Abstract: Safe indoor operation requires autonomous tracking of objects that are not directly visible to the robot. Optical non-line-of-sight (NLoS) methods depend on ambient lighting and scene geometry or on weak multi-bounce returns that need long acquisitions. Millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar offers an attractive alternative: walls act as reflectors for mmWave signals, and frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radars enable fast, practical measurement of range, angle, and velocity. However, the low resolution of these radars leads to spatially ambiguous observations. The Doppler information, although particularly sensitive to target motion, remains underexplored in indoor NLoS localization: existing methods emphasize spatial representations and either collapse or discard the Doppler dimension before learning. To leverage the complete Range-Azimuth-Doppler (RAD) information, we acquire the first indoor NLoS radar dataset that pairs sequential RAD measurements with synchronized position and velocity ground truth under controlled variations in motion: 576 sequences totaling 720,856 frames across 12 trajectories of a mobile robot moving along varying speeds, starting positions, and material configurations. We then formulate NLoS tracking as sequential position-velocity estimation rather than frame-wise localization: a shared 3-D encoder processes the full RAD representation of consecutive frames, separate recurrent branches estimate position and velocity, and post-inference refinement combines the two into a motion-consistent trajectory. Our method achieves 8.95cm position and 1.75cm/s velocity errors on held-out trajectories and generalizes to unseen scenarios involving a moving human target. Together, our dataset and tracker establish a benchmark for tracking hidden moving targets with a low-cost radar.

</details>


### [177] [RoboDuo: Attempt, Check and Atomic Recovery through Code for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34108)
*Jie Wang,Bo Zhang,Renzhi Lu,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboDuo，一种面向 Vision-Language-Action（VLA）模型的双系统闭环框架：通过代码将“编码智能体”与“全任务 VLA”和“原子技能 VLA”连接起来，在长程任务中检测执行是否完成，并在失败时调用原子 VLA 续作未完成步骤，从而提升任务成功率并实现对冻结全任务 VLA 的失败恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型通常将语言指令和当前观测直接映射为动作，但缺少显式判断“任务是否已完成”的闭环机制。对于长程任务，一旦某一步失败，模型可能无法察觉，导致整条任务失败。然而失败 episode 往往并未完全失败，而是仍处于“部分完成”状态；同时未完成步骤可被视为可复用原子技能，可跨不同任务组合调用。因此，作者希望利用编码智能体进行任务规划与完成度判断，并借助原子技能 VLA 对失败轨迹进行补救。

Method: 作者提出 RoboDuo，一个以代码为接口的闭环双系统框架。系统包含三个核心组件：1）编码智能体：负责生成程序/代码，不直接输出动作，而是决定调用哪个 VLA，并具备任务完成性检查能力；2）全任务 VLA：尝试执行完整任务；3）原子技能 VLA：负责执行可复用的原子操作，用于补全剩余步骤。执行流程为：编码智能体先让全任务 VLA 尝试整任务，然后判断是否完成；若失败，则由原子 VLA 完成剩余步骤。该框架无需更新已有全任务 VLA 的权重，即可包裹其进行增强。为了训练原子 VLA 和具有完成检查能力的编码智能体，作者从同一批 LIBERO 演示数据中构建了 LIBERO-Atomic、LIBERO-Code 和 LIBERO-Judge 三类数据集。

Result: 实验表明，RoboDuo 能稳定提升 X-VLA 和 π0.5 在 LIBERO 与 LIBERO-PRO 上的表现，并在长程任务中取得最大增益。此外，在真实机器人上，RoboDuo 能够对冻结的全任务 VLA 产生的失败 rollout 进行恢复，说明该框架具有实际部署价值和闭环纠错能力。

Conclusion: RoboDuo 通过将“代码化规划与完成性判断”与“全任务 VLA + 原子技能 VLA”相结合，为 VLA 系统提供了显式闭环失败恢复机制。其关键贡献在于：利用失败 episode 中的部分完成状态和可复用原子技能，将任务失败转化为可继续执行的分步补救问题；同时以代码作为轻量接口，使已有全任务 VLA 无需重新训练即可增强。结果表明该框架尤其适合长程任务，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map a language instruction and the current observation directly to actions, without checking whether the instruction has been completed. For long-horizon tasks, a single failed step therefore goes unnoticed and leaves the whole task unfinished. Such failed episodes, however, often end with the task partially complete. Moreover, the unfinished steps are reusable atomic skills that recur across demonstrations. A failed attempt can thus be recovered by composing with atomic skills. We propose RoboDuo, a closed-loop dual-system framework that uses code as the interface between a coding agent and two VLAs. The agent writes a program that lets a full-task VLA attempt the whole task and checks whether it is complete; on failure, an atomic VLA finishes the remaining steps. Since the agent emits no actions and only decides which VLA for execution, RoboDuo wraps an existing full-task VLA without updating its weights. To train the atomic VLA and the coding agent with the completion-checking ability, we build LIBERO-Atomic, LIBERO-Code, and LIBERO-Judge from a single set of LIBERO demonstrations. RoboDuo consistently improves X-VLA and $\pi_{0.5}$ on LIBERO and LIBERO-PRO, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks, and recovers failed rollouts of a frozen full-task VLA on a real robot.

</details>


### [178] [TRIAGE: Efficient Vision-Language-Action Inference Needs Both Reuse and Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34519)
*Sooyoung Kwon,Carlee Joe-Wong,Kyunghan Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出TRIAGE，针对VLA模型中视觉token导致的预填充延迟，根据token漂移自适应选择重算、复用KV缓存或剪枝，在保持接近全量重算的机器人操作成功率的同时显著降低嵌入式推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人和人形机器人需要在板载嵌入式设备上运行VLA模型，但每个控制步输入数百个视觉token，使语言骨干的预填充延迟随token数量增长而难以满足实时控制要求。现有免训练加速方法通常对所有被跳过token统一选择“剪枝”或“复用KV缓存”，但两者虽节省相近计算量，对不同token的动作误差影响并不相同。

Method: 论文通过token级诊断发现，选择“复用缓存”还是“剪枝”取决于该token的drift，即其浅层value自缓存计算后所发生的变化。实验表明，在drift阈值以下时，复用缓存不差于剪枝；超过阈值后，复用缓存可导致高达剪枝方案4.4倍的动作误差。基于该发现，作者提出TRIAGE：当缓存drift低于阈值且不过期时判定为有效；在固定重算预算下，优先重算重要且缓存无效的token，复用其他有效缓存token，并剪枝剩余token。该预算直接决定预填充延迟。

Result: 在LIBERO和SimplerEnv上，TRIAGE平均成功率保持在距全量重算3个百分点以内，同时在Jetson AGX Orin上将预填充延迟最高降低48%。仅重算7%的视觉token时，TRIAGE在LIBERO-Object上比最强基线高24.4个百分点。

Conclusion: TRIAGE通过token级drift诊断统一了剪枝和KV复用两类加速策略，根据缓存可靠性自适应决定跳过方式，以固定重算预算实现VLA模型在嵌入式机器人平台上的实时控制加速，同时保持接近全量重算的操作性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are increasingly used for robot manipulation because they map camera images and a language instruction directly to actions. Robots that cannot be tethered to a workstation, such as mobile manipulators and humanoids, must run them on onboard embedded boards, where the prefill latency of the language backbone grows with the hundreds of visual tokens processed at every control step. To meet real-time limits, training-free accelerators recompute only a subset of the visual tokens and skip the rest, either by pruning them or by reusing their cached key-value (KV) states. The two options save nearly the same computation, yet existing methods apply only one of them to every skipped token. Through a token-level diagnostic, we show that which option keeps the action closer to full recomputation depends on the token's drift, the change in its shallow-layer value since its cache was computed. On both backbones tested, reusing tokens below a drift threshold was no worse than pruning them, whereas beyond it, reuse yields up to 4.4x the action error of pruning; the same diagnostic calibrates this threshold per backbone. Building on this finding, we propose TRIAGE, which deems a cache valid when its drift is below this threshold and it is not too old. TRIAGE recomputes a fixed budget of tokens, prioritizing important tokens with invalid caches, reuses every other token with a valid cache, and prunes the rest; prune-only and reuse-only methods are the two extremes of this partition. Because the budget fixes the number of recomputed tokens, it directly sets the prefill latency. On LIBERO and SimplerEnv, TRIAGE stays within 3 pp of full recomputation in average success rate while cutting prefill latency on a Jetson AGX Orin by up to 48%, and with only 7% of visual tokens recomputed, it beats the strongest baseline by 24.4 pp on LIBERO-Object.

</details>


### [179] [Benchmarking Obstacle-Conditioned Safety in Household Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34674)
*Hyunwoong Bae,Jisu Han,Jiyeon Choi,Dohoon Kim,Taesup Moon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 OSMA-Bench 基准和语义敏感度指数 SSI，用于评估家庭导航机器人在不同障碍语义安全等级下是否能产生与之匹配的连续运动调整。实验发现现有 VLM 与 VLA 策略普遍存在“语义到运动接地”不足的问题：虽然能识别障碍危险性，却难以相应调整间隙、速度和平滑度；加入显式参数推理步骤可改善 VLM 策略的运动适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全家庭导航要求机器人根据障碍类型及其与自身的距离动态调节通过间隙、速度和运动平滑度，同时避免不必要的保守行为。现有评测往往不能检验模型的语义安全判断是否真正转化为连续运动层面的差异，因此缺乏能够评估“语义谨慎—运动响应”一致性的基准和指标。

Method: 作者构建空间匹配的场景集，只改变障碍类型，提出 OSMA-Bench 以进行受控比较。同时设计语义敏感度指数 SSI，衡量机器人在间隙、距离相关速度和运动平滑度等方面是否遵循障碍的谨慎程度排序。进一步在 OSMA-Bench 上评估基于视觉语言模型 VLM 的导航策略和视觉语言动作 VLA 策略，并分析其障碍识别能力与运动参数输出之间的一致性；针对 VLM 策略，在障碍识别与导航程序生成之间加入显式参数推理步骤。

Result: 实验表明，无论是 VLM 策略还是 VLA 策略，即使加入显式安全提示，也不能始终根据不同障碍的安全等级连续调整运动。模型能够区分障碍的危险性，但这种区分未能充分反映在实际生成的运动参数或动作中。对 VLM 策略而言，在识别阶段之后、导航程序生成之前加入参数推理步骤，能够提升其运动适应能力。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了当前视觉语言导航模型中的语义到运动接地缺口：模型具备语义层面的安全识别，但缺乏将其稳定转化为谨慎运动行为的能力。OSMA-Bench 和 SSI 为评估与改进这一能力提供了基准和量化指标，提示未来家庭机器人安全导航需要显式建模语义风险到运动参数的映射。

Abstract: Safe household navigation requires robots to regulate clearance and speed according to what an obstacle is and how close it is, without unnecessary conservatism. Yet existing evaluations do not test whether semantic safety distinctions are reflected in continuous motion. To that end, we introduce OSMA-Bench, an Obstacle-Conditioned Safe Motion Adaptation Benchmark built on spatially matched scenario sets that differ only in obstacle type. We also propose the \emph{Semantic Sensitivity Index} (SSI), which measures whether clearance and distance-dependent speed and smoothness follow the caution ordering of obstacles. Evaluating VLM-based and vision-language-action (VLA) policies on the benchmark, we find that neither consistently adapts its motion to obstacle safety, even with explicit safety prompts. Our analysis shows that the policies can correctly distinguish obstacles by safety relevance but fail to reflect this distinction in the motion parameters or actions they produce. For VLM-based policies, which recognize the obstacle and then generate a navigation program, adding an explicit parameter-inference step between these two stages improves adaptation. These findings expose a gap in \emph{semantic-to-motion grounding}, highlighting the need to translate semantic caution into appropriately safe motion.

</details>


### [180] [SceneAgent: An Agentic Pipeline from 3D and 4D Captures to Simulation Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34745)
*Luke Hollis,Tianxing Fan,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SceneAgent 提出一种代理式流水线，将 3D Gaussian Splatting、摄影测量、LiDAR、AI 生成 3DGS 和 4D 动态视频等视觉捕捉自动转化为可交互、可仿真、可用于策略学习的 3D/4D 仿真环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉捕捉虽然能提供细粒度场景外观，但要用于机器人仿真或强化学习，还需要完成物体重建、物理建模、误差修正和动态交互建模，传统流程复杂且容易累积中间错误。

Method: SceneAgent 通过多代理流水线协调关键步骤，包括物体分割、物理参数估计、部件分解、关节建模和可变形仿真，并利用基于仿真器的视觉检查与修复来校正中间错误。其输入涵盖 3DGS、photogrammetry、LiDAR、AI-generated 3DGS 和 4D 动态场景视频。为支持策略学习，系统还生成多样化物体变体“digital sisters”和脚本化示范。

Result: 在 GraspClutter6D 的杂乱抓取场景中，SceneAgent 提升了所有可评分 coding agent 的 1 cm F1 分数；其重建质量优于 SimFoundry，所有 agent 配置均超过 SimFoundry，最高达到 60.75%，而 SimFoundry 为 6.9%。在 4DCodeBench 的三个动态场景中，SceneAgent 将 Gemini 4 Argon 的平均动态 IoU 从 0.86% 提升到 99.09%，并展示了多样化仿真交互和初步真实机器人策略迁移。

Conclusion: SceneAgent 为从视觉数据到交互式仿真环境的自动转换提供了有效方案，能够显著改善静态场景重建、动态场景建模和下游策略学习性能，推动仿真环境在机器人操作与学习中的应用。

Abstract: Visual captures provide detailed scene appearance, but converting them into interactive simulation environments requires object reconstruction, physical modeling, and correction of intermediate errors. We present SceneAgent, an agentic pipeline that coordinates these steps through simulator-based visual inspection and repair. The pipeline processes 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), photogrammetry, LiDAR captures, AI-generated 3DGS scenes, and 4D videos of dynamic scenes, and combines object segmentation, physical parameter estimation, part decomposition, articulation, and deformable simulation. To support policy learning, SceneAgent generates diverse object variants ("digital sisters") and scripted demonstrations. On a cluttered bin scene from GraspClutter6D, SceneAgent raises F1 at 1 cm for every coding agent with scorable outputs both with and without its skills, and its reconstructions are better than SimFoundry: all agent configurations exceed SimFoundry's F1 score, with a best of 60.75% versus 6.9%. On three dynamic scenes from 4DCodeBench, SceneAgent raises the mean dynamic IoU of Gemini 4 Argon from 0.86% to 99.09%. We further demonstrate diverse simulated interactions and preliminary policy transfer to a real robot.

</details>


### [181] [Thermal-Headroom Routing for Efficient Robot Perception in Extreme Heat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35231)
*Sparsh Roy,Kevin Zhou,Arhaan Shah,Shreyan Garg,Vihaan Joshi-Apte,Maxim Kudryashov,Nanda Cheruvanthala*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Thermal-Headroom Routing（THR），一种面向机器人感知的轻量级模型路由策略，利用闭式 RC 热模型预测处理器温度，提前排除会触发热降频的检测器配置，以在精度、时延和能耗之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人感知系统在高温环境中可能因处理器热降频而无法满足推理时延和能效要求。现有 latency-aware routing、固定阈值恒温器或占空比限制等策略往往依赖静态调参，在环境温度或热学参数变化时适应性不足，因此需要一种可预测热余量并动态选择检测配置的机制。

Method: 作者设计 Thermal-Headroom Routing（THR），通过轻量级闭式 RC 热模型预测处理器温度，并在运行时排除预测会越过热降频阈值的 YOLO11 配置。实验使用 4 种 YOLO11 配置、7 条真实感知轨迹、8 个环境温度以及 20 个随机种子，与 latency-aware routing、固定阈值 thermostat、duty cap 和等计算预算控制方法比较。

Result: 在 45–55°C 高温和严格推理截止时间下，THR 在仿真中消除热降频，相比 latency-belief router 将截止时间未满足率降低 3.9–7.5 个百分点，并将按时帧的能耗降低约 12–13%。在另一项采用非重叠帧执行的 deadline-paced 实验中，未满足率降低 6.2–9.5 个百分点。Apple M5 上的测量支持单节点加热近似，但未验证硬件热降频行为。

Conclusion: THR 在环境条件或热参数偏离静态策略调参点时能提升资源受限机器人感知的能效与时限可靠性，但静态 thermostat 或 duty cap 在固定工况下可能达到同等效果；预测式热路由的优势主要体现在环境不确定或热特性变化场景中。

Abstract: Adaptive robot perception systems must balance detector accuracy, inference latency, and energy consumption, particularly when high ambient temperatures cause processors to thermally throttle. We propose Thermal-Headroom Routing (THR), a lightweight model-selection policy that forecasts processor temperature using a closed-form RC thermal model and excludes detector configurations predicted to cross a throttling threshold. We evaluate THR with four YOLO11 configurations across seven real-world perception traces, eight ambient temperatures, and 20 randomized seeds, comparing it with latency-aware routing, fixed-threshold thermostats, duty caps, and a matched-compute-budget control. At ambient temperatures of 45-55 degrees C and a tight inference deadline, THR eliminates throttling in simulation, reduces deadline misses by 3.9-7.5 percentage points, and reduces energy per on-time frame by approximately 12-13% relative to a latency-belief router. A separate experiment using deadline-paced, nonoverlapping frame execution yields miss-rate reductions of 6.2-9.5 percentage points. A tuned thermostat or duty cap can match THR at a fixed operating point; predictive routing is most useful when ambient conditions or calibrated thermal parameters differ from those used to tune static policies. Measurements on an Apple M5 support the single-node heating approximation but do not validate hardware throttling. The results characterize the conditions under which thermal forecasting improves the efficiency and timeliness of resource-constrained robot perception.

</details>


### [182] [RoboDoctor: Training-Free Recursive Self-Improvement for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35604)
*Heyang Yu,Haomin Li,Sihang Li,Jixin Yan,Shiyun Peng,Wanqi Zhong,Tianyu Xin,Yuan Yao,Xiaoyu Tian,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出RoboDoctor：一种无需训练的机器人执行期适应框架，围绕执行反馈进行诊断、落地动作和持久经验复用，通过harness、intervention和operation三个修订层提升机器人任务执行中的适应性与决策修正能力，并在RoboDojo-Sim任务集上取得初步效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略和大语言模型智能体各自具备互补能力，但真实任务执行中需要根据物理交互后果持续调整策略与动作。现有方法若模型权重固定，仍需在执行阶段利用反馈完成适应，因此作者将“执行”本身作为适应发生的关键环节，研究如何通过实时反馈和跨经验记录改进后续决策与物理操作方法。

Method: 提出RoboDoctor训练无关框架，包含三个修订目标：1）harness负责在策略与智能体之间分配控制；2）intervention决定何时介入辅助，同时保留已有策略的有效行为；3）operation根据观察到的物理效果改进具体操作方法。框架将诊断、接地动作和持久经验连接起来，并用经验记录保存决策原因、尝试方法、执行结果及复用条件。

Result: 在包含54个RoboDojo-Sim任务条目的精选归档上，RoboDoctor取得五维平均得分57.10，原生成功率49.44%。记录的交互案例显示系统能够实现策略变化、选择性修正和抓取方式修订，说明执行期适应机制具有实际作用。

Conclusion: RoboDoctor证明了在固定模型权重条件下，通过执行期反馈和经验记录改进机器人任务决策与操作方法的可行性。但当前结果主要刻画了所评测系统中的执行期适应现象，仍需受控实验进一步分离和验证跨episode经验记录的独立贡献。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies and language-model agents provide complementary capabilities, but completing a task also requires adapting their use to the consequences of physical interaction. We study execution as a locus of adaptation: feedback guides the current action and supplies evidence for revising subsequent decisions under fixed model weights. We introduce RoboDoctor, a training-free framework that connects diagnosis, grounded action, and persistent experience across three revision targets. The harness allocates control between policy and agent; intervention determines when to assist while preserving useful policy behavior; and operation refines physical methods through observed effects. Linked experience records retain the reason for a decision, the attempted method, its outcome, and conditions for reuse. On a curated archive of 54 RoboDojo-Sim task entries, RoboDoctor achieves a five-dimension mean score of 57.10, with a native success rate of 49.44%. Recorded interactions illustrate strategy changes, selective correction, and grasp revision. These findings characterize execution-time adaptation in the evaluated system; controlled trials remain necessary to isolate the contribution of cross-episode experience.

</details>


### [183] [VPR-Space: A Benchmark for Visual Place Recognition in Space Analog Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35642)
*Richard Fechner,Peer Neubert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出面向地外表面场景的视觉地点识别基准 VPR-Space，构建数据库、定义可服务于 SLAM 的 VPR 真值，并评测多种 VPR 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流视觉 SLAM 和视觉地点识别在城市场景及特征丰富的自然环境中表现较好，但在月球、火星等大面积、弱纹理、低特征的地外表面环境中面临严重挑战，需要专门基准评估 VPR 在空间探索任务中的实际可用性。

Method: 作者基于模拟地外环境的陆地场景收集 16 组数据库-查询 VPR 数据集；针对 VPR 判定地点是否匹配的病态问题，不再使用固定位置和视角阈值定义真值，而是以该匹配是否有利于后续 SLAM 为准则，利用最优相对位姿估计器组合的性能构建 VPR 真值矩阵，从而要求 VPR 识别那些能带来有效相对位姿估计的图像对；最后评测 9 种代表性 VPR 方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，现有先进 VPR 方法在地外表面类场景中仍有明显性能不足，存在较大改进空间，并可为未来研究方向提供参考；论文还提供数据集预发布版本。

Conclusion: VPR-Space 为评估面向地外 SLAM 的视觉地点识别能力提供了新基准和任务导向的真值定义方式，揭示了当前 VPR 方法在太空场景中的局限，有助于推动适合月球、火星等环境的鲁棒地点识别研究。

Abstract: Visual Place Recognition (VPR) is an important means for loop closure detection in visual Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM). While state-of-the-art VPR and SLAM approaches excel in urban and feature-rich natural environments, the vast and feature-poor environments of extraterrestrial surfaces like on the Moon or Mars pose severe challenges for existing approaches. This paper presents a novel benchmark, VPR-Space, to evaluate VPR capabilities on extraterrestrial surfaces. There are three contributions: (1) We collected sixteen VPR database-query datasets based on existing data from Space Analog Domains (SAD), terrestrial environments that resemble properties of the Moon or Mars. The collection includes a range of challenges regarding environment, viewpoint change, and illumination conditions. (2) To address the ill-posed nature of VPR, we propose a novel approach to generate the ground-truth information in VPR benchmarks. Instead of defining that a query and datbase image show the same place if and only if their camera poses are within fixed position and viewing direction thresholds, we propose to make the decision dependent on whether the match would be valuable in the downstream SLAM task. To avoid the overhead and restrictions of evaluating VPR in-the-loop within SLAM systems, we provide VPR ground-truth (GT) matrices based on the performance of an ensemble of state-of-the-art Relative Pose Estimators (RPE). Under this GT we require a VPR method to identify exactly those image pairs for which a subsequently applied RPE is expected to provide a valuable pose estimate in a SLAM context. (3) We evaluated nine state-of-the-art VPR approaches. The results show considerable room for improvement and indicate directions for future research. We provide a pre-release version of the dataset on github: $\texttt{this https URL}$

</details>


### [184] [Pretraining Knows What, Simulation Teaches How: Compositional Generalization in a Sim-Trained Robot Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35817)
*Benjamin Pikus,Daniel Saunders,Mircea Mironenco,Salman Mohammadi,Mathew Mathew,Sirou Zhu,Divya Makkar,Xuhua Huang,Qinbo Li,Supreeth Narasimhaswamy,Siyuan Cen,Thomas Neff,Dejia Xu,Shyamal Buch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZAP是一种将现成小型视觉语言模型（VLM）通过简单工具接口训练为机器人策略的方法；仅依赖预训练模型、仿真任务上的监督微调和GRPO，不使用真实机器人数据，即可在真实任务中达到高成功率，并展现出跨物体、目标、策略、语言和机械臂的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略需要同时具备两类能力：理解场景中任务要求，以及控制机器人完成动作。已有方法往往依赖真实机器人数据或大模型高成本推理，本文试图利用现成VLM的世界理解与推理能力，以低成本方式让小型VLM学会机器人控制，并提升开放任务泛化性。

Method: 作者提出Zero-real Agent Policy（ZAP）：使用一个27B参数预训练模型引导世界理解与推理，再通过简单工具框架将机器人控制动作暴露给模型；随后在简单仿真任务上进行监督微调（SFT）和GRPO，以训练模型生成可执行动作。训练过程不依赖任何真实世界机器人数据。之后在真实任务、未见物体/目标/策略/语言以及不同机器人本体上进行评测。

Result: ZAP在真实任务上达到96.7%的成功率，在相同工具框架下与GPT-6 Astra相当，并显著超过MolmoAct2 VLA的33.3%；同时比GPT-6 Astra快3倍且成本低17倍。模型可泛化到训练中未出现的物体、目标和策略，例如在试管架中放置试管并按提示选择不同孔位、在空中双臂交接试管、在井字棋板上画X、下棋。在未见单臂Franka机器人上，ZAP在RoboLab-120达到55.9%，比领先VLA/WAM基线高4.9个百分点。

Conclusion: ZAP表明，通过工具化训练框架和仅仿真数据，小型现成VLM可以被转化为高效的真实机器人控制策略；该方法无需真实机器人训练数据即可取得高成功率、低延迟低成本和强跨任务/跨本体泛化，为机器人策略的规模化构建提供了低成本范式。

Abstract: A robot policy needs to understand what a task is asking for in a scene, and how to move the robot to accomplish that task. To achieve these two skills, we introduce Zero-real Agent Policy (ZAP), a recipe that teaches a small off-the-shelf vision-language model to control a robot via a simple tool harness. We use a pretrained 27B parameter model to bootstrap world understanding and reasoning, followed by supervised fine-tuning and GRPO on simple simulated tasks to teach it how to move the robot. Without any real-world robot data during training, ZAP reaches 96.7% success on real-world tasks, matching GPT-6 Astra in our harness and outperforming the MolmoAct2 VLA (33.3%), while running 3x faster and 17x cheaper than GPT-6 Astra. We demonstrate that ZAP also generalizes to completely new objects, goals, strategies, and language by seating a test tube in a rack (and choosing a different hole in the rack when prompted), handing a test tube between arms mid-air, drawing an X on a tic-tac-toe board, and playing chess. These goals and types of manipulation don't appear in our training data. Furthermore, ZAP also transfers to an unseen embodiment. On a single-arm Franka, ZAP reaches 55.9% on RoboLab-120, 4.9 percentage points ahead of the leading VLA/WAM baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Video World Models Need Predictable Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34663)
*Mattia Scardecchia,Sherry Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该摘要提出了一种面向视频世界模型的潜空间优化方法，通过时间可预测性目标和过去条件化解码器，在保持像素级预测精度的同时显著降低训练与推理成本，并提升长时域和分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型有望成为安全、可扩展的具身策略学习与评估工具，但现有方法存在三个关键瓶颈：长时域 rollout 容易累积误差；分布外泛化能力不足；推理阶段计算开销大。传统方法通常依赖面向像素重建的自编码器潜空间，但该潜空间并非为动力学预测优化；而面向潜在规划的世界模型虽然优化潜空间可预测性，却往往牺牲对像素空间未来观测的准确生成。因此，作者希望同时保留二者优势：既学习易于预测的潜空间几何结构，又维持高保真像素生成能力。

Method: 论文的核心思想是将时间可预测性作为视频世界模型潜空间的重要设计准则。作者探索用于表示学习的时间目标，使潜空间中的未来预测尽可能简单；同时采用过去条件化的解码器架构，以改善像素空间预测。整体上，该方法在相同训练预算下优化自编码器训练流程，使模型既适合动力学建模，又能够准确生成未来视频观测。此外，论文还引入视觉语言模型基于像素空间预测判断任务成功，以评估世界模型与真实策略评估的一致性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在相同训练预算下带来稳定性能提升，包括：推理成本最高降低至原来的五分之一；训练速度最高提升七倍以上；在分布外和长时域场景中，像素误差与感知误差降低 15%–24%；与真实世界策略评估相比，其像素空间预测在任务成功判断上表现出更高一致性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，时间可预测性是视频世界模型潜空间设计中的关键因素。将时间表示学习目标与过去条件化解码器结合，能够同时提升像素生成质量、分布外与长时域预测稳定性，并显著降低训练和推理成本。该工作为面向动力学建模的自编码器训练提供了具体设计指导，推动视频世界模型向更高效、更可靠的物理策略评估与规划方向发展。

Abstract: Video world models trained on large-scale robot data promise a safe and scalable approach for learning and evaluating policies that act in the physical world. However, they still struggle with long-horizon rollouts and out-of-distribution generalization, and they remain computationally demanding at inference time. Typically, video world models make predictions in the latent space of a reconstruction-trained autoencoder, which however is not directly optimized for dynamics modeling. On the other hand, world models designed for latent planning usually optimize latents to be predictable, but they forgo accurate prediction of observations in pixel space. In this paper, we show that it is possible to achieve the best of both worlds: a latent geometry optimized to make future prediction as simple as possible without sacrificing accurate prediction of future observations. More precisely, we find that temporal objectives for representation learning, paired with a past-conditioned decoder architecture, lead to consistent improvements in generative video world models at the same training budget. The gains include up to 5x cheaper inference, over 7x faster training, and 15-24% lower pixel and perceptual errors out of distribution and over long horizons. Our world models also agree more closely with real-world policy evaluation when using a vision-language model to judge task success from their pixel-space predictions. Overall, our study indicates that temporal predictability is an important design criterion for the latent space of video world models, and provides concrete guidance on how to incorporate it into the training of autoencoders.

</details>
